1.1 . vimalakalāśrayābhinavasṛṣṭimahā jananī bharitatanuśca pañcamukhaguptarucirjanakaḥ |
1.1 . tadubhayayāmalasphuritabhāvavisargamayaṃ hṛdayamanuttarāmṛtakulaṃ mama sasphuratāt || 1
1.2 . naumi citpratibhāṃ devīṃ parāṃ bhairavayoginīm |
1.2 . mātṛmānaprameyāṃśaśūlāmbujakṛtāspadām || 2
1.3 . naumi devīṃ śarīrasthāṃ nṛtyato bhairavākṛte |
1.3 . prāvṛṇmeghaghanavyomavidyullekhāvilāsinīm || 3
1.4 . dīptajyotiśchaṭāpluṣṭabhedabandhatrayaṃ sphurat |
1.4 . stājjñānaśūlaṃ satpakṣavipakṣotkartanakṣamam || 4
1.5 . svātantryaśaktiḥ kramasaṃsisṛkṣā kramātmatā ceti vibhorvibhūtiḥ |
1.5 . tadeva devītrayamantarāstāmanuttaraṃ me prathayatsvarūpam || 5
1.6 . taddevatāvibhavabhāvimahāmarīcicakreśvarāyitanijasthitireka eva |
1.6 . devīsuto gaṇapatiḥ sphuradindukāntiḥ samyaksamucchalayatānmama saṃvidabdhim || 6
1.7 . rāgāruṇāṃ granthibilāvakīrṇa yo jālamātānavitānavṛtti |
1.7 . kalombhitaṃ bāhyapathe cakāra stānme sa macchandavibhuḥ prasannaḥ || 7
1.8 . traiyambakābhihitasantatitāmraparṇīsanmauktikaprakarakāntiviśeṣabhājaḥ |
1.8 . pūrve jayanti guravo guruśāstrasindhukallolakelikalanāmalakarṇadhārāḥ || 8
1.9 . jayati gurureka eva śrīśrīkaṇṭho bhuvi prathitaḥ |
1.9 . tadaparamūrtirbhagavānmaheśvaro bhūtirājaśca || 9
1.10 . śrīsomānandabodhaśrīmadutpalaviniḥsṛtāḥ |
1.10 . jayanti saṃvidāmodasandarbhā dikprasarpiṇaḥ || 10
1.11 . tadāsvādabharāveśabṛṃhitāṃ matiṣaṭpadīm |
1.11 . gurorlakṣmaṇaguptasya nādasaṃmohinīṃ numaḥ || 11
1.12 . yaḥ pūrṇānandaviśrāntasarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ |
1.12 . sa śrīcukhulako diśyādiṣṭaṃ me gururuttamaḥ || 12
1.13 . jayatājjagaduddhṛtikṣamo'sau bhagavatyā saha śaṃbhunātha ekaḥ |
1.13 . yadudīritaśāsanāṃśubhirme prakaṭo'yaṃ gahano'pi śāstramārgaḥ || 13
1.14 . santi paddhatayaścitrāḥ srotobhedeṣu bhūyasā |
1.14 . anuttaraṣaḍardhārthakrame tvekāpi nekṣyate || 14
1.15 . ityahaṃ bahuśaḥ sadbhiḥ śiṣyasabrahmacāribhiḥ |
1.15 . arthito racaye spaṣṭāṃ pūrṇārthā prakriyāmimām || 15
1.16 . śrībhaṭṭanāthacaraṇābjayugāttathā śrībhaṭṭārikāṃghriyugalādgurusantatiryā |
1.16 . bodhānyapāśaviṣanuttadupāsanotthabodhojjvalo'bhinavagupta idaṃ karoti || 16
1.17 . na tadastīha yanna śrī-mālinīvijayottare |
1.17 . devadevena nirdiṣṭaṃ svaśabdenātha liṅgataḥ || 17
1.18 . daśāṣṭādaśavasvaṣṭabhinnaṃ yacchāsanaṃ vibhoḥ |
1.18 . tatsāraṃ trikaśāstraṃ hi tatsāraṃ mālinīmatam || 18
1.19 . ato'trāntargataṃ sarva saṃpradāyojjhitairbudhaiḥ |
1.19 . adṛṣṭa prakaṭīkurmo gurunāthājñayā vayam || 19
1.20 . abhinavaguptasya kṛtiḥ seyaṃ yasyoditā gurubhirākhyā |
1.20 . trinayanacaraṇasaroruhacintanalabdhaprasiddhiriti || 20
1.21 . śrīśambhunāthabhāskaracaraṇanipātaprabhāpagatasaṃkocam |
1.21 . abhinavaguptahṛdambujametadvicinuta maheśapūjanahetoḥ || 21

ādivākyaṃ

1.22 . iha tāvatsamasteṣu śāstreṣu parigīyate |
1.22 . ajñānaṃ saṃsṛterheturjñānaṃ mokṣaikakāraṇam || 22
1.23 . malamajñānamicchanti saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam |
1.23 . iti proktaṃ tathā va śrīmalinīvijayottare || 23
1.24 . viśeṣaṇena buddhisthe saṃsārottarakālike |
1.24 . saṃbhāvanāṃ nirasyaitadabhāve mokṣamabravīt || 24
1.25 . ajñānamiti na jñānābhāvaścātiprasaṅgataḥ |
1.25 . sa hi loṣṭādike'pyasti na ca tasyāsti saṃsṛtiḥ || 25
1.26 . ato jñeyasya tattvasya sāmastyenāprathātmakam |
1.26 . jñānameva tadajñānaṃ śivasūtreṣu bhāṣitam || 26
1.27 . caitanyamātmā jñānaṃ ca bandha ityatra sūtrayoḥ |
1.27 . saṃśleṣetarayogāśyāmayamarthaḥ pradarśitaḥ || 27
1.28 . caitanyamiti bhāvāntaḥ śabdaḥ svātantryamātrakam |
1.28 . anākṣipraviśeṣaṃ sadāha sūtre purātane || 28
1.29 . dvitīyena tu sūtreṇa kriyāṃ vā karaṇaṃ ca vā |
1.29 . bruvatā tasya cinmātrarūpasya dvaitamucyate || 29
1.30 . dvaitaprathā tadajñānaṃ tucchatvādbandha ucyate |
1.30 . tata eva samucchedyamityāvṛttyānirūpitam || 30
1.31 . svatantrātmātiriktastu tuccho' tuccho'pi kaścana |
1.31 . na mokṣo nāma tannāsya pṛthaṅnāmāpi gṛhyate || 31
1.32 . yattu jñeyasatattvasya pūrṇapūrṇaprathātmakam |
1.32 . taduttarottaraṃ jñānaṃ tattatsaṃsāraśāntidam || 32
1.33 . rāgādyakaluṣo'smyantaḥśūnyo'haṃ kartṛtojjhitaḥ |
1.33 . itthaṃ samāsavyāsābhyāṃ jñānaṃ muñcati tāvataḥ || 33
1.34 . tasmānmukto'pyavacchedādavacchedāntarasthiteḥ |
1.34 . amukta eva muktastu sarvāvacchedavarjitaḥ || 34
1.35 . yattu jñeyasatattvasya jñānaṃ sarvātmanojjhitam |
1.35 . avacchedairna tatkutrāpyajñānaṃ satyamuktidam || 35
1.36 . jñānājñānasvarūpaṃ yaduktaṃ pratyekamapyadaḥ |
1.36 . dvidhā pauruṣabauddhatvabhidoktaṃ śivaśāsane || 36
1.37 . tatra puṃso yadajñānaṃ malākhyaṃ tajjamapyaya |
1.37 . svapūrṇacitkriyārūpaśivatāvaraṇātmakam || 37
1.38 . saṃkocidṛkkriyārūpaṃ tatpaśoravikalpitam |
1.38 . tadajñānaṃ na budghyaṃśo'dhyavasāyādyabhāvataḥ || 38
1.39 . ahamitthamidaṃ vedmītyevamadhyavasāyinī |
1.39 . ṣaṭkañcukābilāṇūtthapratibimbanato yadā || 39
1.40 . dhīrjāyate tadā tādṛgjñānamajñānaśabditam |
1.40 . bauddhaṃ tasya ca tatpauṃsnaṃ poṣaṇīyaṃ ca poṣṭṛca || 40
1.41 . kṣīṇe tu paśusaṃskāre puṃsaḥ prāptaparasthiteḥ |
1.41 . vikasvaraṃ tadvijñānaṃ pauruṣaṃ nirvikalpakam || 41
1.42 . vikasvarāvikalpātmajñānaucityena yāvasā |
1.42 . tadbauddhaṃ yasya tatpauṃsnaṃ prāgvatpoṣyaṃ ca poṣṭṛ ca || 42
1.43 . tatra dīkṣādinā pauṃsnamajñānaṃ dhvaṃsi yadyapi |
1.43 . tathāpi taccharīrānte tajjñānaṃ vyajyate sphuṭam || 43
1.44 . bauddhajñānena tu yadā bauddhamajñānajṛmbhitam |
1.44 . vilīyate tadā jīvanmuktiḥ karatale sthitā || 44
1.45 . dīkṣāpi bauddhavijñānapūrvā satyaṃ vimocikā |
1.45 . tena tatrāpi bauddhasya jñānasyāsti pradhānatā || 45
1.46 . jñānājñānāgataṃ caitaddvitvaṃ svāyambhuve rurau |
1.46 . mataṅgādau kṛtaṃ śrīmatkheṭapālādidaiśikaiḥ || 46
1.47 . tathāvidhāvasāyātmabauddhavijñānasampade |
1.47 . śāstrameva pradhānaṃ yajjñeyatattvapradarśakam || 47
1.48 . dīkṣayā galite'pyantarajñāne pauruṣātmani |
1.48 . dhīgatasyānivṛttatvādvikalpo'pi hi saṃbhaveta || 48
1.49 . dehasadbhāvaparyantamātmabhāvo yato dhiyi |
1.49 . dehānte'pi na mokṣaḥ syātpauruṣājñānahānitaḥ || 49
1.50 . bauddhājñānanivṛttau tu vikalponmūlanāddhruvam |
1.50 . tadaiva mokṣa ityuktaṃ dhātrā śrīmanniśāṭane || 50
1.51 . vikalpayuktacitastu piṇḍapātācchivaṃ brajet |
1.51 . itarastu tadaiveti śāstrasyātra pradhānataḥ || 51
1.52 . jñeyasya hi paraṃ tattvaṃ yaḥ prakāśātmakaḥ śivaḥ |
1.52 . nahyaprakāśarūpasya prākāśyaṃ vastutāpi vā || 52
1.53 . avastutāpi bhāvānāṃ camatkāraikagocarā |
1.53 . yatkuḍyasadṛśī neyaṃ dhīravastvetadityapi || 53
1.54 . prakāśo nāma yaścāyaṃ sarvatraiva prakāśate |
1.54 . anapahnavanīyatvātkiṃ tasminmānakalpanaiḥ || 54
1.55 . pramāṇānyapi vastūnāṃ jīvitaṃ yāni tanvate |
1.55 . teṣāmapi paro jīvaḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ || 55
1.56 . sarvāpahnavahevākadharmāpyevaṃ hi vartate |
1.56 . jñānamātmārthamityetanneti māṃ prati bhāsate || 56
1.57 . apahnutau sādhane vā vastūnāmādyamīdṛśam |
1.57 . yattatra ke pramāṇānāmupapattyupayogite || 57
1.58 . [58 missing]||
1.59 . kāmike tata evoktaṃ hetuvādavivarjitam |
1.59 . tasya devātidevasya parāpekṣā na vidyate || 59
1.60 . parasya tadapekṣatvātsvatantro'yamataḥ sthitaḥ |
1.60 . anapekṣasya vaśino deśakālākṛtikramāḥ || 60
1.61 . niyatā neti sa vibhurnityo viśvākṛtiḥ śivaḥ |
1.61 . vibhutvātsarvago nityabhāvādādyantavarjitaḥ || 61
1.62 . viśvākṛtitvāccidacittadvaicitryāvabhāsakaḥ |
1.62 . tato'sya bahurūpatvamuktaṃ dīkṣottarādike || 62
1.63 . bhuvanaṃ vigraho jyotiḥ khaṃ śabdo mantra eva ca |
1.63 . bindunādādisaṃbhinnaḥ ṣaḍvidhaḥ śiva ucyate || 63
1.64 . yo yadātmakatāniṣṭhastadbhāvaṃ sa prapadyate |
1.64 . vyomādiśabdavijñānātparo mokṣo na saṃśayaḥ || 64
1.65 . viśvākṛtitve devasya tadetaccopalakṣaṇam |
1.65 . anavacchinnatārūḍhāvavacchedalaye'sya ca || 65
1.66 . uktaṃ ca kāmike devaḥ sarvākṛtirnirākṛtiḥ |
1.66 . jaladarpaṇavattena sarvaṃ vyāptaṃ carācaram || 66
1.67 . na cāsya vimutādyo'yaṃ dharmo'nyonyaṃ vibhidyate |
1.67 . eka evāsya dharmo'sau sarvākṣepeṇa vartate || 67
1.68 . tena svātantryaśaktyaiva yukta ityāñjaso vidhiḥ |
1.68 . bahuśaktitvamapyasya tacchaktyaivāviyuktatā || 68
1.69 . śaktiśca nāma bhāvasya svaṃ rūpaṃ mātṛkalpitam |
1.69 . tenādvayaḥ sa evāpi śaktimatparikalpane || 69
1.70 . mātṛklṛpte hi devasya tatra tatra vapuṣyalam |
1.70 . ko bhedo vastuto vahnerdagdhṛpaktṛtvayoriva || 70
1.71 . na vāsau paramārthena na kiṃcidbhāsanādṛte |
1.71 . nahyasti kiṃcittacchaktitadvadbhedo'pi vāstavaḥ || 71
1.72 . svaśaktyudrekajanakaṃ tādātmyādvastuno hi yat |
1.72 . śaktistadapi devyevaṃ bhāntyapyanyasvarūpiṇī || 72
1.73 . śivaścāluptavibhavastathā sṛṣṭo'vabhāsate |
1.73 . svasaṃvinmātṛmakure svātantryādbhāvanādiṣu || 73
1.74 . tasmādyena mukhenaiṣa bhātyanaṃśo'pi tattathā |
1.74 . śaktirityeṣa vastveva śaktitadvatkramaḥ sphuṭaḥ || 74
1.75 . śrīmatkiraṇaśāstre ca tatpraśnottarapūrvakam |
1.75 . anubhāvo vikalpo'pi mānaso na manaḥ śive || 75
1.76 . avijñāya śivaṃ dīkṣā kathamityatra cottaram |
1.76 . kṣudhādyanubhavo naiva vikalpo nahi mānasaḥ || 76
1.77 . rasādyanadhyakṣatve'pi rūpādeva yathā tarum |
1.77 . vikalpo vetti tadvattu nādabindvādinā śivam || 77
1.78 . bahuśaktitvamasyoktaṃ śivasya yadato mahān |
1.78 . kalātattvapurārṇāṇupadādirbhedavistaraḥ || 78
1.79 . sṛṣṭisthititirodhānasaṃhārānugrahādi ca |
1.79 . turyamityapi devasya bahuśaktitvajṛmbhitam || 79
1.80 . jāgratsvapnasuṣuptānyatadatītāni yānyapi |
1.80 . tānyapyamuṣya nāthasya svātantryalaharībharaḥ || 80
1.81 . mahāmantreśamantreśamantrāḥ śivapurogamāḥ |
1.81 . akalau sakalaśceti śivasyaiva vibhūtayaḥ || 81
1.82 . tattvagrāmasya sarvasya dharmaḥ syādanapāyavān |
1.82 . ātmaiva hi svabhāvātmetyuktaṃ śrītriśiromate || 82
1.83 . hṛdisthaṃ sarvadehasthaṃ svabhāvasthaṃ susūkṣmakam |
1.83 . sāmūhyaṃ caiva tattvānāṃ grāmaśabdena kīrtitam || 83
1.84 . ātmaiva dharma ityuktaḥ śivāmṛtapariplutaḥ |
1.84 . prakāśāvasthitaṃ jñānaṃ bhāvābhāvādimadhyataḥ || 84
1.85 . svasthāne vartanaṃ jñeyaṃ draṣṭṛtvaṃ vigatāvṛti |
1.85 . viviktavastukathitaśuddhavijñānanirmalaḥ || 85
1.86 . grāmadharmavṛttiruktastasya sarvaṃ prasiddhyati |
1.86 . ūrdhva tyaktvādho viśetsa rāmastho madhyadeśagaḥ || 86
1.87 . gatiḥ sthānaṃ svapnajāgradunmeṣaṇanimeṣaṇe |
1.87 . dhāvanaṃ plavanaṃ caiva āyāsaḥ śaktivedanam || 87
1.88 . buddhibhedāstathā bhāvāḥ saṃjñāḥ karmāṇyanekaśaḥ |
1.88 . eṣa rāmo vyāpako'tra śivaḥ paramakāraṇam || 88
1.89 . kalmaṣakṣīṇamanasā smṛtimātranirodhanāt |
1.89 . dhyāyate paramaṃ dhyeyaṃ gamāgamapade sthitam || 89
1.90 . paraṃ śivaṃ tu vrajati bhairavākhyaṃ japādapi |
1.90 . tatsvarūpaṃ japaḥ prokto bhāvābhāvapadacyutaḥ || 90
1.91 . tadatrāpi tadīyena svātantryeṇopakalpitaḥ |
1.91 . dūrāsannādiko bhedaścitsvātantryavyapekṣayā || 91
1.92 . evaṃ svātantryapūrṇatvādatidurghaṭakāryayam |
1.92 . kena nāma na rūpeṇa bhāsate parameśvaraḥ || 92
1.93 . nirāvaraṇamābhāti bhātyābṛtanijātmakaḥ |
1.93 . āvṛtānāvṛto bhāti bahudhā bhedasaṃgamāt || 93
1.94 . iti śaktitrayaṃ nāthe svātantryāparanāmakam |
1.94 . icchādibhirabhikhyābhirgurubhiḥ prakaṭīkṛtam || 94
1.95 . devo hyanvarthaśāstroktaiḥ śabdaiḥ samupadiśyate |
1.95 . mahābhairavadevo'yaṃ patiryaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ || 95
1.96 . viśvaṃ bibharti pūraṇadhāraṇayogena tena ca śriyate |
1.96 . savimarśatayā rava rūpataśca saṃsārabhīruhitakṛcca || 96
1.97 . saṃsārabhītijanitādravātparāmarśato'pi hṛdi jātaḥ |
1.97 . prakaṭībhūtaṃ bhavabhayavimarśanaṃ śaktipātato yena || 97
1.98 . nakṣatraprerakakālatattvasaṃśoṣakāriṇo ye ca |
1.98 . kālagrāsasamādhānarasikamanaḥsu teṣu ca prakaṭaḥ || 98
1.99 . saṃkocipaśujanabhiye yāsāṃ ravaṇaṃ svakaraṇadevīnām |
1.99 . antarbahiścaturvidhakhecaryādikagaṇasyāpi || 99
1.100 . tasya svāmī saṃsāravṛttivighaṭanamahābhīmaḥ |
1.100 . bhairava iti gurubhirimairanvarthaiḥ saṃstutaḥ śāstre || 100
1.101 . heyopādeyakathāvirahe svānandaghanatayocchalanam |
1.101 . krīḍā sarvotkarṣeṇavartanecchā tathā svatantratvam || 101
1.102 . vyavaharaṇamabhinne'pi svātmani bhedena saṃjalpaḥ |
1.102 . nikhilāvabhāsanācca dyotanamasya stutiryataḥ sakalam || 102
1.103 . tatpravaṇamātmalābhātprabhṛti samaste'pi kartavye |
1.103 . bodhātmakaḥ samastakriyāmayo dṛkkriyāguṇaśca gatiḥ || 103
1.104 . iti nirvacanaiḥ śivatanuśāstre gurubhiḥ smṛto devaḥ |
1.104 . śāsanarodhanapālanapācanayogātsa sarvamupakurute |
1.104 . tena patiḥ śreyomaya eva śivo nāśivaṃ kimapi tatra || 104
1.105 . īdṛgrūpaṃ kiyadapi rudropendrādiṣu sphuredyena |
1.105 . tenāvacchedanude paramamahatpadaviśeṣaṇamupāttam || 105
1.106 . iti yajjñeyasatattvaṃ darśyate tacchivājñayā |
1.106 . mayā svasaṃvitsattarkapatiśāstratrikakramāt || 106
1.107 . tasya śaktaya evaitāstisro bhānti parādikāḥ |
1.107 . sṛṣṭau sthitau laye turye tenaitā dvādaśoditāḥ || 107
1.108 . tāvānpūrṇasvabhāvo'sau paramaḥ śiva ucyate |
1.108 . tenātropāsakāḥ sākṣāttatraiva pariniṣṭhitāḥ || 108
1.109 . tāsāmapi ca bhedāṃśanyūnādhikyādiyojanam |
1.109 . tatsvātantryabalādeva śāstreṣu paribhāṣitam || 109
1.110 . ekavīro yāmalo'tha triśaktiścaturātmakaḥ |
1.110 . pañcamūrtiḥ ṣaḍātmāyaṃ saptako'ṣṭakabhūṣitaḥ || 110
1.111 . navātmā daśadikchaktirekādaśakalātmakaḥ |
1.111 . dvādaśāramahācakranāyako bhairavastviti || 111
1.112 . evaṃ yāvatsahasrāre niḥsaṃkhyāre'pi vā prabhuḥ |
1.112 . viśvacakre maheśāno viśvaśaktirvijṛmbhate || 112
1.113 . teṣāmapi ca cakrāṇā svavargānugamātmanā |
1.113 . aikyena cakrago bhedastatra tatra nirūpitaḥ || 113
1.114 . catuṣṣaḍdvirdvigaṇanāyogāttraiśirase mate |
1.114 . ṣaṭcakreśvaratā nāthasyoktā citranijākṛteḥ || 114
1.115 . nāmāni cakradevīnāṃ tatra kṛtyavibhedataḥ |
1.115 . saumyaraudrākṛtidhyānayogīnyanvarthakalpanāt || 115
1.116 . ekasya saṃvinnāthasya hyāntarī pratibhā tanuḥ |
1.116 . saumyaṃ vānyanmitaṃ saṃvidūrmicakramupāsyate || 116
1.117 . asya syātpuṣṭirityeṣā saṃviddevī tathoditāt |
1.117 . dhyānātsaṃjalpasaṃmiśrādvyāpārāccāpi bāhyataḥ || 117
1.118 . sphuṭībhūtā satī bhāti tasya tādṛkphalapradā |
1.118 . puṣṭiḥ śuṣkasya sarasībhāvo jalamataḥ sitam || 118
1.119 . anugamya tato dhyānaṃ tatpradhānaṃ pratanyate |
1.119 . ye ca svabhāvato varṇā rasaniḥṣyandino yathā || 119
1.120 . dantyauṣṭhyadantyaprāyāste kaiścidvarṇaiḥ kṛtāḥ saha |
1.120 . taṃ bījabhāvamāgatya saṃvidaṃ sphuṭayanti tām || 120
1.121 . puṣṭiṃ kuru rasenainamāpyāyaya tarāmiti |
1.121 . saṃjalpo'pi vikalpātmā kiṃ tāmeva na pūrayet || 121
1.122 . amṛteyamidaṃ kṣīramidaṃ sarpirbalāvaham |
1.122 . tenāsya bījaṃ puṣṇīyāmityenāṃ pūrayetkriyām || 122
1.123 . tasmādviśveśvaro bodhabhairavaḥ samupāsyate |
1.123 . avacchedānavacchidbhyāṃ bhogamokṣārthibhirjanaiḥ || 123
1.124 . ye'pyanyadevatābhaktā ityato gururādiśat |
1.124 . ye bodhādvyatiriktaṃ hi kiṃcidyājyatayā viduḥ || 124
1.125 . te'pi vedyaṃ viviñcānā bodhābhedena manvate |
1.125 . tenāvicchinnatāmarśarūpāhantāprathātmanaḥ || 125
1.126 . svayaṃ-prathasya na vidhiḥ sṛṣṭyātmāsya ca pūrvagaḥ |
1.126 . vedyā hi devatāsṛṣṭiḥ śakterhetoḥ samutthitā || 126
1.127 . ahaṃrūpā tu saṃvittirnityā svaprathanātmikā |
1.127 . vidhirniyogastryaṃśā ca bhāvanā codanātmikā || 127
1.128 . tadekasiddhā indrādyā vidhipūrvā hi devatāḥ |
1.128 . ahaṃbodhastu na tathā te tu saṃvedyarūpatām || 128
1.129 . unmagnāmeva paśyantastaṃ vidanto'pi no viduḥ |
1.129 . taduktaṃ na vidurmāṃ tu tattvenātaścalanti te || 129
1.130 . calanaṃ tu vyavacchinnarūpatāpattireva yā |
1.130 . devāndevayajo yāntītyādi tena nyarūpyata || 130
1.131 . nimajjya vedyatāṃ ye tu tatra saṃvinmayīṃ sthitim |
1.131 . viduste hyanavacchinnaṃ tadbhaktā api yānti mām || 131
1.132 . sarvatrātra hyahaṃśabdo bodhamātraikavācakaḥ |
1.132 . sa bhoktṛprabhuśabdābhyāṃ yājyayaṣṭṭatayoditaḥ || 132
1.133 . yājamānī saṃvideva yājyā nānyeti coditam |
1.133 . na tvākṛtiḥ kuto'pyanyā devatā na hi socitā || 133
1.134 . vidhiśca noktaḥ ko'pyatra mantrādi vṛttidhāma vā |
1.134 . so'yamātmānamāvṛtya sthito jaḍapadaṃ gataḥ || 134
1.135 . āvṛtānāvṛtātmā tu devādisthāvarāntagaḥ |
1.135 . jaḍājaḍasyāpyetasya dvairūpyasyāsti citratā || 135
1.136 . tasya svatantrabhāvo hi kiṃ kiṃ yanna vicintayet |
1.136 . taduktaṃ triśiraḥśāstre saṃbuddha iti vetti yaḥ || 136
1.136 . jñeyabhāvo hi ciddharmastacchāyācchādayenna tām || 136  |
1.137 . tenājaḍasya bhāgasya pudgalāṇvādisaṃjñinaḥ |
1.137 . anāvaraṇabhāgāṃśe vaicitryaṃ bahudhā sthitam || 137
1.138 . saṃvidrūpe na bhedo'sti vāstavo yadyapi dhruve |
1.138 . tathāpyāvṛtinirhāsatāratamyātsa lakṣyate || 138
1.139 . tadvistareṇa vakṣyāmaḥ śaktipātavinirṇaye |
1.139 . samāpya paratāṃ sthaulyaprasaṃge carcayiṣyate || 139
1.140 . ataḥ kaṃcitpramātāraṃ prati prathayate vibhuḥ |
1.140 . pūrṇameva nijaṃ rūpaṃ kaṃcidaṃśāṃśikākramāt || 140
1.141 . viśvabhāvaikabhāvātmasvarūpaprathanaṃ hi yat |
1.141 . aṇūnāṃ tatparaṃ jñānaṃ tadanyadaparaṃ bahu || 141
1.142 . tacca sākṣādupāyena tadupāyādināpi ca |
1.142 . prathamānaṃ vicitrābhirbhaṃgībhiriha bhidyate || 142
1.143 . tatrāpi svaparadvāradvāritvātsarvaśoṃśaśaḥ |
1.143 . vyavadhānāvyavadhinā bhūyānbhedaḥ pravartate || 143
1.144 . jñānasya cābhyupāyo yo na tadajñānamucyate |
1.144 . jñānameva tu tatsūkṣmaṃ paraṃ tvicchātmakaṃ matam || 144
1.145 . upāyopeyabhāvastu jñānasya sthaulyaviśramaḥ |
1.145 . eṣaiva ca kriyāśaktirbandhamokṣaikakāraṇam || 145
1.146 . tatrādye svaparāmarśe nirvikalpaikadhāmani |
1.146 . yatsphuretprakaṭaṃ sākṣāttadicchākhyaṃ prakīrtitam || 146
1.147 . yathā visphuritadṛśāmanusandhiṃ vināpyalam |
1.147 . bhāti bhāvaḥ sphuṭastadvatkeṣāmapi śivātmatā || 147
1.148 . bhūyo bhūyo vikalpāṃśaniścayakramacarcanāt |
1.148 . yatparāmarśamabhyeti jñānopāyaṃ tu tadviduḥ || 148
1.149 . yattu tatkalpanāklṛptabahirbhūtārthasādhanam |
1.149 . kriyopāyaṃ tadāmnātaṃ bhedo nātrāpavargagaḥ || 149
1.150 . yato nānyā kriyā nāma jñānameva hi tattathā |
1.150 . rūḍheryogāntatāṃ prāptamiti śrīgamaśāsane || 150
1.151 . yogo nānyaḥ kriyā nānyā tattvārūḍhā hi yā matiḥ |
1.151 . svacittavāsanāśāntau sā kriyetyabhidhīyate || 151
1.152 . svacitte vāsanāḥ karmamalamāyāprasūtayaḥ |
1.152 . tāsāṃ śāntinimittaṃ yā matiḥ saṃvitsvabhāvikā || 152
1.153 . sā dehārambhibāhyasthatattvabrātādhiśāyinī |
1.153 . kriyā saiva ca yogaḥ syāttattvānāṃ cillayīkṛtau || 153
1.154 . loke'pi kila gacchāmītyevamantaḥ sphuraiva yā |
1.154 . sā dehaṃ deśamakṣāṃścāpyāviśantī gatikriyā || 154
1.155 . tasmātkriyāpi yā nāma jñānameva hi sā tataḥ |
1.155 . jñānameva vimokṣāya yuktaṃ caitadudāhṛtam || 155
1.156 . mokṣo hi nāma naivānyaḥ svarūpaprathanaṃ hi saḥ |
1.156 . svarūpaṃ cātmanaḥ saṃvinnānyattatra tu yāḥ punaḥ || 156
1.157 . kriyādikāḥ śaktayastāḥ saṃvidrūpādhikā nahi |
1.157 . asaṃvidrūpatāyogāddharmiṇaścānirūpaṇāt || 157
1.158 . parameśvaraśāstre hi na ca kāṇādadṛṣṭivat |
1.158 . śaktīnāṃ dharmarūpāṇāmāśrayaḥ ko'pi kathyate || 158
1.159 . tataśca dṛkkriyecchādyā bhinnāścecchaktayastathā |
1.159 . ekaḥ śiva itīyaṃ vāgvastuśūnyaiva jāyate || 159
1.160 . tasmātsaṃvittvamevaitatsvātantryaṃ yattadapyalam |
1.160 . vivicyamānaṃ bahvīṣu paryavasyati śaktiṣu || 160
1.161 . yataścātmaprathā mokṣastannehāśaṅkyamīdṛśam |
1.161 . nāvaśyaṃ kāraṇātkārya tajjñānyapi na mucyate || 161
1.162 . yato jñānena mokṣasya yā hetuphalatoditā |
1.162 . na sā mukhyā tato nāyaṃ prasaṃga iti niścitam || 162
1.163 . evaṃ jñānasvabhāvaiva kriyā sthūlatvamātmani |
1.163 . yato vahati tenāsyāṃ citratā dṛśyatāṃ kila || 163
1.164 . kriyopāye'bhyupāyānāṃ grāhyabāhyavibhedinām |
1.164 . bhedopabhedavaividhyānniḥsaṃkhyatvamavāntarāt || 164
1.165 . anena caitatpradhvastaṃ yatkecana śaśaṅkire |
1.165 . upāyabhedānmokṣe'pi bhedaḥ syāditi sūrayaḥ || 165
1.166 . malatacchaktividhvaṃsatirobhūcyutimadhyataḥ |
1.166 . hetubhede'pi no bhinnā ghaṭadhvaṃsādivṛttivat || 166
1.167 . tadetattrividhatvaṃ hi śāstre śrīpūrvanāmani |
1.167 . ādeśi parameśitrā samāveśavinirṇaye || 167
1.168 . akiṃciccintakasyaiva guruṇā pratibodhataḥ |
1.168 . utpadyate ya āveśaḥ śāmbhavo'sāvidīritaḥ || 168
1.169 . uccārarahitaṃ vastu cetasaiva vicintayan |
1.169 . yaṃ samāveśamāpnoti śāktaḥ so'trābhidhīyate || 169
1.170 . uccārakaraṇadhyānavarṇasthānaprakalpanaiḥ |
1.170 . yo bhavetsa samāveśaḥ samyagāṇava ucyate || 170
1.171 . akiṃciccintakasyeti vikalpānupayogitā |
1.171 . tayā ca jhaṭiti jñeyasamāpattirnirūpyate || 171
1.172 . sā kathaṃ bhavatītyāha guruṇātigarīyasā |
1.172 . jñeyābhimukhabodhena drākprarūḍhatvaśālinā || 172
1.173 . tṛtīyārthe tasi vyākhyā vā vaiyadhikaraṇyataḥ |
1.173 . āveśaścāsvatantrasya svatadrūpanimajjanāt || 173
1.174 . paratadrūpatā śambhorādyācchaktyavibhāginaḥ |
1.174 . tenāyamatra vākyārtho vijñeyaṃ pronmiṣatsvayam || 174
1.175 . vināpi niścayena drākmātṛdarpaṇabimbitam |
1.175 . mātāramadharīkurvatsvāṃ vibhūtiṃ pradarśayat || 175
1.176 . āste hṛdayanairmalyātiśaye tāratamyataḥ |
1.176 . jñeyaṃ dvidhā ca cinmātraṃ jaḍaṃ cādyaṃ ca kalpitam || 176
1.177 . itarattu tathā satyaṃ tadvibhāgo'yamīdṛśaḥ |
1.177 . jaḍena yaḥ samāveśaḥ sapraticchandakākṛtiḥ || 177
1.178 . caitanyena samāveśastādātmyaṃ nāparaṃ kila |
1.178 . tenāvikalpā saṃvittirbhāvanādyanapekṣiṇī || 178
1.179 . śivatādātmyamāpannā samāveśo'tra śāṃbhavaḥ |
1.179 . tatprasādātpunaḥ paścādbhāvino'tra viniścayāḥ || 179
1.180 . santu tādātmyamāpannā na tu teṣāmupāyatā |
1.180 . vikalpāpekṣayā mānamavikalpamiti bruvan || 180
1.181 . pratyukta eva siddhaṃ hi vikalpenānugamyate |
1.181 . gṛhītamiti suspaṣṭā niścayasya yataḥ prathā || 181
1.182 . gṛhṇāmītyavikalpaikyabalāttu pratipadyate |
1.182 . avikalpātmasaṃvittau yā sphurattaiva vastunaḥ || 182
1.183 . sā siddhirna vikalpāttu vastvapekṣāvivarjitāt |
1.183 . kevalaṃ saṃvidaḥ so'yaṃ nairmalyetaraviśramaḥ || 183
1.184 . yadvikalpānapekṣatvasāpekṣatve nijātmani |
1.184 . niśīthe'pi maṇijñānī vidyutkālapradarśitān || 184
1.185 . tāṃstānviśeṣāṃścinute ratnānāṃ bhūyasāmapi |
1.185 . nairmalyaṃ saṃvidaścedaṃ pūrvābhyāsavaśādatho || 185
1.186 . aniyantreśvarecchāta ityetaccarcayiṣyate |
1.186 . pañcāśadvidhatā cāsya samāveśasya varṇitā || 186
1.187 . tattvaṣaṭtriṃśakaitatsthasphuṭabhedābhisandhitaḥ |
1.187 . etattattvāntare yatpuṃvidyāśaktyātmakaṃ trayam || 187
1.188 . ambhodhikāṣṭhājvalanasaṃkhyairbhedairyataḥ kramāt |
1.188 . puṃvidyāśaktisaṃjñaṃ yattatsarvavyāpakaṃ yataḥ || 188
1.189 . avyāpakebhyastenedaṃ bhedena gaṇitaṃ kila |
1.189 . aśuddhiśuddhyamānatvaśuddhitastu mitho'pi tat || 189
1.190 . bhūtānyadhyakṣasiddhāni kāryahetvanumeyataḥ |
1.190 . tattvavargātpṛthagbhūtasamākhyānyata eva hi || 190
1.191 . sarvapratītisadbhāvagocaraṃ bhūtameva hi |
1.191 . viduścatuṣṭaye cātra sāvakāśe tadāsthitim || 191
1.192 . rudraśaktisamāveśaḥ pañcadhā nanu carcyate |
1.192 . ko'vakāśo bhavettatra bhautāveśādivarṇane || 192
1.193 . prasaṃgādetaditicetsamādhiḥ saṃbhavannayam |
1.193 . nāsmākaṃ mānasāvarjī loko bhinnaruciryataḥ || 193
1.194 . ucyate dvaitaśāstreṣu parameśādvibheditā |
1.194 . bhūtādīnāṃ yathā sātra na tathā dvayavarjite || 194
1.195 . yāvānṣaṭtriṃśakaḥ so'yaṃ yadanyadapi kiṃcana |
1.195 . etāvatī mahādevī rudraśaktiranargalā || 195
1.196 . tata eva dvitīye'sminnadhikāre nyarūpyata |
1.196 . dharāderviśvarūpatvaṃ pāñcadaśyādibhedataḥ || 196
1.197 . tasmādyathā purasthe'rthe guṇādyaṃśāṃśikāmukhāt |
1.197 . niraṃśabhāvasaṃbodhastathaivātrāpi budhyatām || 197
1.198 . ata evāvikalpatvadhrauvyaprābhavavaibhavaiḥ |
1.198 . anyairvā śaktirūpatvāddharmaiḥ svasamavāyibhiḥ || 198
1.199 . sarvaśo'pyatha vāṃśena taṃ vibhuṃ parameśvaram |
1.199 . upāsate vikalpaughasaṃskārādye śrutotthitāt || 199
1.200 . te tattatsvavikalpāntaḥsphurattaddharmapāṭavāt |
1.200 . dharmiṇaṃ pūrṇadharmaughamabhedenādhiśerate || 200
1.201 . ūcivānata eva śrīvidyādhipatirādarāt |
1.201 . tvatsvarūpamavikalpamakṣajā kalpane na viṣayīkaroti cet |
1.201 . antarullikhitacitrasaṃvido no bhaveyuranubhūtayaḥ sphuṭāḥ || 201
1.202 . taduktaṃ śrīmataṅgādau svaśaktikiraṇātmakam |
1.202 . atha patyuradhiṣṭhānamityādyuktaṃ viśeṣaṇaiḥ || 202
1.203 . tasyāṃ divi sudīptātmā niṣkampo'calamūrtimān |
1.203 . kāṣṭhā saiva parā sūkṣmā sarvadikkāmṛtātmikā || 203
1.204 . pradhvastāvaraṇā śāntā vastumātrātilālasā |
1.204 . ādyantoparatā sādhvī mūrtitvenopacaryate || 204
1.205 . tathopacārasyātraitannimitaṃ saprayojanam |
1.205 . tanmukhā sphuṭatā dharmiṇyāśu tanmayatāsthitiḥ || 205
1.206 . ta eva dharmāḥ śaktyākhyāstaistairucitarūpakaiḥ |
1.206 . ākāraiḥ paryupāsyante tanmayībhāvasiddhaye || 206
1.207 . tatra kācitpunaḥ śaktiranantā vā mitāśca vā |
1.207 . ākṣipeddhavatāsattvanyāyāddūrāntikatvataḥ || 207
1.208 . tena pūrṇasvabhāvatvaṃ prakāśatvaṃ cidātmatā |
1.208 . bhairavatvaṃ viśvaśaktīrākṣipedvyāpakatvataḥ || 208
1.209 . sadāśivādayastūrdhvavyāptyabhāvādadhojuṣaḥ |
1.209 . śaktīḥ samākṣipeyustadupāsāntikadūrataḥ || 209
1.210 . itthaṃ-bhāve ca śāktākhyo vaikalpikapathakramaḥ |
1.210 . iha tūkto yatastasmātpratiyogyavikalpakam || 210
1.211 . avikalpapathārūḍho yena yena pathā viśet |
1.211 . dharāsadāśivāntena tena tena śivībhavet || 211
1.212 . nirmale hṛdaye prāgryasphuradbhūmyaṃśabhāsini |
1.212 . prakāśe tanmukhenaiva saṃvitparaśivātmatā || 212
1.213 . evaṃ parecchāśaktyaṃśasadupāyamimaṃ viduḥ |
1.213 . śāmbhavākhyaṃ samāveśaṃ sumatyantenivāsinaḥ || 213
1.214 . śākto'tha bhaṇyate cetodhī-manohaṃkṛti sphuṭam |
1.214 . savikalpatayā māyāmayamicchādi vastutaḥ || 214
1.215 . abhimānena saṃkalpādhyavasāyakrameṇa yaḥ |
1.215 . śāktaḥ sa māyopāyo'pi tadante nirvikalpakaḥ || 215
1.216 . paśorvai yāvikalpā bhūrdaśā sā śāmbhavī param |
1.216 . apūrṇā mātṛdaurātmyāttadapāye vikasvarā || 216
1.217 . evaṃ vaikalpikī bhūmiḥ śākte kartṛtvavedane |
1.217 . yasyāṃ sphuṭe paraṃ tvasyāṃ saṃkocaḥ pūrvanītitaḥ || 217
1.218 . tathā saṃkocasaṃbhāravilāyanaparasya tu |
1.218 . sā yatheṣṭāntarābhāsakāriṇī śaktirujjvalā || 218
1.219 . nanu vaikalpikī kiṃ dhīrāṇave nāsti tatra sā |
1.219 . anyopāyātra tūccārarahitatvaṃ nyarūpayat || 219
1.220 . uccāraśabdenātroktā bahvantena tadādayaḥ |
1.220 . śaktyupāye na santyete bhedābhedau hi śaktitā || 220
1.221 . aṇurnāma sphuṭo bhedastadupāya ihāṇavaḥ |
1.221 . vikalpaniścayātmaiva paryante nirvikalpakaḥ || 221
1.222 . nanu dhī-mānasāhaṃkṛtpumāṃso vyāpnuyuḥ śivam |
1.222 . nādhovartitayā tena kathitaṃ kathamīdṛśam || 222
1.223 . ucyate vastuto'smākaṃ śiva eva tathāvidhaḥ |
1.223 . svarūpagopanaṃ kṛtvā svaprakāśaḥ punastathā || 223
1.224 . dvaitaśāstre mataṅgādau cāpyetatsunirūpitam |
1.224 . adhovyāptuḥ śivasyaiva sa prakāśo vyavasthitaḥ || 224
1.225 . yena buddhi-manobhūmāvapi bhāti paraṃ padam || 225
1.226 . dvāvapyetau samāveśau nirvikalpārṇavaṃ prati |
1.226 . prayāta eva tadrūḍhiṃ vinā naiva hi kiṃcana || 226
1.227 . saṃvittiphalabhiccātra na prakalpyetyato'bravīt |
1.227 . kalpanāyāśca mukhyatvamatraiva kila sūcitam || 227
1.228 . vikalpāpekṣayā yo'pi prāmāṇyaṃ prāha tanmate |
1.228 . tadvikalpakramopāttanirvikalpapramāṇatā || 228
1.229 . ratnatattvamavidvānprāṅniścayopāyacarcanāt |
1.229 . anupāyāvikalpāptau ratnajña iti bhaṇyate || 229
1.230 . abhedopāyamatroktaṃ śāmbhavaṃ śāktamucyate |
1.230 . bhedābhedātmakopāyaṃ bhedopāyaṃ tadāṇavam || 230
1.231 . ante jñāne'tra sopāye samastaḥ karmavistaraḥ |
1.231 . prasphuṭenaiva rūpeṇa bhāvī so'ntarbhaviṣyati || 231
1.232 . kriyā hi nāma vijñānānnānyadvastu kramātmatām |
1.232 . upāyavaśataḥ prāptaṃ tatkriyeti puroditam || 232
1.233 . samyagjñānaṃ ca muktyekakāraṇaṃ svaparasthitam |
1.233 . yato hi kalpanāmātraṃ svaparādivibhūtayaḥ || 233
1.234 . tulye kālpanikatve ca yadaikyasphuraṇātmakaḥ |
1.234 . guruḥ sa tāvadekātmā siddho muktaśca bhaṇyate || 234
1.235 . yāvānasya hi saṃtāno gurustāvatsa kīrtitaḥ |
1.235 . samyagjñānamayaśceti svātmanā mucyate tataḥ || 235
1.236 . tata eva svasaṃtānaṃ jñānī tārayatītyadaḥ |
1.236 . yuktyāgamābhyāṃ saṃsiddhaṃ tāvāneko yato muniḥ || 236
1.237 . tenātra ye codayanti nanu jñānādvimuktatā |
1.237 . dīkṣādikā kriyā ceyaṃ sā kathaṃ muktaye bhavet || 237
1.238 . jñānātmā seti cejjñānaṃ yatrasthaṃ taṃ vimocayet |
1.238 . anyasya mocane vāpi bhavetkiṃ nāsamañjasam |
1.238 . iti te mūlataḥ kṣiptā yattvatrānyaiḥ samarthitam || 238
1.239 . malo nāma kila dravyaṃ cakṣuḥsthapaṭalādivat |

e tantrāloke'bhinavaracite'mutra vijñānasattābhedodgāraprakaṭanapaṭāvāhnike'sminsamāptiḥ |

c2 atha śrītantrālokasya dvitīyamāhnikam

2.1 . yattatrādyaṃ padamaviratānuttarajñaptirūpam |
2.1 . tannirṇetuṃ prakaraṇamidamārabhe'haṃ dvitīyam || 1
2.2 . anupāyaṃ hi yadrūpaṃ ko'rtho deśanayātra vai |
2.2 . sakṛtsyāddeśanā paścādanupāyatvamucyayate || 2
2.3 . anupāyamidaṃ tattvamityupāyaṃ vinā kutaḥ |
2.3 . svayaṃ tu teṣāṃ tattādṛkkiṃ brūmaḥ kila tānprati || 3
2.4 . yaccaturdhoditaṃ rūpaṃ vijñānasya vibhorasau |
2.4 . svabhāva eva mantavyaḥ sa hi nityodito vibhuḥ || 4
2.5 . etāvadbhirasaṃkhyātaiḥ svabhāvairyatprakāśate |
2.5 . ke'pyaṃśāṃśikayā tena viśantyanye niraṃśataḥ || 5
2.6 . tatrāpi cābhyupāyādisāpekṣānyatvayogataḥ |
2.6 . upāyasyāpi no vāryā tadanyatvādvicitratā || 6
2.7 . tatra ye nirmalātmāno bhairavīyāṃ svasaṃvidam |
2.7 . nirupāyāmupāsīnāstadvidhiḥ praṇigadyate || 7
2.8 . tatra tāvatkriyāyogo nābhyupāyatvamarhati |
2.8 . sa hi tasmātsamudbhūtaḥ pratyuta pravibhāvyate || 8
2.9 . jñaptāvupāya eva syāditi cejjñaptirucyate |
2.9 . prakāśatvaṃ svaprakāśe tacca tatrānyataḥ katham || 9
2.10 . saṃvittattvaṃ svaprakāśamityasminkaṃ nu yuktibhiḥ |
2.10 . tadabhāve bhavedviśvaṃ jaḍatvādaprakāśakam || 10
2.11 . yāvānupāyo bāhyaḥ syādāntaro vāpi kaścana |
2.11 . sa sarvastanmukhaprekṣī tatropāyatvabhākkatham || 11
2.12 . tyajāvadhānāni nanu kva nāma dhatse'vadhānaṃ vicinu svayaṃ tat |
2.12 . pūrṇe'vadhānaṃ na hi nāma yuktaṃ nāpūrṇamabhyeti ca satyabhāvam || 12
2.13 . tenāvadhānaprāṇasya bhāvanādeḥ pare pathi |
2.13 . bhairavīye kathaṃkāraṃ bhavetsākṣādupāyatā || 13
2.14 . ye'pi sākṣādupāyena tadrūpaṃ praviviñcate |
2.14 . nūnaṃ te sūryasaṃvittyai khadyotādhitsavo jaḍāḥ || 14
2.15 . kiṃ ca yāvadidaṃ bāhyamāntaropāyasaṃmatam |
2.15 . tatprakāśātmatāmātraṃ śivasyaiva nijaṃ vapuḥ || 15
2.16 . nīlaṃ pītaṃ sukhamiti prakāśaḥ kevalaḥ śivaḥ |
2.16 . amuṣminparamādvaite prakāśātmani ko'paraḥ || 16
2.17 . upāyopeyabhāvaḥ syātprakāśaḥ kevalaṃ hi saḥ || 17
2.18 . idaṃ dvaitama'yaṃ bheda idamadvaitamityapi |
2.18 . prakāśavapurevāyaṃ bhāsate parameśvaraḥ || 18
2.19 . asyāṃ bhūmau sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bandho mokṣaścitarjaḍaḥ |
2.19 . ghaṭakumbhavadekārthāḥ śabdāste'pyekameva ca || 19
2.20 . praśāśe hyaprakāśāṃśaḥ kathaṃ nāma prakāśatām |
2.20 . prakāśamāne tasminvā taddvaitāstasya lopitāḥ || 20
2.21 . aprakāśe'tha tasminvā vastutā kathamucyate |
2.21 . na prakāśaviśeṣatvamata evopapadyate || 21
2.22 . ata ekaprakāśo'yamiti vāde'tra susthite |
2.22 . dūrādāvāritāḥ satyaṃ vibhinnajñānavādinaḥ || 22
2.23 . prakāśamātramuditamaprakāśaniṣedhanāt |
2.23 . ekaśabdasya na tvarthaḥ saṃkhyā cidvyaktibhedabhāk || 23
2.24 . naiṣa śaktirmahādevī na paratrāśrito yataḥ |
2.24 . na caiṣa śaktimāndevo na kasyāpyāśrayo yataḥ || 24
2.25 . naiṣa dhyeyo dhyātrabhāvānna dhyātā dhyeyavarjanāt |
2.25 . na pūjyaḥ pūjakābhāvātpūjyābhāvānna pūjakaḥ || 25
2.26 . na mantro na ca mantryo'sau na ca mantrayitā prabhuḥ |
2.26 . na dīkṣā dīkṣako vāpi na dīkṣāvānmaheśvaraḥ || 26
2.27 . sthānāsananirodhārghasaṃghānāvāhanādikam |
2.27 . visarjanāntaṃ nāstyatra kartṛkarmakriyojjhite || 27
2.28 . na sanna cāsatsadasanna ca tannobhayojjhitam |
2.28 . durvijñeyā hi sāvasthā kimapyetadanuttaram || 28
2.29 . ayamityavabhāso hi yo bhāvo'vacchidātmakaḥ |
2.29 . sa eva ghaṭavalloke saṃstathā naiṣa bhairavaḥ || 29
2.30 . asattvaṃ cāprakāśatvaṃ na kutrāpyupayogitā |
2.30 . viśvasya jīvitaṃ satyaṃ prakāśaikātmakaśca saḥ || 30
2.31 . ābhyāmeva tu hetubhyāṃ na dvyātmā na dvayojjhitaḥ |
2.31 . sarvātmanā hi bhātyeṣa kena rūpeṇa mantryatām || 31
2.32 . śrīmattriśirasi proktaṃ parajñānasvarūpakam |
2.32 . śaktyā garbhāntarvartinyā śaktigarbha paraṃ padam || 32
2.33 . na bhāvo nāpyabhāvo na dvayaṃ vācāmagocarāt |
2.33 . akathyapadavīrūḍhaṃ śaktisthaṃ śaktivarjitam || 33
2.34 . iti ye rūḍhasaṃvittiparamārthapavitritāḥ |
2.34 . anuttarapathe rūḍhāste'bhyupāyāniyantritāḥ || 34
2.35 . teṣāmidaṃ samābhāti sarvato bhāvamaṇḍalam |
2.35 . puraḥsthameva saṃvittibhairavāgnivilāpitam || 35
2.36 . eteṣāṃ sukhaduḥkhāṃśaśaṃkātaṃkavikalpanāḥ |
2.36 . nirvikalpaparāveśamātraśeṣatvamāgatāḥ || 36
2.37 . eṣāṃ na mantro na dhyānaṃ na pūjā nāpi kalpanā |
2.37 . na samayyādikācāryaparyantaḥ ko'pi viśramaḥ || 37
2.38 . samastayantraṇātantratroṭanāṭaṃkadharmiṇaḥ |
2.38 . nānugrahātparaṃ kiṃciccheṣavṛttau prayojanam || 38
2.39 . svaṃ kartavyaṃ kimapi kalayaṃlloka eṣa prayatnānno pārārthyaṃ prati ghaṭayate kāṃcana svapravṛttim |
2.39 . yastu dhvastākhilabhavamalo bhairavībhāvapūrṇaḥ kṛtyaṃ tasya sphuṭamidamiyallokakartavyamātram || 39
2.40 . taṃ ye paśyanti tādrūpyakrameṇāmalasaṃvidaḥ |
2.40 . te'pi tadrūpiṇastāvatyevāsyānugrahātmatā || 40
2.41 . etattattvaparijñānaṃ mukhyaṃ yāgādi kathyate |
2.41 . dīkṣāntaṃ vibhunā śrīmatsiddhayogīśvarīmate || 41
2.42 . sthaṇḍilāduttaraṃ tūraṃ tūrāduttarataḥ paṭaḥ |
2.42 . paṭāddhyānaṃ tato dhyeyaṃ tataḥ syāddhāraṇottarā || 42
2.43 . tato'pi yogajaṃ rūpaṃ tato'pi jñānamuttaram |
2.43 . jñānena hi mahāsiddho bhavedyogīśvarastviti || 43
2.44 . so'pi svātantryadhāmnā cedapyanirmalasaṃvidām |
2.44 . anugrahaṃ cikīrṣustadbhāvinaṃ vidhimāśrayet || 44
2.45 . anugrāhyānusāreṇa vicitraḥ sa ca kathyate |
2.45 . parāparādyupāyaughasaṃkīrṇatvavibhedataḥ || 45
2.46 . tadarthameva cāsyāpi parameśvararūpiṇaḥ |
2.46 . tadabhyupāyaśāstrādiśravaṇādhyayanādaraḥ || 46
2.47 . nahi tasya svatantrasya kāpi kutrāpi khaṇḍanā |
2.47 . nānirmalacitaḥ puṃso'nugrahastvanupāyakaḥ || 47
2.48 . śrīmadūrmimahāśāstre siddhasaṃtānarūpake |
2.48 . idamuktaṃ tathā śrīmatsomānandādidaiśikaiḥ || 48
2.49 . gurorvākyādyuktipracayaracanonmārjanavaśātsamāśvāsācchāstraṃ prati samuditādvāpi kathitāt |
2.49 . vilīne śaṃkābhre tdṛdayagaganodbhāsimahasaḥ prabhoḥ sūryasyeva spṛśata caraṇāndhvāntajayinaḥ || 49
2.50 . idamanuttaradhāmavivecakaṃ vigalitaupayikaṃ kṛtamāhnikam || 50




c3 śrītantrālokasya tṛtīyamāhnikam

3.0 . atha paraupayikaṃ praṇigadyate padamanuttarameva maheśituḥ || 0
3.1 . prakāśamātraṃ yatproktaṃ bhairavīyaṃ paraṃ mahaḥ |
3.1 . tatra svatantratāmātramadhikaṃ pravivicyate || 1
3.2 . yaḥ prakāśaḥ sa sarvasya prakāśatvaṃ prayacchati |
3.2 . na ca tadvyatirekyasti viśvaṃ sadvāvabhāsate || 2
3.3 . ato'sau parameśānaḥ svātmavyomanyanargalaḥ |
3.3 . iyataḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārāḍambarasya pradarśakaḥ || 3
3.4 . nirmale makure yadvadbhānti bhūmijalādayaḥ |
3.4 . amiśrāstadvadekasmiṃścinnāthe viśvavṛttayaḥ || 4
3.5 . sadṛśaṃ bhāti nayanadarpaṇāmbaravāriṣu |
3.5 . tathā hi nirmale rūpe rūpamevāvabhāsate || 5
3.6 . pracchannarāgiṇī kāntapratibimbitasundaram |
3.6 . darpaṇaṃ kucakumbhābhyāṃ spṛśantyapi na tṛpyati || 6
3.7 . na hi sparśo'sya vimalo rūpameva tathā yataḥ |
3.7 . nairmalyaṃ cātiniviḍasajātīyaikasaṃgatiḥ || 7
3.8 . svasminnabhedādbhinnasya darśanakṣamataiva yā |
3.8 . atyaktasvaprakāśasya nairmalyaṃ tadgurūditam || 8
3.9 . nairmalyaṃ mukhyamekasya saṃvinnāthasya sarvataḥ |
3.9 . aṃśāṃśikātaḥ kvāpyanyadvimalaṃ tattadicchayā || 9
3.10 . bhāvānāṃ yatpratīghāti vapurmāyātmakaṃ hi tat |
3.10 . teṣāmevāsti sadvidyāmayaṃ tvapratighātakam || 10
3.11 . tadevamubhayākāramavabhāsaṃ prakāśayan |
3.11 . vibhāti varado bimbapratibimbadṛśākhile || 11
3.12 . yastvāha netratejāṃsi svacchātpratiphalantyalam |
3.12 . viparyasya svakaṃ vaktraṃ gṛhṇantīti sa pṛcchyate || 12
3.13 . dehādanyatra yattejastadadhiṣṭhāturātmanaḥ |
3.13 . tenaiva tejasā jñatve ko'rthaḥ syāddarpaṇena tu || 13
3.14 . viparyastaistu tejobhirgrāhakātmatvamāgataiḥ |
3.14 . rūpaṃ dṛśyeta vadane nije na makurāntare || 14
3.15 . svamukhe sparśavaccaitadrūpaṃ bhāyānmametyalam |
3.15 . na tvasya spṛśyabhinnasya vedyaikāntasvarūpiṇaḥ || 15
3.16 . rūpasaṃsthānamātraṃ tatsparśagandharasādibhiḥ |
3.16 . nyagbhūtaireva tadyuktaṃ vastu tatpratibimbitam || 16
3.17 . nyagbhāvo grāhyatābhāvāttadabhāvo'pramāṇataḥ |
3.17 . sa cārthasaṃgamābhāvātso'pyādarśe'navasthiteḥ || 17
3.18 . ata eva gurutvādirdharmo naitasya lakṣyate |
3.18 . nahyādarśe saṃsthito'sau taddṛṣṭau sa upāyakaḥ || 18
3.19 . tasmāttu naiṣa bhedena yadbhāti tata ucyate |
3.19 . ādhārastatra tūpāyā dīpadṛksaṃvidaḥ kramāt || 19
3.20 . dīpacakṣurvibodhānāṃ kāṭhinyābhāvataḥ param |
3.20 . sarvataścāpi nairmalyānna vibhādarśavatpṛthak || 20
3.21 . etacca devadevena darśitaṃ bodhavṛddhaye |
3.21 . mūḍhānāṃ vastu bhavati tato'pyanyatra nāpyalam || 21
3.22 . pratīghāti svatantraṃ no na sthāyyasthāyi cāpi na |
3.22 . svacchasyaivaiṣa kasyāpi mahimeti kṛpālunā || 22
3.23 . na deśoṃ no rūpaṃ na ca samayayogo na parimā na cānyonyāsaṃgo na ca tadapahānirna ghanatā |
3.23 . na cāvastutvaṃ syānna ca kimapi sāraṃ nijamiti dhruvaṃ mohaḥ śāmyediti niradiśaddarpaṇavidhiḥ || 23
3.24 . itthaṃ pradarśite'mutra pratibimbanavartmani |
3.24 . śabdasya pratibimbaṃ yatpratiśrutketi bhaṇyate || 24
3.25 . na cāsau śabdajaḥ śabda āgacchattvena saṃśravāt |
3.25 . tenaiva vaktrā dūrasthaiḥ śabdasyāśravaṇādapi || 25
3.26 . piṭhirādipidhānāṃśaviśiṣṭachidrasaṃgatau |
3.26 . citratvāccāsya śabdasya pratibimbaṃ mukhādivat || 26
3.27 . idamanyasya vedyasya rūpamityavabhāsate |
3.27 . yathādarśe tathā kenāpyuktamākarṇaye tviti || 27
3.28 . niyamādbimbasāṃmukhyaṃ pratibimbasya yattataḥ |
3.28 . tanmadhyagāḥ pramātāraḥ śṛṇvanti pratiśabdakam || 28
3.29 . mukhyagrahaṃ tvapi vinā pratibimbagraho bhavet |
3.29 . svapaścātsthaṃ priyaṃ paśyeṭṭaṅkitaṃ mukure vapuḥ || 29
3.30 . sāṃmukhyaṃ cocyate tādṛgdarpaṇābhedasaṃsthiteḥ || 30
3.31 . ataḥ kūpādipiṭhirākāśe tatpratibimbitam |
3.31 . vaktrākāśaṃ saśabdaṃ sadbhāti tatparavaktavat || 31
3.32 . yathā cādarśapāścātyabhāgastho vetti no mukham |
3.32 . tathā tathāvidhākāśapaścātstho vetti na dhvanim || 32
3.33 . śabdo na cānabhivyaktaḥ pratibimbati taddhruvam |
3.33 . abhivyaktiśrutī tasya samakālaṃ dvitīyake || 33
3.34 . kṣaṇe tu pratibimbatvaṃ śrutiśca samakālikā |
3.34 . tulyakālaṃ hi no hastatacchāyārūpaniścayaḥ || 34
3.35 . itthaṃ pradarśite'mutra pratibimbasatattvake |
3.35 . prakṛtaṃ brūmahe tatra pratibimbanamarhati || 35
3.36 . śabdo nabhasi sānande sparśadhāmani sundaraḥ |
3.36 . sparśo'nyo'pi dṛḍhāghātaśūlaśītādikodbhavaḥ |
3.36 . parasthaḥ pratibimbatvātsvadehoddhūlanākaraḥ || 36
3.37 . na caiṣa mukhyastatkāryapāramparyāprakāśanāt || 37
3.38 . evaṃ ghrāṇāntare gandho raso dantodake sphuṭaḥ || 38
3.39 . yathā ca rūpaṃ pratibimbitaṃ dṛśorna cakṣuṣānyena vinā hi lakṣyate |
3.39 . tathā rasasparśanasaurabhādikaṃ na lakṣyate'kṣeṇa vinā sthitaṃ tvapi || 39
3.40 . na cāntare sparśanadhāmani sthitaṃ bahiḥspṛśonyākṣadhiyaḥ sa gocaraḥ || 40
3.41 . ato'ntikasthasvakatādṛgindriyaprayojanāntaḥkaraṇairyadā kṛtā |
3.41 . tadā tadāttaṃ pratibimbamindriye svakāṃ kriyāṃ sūyata eva tādṛśīm || 41
3.42 . na tu smṛtānmānasagocarādṛtā bhavetkriyā sā kila vartamānataḥ |
3.42 . ataḥ sthitaḥ sparśavarastadindriye samāgataḥ sanviditastathākriyaḥ || 42
3.43 . asaṃbhave bāhyagatasya tādṛśaḥ sva eva tasminpratibimbitastathā |
3.43 . karoti tāṃ sparśavaraḥ sukhātmikāṃ sa cāpi kasyāmapi nāḍisaṃtatau || 43
3.44 . tena saṃvittimakure viśvamātmānamarpayat |
3.44 . nāthasya vadate'muṣya vimalāṃ viśvarūpatām || 44
3.45 . yathā ca gandharūpaspṛgrasādyāḥ pratibimbitāḥ |
3.45 . tadādhāroparāgeṇa bhānti khaṅge mukhādivat || 45
3.46 . tathā viśvamidaṃ bodhe pratibimbitamāśrayet |
3.46 . prakāśatvasvatantratvaprabhṛtiṃ dharmavistaram || 46
3.47 . yathā ca sarvataḥ svacche sphaṭike sarvato bhavet |
3.47 . pratibimbaṃ tathā bodhe sarvataḥ svacchatājuṣi || 47
3.48 . atyantasvacchatā sā yatsvākṛtyanavabhāsanam |
3.48 . ataḥ svacchatamo bodho na ratnaṃ tvākṛtigrahāt || 48
3.49 . pratibimbaṃ ca bimbena bāhyasthena samarpyate |
3.49 . tasyaiva pratibimbatve kiṃ bimbamavaśiṣyatām || 49
3.50 . yadvāpi kāraṇaṃ kiṃcidbimbatvenābhiṣicyate |
3.50 . tadapi pratibimbatvameti bodhe'nyathā tvasat || 50
3.51 . itthametatsvasaṃvittidṛḍhanyāyāstrarakṣitam |
3.51 . sāmrājyameva viśvatra pratibimbasya jṛmbhate || 51
3.52 . nanu bimbasya virahe pratibimbaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
3.52 . kiṃ kurmo dṛśyate taddhi nanu tadbimbamucyatām || 52
3.53 . naivaṃ tallakṣaṇābhāvādbimbaṃ kila kimucyate |
3.53 . anyāmiśraṃ svatantraṃ sadbhāsamānaṃ mukhaṃ yathā || 53
3.54 . svarūpānapahānena pararūpasadṛkṣatām |
3.54 . pratibimbātmatāmāhuḥ khaḍgādarśatalādivat || 54
3.55 . uktaṃ ca sati bāhye'pi dhīrekānekavedanāt |
3.55 . anekasadṛśākārā na tvaneketi saugataiḥ || 55
3.56 . nanvitthaṃ pratibimbasya lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ taducyate |
3.56 . anyavyāmiśraṇāyogāttadbhedāśakyabhāsanam |
3.56 . pratibimbamiti prāhurdarpaṇe vadanaṃ yathā || 56
3.57 . bodhamiśramidaṃ bodhādbhedenāśakyabhāsanam |
3.57 . paratattvādi bodhe kiṃ pratibimbaṃ na bhaṇyate || 57
3.58 . lakṣaṇasya vyavasthaiṣākasmāccedbimbamucyatām |
3.58 . prājñā vastuni yujyante na tu sāmayike dhvanau || 58
3.59 . nanu na pratibimbasya vinā bimbaṃ bhavetsthitiḥ |
3.59 . kiṃ tataḥ pratibimbe hi bimbaṃ tādātmyavṛtti na || 59
3.60 . ataśca lakṣaṇasyāsya proktasya tadasaṃbhave |
3.60 . na hānirhetumātre tu praśno'yaṃ paryavasyati || 60
3.61 . tatrāpi ca nimittākhye nopādāne kathaṃcana |
3.61 . nimittakāraṇānāṃ ca kadācitkvāpi saṃbhavaḥ || 61
3.62 . ata eva purovartinyāloke smaraṇādinā |
3.62 . nimittena ghanenāstu saṃkrāntadayitākṛtiḥ || 62
3.63 . anyathā saṃvidārūḍhā kāntā vicchedayoginī |
3.63 . kasmādbhāti na vai saṃvidvicchedaṃ purato gatā || 63
3.64 . ata evāntaraṃ kiṃciddhīsaṃjñaṃ bhavatu sphuṭam |
3.64 . yatrāsya vicchidā bhānaṃ saṃkalpasvapnadarśane || 64
3.65 . ato nimittaṃ devasya śaktayaḥ santu tādṛśe |
3.65 . itthaṃ viśvamidaṃ nāthe bhairavīyacidambare |
3.65 . pratibimbamalaṃ svacche na khalvanyaprasādataḥ || 65
3.66 . ananyāpekṣitā yāsya viśvātmatvaṃ prati prabhoḥ |
3.66 . tāṃ parāṃ pratibhāṃ devīṃ saṃgirante hyanuttarām || 66
3.67 . akulasyāsya devasya kulaprathanaśālinī |
3.67 . kaulikī sā parā śaktiraviyukto yayā prabhuḥ || 67
3.68 . tayoryadyāmalaṃ rūpaṃ sa saṃghaṭṭa iti smṛtaḥ |
3.68 . ānandaśaktiḥ saivoktā yato viśvaṃ visṛjyate || 68
3.69 . parāparātparaṃ tattvaṃ saiṣā devī nigadyate |
3.69 . tatsāraṃ tacca hṛdayaṃ sa visargaḥ paraḥ prabhuḥ || 69
3.70 . devīyāmalaśāstre sā kathitā kālakarṣiṇī |
3.70 . mahāḍāmarake yāge śrīparā mastake tathā || 70
3.71 . śrīpūrvaśāstre sā mātṛsadbhāvatvena varṇitā |
3.71 . saṃghaṭṭe'smiṃścidātmatvādyattatpratyavamarśanam || 71
3.72 . icchāśaktiraghorāṇāṃ śaktīnāṃ sā parā prabhuḥ |
3.72 . saiva prakṣubdharūpā cedīśitrī saṃprajāyate || 72
3.73 . tadā ghorāḥ parā devyo jātāḥ śaivādhvadaiśikāḥ |
3.73 . svātmapratyavamarśo yaḥ prāgabhūdekavīrakaḥ || 73
3.74 . jñātavyaviśvonmeṣātmā jñānaśaktitayā sthitaḥ |
3.74 . iyaṃ parāparā devī ghorāṃ yā mātṛmaṇḍalīm || 74
3.75 . sṛjatyavirataṃ śuddhāśuddhamārgaikadīpikām |
3.75 . jñeyāṃśaḥ pronmiṣankṣobhaṃ yadaiti balavattvataḥ || 75
3.76 . ūnatābhāsanaṃ saṃvinmātratve jāyate tadā |
3.76 . rūḍhaṃ tajjñeyavargasya sthitiprārambha ucyate || 76
3.77 . rūḍhireṣā vibodhābdheścitrākāraparigrahaḥ |
3.77 . idaṃ tadbījasaṃdarbhabījaṃ cinvanti yoginaḥ || 77
3.78 . icchāśaktirdvirūpoktā kṣubhitākṣubhitā ca yā |
3.78 . iṣyamāṇaṃ hi sā vastudvairūpyeṇātmani śrayet || 78
3.79 . aciradyutibhāsinyā śaktyā jvalanarūpayā |
3.79 . iṣyamāṇasamāpattiḥ sthairyeṇātha dharātmanā || 79
3.80 . unmeṣaśaktāvastyetajjñeyaṃ yadyapi bhūyasā |
3.80 . tathāpi vibhavasthānaṃ sā na tu prācyajanmabhūḥ || 80
3.81 . icchāśakterataḥ prāhuścātūrūpyaṃ parāmṛtam |
3.81 . kṣobhāntarasyāsadbhāvānnedaṃ bījaṃ ca kasyacit || 81
3.82 . prakṣobhakatvaṃ bījatvaṃ kṣobhādhāraśca yonitā |
3.82 . kṣobhakaṃ saṃvido rūpaṃ kṣubhyati kṣobhayatyapi || 82
3.83 . kṣobhaḥ syājjñeyadharmatvaṃ kṣobhaṇā tadbahiṣkṛtiḥ |
3.83 . antaḥsthaviśvābhinnaikabījāṃśavisisṛkṣutā || 83
3.84 . kṣobho'tadicche tattvecchābhāsanaṃ kṣobhaṇāṃ viduḥ |
3.84 . yadaikyāpattimāsādya tadicchā kṛtinī bhavet || 84
3.85 . kṣobhādhāramimaṃ prāhuḥ śrīsomānandaputrakāḥ |
3.85 . saṃvidāmīṣaṇādīnāmanudbhinnaviśeṣakam || 85
3.86 . yajjñeyamātraṃ tadbījaṃ yadyogādbījatā svare |
3.86 . tasya bījasya saivoktā visisṛkṣā ya udbhavaḥ |
3.86 . yato grāhyamidaṃ bhāsyadbhinnakalpaṃ cidātmanaḥ || 86
3.87 . eṣa kṣobhaḥ kṣobhaṇā tu tūṣṇīṃbhūtānyamātṛgam |
3.87 . haṭhādyadaudāsīnyāṃśacyāvanaṃ saṃvido balāt || 87
3.88 . jātāpi visisṛkṣāsau yadvimarśāntaraikyataḥ |
3.88 . kṛtārthā jāyate kṣobhādhāro'traitatprakīrtitam || 88
3.89 . tatastadāntaraṃ jñeyaṃ bhinnakalpatvamicchati |
3.89 . viśvabījādataḥ sarva bāhyaṃ bimbaṃ vivartsyati || 89
3.90 . kṣobhyakṣobhakabhāvasya satattvaṃ darśitaṃ mayā |
3.90 . śrīmanmaheśvareṇoktaṃ guruṇā yatprasādataḥ || 90

3.212 . dṛṣṭaśrutāditadvastupronmukhatvaṃ svasaṃvidi |
3.212 . cittasaṃbodhanāmoktaḥ śāktollāsabharātmakaḥ || 212

3.215 . tattvarakṣāvidhāne'to visargatraidhamucyate |

3.222 . kakārādisakārāntā visargātpañcadhā sa ca |

3.223 . bindurātmani mūrdhāntaṃ hṛdayādvyāpako hi saḥ |

3.224 . gurorlakṣaṇametāvadādimāntyaṃ ca vedayet |
3.224 . pūjyaḥ so'hamiva jñānī bhairavo devatātmakaḥ || 224
3.225 . ślokagāthādi yatkiṃcidādimāntyayutaṃ tataḥ |
3.225 . tasmādvidaṃstathā sarvaṃ mantratvenaiva paśyati || 225
3.226 . visargaśaktirviśvasya kāraṇaṃ ca nirūpitā |
3.226 . aitareyākhyavedānte parameśena vistarāt || 226
3.227 . yallohitaṃ tadagniryadvīryaṃ sūryenduvigraham |
3.227 . a iti brahma paramaṃ tatsaṃghaṭṭodayātmakam || 227
3.228 . tasyāpi ca paraṃ vīrya pañcabhūtakalātmakam |
3.228 . bhogyatvenānnarūpaṃ ca śabdasparśarasātmakam || 228
3.229 . śabdo'pi madhuro yasmādvīryopacayakārakaḥ |
3.229 . taddhi vīryaṃ paraṃ śuddhaṃ visisṛkṣātmakaṃ matam || 229
3.230 . tadbalaṃ ca tadojaśca te prāṇāḥ sā ca kāntatā |
3.230 . tasmādvīryātprajāstāśca vīrya karmasu kathyate || 230
3.231 . yajñādikeṣu tadvṛṣṭau sauṣadhīṣvatha tāḥ punaḥ |
3.231 . vīrye tacca prajāsvevaṃ visarge viśvarūpatā || 231
3.232 . śabdarāśiḥ sa evokto mātṛkā sāca kīrtitā |
3.232 . kṣobhyakṣobhakatāveśānmālinīṃ tāṃ pracakṣate || 232
3.233 . bījayonisamāpattivisargodayasundarā |
3.233 . mālinī hi parā śaktirnirṇītā viśvarūpiṇī || 233
3.234 . eṣā vastuta ekaiva parā kālasya karṣiṇī |
3.234 . śaktimadbhedayogena yāmalatvaṃ prapadyate || 234
3.235 . tasya pratyavamarśo yaḥ paripūrṇo'hamātmakaḥ |
3.235 . sa svātmani svatantratvādvibhāgamavabhāsayet || 235
3.236 . vibhāgābhāsane cāsya tridhā vapurudāhṛtam |
3.236 . paśyantī madhyamā sthūlā vaikharītyabhiśabditam || 236
3.237 . tāsāmapi tridhā rūpaṃ sthūlasūkṣmaparatvataḥ |
3.237 . tatra yā svarasandarbhasubhagā nādarūpiṇī || 237
3.238 . sā sthūlā khalu paśyantī varṇādyapravibhāgataḥ |
3.238 . avibhāgaikarūpatvaṃ mādhuryaṃ śaktirucyate || 238
3.239 . sthānavāyvādigharṣotthā sphuṭataiva ca pāruṣī |
3.239 . tadasyāṃ nādarūpāyāṃ saṃvitsavidhavṛttitaḥ || 239
3.240 . sājātyāntarma[ttama] yībhūtirjhagityevopalabhyate |
3.240 . yeṣāṃ na tanmayībhūtiste dehādinimajjanam || 240
3.241 . avidanto magnasaṃvinmānāstvahṛdayā iti |
3.241 . yattucarmāvanaddhādi kiṃcittatraiṣa yo dhvaniḥ || 241
3.242 . sa sphuṭāsphuṭarūpatvānmadhyamā sthūlarūpiṇī |
3.242 . madhyāyāścāvibhāgāṃśasadbhāva iti raktatā || 242
3.243 . avibhāgasvaramayī yatra syāttatsurañjakam |
3.243 . avibhāgo hi nirvṛtyai dṛśyatāṃ tālapāṭhataḥ || 243
3.244 . kilāvyaktadhvanau tasminvādane parituṣyati |
3.244 . yā tu sphuṭānāṃ varṇānāmutpattau kāraṇaṃ bhavet || 244
3.245 . sā sthūlā vaikharī yasyāḥ kāryaṃ vākyādi bhūyasā |
3.245 . asminsthūlatraye yattadanusandhānamādivat || 245
3.246 . pṛthakpṛthaktattritayaṃ sūkṣmamityabhiśabdyate |
3.246 . ṣaḍjaṃ karomi madhuraṃ vādayāmi bruve vacaḥ || 246
3.247 . pṛthagevānusandhānatrayaṃ saṃvedyate kila |
3.247 . etasyāpi trayasyādyaṃ yadrupamanupādhimat || 247
3.248 . tatparaṃ tritayaṃ tatra śivaḥ paracidātmakaḥ |
3.248 . vibhāgābhāsanāyāṃ ca mukhyāstisro'tra śaktayaḥ || 248
3.249 . anuttarā parecchā ca parāparatayā sthitā |
3.249 . unmeṣaśaktirjñānākhyā tvapareti nigadyate || 249
3.250 . kṣobharūpātpunastāsāmuktāḥ ṣaṭsaṃvido'malāḥ |
3.250 . āsāmeva samāveśātkriyāśaktitayoditāt || 250
3.251 . saṃvido dvādaśa proktā yāsu sarvaṃ samāpyate |
3.251 . etāvaddevadevasya mukhyaṃ tacchakticakrakam || 251
3.252 . etāvatā devadevaḥ pūrṇaśaktiḥ sa bhairavaḥ |
3.252 . parāmarśātmakatvena visargākṣepayogataḥ || 252
3.253 . iyattākalanājjñānāttāḥ proktāḥ kālikāḥ kvacit |
3.253 . śrīsāraśāstre cāpyuktaṃ madhya ekākṣarāṃ parām || 253
3.254 . pūjayedbhairavātmākhyāṃ yoginīdvādaśāvṛtām |
3.254 . tābhya eva catuḥṣaṣṭiparyantaṃ śakticakrakam || 254
3.255 . ekārataḥ samārabhya sahasrāraṃ pravartate |
3.255 . tāsāṃ ca kṛtyabhedena nāmāni bahudhāgame || 255
3.256 . upāsāśca dvayādvaitavyāmiśrākārayogataḥ |
3.256 . śrīmattraiśirame tacca kathitaṃ vistarādbahu || 256
3.257 . iha no likhitaṃ vyāsabhayāccānupayogataḥ |
3.257 . tā eva nirmalāḥ śuddhā aghorāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 257
3.258 . ghoraghoratarāṇāṃ tu sotṛtvācca tadātmikāḥ |
3.258 . sṛṣṭau sthitau ca saṃhāre tadupādhitrayātyaye || 258
3.259 . tāsāmeva sthitaṃ rūpaṃ bahudhā pravibhajyate |
3.259 . upādhyatītaṃ yadrūpaṃ taddvidhā guravo jaguḥ || 259
3.260 . anullāsādupādhīnāṃ yadvā praśamayogataḥ |
3.260 . praśamaśca dvidhā śāntyā haṭhapākakrameṇa tu || 260
3.261 . alaṃ grāsarasākhyena satataṃ jvalanātmanā |
3.261 . haṭhapākapraśamanaṃ yattṛtīyaṃ tadeva ca |
3.261 . upadeśāya yujyeta bhedendhanavidāhakam || 261
3.262 . nijabodhajaṭharahutabhuji bhāvāḥ sarve samarpitā haṭhataḥ |
3.262 . vijahati bhedavibhāgaṃ nijaśaktyā taṃ samindhānāḥ || 262
3.263 . haṭhapākena bhāvānāṃ rūpe bhinne vilāpite |
3.263 . aśnantyamṛtasādbhūtaṃ viśvaṃ saṃvittidevatāḥ || 263
3.264 . tāstṛptāḥ svātmanaḥ pūrṇa hṛdayaikāntaśāyinam |
3.264 . cidvyomabhairavaṃ devamabhedenādhiśerate || 264
3.265 . evaṃ kṛtyakriyāveśānnāmopāsābahutvataḥ |
3.265 . āsāṃ bahuvidhaṃ rūpamabhede'pyavabhāsate || 265
3.266 . āsāmeva ca devīnāmāvāpodvāpayogataḥ |
3.266 . ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaṭsaptāṣṭanavottaraiḥ || 266
3.267 . rudrārkānyakalāsenāprabhṛtirbhedavistaraḥ |
3.267 . alamanyena bahunā prakṛte'tha niyujyate || 267
3.268 . saṃvidātmani viśvo'yaṃ bhāvavargaḥ prapañcavān |
3.268 . pratibimbatayā bhāti yasya viśveśvaro hi saḥ || 268
3.269 . evamātmani yasyedṛgavikalpaḥ sadodayaḥ |
3.269 . parāmarśaḥ sa evāsau śāṃbhavopāyamudritaḥ || 269
3.270 . pūrṇāhantāparāmarśo yo'syāyaṃ pravivecitaḥ |
3.270 . mantramudrākriyopāsāstadanyā nātra kāścana || 270
3.271 . bhūyobhūyaḥ samāveśaṃ nirvikalpamimaṃ śritaḥ |
3.271 . abhyeti bhairavībhāvaṃ jīvanmuktyaparābhidham || 271
3.272 . ita eva prabhṛtyeṣā jīvanmuktirvicāryate |
3.272 . yatra sūtraṇayāpīyamupāyopeyakalpanā || 272
3.273 . prāktane tvāhnike kācidbhedasya kalanāpi no |
3.273 . tenānupāye tasminko mucyate vā kathaṃ kutaḥ || 273
3.274 . nirvikalpe parāmarśe śāmbhavopāyanāmani |
3.274 . pañcāśadbhedatāṃ pūrvasūtritāṃ yojayedbudhaḥ || 274
3.275 . dharāmevāvikalpena svātmani pratibimbitām |
3.275 . paśyanbhairavatāṃ yāti jalādiṣvapyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 275
3.276 . yāvadante paraṃ tattvaṃ samastāvaraṇordhvagam |
3.276 . vyāpi svatantraṃ sarvajñaṃ yacchivaṃ parikalpitam || 276
3.277 . tadapyakalpitodārasaṃviddarpaṇabimbitam |
3.277 . paśyanvikalpavikalo bhairavībhavati svayam || 277
3.278 . yathā raktaṃ puraḥ paśyannirvikalpakasaṃvidā |
3.278 . tattaddvāraniraṃśaikaghaṭasaṃvittisusthitaḥ || 278
3.279 . tadvaddharādikaikaikasaṃghātasamudāyataḥ |
3.279 . parāmṛśansvamātmānaṃ pūrṇa evāvabhāsate || 279
3.280 . matta evoditamidaṃ mayyeva pratibimbitam |
3.280 . madabhinnamidaṃ ceti tridhopāyaḥ sa śāmbhavaḥ || 280
3.281 . sṛṣṭeḥ sthiteḥ saṃhṛteśca tadetatsūtraṇaṃ kṛtam |
3.281 . yatra sthitaṃ yataśceti tadāha spandaśāsane || 281
3.282 . etāvataiva hyaiśvarya saṃvidaḥ khyāpitaṃ param |
3.282 . viśvātmakatvaṃ cetyanyallakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ nu kathyatām || 282
3.283 . svātmanyeva cidākāśe viśvamasmyavabhāsayan |
3.283 . sraṣṭā viśvātmaka iti prathayā bhairavātmatā || 283
3.284 . ṣaḍadhvajātaṃ nikhilaṃ mayyeva pratibimbitam |
3.284 . sthitikartāhamasmīti sphuṭeyaṃ viśvarūpatā || 284
3.285 . sadoditamahābodhajvālājaṭilatātmani |
3.285 . viśvaṃ dravati mayyetaditi paśyanpraśāmyati || 285
3.286 . anantacitrasadgarbhasaṃsārasvapnasadmanaḥ |
3.286 . ploṣakaḥ śiva evāhamityullāsī hutāśanaḥ || 286
3.287 . jagatsarvaṃ mattaḥ prabhavati vibhedena bahudhā tathāpyetadrūḍhaṃ mayi vigalite tvatra na paraḥ |
3.287 . taditthaṃ yaḥ sṛṣṭisthitivilayabhekīkṛtivaśādanaṃśaṃ paśyetsa sphurati hi turīyaṃ padabhitaḥ || 287
3.288 . tadasminparamopāye śāmbhavādvaitaśālini |
3.288 . ke'pyeva yānti viśvāsaṃ pasmeśena bhāvitāḥ || 288
3.289 . snānaṃ vrataṃ dehaśuddhirdhāraṇā mantrayojanā |
3.289 . adhvaklṛptiryāgavidhirhomajapyasamādhayaḥ || 289
3.290 . ityādikalpanā kāpi nātra bhedena yujyate |
3.290 . parānugrahakāritvamatrasthasya sphuṭaṃ sthitam || 290
3.291 . yadi tādṛganugrāhyo daiśikasyopasarpati |
3.291 . athāsau tādṛśo na syādbhavabhaktyā ca bhāvitaḥ || 291
3.292 . taṃ cārādhayate bhāvitādṛśānugraheritaḥ |
3.292 . tadā vicitraṃ dīkṣādividhiṃ śikṣeta koviṃdaḥ || 292
3.293 . bhāvinyo'pi hyupāsāstā straivāyānti niṣṭhitim |
3.293 . etanmayatvaṃ paramaṃ prāpyaṃ nirvarṇyateśivam || 293
3.294 . iti kathitamidaṃ suvistaraṃ paramaṃ śāmbhavamātmavedanam || 294

c4 atha śrītantrāloke caturthamāhnikam

4.1 . atha śāktamupāyamaṇḍalaṃ kathayāmaḥ paramātmasaṃvide || 1
4.2 . anantarāhnikokte'sminsvabhāve pārameśvare |
4.2 . pravivikṣurvikalpasya kuryātsaṃskāramañjasā || 2
4.3 . vikalpaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ sūte vikalpaṃ svātmasaṃskṛtam |
4.3 . svatulyaṃ so'pi so'pyanyaṃ so'pyanyaṃ sadṛśātmakam || 3
4.4 . caturṣveva vikalpeṣu yaḥ saṃskāraḥ kramādasau |
4.4 . asphuṭaḥ sphuṭatābhāvī prasphuṭansphuṭitātmakaḥ || 4
4.5 . tataḥ sphuṭataro yāvadante sphuṭatamo bhavet |
4.5 . asphuṭādau vikalpe ca bhedo'pyastyāntarālikaḥ || 5
4.6 . tataḥ sphuṭatamodāratādrūpyaparivṛṃhitā |
4.6 . saṃvidabhyeti vimalāmavikalpasvarūpatām || 6
4.7 . ataśca bhairavīyaṃ yattejaḥ saṃvitsvabhāvakam |
4.7 . bhūyo bhūyo vimṛśatāṃ jāyate tatsphuṭātmatā || 7
4.8 . nanu saṃvitparāmraṣṭrī parāmarśamayī svataḥ |
4.8 . parāmṛśyā kathaṃ tāthārūpyasṛṣṭau tu sā jaḍā || 8
4.9 . ucyate svātmasaṃvittiḥ svabhāvādeva nirbharā |
4.9 . nāsyāmapāsyaṃ nādheyaṃ kiṃcidityuditaṃ purā || 9
4.10 . kiṃ tu durghaṭakāritvātsvācchandyānnirmalādasau |
4.10 . svātmapracchādanakrīḍāpaṇḍitaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 10
4.11 . anāvṛtte svarūpe'pi yadātmācchādanaṃ vibhoḥ |
4.11 . saiva māyā yato bheda etāvānviśvavṛttikaḥ || 11
4.12 . tathābhāsanamevāsya dvaitamuktaṃ maheśituḥ |
4.12 . taddvayāpāsanenāyaṃ parāmarśo'bhidhīyate || 12
4.13 . durbhedapādapasyāsya mūlaṃ kṛntanti kovidāḥ |
4.13 . dhārārūḍhena sattarkakuṭhāreṇeti niścayaḥ || 13
4.14 . tāmenāṃ bhāvanāmāhuḥ sarvakāmadughāṃ budhāḥ |
4.14 . sphuṭayedvastu yāpetaṃ manosthapadādapi || 14
4.15 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tatproktaṃ tarko yogāṅgamuttamam |
4.15 . heyādyālocanāttasmāttatra yatnaḥ praśasyate || 15
4.16 . mārge cetaḥ sthirībhūtaṃ heye'pi viṣayecchayā |
4.16 . prerya tena nayettāvadyāvatpadamanāmayam || 16
4.17 . mārgo'tra mokṣopāyaḥ sa heyaḥ śāstrāntaroditaḥ |
4.17 . viṣiṇoti nibadhnāti yecchā niyatisaṃgatam || 17
4.18 . rāgatattvaṃ tayoktaṃ yattena tatrānurajyate |
4.18 . yathā sāmrājyasaṃbhogaṃ dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭvāthabādhame || 18
4.19 . bhoge rajyeta durbuddhistadvanmokṣe'pi rāgataḥ |
4.19 . sa evāṃśaka ityuktaḥ svabhāvākhyaḥ sa tu sphuṭam || 19
4.20 . siddhyaṅgamiti mokṣāya pratyūha iti kovidāḥ |
4.20 . śivaśāsanamāhātmyaṃ vidannapyata eva hi || 20
4.21 . vaiṣṇavādhyeṣu rajyeta mūḍho rāgeṇa rañjitaḥ |
4.21 . yatastāvati sā tasya vāmākhyā śaktiraiśvarī || 21
4.22 . pāñcarātrikavairiñcasaugatādervijṛmbhate |
4.22 . dṛṣṭāḥ sāmrājyasaṃbhogaṃ nindantaḥ ke'pi vāliśāḥ || 22
4.23 . na tu saṃtoṣataḥ sveṣu bhogeṣvāśīḥpravartanāt |
4.23 . evaṃcidbhairavāveśanindātatparamānasāḥ || 23
4.24 . bhavantyatisughorābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ pātitā yataḥ |
4.24 . tena śāṃbhavamāhātmyaṃ jānanyaḥ śāsanāntare || 24
4.25 . āśvasto nottarītavyaṃ tena bhedamahārṇavāt |
4.25 . śrīkāmikāyāṃ proktaṃ ca pāśaprakaraṇe sphuṭam || 25
4.26 . vedasāṃkhyapurāṇajñāḥ pāñcarātraparāyaṇāḥ |
4.26 . ye kecidṛṣayo dhīrāḥ śāstrāntaraparāyaṇāḥ || 26
4.27 . bauddhārhatādyāḥ sarve te vidyārāgeṇa rañjitāḥ |
4.27 . māyāpāśena baddhatvācchivadīkṣāṃ na vindate || 27
4.28 . rāgaśabdena ca proktaṃ rāgatattvaṃ niyāmakam |
4.28 . māyīye tacca taṃ tasmiñchāstre niyamayediti || 28
4.29 . mokṣo'pi vaiṣṇavāderyaḥ svasaṃkalpena bhāvitaḥ |
4.29 . paraprakṛtisāyujyaṃ yadvāpyānandarūpatā || 29
4.30 . viśuddhacittamātraṃ vā dīpavatsaṃtatikṣayaḥ |
4.30 . sa savedyāpavedyātmapralayākalatāmayaḥ || 30
4.31 . taṃ prāpyāpi ciraṃ kālaṃ tadgogābhogabhuktataḥ |
4.31 . tattattvapralayānte tu tadūrdhvāṃ sṛṣṭimāgataḥ || 31
4.32 . mantratvameti saṃbodhādananteśena kalpitāt |
4.32 . etaccāgre taniṣyāma ityāstāṃ tāvadatra tat || 32
4.33 . tenājñajanatāklṛptapravādairyo viḍambitaḥ |
4.33 . asadgurau rūḍhacitsa māyāpāśena rañjitaḥ || 33
4.34 . so'pi sattarkayogena nīyate sadguruṃ prati |
4.34 . sattarkaḥ śuddhavidyaiva sā cecchā parameśituḥ || 34
4.35 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tenoktaṃ sa yiyāsuḥ śivecchayā |
4.35 . bhuktimuktiprasiddhyarthaṃ nīyate sadguruṃ prati || 35
4.36 . śaktipātastu tatraiṣa kramikaḥ saṃpravartate |
4.36 . sthitvā yo'sadgurau śāstrāntare vā satpathaṃ śritaḥ || 36
4.37 . guruśāstragate sattve'sattve cātra vibhedakam |
4.37 . śaktipātasya vaicitryaṃ purastātpravivicyate || 37
4.38 . uktaṃ svacchandaśāstre tatvaiṣṇavādyānpravādinaḥ |
4.38 . sarvānbhramayate māyā sāmokṣe mokṣalipsayā || 38
4.39 . yastu rūḍho'pi tatrodyatparāmarśaviśāradaḥ |

4.172 . iyatīṃ rūpavaicitrīmāśrayantyāḥ svasaṃvidaḥ |

4.192 . ahamityāhureṣaiva prakāśasya prakāśatā || 192

4.193 . vinānena jaḍāste syurjīvā iva vinā hṛdā || 193

4.212 . atra yāge gato rūḍhiṃ kaivalyamadhigacchati |

4.214 . na dvaitaṃ nāpi cādvaitaṃ liṅgapūjādikaṃ na ca |

4.221 . viṣaṃ na muhyate tena tadvadyogī mahāmatiḥ |

4.223 . anyonyāśrayavaiyarthyānavasthā itthamatra hi |

4.255 . uktaṃ bhargaśikhāyāṃ ca mṛtyukālakalādikam |
4.255 . dvaitādvaitavikalpotthaṃ grasate kṛtadhīriti || 255
4.256 . siddhānte liṅgapūjoktā viśvādhvamayatāvide |

4.258 . jaṭādi kaule tyāgo'sya sukhopāyopadeśataḥ |
4.258 . vratacaryā ca mantrārthatādātmyapratipattaye || 258

4.259 . kṣetrapīṭhopapīṭheṣu praveśo vighnaśāntaye || 259
4.260 . mantrādyārādhakasyātha tallābhāyopadiśyate |

4.263 . nirmaryādaṃ svasaṃbodhaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ buddhyatāmiti |
4.263 . parakīyamidaṃ rūpaṃ dhyeyametattu me nijam || 263
4.264 . jvālādiliṅgaṃ cānyasya kapālādi tu me nijam |

4.265 . nāma śaktiśivādyantametasya mama nānyathā |

4.266 . śrīsaṃtatistryambakākhyā tadardhāmardasaṃjñitā |
4.266 . itthamardhacatasro'tra maṭhikāḥ śāṃkare krame || 266
4.267 . yugakrameṇa kūrmādyā mīnāntā siddhasaṃtatiḥ |
4.267 . ādiśabdena ca gharaṃ pallī pīṭhopapīṭhakam || 267
4.268 . mudrā chummeti teṣāṃ ca vidhānaṃ svaparasthitam |
4.268 . tādātmyapratipattyai hi svaṃ saṃtānaṃ samāśrayet || 268
4.269 . bhuñjīta pūjayeccakraṃ parasaṃtāninā nahi |
4.269 . etacca mataśāstreṣu niṣiddhaṃ khaṇḍanā yataḥ || 269
4.270 . akhaṇḍe'pi pare tattve bhedenānena jāyate |
4.270 . evaṃ kṣetrapraveśādi saṃtānaniyamāntataḥ || 270
4.271 . nāsminvidhīyate taddhi sākṣānnaupayikaṃ śive |
4.271 . na tasya ca niṣodho ya nna tattattvasya khaṇḍanam || 271
4.272 . viśvātmano hi nāthasya svasminrūpe vikalpitau |
4.272 . vidhirniṣedho vā śaktau na svarūpasya khaṇḍane || 272
4.273 . paratattvapraveśe tu yameva nikaṭaṃ yadā |
4.273 . upāyaṃ vetti sa grāhyastadā tyājyo'tha vā kvacit || 273
4.274 . na yantraṇātra kāryeti proktaṃ śrītrikaśāsane |
4.274 . samatā sarvadevānāmovallīmantravarṇayoḥ || 274
4.275 . āgamanāṃ gatīnāṃ ca sarvaṃ śivamayaṃ yataḥ |
4.275 . sa hyakhaṇḍitasadbhāvaṃ śivatattvaṃ prapaśyati || 275
4.276 . yo hyakhaṇḍitasadbhāvamātmatattvaṃ prapadyate |
4.276 . ketakīkusumasaurabhe bhṛśaṃ bhṛṅga eva rasiko na makṣikā |
4.276 . bhairavīyaparamādvayārcane ko'pi rajyati maheśacoditaḥ || 276
4.277 . asmiṃśca yoge viśrāntiṃ kurvatāṃ bhavaḍambaraḥ |
4.277 . himānīva mahāgrīṣme svayameva vilīyate || 277
4.278 . alaṃ vātiprasaṅgena bhūyasātiprapañcite |
4.278 . yogyo'bhinavagupto'sminko'pi yāgavidhau budhaḥ || 278
4.279 . ityanuttarapadapravikāse śāktamaupayikamadya viviktam || 279


c5 atha śrītantrāloke pañcamamāhnikam

5.1 . āṇavena vidhinā paradhāma prepsatāmatha nirūpyata etat || 1
5.2 . vikalpasyaiva saṃskāre jāte niṣpratiyogini |
5.2 . abhīṣṭe vastuni prāptirniścitā bhogamokṣayoḥ || 2
5.3 . vikalpaḥ kasyacitsvātmasvātantryādeva susthiraḥ |
5.3 . upāyāntarasāpekṣyaviyogenaiva jāyate || 3
5.4 . kasyacittu vikalpo'sau svātmasaṃskaraṇaṃ prati |
5.4 . upāyāntarasāpekṣastatroktaḥ pūrvako vidhiḥ || 4
5.5 . vikalpo nāma cinmātrasvabhāvo yadyapi sthitaḥ |
5.5 . tathāpi niścayātmāsāvaṇoḥ svātantryayojakaḥ || 5
5.6 . niścayo bahudhā caiṣa tatropāyāśca bhedinaḥ |
5.6 . aṇuśabdena te coktā dūrāntikavibhedataḥ || 6
5.7 . tatra buddhau tathā prāṇe dehe cāpi pramātari |

5.127 . satsvartheṣu sukhādiṣu sphuṭataraṃ yadbhedavandhyodayaṃ yogī tiṣṭhati pūrṇaraśmivibhavastattattvamācīyatām || 127
5.128 . ityuccāravidhiḥ proktaḥ karaṇaṃ pravivicyate |
5.128 . taccetthaṃ triśiraḥśāstre parameśena bhāṣitam || 128
5.129 . grāhyagrāhakacidvyāptityāgākṣepaniveśanaiḥ |
5.129 . karaṇaṃ saptadhā prāhurabhyāsaṃ bodhapūrvakam || 129
5.130 . tadvyāptipūrvamākṣepe karaṇaṃ svapratiṣṭhatā |
5.130 . guruvaktrācca boddhavyaṃ karaṇaṃ yadyapi sphuṭam || 130
5.131 . tathāpyāgamarakṣārthaṃ tadagre varṇayiṣyate |
5.131 . ukto ya eṣa uccārastatra yo'sau sphuran sthitaḥ || 131
5.132 . avyaktānukṛtiprāyo dhvanirvarṇaḥ sa kathyate |
5.132 . sṛṣṭisaṃhārabījaṃ ca tasya mukhyaṃ vapurviduḥ || 132
5.133 . tadabhyāsavaśādyāti kramādyogī cidātmatām |
5.133 . tathā hyanacke sācke vā kādau sānte punaḥpunaḥ || 133
5.134 . smṛte proccārite vāpi sā sā saṃvitprasūyāte |
5.134 . bāhyārthasamayāpekṣā ghaṭādyā dhvanayo'pi ye || 134
5.135 . te'pyarthabhāvanāṃ kuryurmanorājyavadātmani |
5.135 . taduktaṃ parameśena bhairavo vyāpako'khile || 135
5.136 . iti bhairavaśabdasya saṃtatoccāraṇācchivaḥ |
5.136 . śrīmattraiśirase'pyuktaṃ mantroddhārasya pūrvataḥ || 136
5.137 . smṛtiśca smaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ sarvabhāveṣu vastutaḥ |
5.137 . mantrasvarūpaṃ tadbhāvyasvarūpāpattiyojakam || 137
5.138 . smṛtiḥ svarūpajanikā sarvabhāveṣu rañjikā |
5.138 . anekākārarūpeṇa sarvatrāvasthitena tu || 138
5.139 . svasvabhāvasya saṃprāptiḥ saṃvittiḥ paramārthataḥ |
5.139 . vyaktiniṣṭhā tato viddhi sattā sā kīrtitā parā || 139
5.140 . kiṃ punaḥ samayāpekṣāṃ vinā ye bījapiṇḍakāḥ |
5.140 . saṃvidaṃ spandapantyete neyuḥ saṃvidupāyatām || 140
5.141 . vācyābhāvādudāsīnasaṃvitspandātsvadhāmataḥ |
5.141 . prāṇollāsanirodhābhyāṃ bījapiṇḍeṣu pūrṇatā || 141
5.142 . sukhasītkārasatsamyaksāmyaprathamasaṃvidaḥ |
5.142 . saṃvedanaṃ hi prathamaṃ sparśo'nuttarasaṃvidaḥ || 142
5.143 . hṛtkaṇṭhyoṣṭhyatridhāmāntarnitarāṃ pravikāsini |
5.143 . caturdaśaḥ praveśo ya ekīkṛtatadātmakaḥ || 143
5.144 . tato visargoccārāṃśe dvādaśāntapathāvubhau |
5.144 . hṛdayena sahaikadhyaṃ nayate japatatparaḥ || 144
5.145 . kandahṛtkaṇṭhatālvagrakauṇḍilīprakriyāntataḥ |
5.145 . ānandamadhyanāḍyantaḥ spandanaṃ bījamāvahet || 145
5.146 . saṃhārabījaṃ khaṃ hṛtsthamoṣṭhyaṃ phullaṃ svamūrdhani |
5.146 . tejastryaśraṃ tālukaṇṭhe bindurūrdhvapade sthitaḥ || 146
5.147 . ityenayā budho yuktyā varṇajapyaparāyaṇaḥ |
5.147 . anuttaraṃ paraṃ dhāma praviśedacirātsudhīḥ || 147
5.148 . varṇaśabdena nīlādi yadvā dīkṣottare yathā |
5.148 . saṃhāranragnimaruto rudrabinduyutānsmaret || 148
5.149 . hṛdaye tanmayo lakṣyaṃ paśyetsaptadinādatha |
5.149 . visphuliṅgāgnivannīlapītaraktādicitritam || 149
5.150 . jājvalīti hṛdambhoje bījadīpaprabodhitam |
5.150 . dīpavajjvalito bindurbhāsate vighanārkavat || 150
5.151 . svayaṃbhāsātmanānena tādātmyaṃ yātyananyadhīḥ |
5.151 . śivena hematāṃ yadvattāmraṃ sūtena vedhitam || 151
5.152 . upalakṣaṇametacca sarvamantreṣu lakṣayet |
5.152 . yadyatsaṃkalpasaṃbhūtaṃ varṇajālaṃ hi bhautikam || 152
5.153 . tatsaṃvidādhikyavaśādabhautikamiva sthitam |
5.153 . atastathāvidhe rūpe rūḍho rohati saṃvidi || 153
5.154 . anācchāditarūpāyāmanupādhau prasannadhīḥ |
5.154 . nīle pīte sukhe duḥkhe saṃvidrūpamakhaṇḍitam || 154
5.155 . gurubhirbhāṣitaṃ tasmādupāyeṣu vicitratā |
5.155 . uccārakaraṇadhyānavarṇairebhiḥ pradarśitaḥ || 155
5.156 . anuttarapadaprāptāvabhyupāyavidhikramaḥ |
5.156 . akiṃciccintanaṃ vīryaṃ bhāvanāyāṃ ca sā punaḥ || 156
5.157 . dhyāne tadapi coccāre karaṇe so'pi taddhvanau |
5.157 . sa sthānakalpane bāhyamiti kramamupāśrayet || 157
5.158 . laṅghanena paro yogī mandabuddhiḥ krameṇa tu |
5.158 . vīryaṃ vinā yathā ṣaṇṭhastasyāpyastyatha vā balam |
5.158 . mṛtadeha iveyaṃ syādbāhyāntaḥparikalpanā || 158
5.159 . ityāṇave'nuttaratābhyupāyaḥ prokto nayaḥ spaṣṭapathena bāhyaḥ || 159

c6 atha śrītantrāloke

6.1 . sthānaprakalpākhyatayā sphuṭastu bāhyo'bhyupāyaḥ pravivicyate'tha || 1
6.2 . sthānabhedastridhā proktaḥ prāṇe dehe bahistathā |
6.2 . prāṇaśca pañcadhā dehe dvidhā bāhyāntaratvataḥ || 2
6.3 . maṇḍalaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ pātramakṣasūtraṃ sapustakam |
6.3 . liṅgaṃ tūraṃ paṭaḥ pustaṃ pratimā mūrtireva ca || 3
6.4 . ityekādaśadhā bāhyaṃ punastadbahudhā bhavet |
6.4 . tatra prāṇāśrayaṃ tāvadvidhānamupadiśyate || 4
6.5 . adhvā samasta evāyaṃ ṣaḍvidho'pyativistṛtaḥ |
6.5 . yo vakṣyate sa ekatra prāṇe tāvatpratiṣṭhitaḥ || 5
6.6 . adhvanaḥ kalanaṃ yattatkramākramatayā sthitam |
6.6 . kramākramau hi citraikakalanā bhāvagocare || 6
6.7 . kramākramātmā kālaśca paraḥ saṃvidi vartate |
6.7 . kālī nāma parā śaktiḥ saiva devasya gīyate || 7
6.8 . saiva saṃvidbahiḥ svātmagarbhībhūtau kramākramau |
6.8 . sphuṭayantī prarohaṇa prāṇavṛttiriti sthitā || 8
6.9 . saṃvinmātraṃ hi yacchuddhaṃ prakāśaparamārthakam |
6.9 . tanmeyamātmanaḥ projjhya viviktaṃ bhāsate nabhaḥ || 9
6.10 . tadeva śūnyarūpatvaṃ saṃvidaḥ parigīyate |
6.10 . neti neti vimarśena yogināṃ sā parā daśā || 10
6.11 . sa eva khātmā meye'sminbhedite svīkriyonmukhaḥ |
6.11 . patansamucchalattvena prāṇaspandormisaṃjñitaḥ || 11
6.12 . tenāhuḥ kila saṃvitprākprāṇe pariṇatā tathā |
6.12 . antaḥkaraṇatattvasya vāyurāśrayatāṃ gataḥ || 12
6.13 . iyaṃ sā prāṇanāśaktirāntarodyogadohadā |
6.13 . spandaḥ sphurattā viśrāntirjīvo hṛtpratibhā matā || 13
6.14 . sā prāṇavṛttiḥ prāṇādyai rūpaiḥ pañcabhirātmasāt |
6.14 . dehaṃ yatkurute saṃvitpūrṇastenaiṣa bhāsate || 14
6.15 . prāṇanāvṛttitādātmyasaṃvitkhacitadehajām |
6.15 . ceṣṭāṃ paśyantyato mugdhā nāstyanyaditi manvate || 15
6.16 . tāmeva bālamūrkhastrīprāyaveditṛsaṃśritām |
6.16 . matiṃ pramāṇīkurvantaścārvākāstattvadarśinaḥ || 16
6.17 . teṣāṃ tathā bhāvanā caddārḍhyameti nirantaram |
6.17 . taddehabhaṅge suptāḥ syurātādṛgvāsanākṣayāt || 17
6.18 . tadvāsanākṣaye tveṣāmakṣīṇaṃ vāsanāntaram |
6.18 . buddhaṃ kutaścitsaṃsūte vicitrāṃ phalasampadam || 18
6.19 . adārḍhyaśaṅkanātprācyavāsanātādavasthyataḥ |
6.19 . anyakartavyaśaithilyātsaṃbhāvyānuśayatvataḥ || 19
6.20 . atadrūḍhānyajanatākartavyaparilopanāt |
6.20 . nāstikyavāsanāmāhuḥ pāpātpāpīyasīmimām || 20
6.21 . alamaprastutenātha prakṛtaṃ pravivicyate |
6.21 . yāvānsamasta evāyamadhvā prāṇe pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 21
6.22 . dvidhā ca so'dhvā kriyayā mūrtyā ca pravibhajyate |
6.22 . prāṇa eva śikhā śrīmattriśirasyuditā hi sā || 22
6.23 . baddhā yāgādikāle tuṃ niṣkalatvācchivātmikā |
6.23 . yato'horātramadhye'syāścaturviṃśatidhā gatiḥ || 23
6.24 . prāṇavikṣeparandhrākhyaśataiścitraphalapradā |
6.24 . kṣapā śaśī tathāpāno nāda ekatra tiṣṭhati || 24
6.25 . jīvādityo na codgacchettuṭyardhaṃ sāndhyamīdṛśam |
6.25 . ūrdhvavaktro raviścandro'dhomukho vahnirantare || 25
6.26 . mādhyāhnikī mokṣadā syādvyomamadhyasthito raviḥ |
6.26 . anastamitasāro hi jantucakraprabodhakaḥ || 26
6.27 . binduḥ prāṇo hyahaścaiva ravirekatra tiṣṭhati |
6.27 . mahāsandhyā tṛtīyā tu supraśāntātmikā sthitā || 27
6.28 . evaṃ baddhā śikhā yatra tattatphalaniyojikā |
6.28 . ataḥ saṃvidi sarvo'yamadhvā viśramya tiṣṭhati || 28
6.29 . amūrtāyāḥ sarvagatvānniṣkriyāyāśca saṃvidaḥ |
6.29 . mūrtikriyābhāsanaṃ yatsa evādhvā maheśituḥ || 29
6.30 . adhvā krameṇa yātavye pade saṃprāptikāraṇam |
6.30 . dvaitināṃ bhogyabhāvāttu prabuddhānāṃ yato'dyate || 30
6.31 . iha sarvatra śabdānāmanvarthaṃ carcayedyataḥ |
6.31 . uktaṃ śrīmanniśācāre saṃjñātra trividhā matā || 31
6.32 . naimittikī prasiddhā ca tathānyā pāribhāṣikī |
6.32 . pūrvatve vā pradhānaṃ syāttatrāntarbhāvayettataḥ || 32
6.33 . ato'dhvaśabdasyokteyaṃ niruktirnoditāpi cet |
6.33 . kvacitsvabuddhyā sāpyūhyā kiyallekhyaṃ hi pustake || 33
6.34 . tatra kriyābhāsanaṃ yatso'dhvā kālāhva ucyate |
6.34 . varṇamantrapadābhikhyamatrāste'dhvatrayaṃ sphuṭam || 34
6.35 . yastu mūrtyavabhāsāṃśaḥ sa deśādhvā nigadyate |
6.35 . kalātattvapurābhikhyamantarbhūtamiha trayam || 35
6.36 . trikadvaye'tra pratyekaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ vapuḥ |
6.36 . yato'sti tena sarvo'yamadhvā ṣaḍvidha ucyate || 36
6.37 . ṣaḍvidhādadhvanaḥ prācyaṃ yadetattritayaṃ punaḥ |
6.37 . eṣa eva sa kālādhvā prāṇe spaṣṭaṃ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 37
6.38 . tattavamadhyasthitātkālādanyo'yaṃ kāla ucyate |
6.38 . eṣa kālo hi devasya viśvābhāsanakāriṇī || 38
6.39 . kriyāśaktiḥ samastānāṃ tattvānāṃ ca paraṃ vapuḥ |
6.39 . etadīśvaratattvaṃ tacchivasya vapurucyate || 39
6.40 . udriktābhogakāryātmaviśvaikātmyamidaṃ yataḥ |
6.40 . etadīśvararūpatvaṃ paramātmani yatkila || 40
6.41 . tatpramātari māyīye kālatattvaṃ nigadyate |
6.41 . śivādiśuddhavidyāntaṃ yacchivasya svakaṃ vapuḥ || 41
6.42 . tadeva puṃso māyādirāgāntaṃ kañcukībhavet |
6.42 . anāśritaṃ yato māyā kalāvidye sadāśivaḥ || 42
6.43 . īśvaraḥ kālaniyatī sadvidyā rāga ucyate |
6.43 . anāśritaḥ śūnyamātā buddhimātā sadāśivaḥ || 43
6.44 . īśvaraḥ prāṇamātā ca vidyā dehapramātṛtā |
6.44 . anāśrayo hi śūnyatvaṃ jñānameva hi buddhitā || 44
6.45 . viśvātmatā ca prāṇatvaṃ dehe vedyaikatānatā |
6.45 . tena prāṇapathe viśvākalaneyaṃ virājate || 45
6.46 . yena rūpeṇa tadvacmaḥ sadbhistadavadhīyatām |
6.46 . dvādaśāntāvadhāvasmindehe yadyapi sarvataḥ || 46
6.47 . otaprotātmakaḥ prāṇastathāpītthaṃ na susphuṭaḥ |
6.47 . yatno jīvanamātrātmā tatparaśca dvidhā mataḥ || 47
6.48 . saṃvedyaścāpyasaṃvedyo dvidhetthaṃ bhidyate punaḥ |
6.48 . sphuṭāsphuṭatvāddvaividhyaṃ pratyekaṃ paribhāvayet || 48
6.49 . saṃvedyajīvanābhikhyaprayatnaspandasundaraḥ |
6.49 . prāṇaḥ kandātprabhṛtyeva tathāpyatra na susphuṭaḥ || 49
6.50 . kandādhārātprabhṛtyeva vyavasthā tena kathyate |
6.50 . svacchandaśāstre nāḍīnāṃ vāyvādhāratayā sphuṭam || 50
6.51 . tatrāpi tu prayatno'sau na saṃvedyatayā sthitaḥ |
6.51 . vedyayatnāttu hṛdayātprāṇacāro vibhajyate || 51
6.52 . prabhoḥ śivasya yā śaktirvāmā jyeṣṭhā ca raudrikā |
6.52 . satadanyatamāvātmaprāṇau yatnavidhāyinau || 52
6.53 . prabhuśaktiḥ kvacinmukhyā yathāṅgamarudīraṇe |
6.53 . ātmaśaktiḥ kvacitkandasaṃkocaspandane yathā || 53
6.54 . prāṇaśaktiḥ kvacitprāṇacāre hārde yathā sphuṭam |
6.54 . trayaṃ dvayaṃ vā mukhyaṃ syādyogināmavadhāninām || 54
6.55 . avadhānādadṛṣṭāṃśādbalavattvādatheraṇāt |
6.55 . viparyayo'pi prāṇātmaśaktīnāṃ mukhyatāṃ prati || 55
6.56 . vāmā saṃsāriṇāmīśā prabhuśaktirvidhāyinī |
6.56 . jyeṣṭhā tu suprabuddhānāṃ bubhutsūnāṃ ca raudrikā || 56
6.57 . vāmā saṃsāravamanā jyeṣṭhā śivamayī yataḥ |
6.57 . drāvayitrī rujāṃ raudrī roddhrī cākhilakarmaṇām || 57
6.58 . sṛṣṭyāditattvamajñātvā na mukto nāpi mocayet |
6.58 . uktaṃ ca śrīyogacāre mokṣaḥ sarvaprakāśanāt || 58
6.59 . utpattisthitisaṃhārān ye na jānanti yoginaḥ |
6.59 . na muktāste tadajñānabandhanaikādhivāsitāḥ || 59
6.60 . sṛṣṭyādayaśca te sarve kālādhīnā na saṃśayaḥ |
6.60 . sa ca prāṇātmakastasmāduccāraḥ kathyate sphuṭaḥ || 60
6.61 . hṛdayātprāṇacāraśca nāsikyadvādaśāntataḥ |
6.61 . ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulo jantoḥ sarvasya svāṅgulakramāt || 61
6.62 . kṣodiṣṭhe vā mahiṣṭhe vā dehe tādṛśa eva hi |
6.62 . vīryamojo balaṃ spandaḥ prāṇacāraḥ samaṃ tataḥ || 62
6.63 . ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgule cāre yadgamāgamayugmakam |
6.63 . nālikātithimāsābdatatsaṅghro'tra sphuṭaṃ sthitaḥ || 63
6.64 . tuṭiḥ sapādāṅgulayukprāṇastāḥ ṣoḍaśocchvasan |
6.64 . niḥśvasaṃścātra caṣakaḥ sapañcāṃśe'ṅgule'ṅgule || 64
6.65 . śvāsapraśvāsayornālī proktāhorātra ucyate |
6.65 . navāṅgulāmbudhituṭau praharāste'bdhayo dinam || 65
6.66 . nirgame'ntarniśenendū tayoḥ saṃdhye tuṭerdale |
6.66 . ketuḥ sūrye vidhau rāhurbhaumādervārabhāginaḥ || 66
6.67 . praharadvayamanyeṣāṃ grahāṇāmudayo'ntarā |
6.67 . siddhirdavīyasī mokṣo'bhicāraḥ pāralaukikī || 67
6.68 . aihikī dūranaikaṭyātiśayā praharāṣṭake |
6.68 . madhyāhnamadhyaniśayorabhijinmokṣabhogadā || 68
6.69 . nakṣatrāṇāṃ tadanyeṣāmudayo madhyataḥ kramāt |
6.69 . nāgā lokeśamūrtīśā gaṇeśā jalatattvataḥ || 69
6.70 . pradhānāntaṃ nāyakāśca vidyātattvādhināyakāḥ |
6.70 . sakalādyāśca kaṇṭhyoṣṭhyaparyantā bhairavāstathā || 70
6.71 . śaktayaḥ pārameśvaryo vāmaśā vīranāyakāḥ |
6.71 . aṣṭāvaṣṭau ye ya itthaṃ vyāpyavyāpakatājuṣaḥ || 71
6.72 . sthūlasūkṣmāḥ kramātteṣāmudayaḥ praharāṣṭake |
6.72 . dine krūrāṇi saumyāni rātrau karmāṇyasaṃśayam || 72
6.73 . krūratā saumyatā vābhisandherapi nirūpitā |
6.73 . dinarātrikṣaye muktiḥ sā vyāptidhyānayogataḥ || 73
6.74 . te coktāḥ parameśena śrīmadvīrāvalīkule |
6.74 . sitāsitau dīrghahrasvau dharmādharmau dinakṣape || 74
6.75 . kṣīyete yadi taddīkṣā vyāptyā dhyānena yogataḥ |
6.75 . ahorātraḥ prāṇacāre kathito māsa ucyate || 75
6.76 . dinaṃ kṛṣṇo niśā śuklaḥ pakṣau karmasu pūrvavat |
6.76 . yāḥ ṣoḍaśoktāstithayastāsu ye pūrvapaścime || 76
6.77 . tayostu viśramo'rdhe'rdhe tithyaḥ pañcadaśetarāḥ |
6.77 . sapāde dvyaṅgule tithyā ahorātro vibhajyate || 77
6.78 . prakāśaviśramavaśāttāveva hi dinakṣape |
6.78 . saṃvitpratikṣaṇaṃ yasmātprakāśānandayoginī || 78
6.79 . tau klṛptau yāvati tayā tāvatyeva dinakṣape |
6.79 . yāvatyeva hi saṃvittiruditoditasusphuṭā || 79
6.80 . tāvāneva kṣaṇaḥ kalpo nimeṣo vā tadastvapi |
6.80 . yāvānevodayo vittervedyaikagrahatatparaḥ || 80
6.81 . tāvadevāstamayanaṃ veditṛsvātmacarvaṇam |
6.81 . vedye ca bahirantarvā dvaye vātha dvayojjhite || 81
6.82 . sarvathā tanmayībhūtirdinaṃ vettṛsthatā niśā |
6.82 . veditā vedyaviśrānto vettā tvantarmukhasthitiḥ || 82
6.83 . purā vicārayanpaścātsattāmātrasvarūpakaḥ |
6.83 . jāgradveditṛtā svapno vettṛbhāvaḥ purātanaḥ || 83
6.84 . paraḥ suptaṃ kṣaye rātridinayosturyamadvayam |
6.84 . kadācidvastuviśrāntisāmyenātmani carvaṇam || 84
6.85 . vedyavedakasāmyaṃ tatsā rātridinatulyatā |
6.85 . vedye viśrāntiradhikā dinadairghyāya tatra tu || 85
6.86 . nyūnā syātsvātmaviśrāntirviparīte viparyayaḥ |
6.86 . svātmautsukye prabuddhe hi vedyaviśrāntiralpikā || 86
6.87 . itthameva divārātrinyūnādhikyakramaṃ vadet |
6.87 . yathā deheṣvahorātranyūnādhikyādi no samam || 87
6.88 . tathā pureṣvapītyevaṃ tadviśeṣeṇa noditam |
6.88 . śrītraiyambakasantānavitatāmbarabhāskaraḥ || 88

6.95 . candrasūryātmanā dehaṃ pūrayetpravilāpayet |

6.102 . arkaḥ pramāṇaṃ somastu meyaṃ jñānakriyātmakau |

6.107 . grāsamokṣāntare snānadhyānahomajapādikam |

6.108 . grāsyagrāsakatākṣobhaprakṣaye kṣaṇamāviśan |

6.109 . tithiccheda ṛṇaṃ kāso vṛddhirniḥśvasanaṃ dhanam |

6.113 . aihikaṃ grahaṇe cātra sādhakānāṃ mahāphalam |

6.116 . praveśe tu tulāsthe'rke tadeva viṣuvadbhavet |

6.117 . garbhatā prodbubhūṣiṣyadbhāvaścāthodbubhūṣutā |

6.119 . āmutrike jhaṣaḥ kumbho mantrādeḥ pūrvasevane |

6.129 . iti prāṇodaye yo'yaṃ kālaḥ śaktyekavigrahaḥ |

6.143 . tadīyenāplutaṃ viśvaṃ tiṣṭhettāvanniśāgame |

6.194 . kṣīyate tadupāsāyāṃ yenordhvādharaḍambaraḥ |

6.196 . saṃcaransarvatodikkaṃ daśadhaiva vibhāvyate |

6.197 . nāḍyantaraśritā nāḍīḥ krāmandehe samasthitiḥ |

6.199 . indvarkāgnimaye mukhye caraṃstiṣṭhatyaharniśam |

6.200 . tāvadvahannahorātraṃ caturviṃśatidhā caret |

6.202 . madhyāhne dakṣaviṣuvannavaprāṇaśatīṃ vahet |

6.203 . navāsuśatamekaikaṃ tato viṣuvaduttaram |

6.204 . yadvattathāntaḥ saṅkrāntirnavaprāṇaśatāni sā |
