6.205 . ārabhyāharniśāvṛddhihrāsasaṅkrāntigo'pyasau |
6.205 . rātryantadinapūrvāṃśau madhyāhno divasakṣayaḥ || 205
6.206 . sa śarvaryudayo madhyamudakto viṣutedṛśī |
6.206 . vyāptau viṣeryato vṛttiḥ sāmyaṃ ca vyāptirucyate || 206
6.207 . tadarhati ca yaḥ kālo viṣuvattadihoditaḥ |
6.207 . viṣuvatprabhṛti hrāsavṛddhī ye dinarātrige || 207
6.208 . tatkrameṇaiva saṃkrāntihrāsavṛddhī divāniśoḥ |
6.208 . itthaṃ samānamaruto varṣadvayavikalpanam || 208
6.209 . cāra ekatra nahyatra śvāsapraśvāsacarcanam |
6.209 . samāne'pi tuṭeḥ pūrvaṃ yāvatṣaṣṭyabdagocaram || 209
6.210 . kālasaṃkhyā susūkṣmaikacāragā gaṇyate budhaiḥ |
6.210 . saṃdhyāpūrvāhṇamadhyāhnamadhyarātrādi yatkila || 210
6.211 . antaḥsaṃkrāntigaṃ grāhyaṃ tanmukhyaṃ tatphaloditeḥ |
6.211 . uktaḥ samānagaḥ kāla udāne tu nirūpyate || 211
6.212 . prāṇavyāptau yaduktaṃ tadudāne'pyatra kevalam |
6.212 . nāsāśaktyantayoḥ sthāne brahmarandhrordhvadhāmanī || 212
6.213 . tenodāne'tra hṛdayānmūrdhanyadvādaśāntagam |
6.213 . tuṭyādiṣaṣṭivarṣāntaṃ viśvaṃ kālaṃ vicārayet || 213
6.214 . vyāne tu viśvātmamaye vyāpake kramavarjite |
6.214 . sūkṣmasūkṣmocchaladrūpamātraḥ kālo vyavasthitaḥ || 214
6.215 . sṛṣṭiḥ pravilayaḥ sthemā saṃhāro'nugraho yataḥ |
6.215 . kramātprāṇādike kāle taṃ taṃ tatrāśrayettataḥ || 215
6.216 . prāṇacāre'tra yo varṇapadamantrodayaḥ sthitaḥ |
6.216 . yatnajo'yatnajaḥ sūkṣmaḥ paraḥ sthūlaḥ sa kathyate || 216
6.217 . eko nādātmako varṇaḥ sarvavarṇāvibhāgavān |
6.217 . so'nastamitarūpatvādanāhata ihoditaḥ || 217
6.218 . sa tu bhairavasadbhāvo mātṛsadbhāva eṣa saḥ |
6.218 . parā saikākṣarā devī yatra līnaṃ carācaram || 218
6.219 . hrasvārṇatrayamekaikaṃ ravyaṅgulamathetarat |
6.219 . praveśa iti ṣaḍvarṇāḥ sūryendupathagāḥ kramāt || 219
6.220 . ikārokārayorādisandhau saṃdhyakṣaradvayam |
6.220 . e+o iti praveśe tu ai+au iti dvayaṃ viduḥ || 220
6.221 . ṣaṇṭhārṇāni praveśe tu dvādaśāntalalāṭayoḥ |
6.221 . gale hṛdi ca bindvarṇavisargau paritaḥsthitau || 221
6.222 . kādipañcakamādyasya varṇasyāntaḥ sadoditam |
6.222 . evaṃ sasthānavarṇānāmantaḥ sā sārṇasantatiḥ || 222
6.223 . hṛdyeṣa prāṇarūpastu sakāro jīvanātmakaḥ |
6.223 . binduḥ prakāśo hārṇaśca pūraṇātmatayā sthitaḥ || 223
6.224 . uktaḥ paro'yamudayo varṇānāṃ sūkṣma ucyate |
6.224 . praveśe ṣoḍaśaunmukhye ravayaḥ ṣaṇṭhavarjitāḥ || 224
6.225 . tadevendvarkamatrānye varṇāḥ sūkṣmodayastvayam |
6.225 . kālo'rdhamātraḥ kādīnāṃ trayastriṃśata ucyate || 225
6.226 . mātrā hrasvāḥ pañca dīrghāṣṭakaṃ dvistriḥ plutaṃ tu lṝ |
6.226 . ekāśītimimāmardhamātrāṇāmāha no guruḥ || 226
6.227 . yadvaśādbhagavānekāśītikaṃ mantramabhyadhāt |
6.227 . ekāśītipadā devī śaktiḥ proktā śivātmikā || 227
6.228 . śrīmātaṅge tathā dharmasaṃghātātmā śivo yataḥ |
6.228 . tathā tathā parāmarśaśakticakreśvaraḥ prabhuḥ || 228
6.229 . sthūlaikāśītipadajaparāmarśairvibhāvyate |
6.229 . tata eva parāmarśo yāvatyekaḥ samāpyate || 229
6.230 . tāvattatpadamuktaṃ no suptiṅniyamayāntritam |
6.230 . ekāśītipadodāravimarśaktamabṛṃhitaḥ || 230
6.231 . sthūlopāyaḥ paropāyastveṣa mātrākṛto layaḥ |
6.231 . ardhamātrā nava nava syuścaturṣu caturṣu yat || 231
6.232 . aṅguleṣviti ṣaṭtriṃśatyekāśītipadodayaḥ |
6.232 . aṅgule navabhāgena vibhakte navamāśakāḥ || 232
6.233 . vedā mātrārdhamanyattu dvicatuḥṣaṅguṇaṃ trayam |
6.233 . evamaṅgularandhrāṃśacatuṣkadvayagaṃ laghu || 233
6.234 . dīrghaṃ plutaṃ kramāddvitriguṇamardhaṃ tato'pi hal |
6.234 . kṣakārastryardhamātrātmā mātrikaḥ satathāntarā || 234
6.235 . viśrāntāvardhamātrāsya tasmiṃstu kalite sati |
6.235 . aṅgulārdhe'dribhāgena tvardhamātrā purā punaḥ || 235
6.236 . kṣakāraḥ sarvasaṃyogagrahaṇātmā tu sarvagaḥ |
6.236 . sarvavarṇodayādyantasandhiṣūdayabhāgvibhuḥ || 236
6.237 . itthaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśake cāre varṇānāmudayaḥ phale |
6.237 . krūre saumye vilomena hādi yāvadapaścimam || 237
6.238 . hṛdyakāro dvādaśānte hakārastadidaṃ viduḥ |
6.238 . ahamātmakamadvaitaṃ yaḥ prakāśātmaviśramaḥ || 238
6.239 . śivaśaktyavibhāgena mātraikāśītikā tviyam |
6.239 . dvāsaptatāvaṅguleṣu dviguṇatvena saṃsaret || 239
6.240 . uktaḥ sūkṣmodayastraidhaṃ dvidhoktastu parodayaḥ |
6.240 . atha sthūlodayo'rṇānāṃ bhaṇyate guruṇoditaḥ || 240
6.241 . ekaikamardhapraharaṃ dine vargāṣṭakodayaḥ |
6.241 . rātrau ca hrāsavṛddhyatra kecidāhurna ke'pi tu || 241
6.242 . eṣa vargodayo rātrau divā cāpyardhayāmagaḥ |
6.242 . prāṇatrayodaśaśatī pañcāśadadhikā ca sā || 242
6.243 . adhyardhā kila saṃkrāntirvarge varge divāniśoḥ |
6.243 . tadaikye tūdayaścāraśatānāṃ saptaviṃśatiḥ || 243
6.244 . nava vargāṃstu ye prāhusteṣāṃ prāṇaśatī svīn[viḥ] |
6.244 . satribhāgaiva saṃkrāntirvarge pratyekamucyate || 244
6.245 . aharniśaṃ tadaikye tu śatānāṃ śruticakṣuṣī |
6.245 . sthūlo vargodayaḥ so'yamathārṇodaya ucyate || 245
6.246 . ekaikavarṇe prāṇānāṃ dviśataṃ ṣoḍaśādhikam |
6.246 . bahiścaṣakaṣaṭtriṃśaddina itthaṃ tathāniśi || 246
6.247 . śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tatra raudraṃ śāktamathottaram |
6.247 . yāmalasthitiyoge tu rudraśaktyavibhāgitā || 247
6.248 . dinarātryavibhāge tu dṛgvahnyabdhyasucāraṇāḥ |
6.248 . sapañcamāṃśā nāḍī ca bahirvarṇodayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 248
6.249 . iti pañcāśikā seyaṃ varṇānāṃ paricarcitā |
6.249 . ekonāṃ ye tu tāmāhustanmataṃ saṃpracakṣmahe || 249
6.250 . vedāścārāḥ pañcamāṃśanyūnaṃ cārārdhamekaśaḥ |
6.250 . varṇe'dhikaṃ taddviguṇamavibhāge divāniśoḥ || 250
6.251 . sthūlo varṇodayaḥ so'yaṃ purā sūkṣmo nigadyate || 251
6.252 . iti kālatattvamuditaṃ śāstramukhāgamanijānubhavasiddham || 252

c7 atha śrītantrāloke

7.1 . atha paramarahasyo'yaṃ cakrāṇāṃ bhaṇyate'bhyudayaḥ || 1
7.2 . ityayatnajamākhyātaṃ yatnajaṃ tu nigadyate |
7.2 . bījapiṇḍātmakaṃ sarvaṃ saṃvidaḥ spandanātmatām || 2
7.3 . vidadhatparasaṃvittāvupāya iti varṇitam |
7.3 . yathāraghaṭṭacakrāgraghaṭīyantraughavāhanam || 3
7.4 . ekānusaṃdhiyatnena citraṃ yantrodayaṃ bhajet |
7.4 . ekānusaṃdhānabalājjāte mantrodaye'niśam || 4
7.5 . tanmantradevatā yatnāttādātmyena prasīdati |
7.5 . khe rasaikākṣi nityotthe tadardhaṃ dvikapiṇḍake || 5
7.6 . trike sapta sahasrāṇi dviśatītyudayo mataḥ |
7.6 . catuṣke tu sahasrāṇi pañca caiva catuḥśatī || 6
7.7 . pañcārṇe'bdhisahasrāṇi triśatī viṃśatistathā |
7.7 . ṣaṭke sahasratritayaṃ ṣaṭśatī codayo bhavet || 7
7.8 . saptake trisahasraṃ tu ṣaḍaśītyadhikaṃ smṛtam |
7.8 . śataistu saptaviṃśatyā varṇāṣṭakavikalpite || 8
7.9 . caturviṃśatiśatyā tu navārṇeṣūdayo bhavet |
7.9 . adhiṣaṣṭyekaviṃśatyā śatānāṃ daśavarṇake || 9
7.10 . ekānnaviṃśatiśataṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ śivārṇake |
7.10 . aṣṭādaśa śatāni syurudayo dvādaśārṇake || 10
7.11 . trayodaśārṇe dvāṣaṣṭyā śatāni kila ṣoḍaśa |
7.11 . tricatvāriṃśatā pañcadaśeti bhuvanārṇake || 11
7.12 . caturdaśaśatī khābdhiḥ syātpañcadaśavarṇake |
7.12 . trayodaśaśatī sārdhā ṣoḍaśārṇe tu kathyate || 12
7.13 . śatadvādaśikā saptadaśārṇe saikasaptatiḥ |
7.13 . aṣṭādaśārṇe vijñeyā śatadvādaśikā budhaiḥ || 13
7.14 . caturviṃśatisaṃkhyāke cakre navaśatī bhavet |
7.14 . saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyāte tūdayo'ṣṭaśatātmakaḥ || 14
7.15 . dvātriṃśake mahācakre ṣaṭśatī pañcasaptatiḥ |
7.15 . dvicaturviṃśake cakre sārdhāṃ śatacatuṣṭayīm || 15
7.16 . udayaṃ piṇḍayogajñaḥ piṇḍamantreṣu lakṣayet |
7.16 . catuṣpañcāśake cakre śatānāṃ tu catuṣṭayam || 16
7.17 . saptatriṃśatsahārdhena triśatyaṣṭāṣṭake bhavet |
7.17 . ardhamardhatribhāgaśca ṣaṭṣaṣṭirdviśatī bhavet || 17
7.18 . ekāśītipade cakre udayaḥ prāṇacāragaḥ |
7.18 . cakre tu ṣaṇṇavatyākhye sapādā dviśatī bhavet || 18
7.19 . aṣṭottaraśate cakre dviśatastūdayo bhavet |
7.19 . krameṇetthamidaṃ cakraṃ ṣaṭkṛtvo dviguṇaṃ yadā || 19
7.20 . tato'pi dviguṇe'ṣṭāṃśasyārdhamadhyardhamekakam |
7.20 . tato'pi sūkṣmakuśalairardhārdhādiprakalpane || 20
7.21 . bhāgaṣoḍaśakasthityā sūkṣmaścāro'bhilakṣyate |
7.21 . evaṃ prayatnasaṃruddhaprāṇacārasya yoginaḥ || 21
7.22 . krameṇa prāṇacārasya grāsa evopajāyate |
7.22 . prāṇagrāsakramāvāptakālasaṃkarṣaṇasthitiḥ || 22
7.23 . saṃvidekaiva pūrṇā syājjñānabhedavyapohanāt |
7.23 . tathā hi prāṇacārasya navasyānudaye sati || 23
7.24 . na kālabhedajanito jñānabhedaḥ prakalpate |
7.24 . saṃvedyabhedānna jñānaṃ bhinnaṃ śikharivṛttavat || 24
7.25 . kālastu bhedakastasya sa tu sūkṣmaḥ kṣaṇo mataḥ |
7.25 . saukṣmyasya cāvadhirjñānaṃ yāvattiṣṭhati sa kṣaṇaḥ || 25
7.26 . anyathā na sa nirvaktuṃ nipuṇairapi pāryate |
7.26 . jñānaṃ kiyadbhavettāvattadabhāvo na bhāsate || 26
7.27 . tadabhāvaśca no tāvadyāvattatrākṣavartmani |
7.27 . arthe vātmapradeśe vā na saṃyogavibhāgitā || 27
7.28 . sā cedudayate spandamayī tatprāṇagā dhruvam |
7.28 . bhavedeva tataḥ prāṇaspandābhāve na sā bhavet || 28
7.29 . tadabhāvānna vijñānābhāvaḥ saivaṃ tu saiva dhīḥ |
7.29 . na cāsau vastuto dīrghā kālabhedavyapohanāt || 29
7.30 . vastuto hyata eveyaṃ kālaṃ saṃvinna saṃspṛśet |
7.30 . ata ekaiva saṃvittirnānārūpe tathātathā || 30
7.31 . vindānā nirvikalpāpi vikalpo bhāvagocare |
7.31 . spandāntaraṃ na yāvattaduditaṃ tāvadeva saḥ || 31
7.32 . tāvāneko vikalpaḥ syādvividhaṃ vastu kalpayan |
7.32 . ye tvitthaṃ na vidusteṣāṃ vikalpo nopapadyate || 32
7.33 . sa hyeko na bhavetkaścittrijagatyāpi jātucit |
7.33 . śabdārūṣaṇayā jñānaṃ vikalpaḥ kila kathyate || 33
7.34 . sā ca syātkramikaivetthaṃ kiṃ kathaṃ ko vikalpayet |
7.34 . ghaṭa ityapi neyānsyādvikalpaḥ kā kathā sthitau || 34
7.35 . na vikalpaśca ko'pyasti yo mātrāmātraniṣṭhitaḥ |
7.35 . na ca jñānasamūho'sti teṣāmayugapatsthiteḥ || 35
7.36 . tenāstaṅgata evaiṣa vyavahāro vikalpajaḥ |
7.36 . tasmātspandāntaraṃ yāvannodiyāttāvadekakam || 36
7.37 . vijñānaṃ tadvikalpātmadharmakoṭīrapi spṛśet |
7.37 . ekāśītipadodāraśaktyāmarśātmakastataḥ || 37
7.38 . vikalpaḥ śivatādāyī pūrvameva nirūpitaḥ |
7.38 . yathā karṇau nartayāmītyevaṃ yatnāttathā bhavet || 38
7.39 . cakracāragatādyatnāttadvattaccakragaiva dhīḥ |
7.39 . japahomārcanādīnāṃ prāṇasāmyamato vidhiḥ || 39
7.40 . siddhāmate kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ prāṇasamonmanā |
7.40 . uktaṃ ca yoginīkaule tadetatparameśinā || 40
7.41 . padamantrākṣare cakre vibhāgaṃ śaktitattvagam |
7.41 . padeṣu kṛtvā mantrajño japādau phalabhāgbhavet || 41
7.42 . dvitrisaptāṣṭasaṃkhyātaṃ lopayecchatikodayam |
7.42 . iti śaktisthitā mantrā vidyā vā cakranāyakāḥ || 42
7.43 . padapiṇḍasvarūpeṇa jñātvā yojyāḥ sadā priye |
7.43 . nityodaye mahātattve udayasthe sadāśive || 43
7.44 . ayuktāḥ śaktimārge tu na japtāścodayena ye |
7.44 . te na siddhyanti yatnena japtāḥ koṭiśatairapi || 44
7.45 . mālāmantreṣu sarveṣu mānaso japa ucyate |
7.45 . upāṃśurvā śaktyudayaṃ teṣāṃ na parikalpayet || 45
7.46 . padamantreṣu sarveṣu yāvattatpadaśaktigam |
7.46 . śakyate satataṃ yuktaistāvajjapyaṃ tu sādhakaiḥ || 46
7.47 . tāvatī teṣu vai saṃkhyā padeṣu padasaṃjñitā |
7.47 . tāvantamudayaṃ kṛtvā tripadoktyāditaḥ kramāt || 47
7.48 . dvādaśākhye dvādaśite cakre sārdhaṃ śataṃ bhavet |
7.48 . udayastaddhi sacatuścatvāriṃśacchataṃ bhavet || 48
7.49 . ṣoḍaśākhye dvādaśite dvānavatyadhike śate |
7.49 . cārārdhena samaṃ proktaṃ śataṃ dvādaśakādhikam || 49
7.50 . ṣoḍaśākhye ṣoḍaśite bhaveccaturaśītigaḥ |
7.50 . udayo dviśataṃ taddhi ṣaṭpañcāśatsamuttaram || 50
7.51 . cārāṣṭabhāgāṃstrīnatra kathayantyadhikānbudhāḥ |
7.51 . aṣṭāṣṭake dvādaśite pādārdhaṃ viṃśatiṃ vasūn || 51
7.52 . udayaḥ saptaśatikā sāṣṭā ṣaṣṭiryato hi saḥ |
7.52 . eṣa cakrodayaḥ proktaḥ sādhakānāṃ hitāvahaḥ || 52
7.53 . niruddhya mānasīrvṛttīścakre viśrāntimāgataḥ |
7.53 . vyutthāya yāvadviśrāmyettāvaccārodayo hyayam || 53
7.54 . pūrṇe samudaye tvatra praveśaikātmyanirgamāḥ |
7.54 . traya ityata evoktaḥ siddhau madhyodayo varaḥ || 54
7.55 . ādyantodayanirmuktā madhyamodayasaṃyutāḥ |
7.55 . mantravidyācakragaṇāḥ siddhibhājo bhavanti hi || 55
7.56 . mantracakrodayajñastu vidyācakrodayārthavit |
7.56 . kṣipraṃ siddhyediti proktaṃ śrīmaddviṃśatike trike || 56
7.57 . dvistriścaturvā mātrābhirvidyāṃ vā cakrameva vā |
7.57 . tattvodayayutaṃ nityaṃ pṛthagbhūtaṃ japetsadā || 57
7.58 . piṇḍākṣarapadairmantramekaikaṃ śaktitattvagam |
7.58 . bahvakṣarastu yo mantro vidyā vā cakrameva vā || 58
7.59 . śaktisthaṃ naiva taṃ tatra vibhāgastvoṃnamontagaḥ |
7.59 . asmiṃstattvodaye tasmādahorātrastriśastriśaḥ || 59
7.60 . vibhajyate vibhāgaśca punareva triśastriśaḥ |
7.60 . pūrvodaye tu viśramya dvitīyenollasedyadā || 60
7.61 . viśeccārdhardhikāyogāttadoktārdhodayo bhavet |
7.61 . yadā pūrṇodayātmā tu samaḥ kālastrike sphuret || 61
7.62 . praveśaviśrāntyullāse syātsvatryaṃśodayastadā |
7.62 . etyeṣa kālavibhavaḥ prāṇa eva pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 62
7.63 . sa spade khe sa taccityāṃ tenāsyāṃ viśvaniṣṭhiatiḥ |
7.63 . ataḥ saṃvitpratiṣṭhānau yato viśvalayodayau || 63
7.64 . śaktyante'dhvani tatspandāsaṃkhyātā vāstavī tataḥ |
7.64 . uktaṃ śrīmālinītantre gātre yatraiva kutracit || 64
7.65 . vikāra upajāyeta tattattvaṃ tattvamuttamam |
7.65 . prāṇe pratiṣṭhitaḥ kālastadāviṣṭā ca yattanuḥ || 65
7.66 . dehe pratiṣṭhitasyāsya tato rūpaṃ nirūpyate |
7.66 . citspandaprāṇavṛttīnāmantyā yā sthūlatā suṣiḥ || 66
7.67 . sā nāḍīrūpatāmetya dehaṃ saṃtānayedimam |
7.67 . śrīsvacchande'ta evoktaṃ yathā parṇaṃ svatantubhiḥ || 67
7.68 . vyāptaṃ tadvattanurdvāradvāribhāvena nāḍibhiḥ |
7.68 . pādāṅguṣṭhādikordhvasthabrahmakuṇḍalikāntagaḥ || 68
7.69 . kālaḥ samastaścaturaśītāvevāṅguleṣvitaḥ |
7.69 . dvādaśāntāvadhiṃ kiṃcitsūkṣmakālasthitiṃ viduḥ || 69
7.70 . ṣaṇṇavatyāmadhaḥ ṣaḍdvikramāccāṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
7.70 . atra madhyamasaṃcāriprāṇodayalayāntare || 70
7.71 . viśve sṛṣṭilayāste tu citrā vāyvantarakramāt |
7.71 . ityeṣa sūkṣmaparimarśanaśīlanīyaścakrodayo'nubhavaśāstradṛśā mayoktaḥ || 71


c8 atha śītantrāloke aṣṭamamāhnikaṃ

8.1 . deśādhvano'pyatha samāsavikāsayogātsaṃgīyate vidhirayaṃ śivaśāstradṛṣṭaḥ || 1
8.2 . vicārito'yaṃ kālādhvā kriyāśaktimayaḥ prabhoḥ |
8.2 . mūrtivaicitryajastajjo deśādhvātha nirūpyate || 2
8.3 . adhvā samasta evāyaṃ cinmātre saṃpratiṣṭhitaḥ |
8.3 . yattatra nahi viśrāntaṃ tannabhaḥkusumāyate || 3
8.4 . saṃviddvāreṇa tatsṛṣṭe śūnye dhiyi marutsu ca |
8.4 . nāḍīcakrānucakreṣu barhirdehe'dhvasaṃsthitiḥ || 4
8.5 . tatrādhvaivaṃ nirūpyo'yaṃ yatastatprakriyākramam |
8.5 . anusaṃdadhadeva drāgyogī bhairavatāṃ vrajet || 5
8.6 . didṛkṣayaiva sarvārthān yadā vyāpyāvatiṣṭhate |
8.6 . tadā kiṃ bahunoktena ityuktaṃ spandaśāsane || 6
8.7 . jñātvā samastamadhvānaṃ tadīśeṣu vilāpayet |
8.7 . tān dehaprāṇadhīcakre pūrvavadgālayetkramāt || 7
8.8 . tatsamastaṃ svasaṃvittau sā saṃvidbharitātmikā |
8.8 . upāsyamānā saṃsārasāgarapralayānalaḥ || 8
8.9 . śrīmahīkṣottare caitānadhveśān gururabravīt |
8.9 . brahmānantātpradhānāntaṃ viṣṇuḥ puṃsaḥ kalāntagam || 9
8.10 . rudro granthau ca māyāyāmīśaḥ sādākhyagocare |
8.10 . anāśritaḥ śivastasmādvyāptā tadvyāpakaḥ paraḥ || 10
8.11 . evaṃ śivatvamāpannamiti matvā nyarūpyata |
8.11 . na prakriyāparaṃ jñānamiti svacchandaśāsane || 11
8.12 . triśiraḥśāsane bodho mūlamadhyāgrakalpitaḥ |
8.12 . ṣaṭtriṃśattattvasaṃrambhaḥ smṛtirbhedavikalpanā || 12
8.13 . avyāhatavibhāgo'smibhāvo mūlaṃ tu bodhagam |
8.13 . samastatattvabhāvo'yaṃ svātmanyevāvibhāgakaḥ || 13
8.14 . bodhamadhyaṃ bhavetkiṃcidādhārādheyalakṣaṇam |
8.14 . tattvabhedavibhāgena svabhāvasthitilakṣaṇam || 14
8.15 . bodhāgraṃ tattu vidbodhaṃ nistaraṅgaṃ bṛhatsukham |
8.15 . saṃvidekātmatānītabhūtabhāvapurādikaḥ || 15
8.16 . avyavacchinnasaṃvittirbhairavaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
8.16 . śrīdevyāyāmale coktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśattattvasundaram || 16
8.17 . adhvānaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ dhyāyansadyaḥ śivamayo bhavet |
8.17 . yadyapyamuṣya nāthasya saṃvittyanatirekiṇaḥ || 17
8.18 . pūrṇasyordhvādimadhyāntavyavasthā nāsti vāstavī |
8.18 . tathāpi pratipattṝṇāṃ pratipādayitustathā || 18
8.19 . svasvarūpānusāreṇa madhyāditvādikalpanāḥ |
8.19 . tataḥ pramātṛsaṃkalpaniyamātpārthivaṃ viduḥ || 19
8.20 . tattvaṃ sarvāntarālasthaṃ yatsarvāvaraṇairvṛtam |
8.20 . tadatra pārthive tattve kathyate bhuvanasthitiḥ || 20
8.21 . netā kaṭāharudrāṇāmanantaḥ kāmasevinām |
8.21 . potārūḍho jalasyāntarmadyapānavighūrṇitaḥ || 21
8.22 . sa devaṃ bhairavaṃ dhyāyannāgaiśca parivāritaḥ |
8.22 . kālāgrerbhuvanaṃ cordhve koṭiyojanamucchritam || 22
8.23 . lokānāṃ bhasmasādbhāvabhayānnordhva sa vīkṣate |
8.23 . sa ca vyāptāpi viśvasya yasmātpluṣyannimāṃ bhuvam || 23
8.24 . narakebhyaḥ purā vyaktastenāsau tadadho mataḥ |
8.24 . daśa koṭyo vibhorjvālā tadardha śūnyamūrdhvataḥ || 24
8.25 . tadūrdhve narakādhīśāḥ kramādduḥkhaikavedanāḥ |
8.25 . śdho madhye tadūrdhve ca sthitā bhedāntarairvṛtāḥ || 25
8.26 . avīcikumbhīpākākhyarauravāsteṣvanukramāt |
8.26 . ekādaśaikādaśa ca daśetyantaḥ śarāgni tat || 26
8.27 . pratyekameṣāmekonā koṭirucchritirantaram |
8.27 . lakṣamatra khavedāsyasaṃkhyānāmantarā sthitiḥ || 27
8.28 . kūṣmāṇḍa ūrdhve lakṣonakoṭisthānastadīśitā |
8.28 . śāstraviruddhācaraṇātkṛṣṇaṃ ye karma vidadhate || 28
8.29 . tatra bhīmairlokapuruṣaiḥ pīḍyante bhogaparyantam |
8.29 . ye sakṛdapi parameśaṃ śivamekāgreṇa cetasā śaraṇam || 29
8.30 . yānti na te narakayujaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ teṣāṃ sukhālpatādāyi |
8.30 . sahasranavakotsedhamekāntaramatha kramāt || 30
8.31 . pātālāṣṭakamekaikamaṣṭame hāṭakaḥ prabhuḥ |
8.31 . pratilokaṃ niyuktātmā śrīkaṇṭho haṭhato bahūḥ || 31
8.32 . siddhīrdadātyasāvevaṃ śrīmadrauravaśāsane |
8.32 . vratino ye cikarmasthā niṣiddhācārakāriṇaḥ || 32
8.33 . dīkṣitā api ye luptasamayā naca kurvate |
8.33 . prāyaścittāṃstathā tatsthā vāmācārasya dūṣakāḥ || 33
8.34 . devāgnidravyavṛttyaṃśajīvinaścottamasthitāḥ |
8.34 . adhaḥsthagāruḍādyanyamantrasevāparāyaṇāḥ || 34
8.35 . te hāṭakavibhoragre kiṅkarā vividhātmakāḥ |
8.35 . te tu tatrāpi deveśaṃ bhaktyā cetparyupāsate || 35
8.36 . tadīśatattve līyante kramācca parame śive |
8.36 . anyathā ye tu vartante tadbhoganiratātmakāḥ || 36
8.37 . te kālavahnisaṃtāpadīnākrandaparāyaṇāḥ |
8.37 . guṇatattve nilīyante tataḥ sṛṣṭimukhe punaḥ || 37
8.38 . pātyante mātṛbhirghorayātanaughapurassaram |
8.38 . adhamādhamadeheṣu nijakarmānurūpataḥ || 38
8.39 . mānuṣānteṣu tatrāpi kecinmantravidaḥ kramāt |
8.39 . mucyante'nye tu badhyante pūrvakṛtyānusārataḥ || 39
8.40 . ityeṣa gaṇavṛttānto nāmnā hulahulādinā |
8.40 . proktaṃ bhagavatā śrīmadānandādhikaśāsane || 40
8.41 . pātālordhve sahasrāṇi viṃśatirbhūkaṭāhakaḥ |
8.41 . siddhātantre tu pātālapṛṣṭhe yakṣīsamāvṛtam || 41
8.42 . bhadrakālyāḥ puraṃ yatra tābhiḥ krīḍanti sādhakāḥ |
8.42 . tatastamastaptabhūmistataḥśūnyaṃ tato'hayaḥ || 42
8.43 . etāni yātanāsthānaṃ gurumantrādidūṣiṇām |
8.43 . tato bhūmyūrdhva [madhya] to meruḥ sahasrāṇi sa ṣoḍaśa || 43
8.44 . magnastanmūlavistārastaddvayenordhvavistṛtiḥ |
8.44 . sahasrābdhivasūcchrāyo haimaḥ sarvāmarālayaḥ || 44
8.45 . madhyordhvādhaḥ samudvṛttaśarāvacaturaśrakaḥ |
8.45 . bhairavīyaṃ ca talliṅgaṃ dharaṇī cāsya pīṭhikā || 45
8.46 . sarve devā nilīnā hi tatra tatpūjitaṃ sadā |
8.46 . madhye merusabhā dhātustadīśadiśi ketanam || 46
8.47 . jyotiṣkaśikharaṃ śaṃbhoḥ śrīkaṇṭhāṃśaśca sa prabhuḥ |
8.47 . avaruhya sahasrāṇi manovatyāścaturdaśa || 47
8.48 . cakravāṭaścaturdikko meruratra tu lokapāḥ |
8.48 . amarāvatikendrasya pūrvasyāṃ dakṣiṇena tām || 48
8.49 . atsaraḥsiddhasādhyāstāmuttareṇa vināyakāḥ |
8.49 . tejovatī svadiśyagneḥ purī tāṃ paścimena tu || 49
8.50 . viśvedevā viśvakarmā kramāttadanugāśca ye |
8.50 . yāmyāṃ saṃyamanī tāṃ tu paścimena kramātsthitāḥ || 50
8.51 . mātṛnandā svasaṃkhyātā rudrāstatsādhakāstathā |
8.51 . kṛṣṇāṅgārā nirṛtiśca tāṃ pūrveṇa piśācakāḥ || 51
8.52 . rakṣāṃsi siddhagandharvāstūttareṇottareṇa tām |
8.52 . vāruṇī śuddhavatyākhyā bhūtaugho dakṣiṇena tām || 52
8.53 . uttareṇottareṇaināṃ vasuvidyādharāḥ kramāt |
8.53 . vāyorgandhavatī tasyā dakṣiṇe kinnarāḥ punaḥ || 53
8.54 . vīṇāsarasvatī devī nāradastumburustathā |
8.54 . mahodayendorguhyāḥ syuḥ paścime'syāḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 54
8.55 . kuberaḥ karmadevāśca tathā tatsādhakā api |
8.55 . yaśasvinī maheśasya tasyāḥ paścimato hariḥ || 55
8.56 . dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇe brahmāśvinau dhanvantariḥ kramāt |
8.56 . mairave cakravāṭe'sminnevaṃ mukhyāḥ puro'ṣṭadhā || 56
8.57 . antarālagatāstvanyāḥ punaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ |
8.57 . iṣṭāpūrtaratāḥ puṇye varṣeye bhārate narāḥ || 57
8.58 . te merugāḥ sakṛcchambhuṃ ye vārcanti yathocitam |
8.58 . meroḥ pradakṣiṇāpyodagdikṣu viṣkambhaparvatāḥ || 58
8.59 . mandaro gandhamādaśca vipulo'tha supārśvakaḥ |
8.59 . sitapītanīlaraktāste kramātpādaparvatāḥ || 59
8.60 . etairbhuvamavaṣṭabhya merustiṣṭhati niścalaḥ |
8.60 . caitrarathanandanākhye vaiśrājaṃ pitṛvanaṃ vanānyāhuḥ || 60
8.61 . raktodamānasasitaṃ bhadraṃ caitaccatuṣṭayaṃ sarasām |
8.61 . vṛkṣāḥ kadambajambvaśvatthanyagrodhakāḥ kramaśaḥ || 61
8.62 . eṣu ca caturṣvacaleṣu trayaṃ trayaṃ kramaśa etadāmnātam |
8.62 . mervadho lavaṇābdhyantaṃ jambudvīpaḥ samantataḥ || 62
8.63 . lakṣamātraḥ sa navadhā jāto maryādaparvataiḥ |
8.63 . niṣadho hemakūṭaśca himavāndakṣiṇe trayaḥ || 63
8.64 . lakṣaṃ sahasranavatistadaśītiriti kramāt |
8.64 . nīlaḥ śvetastriśṛṅgaśca tāvantaḥ savyataḥ punaḥ || 64
8.65 . meroḥ ṣaḍete maryādācalāḥ pūrvāparāyatāḥ |
8.65 . pūrvato mālyavānpaścādgandhamādanasaṃjñitaḥ || 65
8.66 . savyottarāyatau tau tu catustriṃśatsahasrakau |
8.66 . aṣṭāvete tato'pyanyau dvau dvau pūrvādiṣu kramāt || 66
8.67 . jāṭharaḥ kūṭahimavadyātrajārudhiśṛṅgiṇaḥ |
8.67 . evaṃ sthito vibhāgo'tra varṣasiddhyai nirūpyate || 67
8.68 . samantāccakravāṭādho'narkendu caturaśrakam |
8.68 . sahasranavavistīrṇamilākhyaṃ trimukhāyuṣam || 68
8.69 . meroḥ paścimato gandhamādo yastasya paścime |
8.69 . ketumālaṃ kulādrīṇāṃ saptakena vibhūṣitam || 69
8.70 . meroḥ pūrva mālyavānyo bhadrāśvastasya pūrvataḥ |
8.70 . sahasradaśakāyustatsapañcakulaparvatam || 70
8.71 . pūrvapaścimataḥ savyottarataśca kramādime |
8.71 . dvātriṃśacca catustriṃśatsahasrāṇi nirūpite || 71
8.72 . merorudakśṛṅgavānyastadbahiḥ kuruvarṣakam |
8.72 . cāpavannavasāhasramāyustatra trayodaśa || 72
8.73 . kuruvarṣasyottare'tha vāyavye'bdhau kramāccharāḥ |
8.73 . daśa ceti sahasrāṇi dvīpau candro'tha bhadrakaḥ || 73
8.74 . yau śvetaśṛṅgiṇau merorvāme madhye hiraṇmayam |
8.74 . tayornavakavistīrṇamāyuścārdhatrayodaśa || 74
8.75 . tatra vai vāmataḥ śvetanīlayo ramyako'ntare |
8.75 . sahasranavavistīrṇamāyurdvādaśa tāni ca || 75
8.76 . merordakṣiṇato hemaniṣadhau yau tadantare |
8.76 . haryākhyaṃ navasāhasraṃ tatsahasrādhikāyuṣam || 76
8.77 . tatraiva dakṣiṇe hemahimavaddvitayāntare |
8.77 . kainnaraṃ navasāhasraṃ tatsahasrādhikāyuṣam || 77
8.78 . tatraiva dakṣiṇe merorhimavānyasya dakṣiṇe |
8.78 . bhārataṃ navasāhasraṃ cāpavatkarmabhogabhūḥ || 78
8.79 . ilāvṛtaṃ ketubhadraṃ kuruhairaṇyaramyakam |
8.79 . harikinnaravarṣe ca bhogabhūrna tu karmabhūḥ || 79
8.80 . atra bāhulyataḥ karmabhūbhāvo'trāpyakarmaṇām |
8.80 . paśūnāṃ karmasaṃskāraḥ syāttādṛgdṛḍhasaṃskṛteḥ || 80
8.81 . saṃbhavantyapyasaṃskārā bhārate'nyatra cāpi hi |
8.81 . dṛḍhaprāktanasaṃskārādīśecchātaḥ śubhāśubham || 81
8.82 . sthānāntare'pi karmāsti dṛṣṭaṃ tacca purātane |
8.82 . tatra tretā sadā kālo bhārate tu caturyugam || 82
8.83 . bhārate navakhaṇḍaṃ ca sāmudreṇāmbhasātra ca |
8.83 . sthalaṃ pañcaśatī tadvajjalaṃ ceti vibhajyate || 83
8.84 . indraḥ kaśerustāmrābho nāgīyaḥ prāggabhastimān |
8.84 . saumyagāndharvavārāhāḥ kanyākhyaṃ cāsamudrataḥ || 84
8.85 . kanyādvīpe ca navame dakṣiṇenābdhimadhyagāḥ |
8.85 . upadvīpāḥ ṣaṭkulādrisaptakena vibhūṣite || 85
8.86 . aṅgayavamalayaśaṅkuḥ kumudavarāhau ca malayago'gastya |
8.86 . tatraiva ca trikūṭe laṅkā ṣaḍamī hyupadvīpāḥ || 86
8.87 . dvīpopadvīpagāḥ prāyo mlecchā nānāvidhā janāḥ |
8.87 . muktākāñcanaratnāḍhyā iti śrīruruśāsane || 87
8.88 . bhārate yatkṛtaṃ karma kṣapitaṃ vāpyavīcitaḥ |
8.88 . śivāntaṃ tena muktirvā kanyākhye tu viśeṣataḥ || 88
8.89 . mahākālādikā rudrakoṭiratraiva bhārate |
8.89 . gaṅgādipañcaśatikā janma tenātra durlabham || 89
8.90 . anyavarṣeṣu paśuvadbhogātkarmātivāhanam |
8.90 . prāpyaṃ manorathātītamapi bhāratajanmanām || 90
8.91 . nānāvarṇāśramācārasukhaduḥkhavicitratā |
8.91 . kanyādvīpe yatastena karmabhūḥ seyamuttamā || 91
8.92 . puṃsā sitāsitānyatra kurvatāṃ kila siddhyataḥ |
8.92 . parāparau svarnirayāviti rauravavārtike || 92
8.93 . evaṃ meroradho jambūrabhito yaḥ sa vistarāt |
8.93 . syātsaptadaśadhā khaṇḍairnavabhistu samāsataḥ || 93
8.94 . manoḥ svāyaṃbhuvasyāsan sutā daśa tatastrayaḥ |
8.94 . prāvrajannatha jambvākhye rājā yo'gnīdhranāmakaḥ || 94
8.95 . tasyābhavannava sutāstato'yaṃ navakhaṇḍakaḥ |
8.95 . nābhiryo navamastasya naptā bharata ārṣabhiḥ || 95
8.96 . tasyāṣṭau tanayāḥ sākaṃ kanyayā navamoṃ'śakaḥ |
8.96 . bhuktaistairnavadhā tasmāllakṣayojanamātrakāt || 96
8.97 . lakṣaikamātro lavaṇastadbāhye'sya puro'drayaḥ |
8.97 . ṛṣabho dundubhirdhūmraḥ kaṅkadroṇendavo hyudak || 97
8.98 . varāhanandanāśokāḥ paścātsahabalāhakau |
8.98 . dakṣiṇa cakramainākau vāḍavo'ntastayoḥ sthitaḥ || 98
8.99 . abdherdakṣiṇataḥ khākṣisahasrātikramādgiriḥ |
8.99 . vidyutvāṃstrisahasrocchridāyāmo'tra phalāśinaḥ || 99
8.100 . maladigdhā dīrghakeśaśmaśravo gosadharmakāḥ |
8.100 . nagnāḥ saṃvatsarāśītijīvinastṛṇabhojinaḥ || 100
8.101 . niryantrāṇi sadā tatra dvārāṇi bilasiddhaye |
8.101 . ityetadgurubhirgītaṃ śrīmadrauravaśāsane || 101
8.102 . itthaṃ ya eṣa lavaṇasamudraḥ pratipāditaḥ |
8.102 . tadbahiḥ ṣaḍamī dvīpāḥ pratyekaṃ svārṇavairvṛtāḥ || 102
8.103 . kramadviguṇitāḥ ṣaḍbhirmanuputrairadhiṣṭhitāḥ |
8.103 . śākakuśakrauñcāḥ śalmaligomedhābjamiti ṣaḍdvīpāḥ |
8.103 . kṣīradadhisarpiraikṣavamadirāmadhurāmbukāḥ ṣaḍambudhayaḥ || 103
8.104 . medhātithirvapuṣmāñjyotiṣmāndyutimatā havī rājā |
8.104 . saṃvara iti śākādiṣu jambudvīpe nyarūpi cāgnīdhraḥ || 104
8.105 . girisaptakaparikalpitatāvatkhaṇḍāstu pañca śākādyāḥ |
8.105 . puṣkarasaṃjño dvidalo hariyamavaruṇendavo'tra pūrvādau || 105
8.106 . tripañcāśacca lakṣāṇi dvikoṭyayutapañcakam |
8.106 . svādvarṇavāntaṃ mervardhādyojananāmiyaṃ pramā || 106
8.107 . saptamajaladherbāhye haimī bhūḥ koṭidaśakamatha lakṣam |
8.107 . ucchrityā vistārādayutaṃ loketarācalaḥ kathitaḥ || 107
8.108 . lokālokadigaṣṭaka saṃsthaṃ rudrāṣṭakaṃ salokeśam |
8.108 . kevalamityapi kecillokālokāntare ravirna bahiḥ || 108
8.109 . pitṛdevapathāvasyodagdakṣiṇagau svajātpare vīthyau |
8.109 . bhānoruttaradakṣiṇamayanadvayametadeva kathayanti || 109
'sarveṣāmuttaro merurlokālokaśca dakṣiṇaḥ.'
8.110 . udayāstamayāvitthaṃ sūryasya paribhāvayet || 110
8.111 . ardharātro'marāvatyāṃ yāmyāyāmastameva ca |
8.111 . madhyandinaṃ tadvāruṇyāṃ saumye sūryodayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 111
8.112 . udayo yo'marāvatyāṃ so'rdharātro yamālaye |
8.112 . ke'staṃ saumye ca madhyāhna itthaṃ sūryagatāgate || 112
8.113 . pañcatriṃ śatkoṭisaṃkhyā lakṣāṇyekonaviṃśatiḥ |
8.113 . catvāriṃśatsahasrāṇi dhvāntaṃ lokācalādbahiḥ || 113
8.114 . saptasāgaramānastu garbhodākhyaḥ samudrarāṭ |
8.114 . lokālokasya parato yadgarbhe nikhilaiva bhūḥ || 114
8.115 . siddhātantre'tra garbhābdhestīre kauśeyasaṃjñitam |
8.115 . maṇḍalaṃ garuḍastatra siddhapakṣasamāvṛtaḥ || 115
8.116 . krīḍantiṃ parvatāgre te nava cātra kulādrayaḥ |
8.116 . tata uṣṇodakāstriṃśannadyaḥpātālagāstataḥ || 116
8.117 . caturdiṅnaimirodyānaṃ yoginīsevitaṃ sadā |
8.117 . tato merustato nāgā meghā hemāṇḍakaṃ tataḥ || 117
8.118 . brahmaṇo'ṇḍakaṭāhena merorardhena koṭayaḥ |
8.118 . pañcāśadevaṃ daśasu dicu bhūrlokasaṃjñitam || 118
8.119 . paśukhagamṛgatarumānuṣasarīsṛpaiḥ ṣaḍbhireṣa bhūrlokaḥ |
8.119 . vyāptaḥ piśācarakṣogandharvāṇāṃ sayakṣāṇām || 119
8.120 . vidyābhṛtāṃ ca kiṃ vā bahunā sarvasya bhūtasargasya |
8.120 . abhimānato yatheṣṭaṃ bhogasthānaṃ nivāsaśca || 120
8.121 . bhuvarlokastathā tvārkāllakṣamekaṃ tadantare |
8.121 . daśa vāyupathāste ca pratyekamayutāntarāḥ || 121
8.122 . ādyo vāyupathastatra vitataḥ paricarcyate |
8.122 . pañcāśadyojanordhve syādṛtarddhirnāma mārutaḥ || 122
8.123 . āpyāyakaḥ sa jantūnāṃ tataḥ prācetaso bhavet |
8.123 . pañcāśadyojanādūrdhva tasmādūrdhva śatena tu || 123
8.124 . senānīvāyuratraite mūkameghāstaḍinmucaḥ |
8.124 . ye mahyāḥ krośamātreṇa tiṣṭhanti jalavarṣiṇaḥ || 124
8.125 . tebhya ūrdhva śatānmeghā bhekādiprāṇivarṣiṇaḥ |
8.125 . pañcāśadūrdhvamogho'tra viṣavāripravarṣiṇaḥ || 125
8.126 . meghāḥ skandodbhavāścānye piśācā oghamārute |
8.126 . tataḥ pañcāśadūrdhvaṃ syurmeghā mārakasaṃjñakāḥ || 126
8.127 . tatra sthāne mahādevajanmānaste vināyakāḥ |
8.127 . ye haranti kṛtaṃ karma narāṇāmakṛtātmanām || 127
8.128 . pañcāśadūrdhvaṃ vajrāṅko vāyuratropalāmbudāḥ |
8.128 . vidyādharādhamāścātra vajrāṅke saṃpratiṣṭhitāḥ || 128
8.129 . ye vidyāpauruṣe ye ca śmaśānādiprasādhane |
8.129 . mṛtāstatsiddhisiddhāste vajrāṃke maruti sthitāḥ || 129
8.130 . pañcāśadūrdhvaṃ vajrāṃkādvaidyuto'śanivarṣiṇaḥ |
8.130 . abdā apsarasaścātra ye ca puṇyakṛto narāḥ || 130
8.131 . bhṛgau vahnau jale ye ca saṃgrāme cānivartinaḥ |
8.131 . gograhe vadhyamokṣe vā mṛtāste vaidyute sthitāḥ || 131
8.132 . vaidyutādraivatastāvāṃstatra puṣṭivahāmbudāḥ |
8.132 . ūrdhvaṃ ca rogāmbumucaḥ saṃvartāstadanantare || 132
8.133 . rocanāñjanabhasmādisiddhāstatraiva raivate |
8.133 . krodhodakamucāṃ sthānaṃ viṣāvartaḥ sa mārutaḥ || 133
8.134 . pañcāśadūrdhvaṃ tatraiva durdinābdā hutāśajāḥ |
8.134 . vidyādharaviśeṣāśca tathā ye parameśvaram || 134
8.135 . gāndharveṇa sadārcanti viṣāvarte'tha te sthitāḥ |
8.135 . viṣāvartācchatādūrdhva durjayaḥ śvāsasaṃbhavaḥ || 135
8.136 . brahmaṇo'tra sthitā meghāḥ pralaye vātakāriṇaḥ |
8.136 . puṣkarābdā vāyugamā gandharvāśca parāvahe || 136
8.137 . jīmūtameghāstatsaṃjñāstathā vidyādharottamāḥ |
8.137 . ye ca rūpavratā lokā āvahe te pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 137
8.138 . mahāvahe tvīśakṛtāḥ prajāhitakarāmbudāḥ |
8.138 . mahāparivahe meghāḥ kapālotthā maheśituḥ || 138
8.139 . mahāparivahānto'yamṛtarddheḥ prāṅmarutpathaḥ |
8.139 . agnikanyā mātaraśca rudraśaktyā tvadhiṣṭhitāḥ || 139
8.140 . dvitīye tatpare siddhacāraṇā nijakarmajāḥ |
8.140 . turye devāyudhānyaṣṭau diggajāḥ pañcame punaḥ || 140
8.141 . ṣaṣṭhe garutmānanyasmiṅgaṅgānyatra vṛṣo vibhuḥ |
8.141 . dakṣastu navame brahmaśaktyā samadhiti[ni]ṣṭhitaḥ || 141
8.142 . daśame vasavo rudrā ādityāśca marutpathe |
8.142 . navayojanasāhasro vigraho'rkasya maṇḍalam || 142
8.143 . triguṇaṃ jñānaśaktiḥ sā tapatyarkatayā prabhoḥ |
8.143 . svarlokastu bhuvarlokāddhruvāntaṃ paribhāṣyate || 143
8.144 . sūryāllakṣeṇa śītāṃśuḥ kriyāśaktiḥ śivasya sā |
8.144 . candrāllakṣeṇa nākṣatraṃ tato lakṣadvayena tu || 144
8.145 . pratyekaṃ bhaumataḥ sūryasutānte pañcakaṃ viduḥ |
8.145 . saurāllakṣeṇa saptarṣivargastasmāddhruvastathā || 145
8.146 . brahmaivāpararūpeṇa brahmasthāne dhruvo'calaḥ |
8.146 . medhībhūto vimānānāṃ sarveṣāmupari dhruvaḥ || 146
8.147 . atra baddhāni sarvāṇyapyūhyante'nilamaṇḍale |
8.147 . svassapta mārutaskandhā āmeghādyāḥ pradhānataḥ || 147
8.148 . itaśca kratuhotrādi kṛtvā jñānavivarjitāḥ |
8.148 . svaryānti tatkṣaye lokaṃ mānuṣyaṃ puṇyaśeṣataḥ || 148
8.149 . evaṃ bhūmerdhruvāntaṃ syāllakṣāṇi daśa pañca ca |
8.149 . dve koṭī pañca cāśītirlakṣāṇi svargato mahān || 149
8.150 . mārkaṇḍādyā ṛṣimunisiddhāstatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
8.150 . nivartitādhikārāśca devā mahati saṃsthitāḥ || 150
8.151 . mahāntarāle tatrānye tvadhikārabhujo janāḥ |
8.151 . aṣṭau koṭyo mahallokājjano'tra kapilādayaḥ || 151
8.152 . tiṣṭhanti sādhyāstatraiva bahavaḥ sukhabhāginaḥ |
8.152 . janāttaporkakoṭyo'tra sanakādyā mahādhiyaḥ || 152
8.153 . prajāpatīnāṃ tatrādhikāro brahmātmajanmanām |
8.153 . brahmālayastu tapasaḥ satyaḥ ṣoḍaśa koṭayaḥ || 153
8.154 . tatra sthitaḥ sa svayambhūrviśvamāviṣkarotyadaḥ |
8.154 . satye vedāstathā cānye karmadhyānena bhāvitāḥ || 154
8.155 . ānandaniṣṭhāstatrordhvekoṭirvairiñcamāsanam |
8.155 . brahmāsanātkoṭiyugmaṃ puraṃ viṣṇornirūpitam || 155
8.156 . dhyānapūjājapairviṣṇorbhaktā gacchanti tatpadam |
8.156 . vaiṣṇavātsaptakoṭībhirbhuvanaṃ parameśituḥ || 156
8.157 . rudrasya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakarturbrahmāṇḍavartmani |
8.157 . dīkṣājñānavihīnā ye liṅgārādhanatatparāḥ || 157
8.158 . te yāntyaṇḍāntare raudraṃ puraṃ nādhaḥ kadācana |
8.158 . tatsthāḥ sarve śivaṃ yānti rudrāḥ śrīkaṇṭhadīkṣitāḥ || 158
8.159 . adhikārakṣaye sākaṃ rudrakanyāgaṇena te |
8.159 . puraṃ puraṃ ca rudrordhvamuttarottaravṛddhitaḥ || 159
8.160 . brahmāṇḍādhaśca rudrordhva daṇḍapāṇeḥ puraṃ sa ca |
8.160 . śivecchayā dṛṇātyaṇḍaṃ mokṣamārga karoti ca || 160
8.161 . śarvarudrau bhīmabhavāvugro devo mahānatha |
8.161 . īśāna iti bhūrlokātsapta lokeśvarāḥ śivāḥ || 161
8.162 . sthūlairviśeṣairārabdhāḥ sapta lokāḥ pare punaḥ |
8.162 . sūkṣmairiti guruścaiva rurau samyaṅnyarūpayat || 162
8.163 . ye brahmaṇādisarge svaśarīrānnirmitāḥ prabhūtākhyāḥ |
8.163 . sthūlāḥ pañca viśeṣāḥ saptāmī tanmayā lokāḥ || 163
8.164 . parato liṅgādhāraiḥ sūkṣmaistanmātrajairmahābhūtaiḥ |
8.164 . lokānāmāvaraṇairviṣṭabhya parasperaṇa gandhādyaiḥ || 164
8.165 . kālāgnerdaṇḍapāṇyantamaṣṭānavatikoṭayaḥ |
8.165 . ata ūrdhvaṃ kaṭāho'ṇḍe sa ghanaḥ koṭiyojanam || 165
8.166 . pañcāśatkoṭayaścordhvaṃ bhūpṛṣṭhādadharaṃ tathā |
8.166 . evaṃ koṭiśataṃ bhūḥ syātsauvarṇastaṇḍulastataḥ || 166
8.167 . śatarudrāvadhirhuphaṭbhedayettattu duḥśamam |
8.167 . pratidikkaṃ daśa daśetyevaṃ rudraśataṃ bahiḥ || 167
8.168 . brahmāṇḍādhārakaṃ tacca svaprabhāveṇa sarvataḥ |
8.168 . aṇḍasvarūpaṃ gurubhiścoktaṃ śrīrauravādiṣu || 168
8.169 . vyakterabhimukhībhūtaḥ pracyutaḥ śaktirūpataḥ |
8.169 . āvāpavānanirbhakto vastupiṇḍo'ṇḍa ucyate || 169
8.170 . tamoleśānuviddhasya kapālaṃ sattvamuttaram |
8.170 . rajo'nuviddhaṃ nirmṛṣṭaṃ sattvamasyādharaṃ tamaḥ || 170
8.171 . vastupiṇḍa iti proktaṃ śivaśaktisamūhabhāk |
8.171 . aṇḍaḥ syāditi tadvyaktau saṃmukhībhāva ucyate || 171
8.172 . tathāpi śivamagnānāṃ śaktīnāmaṇḍatā bhavet |
8.172 . tadartha vākyamaparaṃ tā hi na cyutaśaktitaḥ || 172
8.173 . tanvakṣādau mā prasāṅkṣīdaṇḍateti padāntaram |
8.173 . tanvakṣādiṣu naivāste kasyāpyāvāpanaṃ yataḥ || 173
8.174 . tanvakṣasamudāyatve kathamekatvamityataḥ |
8.174 . anirbhakta iti proktaṃ sājātyaparidarśakam || 174
8.175 . vināpi vastupiṇḍākhyapadenaikaikaśo bhavet |
8.175 . tattveṣvaṇḍasvabhāvatvaṃ nanvevamapi kiṃ na tat || 175
8.176 . guṇatanmātrabhūtaughamaye tattve prasajyate |
8.176 . ucyate vastuśabdena tanvakṣabhuvanātmakam || 176
8.177 . rūpamuktaṃ yatastena tatsamūho'ṇḍa ucyate |
8.177 . bhavecca tatsamūhatvaṃ patyurviśvavapurbhṛtaḥ || 177
8.178 . tadartha bhedakānyanyānyupāttānīti darśitam |
8.178 . tāvanmātrāsvavasthāsu māyādhīne'dhvamaṇḍale || 178
8.179 . mā bhūdaṇḍatvamityāhuranye bhedakayojanam |
8.179 . itthamuktaviriñcāṇḍamṛto rudrāḥ śataṃ hi yat || 179
8.180 . teṣāṃ sve patayo rudrā ekādaśa mahārciṣaḥ |
8.180 . ananto'tha kapālyāgniryamanairṛtakau balaḥ || 180
8.181 . śīghro nidhīśo vidyeśaḥ śambhuḥ savīrabhadrakaḥ |
8.181 . madhu madhukṛtaḥ kadambaṃ kesarajālāni yadvadāvṛṇate || 181
8.182 . tadvatte śivarudrā brahmāṇḍamasaṃkhyaparivārāḥ |
8.182 . śarāṣṭaniyutaṃ koṭirityeṣāṃ sanniveśanam || 182
8.183 . śrīkaṇṭhādhiṣṭhitāste ca sṛjanti saṃharanti ca |
8.183 . īśvaratvaṃ diviṣadāmiti rauravavārtike || 183
8.184 . siddhātantre tu hemāṇḍācchatakoṭerbahiḥ śatam |
8.184 . aṇḍānāṃ kramaśo dvidviguṇaṃ rūpyādiyojitam || 184
8.185 . teṣu krameṇa brahmāṇaḥ saṃsyurdviguṇajīvitāḥ |
8.185 . kṣīyante kramaśaste ca tadante tattvamammayam || 185
8.186 . dharāto'tra jalādi syāduttarottarataḥ kramāt |
8.186 . daśadhāhaṅkṛtāntaṃ dhīstasyāḥ syācchatadhā tataḥ || 186
8.187 . sahasradhā vyaktamataḥ pauṃsnaṃ daśasahasradhā |
8.187 . niyatirlakṣadhā tasmāttasyāstu daśalakṣadhā || 187
8.188 . kalāntaṃ koṭidhā tasmānmāyā viddaśakoṭidhā |
8.188 . īśvaraḥ śatakoṭiḥ syāttasmātkoṭisahasradhā || 188
8.189 . sādākhyaṃ vyaśnute tacca śaktirvṛndena saṃkhyayā |
8.189 . vyāpinī sarvamadhvānaṃ vyāpyadevī vyavasthitā || 189
8.190 . aprameyaṃ tataḥ śuddhaṃ śivatattvaṃ paraṃ viduḥ |
8.190 . jalādeḥ śivatattvāntaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ kenacicchivāt || 190
8.191 . ṛte tataḥ śivajñānaṃ paramaṃ mokṣakāraṇam |
8.191 . tathā cāha mahādevaḥ śrīmatsvacchandaśāsane || 191
8.192 . nānyathā mokṣamāyāti paśurjñānaśatairapi |
8.192 . śivajñānaṃ na bhavati dīkṣāmaprāpya śāṅkarīm || 192
8.193 . prāktanī pārameśī sā pauruṣeyī ca sā punaḥ |
8.193 . śatarudrordhvato bhadrakālyā nīlaprabhaṃ jayam || 193
8.194 . na yajñadānatapasā prāpyaṃ kālyāḥ puraṃ jayam |
8.194 . tadbhaktāstatra gacchanti tanmaṇḍalasudīkṣitāḥ || 194
8.195 . nirbījadīkṣayā mokṣaṃ dadāti parameśvarī |
8.195 . vidyeśāvaraṇe dīkṣāṃ yāvatīṃ kurute nṛṇām || 195
8.196 . tāvatīṃ gatimāyānti bhuvane'tra niveśitāḥ |
8.196 . tataḥ koṭyā vīrabhadro yugāntāgnisamaprabhaḥ || 196
8.197 . vijayākhyaṃ puraṃ cāsya ye smaranto maheśvaram |
8.197 . jaleṣu maruṣu cāgnau śiraśchedena vā mṛtāḥ || 197
8.198 . te yānti bodhamaiśānaṃ vīrabhadraṃ mahādyutim |
8.198 . vairabhadrordhvataḥ koṭirviṣkambhādvistṛtaṃ tridhā || 198
8.199 . rudrāṇḍaṃ sālilaṃ tvaṇḍaṃ śakracāpākṛti sthitam |
8.199 . ā vīrabhadrabhuvanādbhadrakālyālayāttathā || 199
8.200 . trayodaśabhiranyaiśca bhuvanairupaśobhitam |
8.200 . tato bhuvaḥ sahādreḥ pūrgandhatanmātradhāraṇāt || 200
8.201 . mṛtā gacchanti tāṃ bhūmiṃ dharitryāḥ paramāṃ budhāḥ |
8.201 . abdheḥ puraṃ tatastvāpyaṃ rasatanmātradhāraṇāt || 201
8.202 . tataḥ śriyaḥ puraṃ rudrakrīḍāvataraṇeṣvatha |
8.202 . prayāgādau śrīgirau ca viśeṣānmaraṇena tat || 202
8.203 . sārasvataṃ puraṃ tasmācchabdabrahmavidāṃ padam |
8.203 . rudrocitāstā mukhyatvādrudrebhyo'nyāstathā sthitāḥ || 203
8.204 . pureṣu bahudhā gaṅgā devādau śrīḥ sarasvatī |
8.204 . lakulādyamareśāntā aṣṭāvapsu surādhipāḥ || 204
8.205 . tatastu taijasaṃ tattvaṃ śivāgneratra saṃsthitiḥ |
8.205 . te cainaṃ vahnimāyānti vāhnīṃ ye dhāraṇāṃ śritāḥ || 205
8.206 . bhairavādiharīndvantaṃ taijase nāyakāṣṭakam |
8.206 . prāṇasya bhuvanaṃ vāyordaśadhā daśadhā tu tat || 206
8.207 . dhyātvā tyaktvātha vā prāṇān kṛtvā tatraiva dhāraṇām |
8.207 . taṃ viśanti mahātmāno vāyubhūtāḥ khamūrtayaḥ || 207
8.208 . bhīmādigayaparyantamaṣṭakaṃ vāyutattvagam |
8.208 . khatattve bhuvanaṃ vyomnaḥ prāpyaṃ tadvyomadhāraṇāt || 208
8.209 . vastrāpadāntaṃ sthāṇvādi vyomatattve surāṣṭakam |
8.209 . adīkṣitā ye bhūteṣu śivatattvābhimāninaḥ || 209
8.210 . jñānahīnā api prauḍhadhāraṇāste'ṇḍato bahiḥ |
8.210 . dharābdhitejo'nilakhapuragā dīkṣitāśca vā || 210
8.211 . tāvatsaṃskārayogārthaṃ na paraṃ padamīhitum |
8.211 . tathāvidhāvatāreṣu mṛtāścāyataneṣu ye || 211
8.212 . tatpadaṃ te samāsādya kramādyānti śivātmatām |
8.212 . punaḥ punaridaṃ coktaṃ śrīmaddevyākhyayāmale || 212
8.213 . śrīkāmikāyāṃ kaśmīravarṇane coktavānvibhuḥ |
8.213 . sureśvarīmahādhāmni ye mriyante ca tatpure || 213
8.214 . brāhmaṇādyāḥ saṅkarāntāḥ paśavaḥ sthāvarāntagāḥ |
8.214 . rudrajātaya evaite ityāha bhagavāñchivaḥ || 214
8.215 . ākāśāvaraṇādūrdhvamahaṅkārādadhaḥ priye |
8.215 . tanmātrādimano'ntānāṃ purāṇi śivaśāsane || 215
8.216 . pañcavarṇayutaṃ gandhatanmātramaṇḍalaṃ mahat |
8.216 . ācchādya yojanānekakoṭibhiḥ sthitamantarā || 216
8.217 . evaṃ rasādimātrāṇāṃ maṇḍalāni svavarṇata |
8.217 . śarvo bhavaḥ paśupatirīśo bhīma iti kramāt || 217
8.218 . tanmātreśā yadicchātaḥ śabdādyāḥ khādikāriṇaḥ |
8.218 . tataḥ sūryenduvedānāṃ maṇḍalāni vibhurmahān || 218
8.219 . ugraścetyeṣu patayastebhyo'rkendū sayājakau |
8.219 . ityaṣṭau tanavaḥ śaṃbhoryāḥ parāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 219
8.220 . aparā brahmaṇo'ṇḍe tā vyāpya sarvaṃ vyavasthitāḥ |
8.220 . kalpe kalpe prasūyante dharādyāstābhya eva tu || 220
8.221 . tato vāgādikarmākṣayuktaṃ karaṇamaṇḍalam |
8.221 . agnīndraviṣṇumitrāḥ sabrahmāṇasteṣu nāyakāḥ || 221
8.222 . prakāśamaṇḍalaṃ tasmācchrutaṃ buddhyakṣapañcakam |
8.222 . digvidyudarkavaruṇabhuvaḥ śrotrādidevatāḥ || 222
8.223 . prakāśamaṇḍalādūrdhvaṃ sthitaṃ pañcārthamaṇḍalam |
8.223 . manomaṇḍalametasmātsomenādhiṣṭhitaṃ yataḥ || 223
8.224 . bāhyadeveṣvadhiṣṭhātā sāmyaiśvaryasukhātmakaḥ |
8.224 . manodevastato divyaḥ somo vibhurudīritaḥ || 224
8.225 . tato'pi sakalākṣāṇāṃ yonerbuddhyakṣajanmanaḥ |
8.225 . sthūlādicchagalāntāṣṭayuktaṃ cāhaṅkṛteḥ puram || 225
8.226 . buddhitattvaṃ tato devayonyaṣṭakapurādhipam |
8.226 . paiśācaprabhṛtibrāhmaparyantaṃ tacca kīrtitam || 226
8.227 . etāni devayonīnāṃ sthānānyeva purāṇyataḥ |
8.227 . avatīryātmajanmānaṃ dhyāyantaḥ saṃbhavanti te || 227
8.228 . parameśaniyogācca codyamānāśca māyayā |
8.228 . niyāmitā niyatyā ca brahmaṇo'vyaktajanmanaḥ || 228
8.229 . vyajyante tena sargādau nāmarūpairanekadhā |
8.229 . svāṃśanaiva mahātmāno na tyajanti svaketanam || 229
8.230 . uktaṃ ca śivatanāvidamadhikārapadasthitena guruṇā naḥ |
8.230 . aṣṭānāṃ devānāṃ śaktyāvirbhāvayonayo hyetāḥ || 230
8.231 . tanubhogāḥ punareṣāmadhaḥ prabhūtātmakāḥ proktāḥ |
8.231 . catvāriṃśattulyopabhogadeśādhikāni bhuvanāni || 231
8.232 . sādhanabhedātkevalamaṣṭakapañcakatayoktāni |
8.232 . etāni bhaktiyogaprāṇatyāgādigamyāni || 232
8.233 . teṣūmāpatireva prabhuḥ svatantrendriyo vikaraṇātmā |
8.233 . taratamayogena tato'pi devayonyaṣṭakaṃ lakṣyaṃ tu || 233
8.234 . lokānāmakṣāṇi ca viṣayaparicchittikaraṇāni |
8.234 . gandhādermahadantādekādhikyena jātamaiśvaryam || 234
8.235 . aṇimādyātmakamasminpaiśācādye viriñcānte |
8.235 . jñātvaivaṃ śodhayedbuddhiṃ sārdhaṃ puryaṣṭakendriyaiḥ || 235
8.236 . krodheśāṣṭakamānīlaṃ saṃvartādyaṃ tato viduḥ |
8.236 . tejoṣṭakaṃ balādhyakṣaprabhṛtikrodhanāṣtakāt || 236
8.237 . akṛtādi tato buddhau yogāṣṭakamudāhṛtam |

8.240 . pratikalpaṃ nāmabhedairbhaṇyate sā maheśvarī |

8.241 . śrīkaṇṭha eva parayā mūrtyomāpatirucyate |

8.243 . aṃśena mānuṣe loke dhātrā tā hyavatāritāḥ |
8.243 . svacchandāstāḥ parāścānyāḥ pare vyomni vyavasthitāḥ || 243
8.244 . svacchandaṃ tā niṣevante saptadheyamumā yataḥ |
8.244 . umāpatipurasyordhva sthitaṃ mūrtyaṣṭakaṃ param || 244
8.245 . śarvādikaṃ yasya sṛṣṭirdharādyā yājakāntataḥ |

8.260 . kṣobhāntaraṃ tataḥ kārya bījocchūnāṅkurādivat |

8.264 . yonīratītya gauṇe skandhe syuryogadātāraḥ |

8.266 . dharmajñānavirāgānaiśvaryaṃ tatphalāni vividhāni |
8.266 . yacchanti guṇebhyo'mī puruṣebhyo yogadātāraḥ || 266
8.267 . tebhyaḥ parato bhuvanaṃ sattvādiguṇāsanasya devasya |

8.270 . yasyecchātaḥ sattvādiguṇaśarīrā visṛjati rudrāṇī |

8.271 . paśupatirindropendraviriñcairatha tadupalambhato devaiḥ |

8.273 . pañcāntakaikavīrau ca śikhodaścāṣṭa tatra te |

8.275 . te sarve'tra vinihitā rudrāśca tadutthabhogabhujaḥ |

8.279 . atattve tattvabuddhyā yaḥ santoṣastuṣṭiratra sā |

8.288 . bhuvanaṃ dehadharmāṇāṃ daśānāṃ vigrahāṣṭakāt |

8.290 . ārabhya dehapāśākhyaṃ puraṃ buddhiguṇāstataḥ |

8.291 . pāśā āgantukagāṇeśavaidyeśvarabheditāḥ |

8.292 . yatkiṃcitparamādvaitasaṃvitsvātantryasundarāt |

8.294 . uktānuktāstu ye pāśāḥ paratantroktalakṣaṇāḥ |

8.295 . puṃsa ūrdhva tu niyatistatrasthāḥ śaṃkarā daśa |

8.297 . puraṃ cāśuddhavidyāyāṃ syācchaktinavakojjvalam |

8.324 . tena kalādidharāntaṃ yaduktamāvaraṇajālamakhilaṃ tat |

8.350 . tāvadasaṃkhyātānāṃ jantūnāṃ nirvṛtiṃ kurute |

8.351 . ālokayanti devaṃ hṛdayasthaṃ kāraṇaṃ paramam |

8.352 . saptānudhyāyantyapi mantrāṇāṃ koṭayaḥ śuddhāḥ |
8.352 . māyādiravīcyanto bhavastvanantādirucyate'pyabhavaḥ || 352
8.353 . śivaśuddhaguṇādhīkārāntaḥ so'pyeṣa heyaśca |
8.353 . atrāpi yato dṛṣṭānugrāhyāṇāṃ niyojyatā śaivī || 353
8.354 . iṣṭā ca tannivṛttirhyabhavastvadhare na bhūyate yasmāt |
8.354 . patyurapasarpati yataḥ kāraṇatā kāryatā ca siddhebhyaḥ || 354
8.355 . kañcukavacchivasiddhau tāvatibhavasaṃjñayātimadhyasthau |
8.355 . dharmajñānavirāgaiśyacatuṣṭayapuraṃ tu yat || 355
8.356 . rūpāvaraṇasaṃjñaṃ tattattve'sminnaiśvare viduḥ |
8.356 . vāmā jyeṣṭhā ca raudrīti bhuvanatrayaśobhitam || 356
8.357 . sūkṣmāvaraṇamākhyātamīśatattve gurūttamaiḥ |
8.357 . aiśātsādāśivaṃ jñānakriyāyugalamaṇḍitam || 357
8.358 . śuddhāvaraṇamityāhuruktā śuddhāvṛteḥ param |
8.358 . vidyāvṛtistato bhāvābhāvaśaktidvayojjvalā || 358
8.359 . śaktyāvṛtiḥ pramāṇākhyā tataḥ śāstre nirūpitā |
8.359 . śaktyāvṛtestu tejasvidhruveśābhyāmalaṅkṛtam || 359
8.360 . tejasvyāvaraṇaṃ vedapurā mānāvṛtistataḥ |
8.360 . mānāvṛteḥ suśuddhāvṛtpuratritayaśobhitā || 360
8.361 . suśuddhāvaraṇādūrdhva śaivamekapuraṃ bhavet |
8.361 . śivāvṛterūrdhvamāhurmokṣāvaraṇasaṃjñitam || 361
8.362 . asyāṃ mokṣāvṛtau rudrā ekādaśa nirūpitāḥ |
8.362 . mokṣāvaraṇatastvekapuramāvaraṇaṃ dhruvam || 362
8.363 . ūrdhve dhruvāvṛtericchāvaraṇaṃ tatra te śivāḥ |
8.363 . īśvarecchāgṛhāntasthāstatpuraṃ caikamucyate || 363
8.364 . icchāvṛteḥ prabuddhākhyaṃ digrudrāṣṭakacarcitam |
8.364 . prabuddhāvaraṇādūrdhva samayāvaraṇaṃ mahat || 364
8.365 . bhuvanaiḥ pañcabhirgarbhīkṛtānantasamāvṛti |
8.365 . sāmayātsauśivaṃ tatra sādākhyaṃ bhuvanaṃ mahat || 365
8.366 . tasminsadāśivo devastasya savyāpasavyayoḥ |
8.366 . jñānakriye parecchā tu śaktirutsaṅgagāminī || 366
8.367 . sṛṣṭyādipañcakṛtyāni kurute sa tayecchayā |
8.367 . pañca brahmāṇyaṅgaṣaṭkaṃ sakalādyaṣṭakaṃ śivāḥ || 367
8.368 . daśāṣṭādaśa rudrāśca taireva suśivo vṛtaḥ |
8.368 . sadyo vāmāghorau puruṣeśau brahmapañcakaṃ hṛdayam || 368
8.369 . mūrdhaśikhāvarmadṛgastramaṅgāni ṣaṭprāhuḥ |
8.369 . sakalākalaśūnyaiḥ saha kalāḍhyakhamalaṅkṛte kṣapaṇamantyam || 369
8.370 . kaṇṭhyauṣṭhyamaṣṭamaṃ kila sakalāṣṭakametadāmnātam |
8.370 . oṃ kāraśivau dīpto hetvīśadaśeśakau suśivakālau || 370
8.371 . sūkṣmasutejaḥśarvāḥ śivāḥ daśaite'tra pūrvādeḥ |
8.371 . vijayo niḥśvāsaśca svāyambhuvo vahnivīrarauravakāḥ || 371
8.372 . mukuṭavisarenduvinduprodgītā lalitasiddharudrau ca |
8.372 . santānaśivau parakiraṇapārameśā iti smṛtā rudrāḥ || 372
8.373 . sarveṣāmeteṣāṃ jñānāni viduḥ svatulyanāmāni |
8.373 . mantramunikoṭiparivṛta matha vibhuvāmādirudratacchaktiyutam || 373
8.374 . tārādiśaktijuṣṭaṃ suśivāsanamatisitakajamasaṃkhyadalam |
8.374 . yaḥ śaktirudravargaḥ parivāre viṣṭare ca suśivasya || 374
8.375 . pratyekamasya nijanijaparivāre parārdhakoṭayo'saṃkhyāḥ |
8.375 . māyāmalanirmuktāḥ kevalamadhikāramātrasaṃrūḍhāḥ || 375
8.376 . suśivāvaraṇe rudrāḥ sarvajñāḥ sarvaśaktisampūrṇāḥ |
8.376 . adhikārabandhavilaye śāntāḥ śivarūpiṇo punarbhavinaḥ || 376
8.377 . ūrdhve bindvāvṛtirdīptā tatra tatra padmaṃ śaśiprabham |
8.377 . śāntyatītaḥ śivastatra tacchaktyutsaṅgabhūṣitaḥ || 377
8.378 . nivṛttyādikalāvargaparivārasamāvṛtaḥ |
8.378 . asaṃkhyarudratacchaktipurakoṭibhirāvṛtaḥ || 378
8.379 . śrīmanmataṅgaśāstre ca layākhyaṃ tattvamuttamam |
8.379 . pāribhāṣikamityetannāmnā bindurihocyate || 379
8.380 . caturmūrtimayaṃ śubhraṃ yattatsakalaniṣkalam |
8.380 . tasminbhogaḥ samuddiṣṭa ityatredaṃ ca varṇitam || 380
8.381 . nivṛttyādeḥ susūkṣmatvāddharādyārabdhadehatā |
8.381 . mātuḥ sphūrjanmahājñānalīnatvānna vibhāvyate || 381
8.382 . udrikta taijasatvena hemno bhūparamāṇavaḥ |
8.382 . yathā pṛthaṅna bhāntyevamūrdhvādhorudradehagāḥ || 382
8.383 . bindūrdhve'rdhenduretasya kalā jyotsnā ca tadvatī |
8.383 . kāntiḥ prabhā ca vimalā pañcaitā rodhikāstataḥ || 383
8.384 . rundhanī rodhanī roddhrī jñānabodhā tamopahā |
8.384 . etāḥ pañca kalāḥ prāhurnirodhinyāṃ gurūttamāh || 384
8.385 . ardhamātraḥ smṛto bindurvyomarūpī catuṣkalaḥ |
8.385 . tadardhamardhacandrastadaṣṭāṃśena nirodhikā || 385
8.386 . hetūnbrahmādikān runddhe rodhikāṃ tāṃ tyajettataḥ |
8.386 . nirodhikāmimāṃ bhittvā sādākhyaṃ bhuvanaṃ param || 386
8.387 . pararūpeṇa yatrāste pañcamantramahātanuḥ |
8.387 . ityardhendunirodhyantabindvāvṛtyūrdhvato mahān || 387
8.388 . nādaḥ kiñjalkasadṛśo mahadbhiḥ puruṣairvṛtaḥ |
8.388 . catvāri bhuvanānyatra dikṣu madhye ca pañcamam || 388
8.389 . indhikā dīpikā caiva rodhikā mocikordhvagā |
8.389 . madhye'tra padmaṃ tatrordhvagāmī tacchaktibhirvṛtaḥ || 389
8.390 . nādordhvatastu sauṣumnaṃ tatra tacchaktibhṛtprabhuḥ |
8.390 . tadīśaḥ piṅgalelābhyāṃ vṛtaḥ savyāpasavyayoḥ || 390
8.391 . yā prabhoraṅkagā devī suṣumnā śaśisaprabhā |
8.391 . grathito'dhvā tayā sarva ūrdhvaścādhastanastathā || 391
8.392 . nādaḥsuṣumnādhārastu bhittvā viśvamidaṃ jagat |
8.392 . adhaḥśaktyā vinirgacchedūrdhvaśaktyā ca mūrdhataḥ || 392
8.393 . nāḍyā brahmabile līnaḥ so'vyaktadhvanirakṣaraḥ |
8.393 . nadansarveṣu bhūteṣu śivaśaktyā hyadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 393
8.394 . suṣumnordhve brahmabilasaṃjñayāvaraṇaṃ tridṛk |
8.394 . tatra brahmā sitaḥ śūlī pañcāsyaḥ śaśiśekharaḥ || 394
8.395 . tasyotsaṅge parā devī brahmāṇī mokṣamārgagā |
8.395 . roddhrī dātrī ca mokṣasya tāṃ bhittvā cordhvakuṇḍalī || 395
8.396 . śaktiḥ suptāhisadṛśī sā viśvādhāra ucyate |
8.396 . tasyāṃ sūkṣmā susūkṣmā ca tathānye amṛtāmite || 396
8.397 . madhyato vyāpinī tasyāṃ vyāpīśo vyāpinīdharaḥ |
8.397 . śaktitattvamidaṃ yasya prapañco'yaṃ dharāntakaḥ || 397
8.398 . śivatattvaṃ tatastatra caturdikkaṃ vyavasthitāḥ |
8.398 . vyāpī vyomātmako'nanto'nāthastacchaktibhāginaḥ || 398
8.399 . madhye tvanāśritaṃ tatra devadevo hyanāśritaḥ |
8.399 . tacchaktyutsaṅgabhṛtsūryaśatakoṭisamaprabhaḥ || 399
8.400 . śivatattvordhvataḥ śaktiḥ parā sā samanāhvayā |
8.400 . sarveṣāṃ kāraṇānāṃ sā kartṛbhūtā vyavasthitā || 400
8.401 . bibhartyaṇḍānyanekāni śivena samadhiṣṭhitā |
8.401 . tadārūḍhaḥ śivaḥ kṛtyapañcakaṃ kurute prabhuḥ || 401
8.402 . samanā karaṇaṃ tasya hetukarturmahośituḥ |
8.402 . anāśritaṃ tu vyāpāre nimittaṃ heturucyate || 402
8.403 . tayādhitiṣṭhati vibhuḥ kāraṇānāṃ tu pañcakam |
8.403 . anāśrito'nāthamayamanantaṃ khavapuḥ sadā || 403
8.404 . sa vyāpinaṃ prerayati svaśaktyā karaṇena tu |
8.404 . karmarūpā sthitā māyā yadadhaḥ śaktikuṇḍalī || 404
8.405 . nādabindvādikaṃ kāryamityādijagadudbhavaḥ |
8.405 . yatsadāśivaparyantaṃ pārthivādyaṃ ca śāsane || 405
8.406 . tatsarva prākṛtaṃ proktaṃ vināśotpattisaṃyutam |
8.406 . atha sakalabhuvanamānaṃ yanmahyaṃ nigaditaṃ nijairgurubhiḥ || 406
8.407 . tadvakṣyate samāsādbuddhau yenāśu saṅkrāmet |
8.407 . aṇḍasyāntaranantaḥ kālaḥ kūṣmāṇḍahāṭakau brahmaharī || 407
8.408 . rudrāḥ śataṃ savīraṃ bahirnivṛttistu sāṣṭaśatabhuvanā syāt |
8.408 . jalatejaḥsamīranabho'haṃkṛddhīmūlasaptake pratyekam || 408
8.409 . aṣṭau ṣaṭpañcāśadbhuvanā tena pratiṣṭheti kalā kathitā |
8.409 . atra prāhuḥ śodhyānaṣṭau kecinnijāṣṭakādhipatīn || 409
8.410 . anye tu samastānāṃ śodhyatvaṃ varṇayanti bhuvanānām |
8.410 . śrībhūtirājamiśrā guravaḥ prāhuḥ punarbahī rudraśatam || 410
8.411 . aṣṭāvantaḥ sākaṃ śarveṇetīdṛśī nivṛttiriyaṃ syāt |

8.412 . ityaṣṭakaṃ jale'nau vahnyatiguhyadvayaṃ maruti vāyoḥ |
8.412 . svapuraṃ gayādi khe ca vyoma pavitrāṣṭakaṃ ca bhuvanayugam || 412
8.413 . abhimāne'haṅkāracchagalādyaṣṭakamathāntarā nabho'haṃkṛt |
8.413 . tanmātrārkenduśratipurāṣṭakaṃ buddhikarmadevānām || 413
8.414 . daśa tanmātrasamūhe bhuvanaṃ punarakṣavargavinipatite |
8.414 . manasaścetyabhimāne dvāviṃśatireva bhuvanānām || 414
8.415 . dhiyi daivīnāmaṣṭau kruttejoyogasaṃjñakaṃ trayaṃ tadumā |
8.415 . tatpatiratha mūrtyaṣṭakasuśivadvādaśakavīrabhadrāḥ syuḥ || 415
8.416 . tadatha mahādevāṣṭakamiti buddhau saptadaśa saṃkhyā |
8.416 . guṇatattve paṅktitrayamiti ṣaṭpañcāśataṃ purāṇi viduḥ || 416
8.417 . yadyapi guṇasāmyātmani mūle krodheśvarāṣṭakaṃ tathāpi dhiyi |

8.419 . guravo guruśiṣyā ṛṣivarga iḍādiśca vigrahāṣṭakayuk |

8.420 . kāmādisaptaviṃśakamāgantu tathā gaṇeśavidyeśamayau |

8.421 . niyatau śaṅkaradaśakaṃ kāle śivadaśakamiti puradvitayam |

8.422 . bhuvanaṃ bhuvanaṃ niśi puṭapuratrayaṃ vākpuraṃ pramāṇapuram |

8.423 . vāmeśarūpasūkṣmaṃ śuddhaṃ vidyātha śaktitejasvimitiḥ |

8.424 . bindvardhendunirodhyaḥ parasauśivamindhikādipurasauṣumne || 424
8.425 . paranādo brahmabilaṃ sūkṣmādiyutordhvakuṇḍalī śaktiḥ |
8.425 . vyāpivyomānantānāthānāśritapurāṇi pañca tataḥ || 425
8.426 . ṣaṣṭhaṃ ca paramamanāśritamatha samanābhuvanaṣoḍaśī yadi vā |
8.426 . bindvāvaraṇaṃ parasauśivaṃ ca pañcendhikādibhuvanāni || 426
8.427 . sauṣumnaṃ brahmabilaṃ kuṇḍalinī vyāpipañcakaṃ samanā |
8.427 . iti ṣoḍaśabhuvaneyaṃ tattvayugaṃ śāntyatītā syāt || 427
8.428 . śrīmanmataṅgaśāstre ca kramo'yaṃ purapūgagaḥ |
8.428 . kālāgnirnarakāḥ khābdhiyutaṃ mukhyatayā śatam || 428
8.429 . kūṣmāṇḍaḥ saptapātālī saptalokī maheśvaraḥ |
8.429 . ityaṇḍamadhyaṃ tadbāhye śataṃ rudrā iti sthitāḥ || 429
8.430 . sthānānāṃ dviśatī bhūmiḥ saptapañcāśatā yutā |
8.430 . pañcāṣṭakasya madhyāddvātriṃśadbhūtacatuṣṭaye || 430
8.431 . tanmātreṣu ca pañca syurviśvedevāstato'ṣṭakam |
8.431 . pañcamaṃ sendriye garve buddhau devāṣṭakaṃ guṇe || 431
8.432 . yogāṣṭakaṃ krodhasaṃjñaṃ mūle kāle sanaiyate |
8.432 . patadrugādyāścāṅguṣṭhamātrādyā rāgatattvagāḥ || 432
8.433 . dvādaśaikaśivādyāḥ syurvidyāyāṃ kalane daśa |
