8.434 . śaivāḥ kecidihānantāḥ śraikaṇṭhā iti saṃgrahaḥ |
8.434 . yatra yadā parabhogān bubhukṣate tatra yojanaṃ kāryam || 434
8.435 . śodhanamatha taddhānau śeṣaṃ tvantargataṃ kāryam |
8.435 . ityāgamaṃ prathayituṃ darśitametadvikalpitaṃ tena || 435
8.436 . anye'pi bahuvikalpāḥ svadhiyācāryaiḥ samabhyūhyāḥ |
8.436 . śrīpūrvaśāsane punaraṣṭādaśādhikaṃ śataṃ kathitam || 436
8.437 . tadiha pradhānamadhikaṃ saṃkṣepeṇocyate śodhyam |
8.437 . kālāgniḥ kūṣmāṇḍo narakeśo hāṭako'tha bhūtalapaḥ || 437
8.438 . brahmā munilokeśo rudrāḥ pañcāntarālasthāḥ |
8.438 . adhare'nantaḥ prācyāḥ kapālivahnyantanirṛtibalākhyāḥ || 438
8.439 . laghunidhipatividyādhipaśambhūrdhvāntaṃ savīrabhadrapati |
8.439 . ekādaśabhirbāhye brahmāṇḍaṃ pañcabhistathāntarikaiḥ || 439
8.440 . iti ṣoḍaśapurametannivṛttikalayeha kalanīyam |
8.440 . lakulīśabhārabhūtī diṇḍyāṣāḍhī ca puṣkaranimeṣau || 440
8.441 . prabhāsasureśāviti salile pratyātmakaṃ saparivāre |
8.441 . bhairavakedāramahākālā madhyāmrajalpākhyāḥ || 441
8.442 . śrīśailahariścandrāviti guhyāṣṭakamidaṃ mahasi |
8.442 . bhīmendrāṭṭahāsavimalakanakhalanākhalakurusthitigayākhyāḥ || 442
8.443 . atiguhyāṣṭakametanmaruti ca satanmātrake ca sākṣe ca |
8.443 . sthāṇusuvarṇākhyau kila bhadro gokarṇako mahālayakaḥ || 443
8.444 . avimuktarudrakoṭī vastrāpada ityadaḥ pavitraṃ khe |
8.444 . sthūlasthūleśaśaṅkuśrutikālañjarāśca maṇḍalabhṛt || 444
8.445 . mākoṭāṇḍadvitayacchagalāṇḍā aṣṭakaṃ hyahaṅkāre |
8.445 . anye'haṅkārāntastanmātrāṇīndriyāṇi cāpyāhuḥ || 445
8.446 . dhiyi yonyaṣṭakamuktaṃ prakṛtau yogāṣṭakaṃ kilākṛtaprabhṛti |
8.446 . iti saptāṣṭakabhuvanā pratiṣṭhitiḥ salilato hi mūlāntā || 446
8.447 . nari vāmo bhīmograu bhaveśavīrāḥ pracaṇḍagaurīśau |
8.447 . ajasānantaikaśivau vidyāyāṃ krodhacaṇḍayugmaṃ syāt || 447
8.448 . saṃvarto jyotiratho kalāniyatyāṃ ca sūrapañcāntau |
8.448 . vīraśikhīśaśrīkaṇṭhasaṃjñametattrayaṃ ca kāle syāt || 448
8.449 . samahātejā vāmo bhavodbhavaścaikapiṅgaleśānau |
8.449 . bhuvaneśapuraḥsarakāvaṅguṣṭha ime niśi sthitā hyaṣṭau || 449
8.450 . aṣṭāviṃśatibhuvanā vidyā puruṣānniśāntamiyam |
8.450 . hālāhalarudrakrudambikāghorikāḥ savāmāḥ syuḥ || 450
8.451 . vidyāyāṃ vidyeśāstvaṣṭāvīśe sadāśive pañca |
8.451 . vāmā jyeṣṭhā raudrī śaktiḥ sakalā ca śontayam || 451
8.452 . aṣṭādaśa bhuvanā syātśāntyatītā tvabhuvanaiva |
8.452 . iti deśādhvavibhāgaḥ kathitaḥ śrīśambhunā samādiṣṭaḥ || 452



c9 śrītantrālokasya navamamāhnikam

9.1 . atha tattvapravibhāgo vistarataḥ kathyate kramaprāptaḥ || 1
9.2 . yānyuktāni purāṇyamūni vividhaibhadairyadeṣvanvitaṃ rūpaṃ bhāti paraṃ prakāśaniviḍaṃ devaḥ sa ekaḥ śivaḥ |
9.2 . tatsvātantryarasātpunaḥ śivapadādbhede vibhāte paraṃ yadrūpaṃ bahudhānugāmi tadidaṃ tattvaṃ vibhoḥ śāsane || 2
9.3 . tathāhi kālasadanādvīrabhadrapurāntagam |
9.3 . dhṛtikāṭhinyagarimādyavabhāsāddharātmatā || 3
9.4 . evaṃ jalāditattveṣu vācyaṃ yāvatsadāśive |
9.4 . svasminkārye'tha dharmaughe yadvāpi svasadṛgguṇe || 4
9.5 . āste sāmānyakalpena tananādvyāptṛbhāvataḥ |
9.5 . tattattvaṃ kramaśaḥ pṛthvīpradhānaṃ puṃśivādayaḥ || 5
9.6 . dehānāṃ bhuvanānāṃ ca na prasaṅgastato bhavet |
9.6 . śrīmanmataṅgaśāstrādau taduktaṃ parameśinā || 6
9.7 . tatraiṣāṃ darśyate dṛṣṭaḥ siddhayogīśvarīmate |
9.7 . kāryakāraṇabhāvo yaḥ śivecchāparikalpitaḥ || 7
9.8 . vastutaḥ sarvabhāvānāṃ karteśānaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ |
9.8 . asvatantrasya kartṛtvaṃ nahi jātūpapadyate || 8
9.9 . svatantratā ca cinmātravapuṣaḥ parameśituḥ |
9.9 . svatantraṃ ca jaḍaṃ ceti tadanyonyaṃ virudhyate || 9
9.10 . jāḍyaṃ pramātṛtantratvaṃ svātmasiddhimapi prati |
9.10 . na kartṛtvādṛte cānyatkāraṇatvaṃ hi labhyate || 10
9.11 . tasminsati hi tadbhāva ityapekṣaikajīvitam |
9.11 . nirapekṣeṣu bhāveṣu svātmaniṣṭhatayā katham || 11
9.12 . sa pūrvamatha paścātsa iti cetpūrvapaścimau |
9.12 . svabhāve'natiriktau cetsama ityavaśiṣyate || 12
9.13 . bījamaṅkura ityasmin satattve hetutadvatoḥ |
9.13 . ghaṭaḥ paṭaśceti bhavetkāryakāraṇatā na kim || 13
9.14 . bījamaṅkurapatrāditayā pariṇameta cet |
9.14 . atatsvabhāvavapuṣaḥ sa svabhāvo na yujyate || 14
9.15 . sa tatsvabhāva iti cettarhi bījāṅkurā nije |
9.15 . tāvatyeva na viśrāntau tadanyātyantasaṃbhavāt || 15
9.16 . tataśca citrākāro'sau tāvānkaścitprasajyate |
9.16 . astu cetna jaḍe'nyonyaviruddhākārasaṃbhavaḥ || 16
9.17 . krameṇa citrākāro'stu jaḍaḥ kiṃ nu viruddhyate |
9.17 . kramo'kramo vā bhāvasya na svarūpādhiko bhavet || 17
9.18 . tathopalambhamātraṃ tau upalambhaśca kiṃ tathā |
9.18 . upalabdhāpi vijñānasvabhāvo yo'sya so'pi hi || 18
9.19 . kramopalambharūpatvātkrameṇopalabheta cet |
9.19 . tasya tarhi kramaḥ ko'sau tadanyānupalambhataḥ || 19
9.20 . svabhāva iti cennāsau svarūpādadhiko bhavet |
9.20 . svarūpānadhikasyāpi kramasya svasvabhāvataḥ || 20
9.21 . svātantryādbhāsanaṃ syāccetkimanyadbrūmahe vayam |
9.21 . itthaṃ śrīśiva evaikaḥ karteti paribhāṣyate || 21
9.22 . kartṛtvaṃ caitadetasya tathāmātrāvabhāsanam |
9.22 . tathāvabhāsanaṃ cāsti kāryakāraṇabhāvagam || 22
9.23 . yathā hi ghaṭasāhityaṃ paṭasyāpyavabhāsate |
9.23 . tathā ghaṭānantaratā kiṃ tu sā niyamojjhitā || 23
9.24 . ato yanniyamenaiva yasmādābhātyanantaram |
9.24 . tattasya kāraṇaṃ brūmaḥ sati rūpānvaye'dhike || 24
9.25 . niyamaśca tathārūpabhāsanāmātrasārakaḥ |
9.25 . bījādaṅkura ityevaṃ bhāsanaṃ nahi sarvadā || 25
9.26 . yogīcchānantarodbhūtatathābhūtāṅkuro yataḥ |
9.26 . iṣṭe tathāvidhākāre niyamo bhāsate yataḥ || 26
9.27 . svapne ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ hetutadvatsvabhāvatā |
9.27 . bhāsate niyamenaiva bādhāśūnyena tāvati || 27
9.28 . tato yāvati yādrūpyānniyamo bādhavarjitaḥ |
9.28 . bhāti tāvati tādrūpyāddṛḍhahetuphalātmatā || 28
9.29 . tathābhūte ca niyame hetutadvattvakāriṇi |
9.29 . vastutaścinmayasyaiva hetutā taddhi sarvagam || 29
9.30 . ata eva ghaṭodbhūtau sāmagrī heturucyate |
9.30 . sāmagrī ca samagrāṇāṃ yadyekaṃ neṣyate vapuḥ || 30
9.31 . hetubhedānna bhedaḥ syātphale taccāsamañjasam |
9.31 . yadyasyānuvidhatte tāmanvayavyatirekitām || 31
9.32 . tattasya hetu cetso'yaṃ kuṇṭhatarko na naḥ priyaḥ |
9.32 . samagrāśca yathā daṇḍasūtracakrakarādayaḥ || 32
9.33 . dūrāśca bhāvinaścetthaṃ hetutveneti manmahe |
9.33 . yadi tatra bhavenmerurbhaviṣyanvāpi kaścana || 33
9.34 . na jāyeta ghaṭo nūnaṃ tatpratyūhavyapohitaḥ |
9.34 . yathā ca cakraṃ niyate deśe kāle ca hetutām || 34
9.35 . yāti karkisumervādyāstadvatsvasthāvadhi sthitāḥ |
9.35 . tathā ca teṣāṃ hetunāṃ saṃyojanaviyojane || 35
9.36 . niyate śiva evaikaḥ svatantraḥ kartṛtāmiyāt |
9.36 . kumbhakārasya yā saṃvitcakradaṇḍādiyojane || 36
9.37 . śiva eva hi sā yasmātsaṃvidaḥ kā viśiṣṭatā |
9.37 . kaumbhakārī tu saṃvittiravacchedāvabhāsanāt || 37
9.38 . bhinnakalpā yadi kṣepyā daṇḍacakrādimadhyataḥ |
9.38 . tasmādekaikanirmāṇe śivo viśvaikavigrahaḥ || 38
9.39 . karteti puṃsaḥ kartṛtvābhimāno'pi vibhoḥ kṛtiḥ |
9.39 . ata eva tathābhānaparamārthatayā sthiteḥ || 39
9.40 . kāryakāraṇabhāvasya loke śāstre ca citratā |
9.40 . māyāto'vyaktakalayoriti rauravasaṃgrahe || 40
9.41 . śrīpūrve tu kalātattvādavyaktamiti kathyate |
9.41 . tata eva niśākhyānātkalībhūtādaliṅgakam || 41
9.42 . iti vyākhyāsmadukte'sminsati nyāye'tiniṣphalā |
9.42 . loke ca gomayātkīṭātsaṃkalpātsvapnataḥ smṛteḥ || 42
9.43 . yogīcchāto dravyamantraprabhāvādeśca vṛścikaḥ |
9.43 . anya eva sa cetkāmaṃ kutaścitsvaviśeṣataḥ || 43
9.44 . sa tu sarvatra tulyastatparāmarśaikyamasti tu |
9.44 . tata eva svarūpe'pi krame'pyanyādṛśī sthitiḥ || 44
9.45 . śāstreṣu yujyate citrāttathābhāvasvabhāvataḥ |
9.45 . puṃrāgavitkalākālamāyā jñānottare kramāt || 45
9.46 . niyatirnāsti vairiñce kalordhve niyatiḥ śratā |
9.46 . puṃrāgavittrayādūrdhvaṃ kalāniyatisaṃpuṭam || 46
9.47 . kālo māyeti kathitaḥ kramaḥ kiraṇaśāstragaḥ |
9.47 . pumānniyatyā kālaśca rāgavidyākalānvitaḥ || 47
9.48 . ityeṣa krama uddiṣṭo mātaṅge pārameśvare |
9.48 . kāryakāraṇabhāvīye tattve itthaṃ vyavasthite || 48
9.49 . śrīpūrvaśāstre kathitāṃ vacmaḥ kāraṇakalpanām |
9.49 . śivaḥ svatantradṛgrūpaḥ pañcaśaktisunirbharaḥ || 49
9.50 . svātantryabhāsitabhidā pañcadhā pravibhajyate |
9.50 . cidānandeṣaṇājñānakriyāṇāṃ susphuṭatvataḥ || 50
9.51 . śivaśaktisadeśānavidyākhyaṃ tattvapañcakam |
9.51 . ekaikatrāpi tattve'smin sarvaśaktisunirbhare || 51
9.52 . tattatprādhānyayogena sa sa bhedo nirūpyate |
9.52 . tathāhi svasvatantratvaparipūrṇatayā vibhuḥ || 52
9.53 . niḥsaṃkhyairbahubhī rūpairbhātyavacchedavarjanāt |
9.53 . śāṃbhavāḥ śaktijā mantramaheśā mantranāyakāh || 53
9.54 . mantrā iti viśuddhāḥ syuramī pañca gaṇāḥ kramāt |
9.54 . svasminsvasmin gaṇe bhāti yadyadrūpaṃ samanvayi || 54
9.55 . tadeṣu tattvamityuktaṃ kālāgnyāderdharādivat |
9.55 . tena yatprāhurākhyānasādṛśyena viḍambitāḥ || 55
9.56 . gurūpāsāṃ vinaivāttapustakābhīṣṭadṛṣṭayaḥ |
9.56 . brahmā nivṛttyadhipatiḥ pṛthaktattvaṃ na gaṇyate || 56
9.57 . sadāśivādyāstu pṛthaggaṇyanta iti ko nayaḥ |
9.57 . brahmaviṣṇuhareśānasuśivānāśritātmani || 57
9.58 . ṣaṭke kāraṇasaṃjñe'rdhajaratīyamiyaṃ kutaḥ |
9.58 . iti tanmūlato dhvastaṃ gaṇitaṃ nahi kāraṇam || 58
9.59 . yathā pṛthivyadhipatirnṛpastattvāntaraṃ nahi |
9.59 . tathā tattatkaleśānaḥ pṛthaktattvāntaraṃ katham || 59
9.60 . tadevaṃ pañcakamidaṃ śuddho'dhvā paribhāṣyate |
9.60 . tatra sākṣācchivecchaiva kartryābhāsitabhedikā || 60
9.61 . īśvarecchāvaśakṣubdhabhogalolikacidgaṇān |
9.61 . saṃvibhaktumaghoreśaḥ sṛjatīha sitetaram || 61
9.62 . aṇūnāṃ lolikā nāma niṣkarmā yābhilāṣitā |
9.62 . apūrṇaṃmanyatājñānaṃ malaṃ sāvacchidojjhitā || 62
9.63 . yogyatāmātramevaitadbhāvyavacchedasaṃgrahe |
9.63 . malastenāsya na pṛthaktattvabhāvo'sti rāgavat || 63
9.64 . niravacchedakarmāṃśamātrāvacchedatastu sā |
9.64 . rāgaḥ puṃsi dhiyo dharmaḥ karmabhedavicitratā || 64
9.65 . apūrṇamanyatā ceyaṃ tathārūpāvabhāsanam |
9.65 . svatantrasya śivasyecchā ghaṭarūpo yathā ghaṭaḥ || 65
9.66 . svātmapracchādanecchaiva vastubhūtastathā malaḥ |
9.66 . yathaivāvyatiriktasya dharāderbhāvitātmatā || 66
9.67 . tathaivāsyeti śāstreṣu vyatiriktaḥ sthito malaḥ |
9.67 . vyatiriktaḥ svatantrastu na ko'pi śakaṭādivat || 67
9.68 . tatsadvitīyā sāśuddhiḥ śivamuktāṇugā na kim |
9.68 . malasya roddhrī kāpyasti śaktiḥ sa cāpyamuktagā || 68
9.69 . iti nyāyojjhito vādaḥ śraddhāmātraikakalpitaḥ |
9.69 . roddhrī śaktirjaḍasyāsau svayaṃ naiva pravartate || 69
9.70 . svayaṃ pravṛttau viśvaṃ syāttathā ceśanikā pramā |
9.70 . malasya roddhrīṃ tāṃ śaktimīśaścetsaṃyunakti tat || 70
9.71 . kīdṛśaṃ pratyaṇumiti praśne nāstyuttaraṃ vacaḥ |
9.71 . malaścāvaraṇaṃ tacca nāvāryasya viśeṣakam || 71
9.72 . upalambhaṃ vihantyetadghaṭasyeva paṭāvṛtiḥ |
9.72 . malenāvṛtarūpāṇāmaṇūnāṃ yatsatattvakam || 72
9.73 . śiva eva ca tatpaśyettasyaivāsau malo bhavet |
9.73 . vibhorjñānakriyāmātrasārasyāṇugaṇasya ca || 73
9.74 . tadabhāvo malo rūpadhvaṃsāyaiva prakalpate |
9.74 . dharmāddharmiṇi yo bhedaḥ samavāyena caikatā || 74
9.75 . na tadbhavadbhiruditaṃ kaṇabhojanaśiṣyavat |
9.75 . nāmūrtena na mūrtena prāvarītuṃ ca śakyate || 75
9.76 . jñānaṃ cākṣuṣaraśmīnāṃ tathābhāve saratyapi |
9.76 . sa eva ca malo mūrtaḥ kiṃ jñānena na vedyate || 76
9.77 . sarvageṇa tataḥ sarvaḥ sarvajñatvaṃ na kiṃ bhajet |
9.77 . yaśca dhvāntātprakāśasyāvṛtistatpratighātibhiḥ || 77
9.78 . mūrtānāṃ pratighastejo'ṇūnāṃ nāmūrta īdṛśam |
9.78 . na ca cetanamātmānamasvatantro malaḥ kṣamaḥ || 78
9.79 . āvarītuṃ na cācyaṃ ca madyāvṛtinidarśanam |
9.79 . uktaṃ bhavadbhirevetthaṃ jaḍaḥ kartā nahi svayam || 79
9.80 . svatantrasyeśvarasyaitāḥ śaktayaḥ prerikāḥ kila |
9.80 . ataḥ karmavipākajñaprabhuśaktibaleritam || 80
9.81 . madyaṃ sūte madaṃ duḥkhasukhamohaphalātmakam |
9.81 . na ceśapreritaḥ puṃso mala āvṛṇuyādyataḥ || 81
9.82 . nirmale puṃsi neśasya prerakatvaṃ tathocitam |
9.82 . tulye nirmalabhāve ca prerayeyurna te katham || 82
9.83 . tamīśaṃ prati yuktaṃ yadbhūyasāṃ syātsadharmatā |
9.83 . tena svarūpasvātantryamātraṃ malavijṛmbhitam || 83
9.84 . nirṇītaṃ vitataṃ caitanmayānyatretyalaṃ punaḥ |
9.84 . malo'bhilāṣaścājñānamavidyā lolikāprathā || 84
9.85 . bhavadoṣo'nuplavaśca glāniḥ śoṣo vimūḍhatā |
9.85 . ahaṃmamātmatātaṅko māyāśaktirathāvṛtiḥ || 85
9.86 . doṣabījaṃ paśutvaṃ ca saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam |
9.86 . ityādyanvarthasaṃjñābhistatra tatraiṣa bhaṇyate || 86
9.87 . asmin sati bhavati bhavo duṣṭo bhedātmaneti bhavadoṣaḥ |
9.87 . mañcavadasmin duḥkhasroto'ṇūn vahati yatplavastena || 87
9.88 . śeṣāstu sugamarūpāḥ śabdāstatrārthamūhayeducitam |
9.88 . saṃsārakāraṇaṃ karma saṃsārāṅkura ucyate || 88
9.89 . caturdaśavidhaṃ bhūtavaiciatryaṃ karmajaṃ yataḥ |
9.89 . ata eva sāṃkhyayogapāñcarātrādiśāsane || 89
9.90 . ahaṃmameti saṃtyāgo naiṣkarmyāyopadiśyate |
9.90 . niṣkarmā hi sthite mūlamale'pyajñānanāmani || 90
9.91 . vaicitryakāraṇābhāvānnordhva sarati nāpyadhaḥ |
9.91 . kevalaṃ pārimityena śivābhedamasaṃspṛśan || 91
9.92 . vijñānakevalī proktaḥ śuddhacinmātrasaṃsthitaḥ |
9.92 . sa punaḥ śāṃbhavecchātaḥ śivābhedaṃ parāmṛśan || 92
9.93 . kramānmantreśatannetṛrūpo yāti śivātmatām |
9.93 . nanu kāraṇametasya karmaṇaścenmalaḥ katham || 93
9.94 . sa vijñānākalasyāpi na sūte karmasaṃtatim |
9.94 . maivaṃ sa hi malo jñānākale didhvaṃsiṣuḥ katham || 94
9.95 . hetuḥ syāddhvaṃsamānatvaṃ svātantryādeva codbhavet |
9.95 . didhvaṃsiṣudhvaṃsamānadhvastākhyāsu tisṛṣvatha || 95
9.96 . daśāsvantaḥ kṛtāvasthāntarāsu svakramasthiteḥ |
9.96 . vijñānākalamantreśatadīśāditvakalpanā || 96
9.97 . tataśca supte turye ca vakṣyate bahubhedatā |
9.97 . ataḥ pradhvaṃsanaunmukhyakhilībhūtasvaśaktikaḥ || 97
9.98 . karmaṇo hetutāmetu malaḥ kathamivocyatām |
9.98 . kiṃ ca karmāpi na malādyataḥ karma kriyātmakam || 98
9.99 . kriyā ca kartṛtārūpātsvātantryānna punarmalāt |
9.99 . yā tvasya karmaṇaścitraphaladatvena karmatā || 99
9.100 . prasiddhā sā na saṃkocaṃ vinātmani malaśca saḥ |
9.100 . vicitraṃ hi phalaṃ bhinnaṃ bhogyatvenābhimanyate || 100
9.101 . bhoktaryātmani teneyaṃ bhedarūpā vyavasthitiḥ |
9.101 . iti svakāryaprasave sahakāritvamāśrayan || 101
9.102 . sāmarthyavyañjakatvena karmaṇaḥ kāraṇaṃ malaḥ |
9.102 . nanvevaṃ karmasadbhāvānmalasyāpi sthiteḥ katham || 102
9.103 . vijñānākalatā tasya saṃkoco hyasti tādṛśaḥ |
9.103 . maivamadhvastasaṃkoco'pyasau bhāvanayā dṛḍham || 103
9.104 . nāhaṃ karteti manvānaḥ karmasaṃskāramujjhati |
9.104 . phaliṣyatīdaṃ karmeti yā dṛḍhā vṛttirātmani || 104
9.105 . sa saṃskāraḥ phalāyeha na tu smaraṇakāraṇam |
9.105 . apradhvaste'pi saṃkoce nāhaṃ karteti bhāvanāt || 105
9.106 . na phalaṃ kṣīvamūḍhādeḥ prāyaścitte'tha vā kṛte |
9.106 . yanmayādya tapastaptaṃ tadasmai syāditi sphuṭam || 106
9.107 . abhisaṃdhimataḥ karma na phaledabhisandhitaḥ |
9.107 . tathābhisaṃdhānākhyāṃ tu mānase karma saṃskriyām || 107
9.108 . phaloparaktāṃ vidadhatkalpate phalasampade |
9.108 . yastu tatrāpi dārḍhyena phalasaṃskāramujjhati || 108
9.109 . sa tatphalatyāgakṛtaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ phalamaśnute |
9.109 . anayā paripāṭyā yaḥ samastāṃ karmasaṃtatim || 109
9.110 . anahaṃyutayā projjhetsasaṃkoco'pi so'kalaḥ |
9.110 . nanvitthaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcidātmīyamabhisaṃdhitaḥ || 110
9.111 . parasmai syānna vijñātaṃ bhavatā tāttvikaṃ vacaḥ |
9.111 . tasya bhoktustathā cetsyādabhisaṃdhiryathātmani || 111
9.112 . tadavaśyaṃ parasyāpi satastadduṣkṛtaṃ bhavet |
9.112 . parābhisaṃdhisaṃvittau svābhisaṃdhirdṛḍhībhavet || 112
9.113 . abhisaṃdhānavirahe tvasya no phalayogitā |
9.113 . na me duṣkṛtamityeṣā rūḍhistasyāphalāya sā || 113
9.114 . parābhisandhivicchede svātmanānabhisaṃhitau |
9.114 . dvayorapi phalaṃ na syānnāśahetuvyavasthiteḥ || 114
9.115 . sukhahetau sukhe cāsya sāmānyādabhisaṃdhitaḥ |
9.115 . nirviśeṣādapi nyāyyā dharmādiphalabhoktṛtā || 115
9.116 . duḥkhaṃ me duḥkhaheturvā stādityeṣa punarna tu |
9.116 . sāmānyo'pyabhisaṃdhiḥ syāttadadharmasya nāgamaḥ || 116
9.117 . prakṛtaṃ brūmahe jñānākalasyoktacarasya yat |
9.117 . anahaṃyutayā sarvā vilīnāḥ karmasaṃskriyāḥ || 117
9.118 . tasmādasya na karmāsti kasyāpi sahakāritām |
9.118 . malaḥ karotu tenāyaṃ dhvaṃsamānatvamaśnute || 118
9.119 . apadhvastamalastvantaḥśivāveśavaśīkṛtaḥ |
9.119 . ahaṃbhāvaparo'pyeti na karmādhīnavṛttitām || 119
9.120 . uktaṃ śrīpūrvaśāstre ca tadetatparameśinā |
9.120 . malamajñānamicchanti saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam || 120
9.121 . dharmādharmātmakaṃ karma sukhaduḥkhādilakṣaṇam |
9.121 . lakṣayetsukhaduḥkhādi svaṃ kārya hetubhāvataḥ || 121
9.122 . nahi hetuḥ kadāpyāste vinā kārya nijaṃ kvacit |
9.122 . hetutā yogyataivāsau phalānantaryabhāvitā || 122
9.123 . pūrvakasya tu hetutvaṃ pāramparyeṇa kiṃ ca tat |
9.123 . lakṣyate sukhaduḥkhādyaiḥ samāne dṛṣṭakāraṇe || 123
9.124 . citrairhetvantaraṃ kiṃcittacca karmeha darśanāt |
9.124 . svāṅge prasādaraukṣyādi jāyamānaṃ svakarmaṇā || 124
9.125 . dṛṣṭamityanyadehasthaṃ kāraṇaṃ karma kalpyāte |
9.125 . ihāpyanyānyadehasthe sphuṭaṃ karmaphale yataḥ || 125
9.126 . kṛṣikarma madhau bhogaḥ śaradyanyā ca sā tanuḥ |
9.126 . anusaṃdhāturekasya saṃbhavastu yatastataḥ || 126
9.127 . tasyaiva tatphalaṃ citraṃ karma yasya purātanam |
9.127 . kṣīvo'pi rājā sūdaṃ cedādiśetprātarīdṛśam || 127
9.128 . bhojayetyanusaṃdhānādvinā prāpnoti tatphalam |
9.128 . itthaṃ janmāntaropāttakarmāpyadyānusaṃdhinā || 128
9.129 . vinā bhuṅkte phalaṃ hetustatra prācyā hyakampatā |
9.129 . ata eva kṛtaṃ karma karmaṇā tapasāpi vā || 129
9.130 . jñānena vā nirudhyeta phalapākeṣvanunmukham |
9.130 . ārabdhakāryaṃ dehe'smin yatpunaḥ karma tatkatham || 130
9.131 . ucchidyatāmantyadaśaṃ niroddhuṃ nahi śakyate |
9.131 . tatraiva dehe yattvanyadadyagaṃ vā purātanam || 131
9.132 . karma tajjñānadīkṣādyaiḥ śaṇḍhīkartuṃ prasahyate |
9.132 . tathā saṃskāradārḍhya hi phalāya dṛḍhatā punaḥ || 132
9.133 . yadā yadā vinaśyeta karmadhvastaṃ tadā tadā |
9.133 . ato mohaparādhīno yadyapyakṛta kiṃcana || 133
9.134 . tathāpi jñānakāle tatsarvameva pradahyate |
9.134 . uktaṃ ca śrīpare'hānādānaḥ sarvadṛgulvaṇaḥ || 134
9.135 . muhūrtānnirdahetsarva dehasthamakṛtaṃ kṛtam |
9.135 . dehasthamiti dehena saha tādātmyamāśritā || 135
9.136 . svācchandyātsaṃvidevoktā tatrasthaṃ karma dahyate |
9.136 . dehaikyavāsanātyāgātsa ca viśvātmatāsthiteḥ || 136
9.137 . akālakalite vyāpinyabhinne yā hi saṃskriyā |
9.137 . saṃkoca eva sānena so'pi dehaikatāmayaḥ || 137
9.138 . etatkārmamalaṃ proktaṃ yena sākaṃ layākalāḥ |
9.138 . syurguhāgahanāntaḥsthāḥ suptā iva sarīsṛpāḥ || 138
9.139 . tataḥ prabuddhasaṃskārāste yathocitabhāginaḥ |
9.139 . brahmādisthāvarānte'smin saṃsranti punaḥ punaḥ || 139
9.140 . ye punaḥ karmasaṃskārahānyai prārabdhabhāvanāḥ |
9.140 . bhāvanāpariniṣpattimaprāpya pralayaṃ gatāḥ || 140
9.141 . mahāntaṃ te tathāntaḥsthabhāvanāpākasauṣṭhavāt |
9.141 . mantratvaṃ pratipadyante citrāccitraṃ ca karmataḥ || 141
9.142 . asya kārmamalasyeyanmāyāntādhvavisāriṇaḥ |
9.142 . pradhānaṃ kāraṇaṃ proktamajñānātmāṇavo malaḥ || 142
9.143 . kṣobho'sya lolikākhyasya sahakāritayā sphuṭam |
9.143 . tiṣṭhāsāyogyataunmukhyamīśvarecchāvaśācca tat || 143
9.144 . na jaḍaścidadhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā kvāpi kṣamo yataḥ |
9.144 . aṇavo nāma naivānyatprakāśātmā maheśvaraḥ || 144
9.145 . cidacidrūpatābhāsī pudgalaḥ kṣetravitpaśuḥ |
9.145 . cidrūpatvācca sa vyāpī nirguṇo niṣkriyastataḥ || 145
9.146 . yogopāyepsako nityo mūrtivandhyaḥ prabhāṣyate |
9.146 . acittvādajñatā bhedo bhogyādbhoktrantarādatha || 146
9.147 . teṣāmaṇūnāṃ sa mala īśvarecchāvaśādbhṛśam |
9.147 . prabudhyate tathā coktaṃ śāstre śrīpūrvanāmani || 147
9.148 . īśvarecchāvaśādasya bhogecchā saṃprajāyate |
9.148 . bhogecchorupakārārthamādyo mantramaheśvaraḥ || 148
9.149 . māyāṃ vikṣobhya saṃsāraṃ nirmimīte vicitrakam |
9.149 . māyā ca nāma devasya śaktiravyatirekiṇī || 149
9.150 . bhedāvabhāsasvātantryaṃ tathāhi sa tayā kṛtaḥ |
9.150 . ādyo bhedāvabhāso yo vibhāgamanupeyivān || 150
9.151 . garbhīkṛtānantabhāvivibhāsā sā parā niśā |
9.151 . sā jaḍā bhedarūpatvātkāryaṃ cāsyā jaḍaṃ yataḥ || 151
9.152 . vyāpinī viśvahetutvātsūkṣmā kāryaikakalpanāt |
9.152 . śivaśaktyavinābhāvānnityaikā mūlakāraṇam || 152
9.153 . acetanamanekātma sarva kārya yathā ghaṭaḥ |
9.153 . pradhānaṃ ca tathā tasmātkārya nātmā tu cetanaḥ || 153
9.154 . ata evādhvani proktā pūrvaṃ māyā dvidhā sthitā |
9.154 . yathā ca māyā devasya śaktirabhyeti bhedinam || 154
9.155 . tattvabhāvaṃ tathānyo'pi kalādistattvavistaraḥ |
9.155 . niruddhaśakteryā kiṃcitkartṛtodvalanātmikā || 155
9.156 . nāthasya śaktiḥ sādhastātpuṃsaḥ kṣeptrī kalocyate |
9.156 . evaṃ vidyādayo'pyete dharāntāḥ paramārthataḥ || 156
9.157 . śivaśaktimayā eva proktanyāyānusārataḥ |
9.157 . tathāpi yatpṛthagbhānaṃ kalāderīśvarecchayā || 157
9.158 . tato jaḍatve kāryatve pṛthaktattvasthitau dhruvam |
9.158 . upādānaṃ smṛtā māyā kvacittatkāryameva ca || 158
9.159 . tathāvabhāsacitraṃ ca rūpamanyonyavarjitam |
9.159 . yadbhāti kila saṃkalpe tadasti ghaṭavadvahiḥ || 159
9.160 . khapuṣpādyastitāṃ brūmastato na vyabhicāritā |
9.160 . khapuṣpaṃ kāladiṅmātṛsāpekṣaṃ nāstiśabdataḥ || 160
9.161 . dharādivattathātyantābhāvo'pyevaṃ vivicyatām |
9.161 . yatsaṃkalpyaṃ tathā tasya bahirdeho'sti cetanaḥ || 161
9.162 . caitravatsauśivāntaṃ tatsarva tādṛśadehavat |
9.162 . yasya deho yathā tasya tajjātīyaṃ puraṃ bahiḥ || 162
9.163 . ataḥ suśivaparyantā siddhā bhuvanapaddhatiḥ |
9.163 . ātmanāṃ tatpuraṃ prāpyaṃ deśatvādanyadeśavat || 163
9.164 . ātmanāmadhvabhoktṛtvaṃ tato'yatnena siddhyati |
9.164 . sā māyā kṣobhamāpannā viśvaṃ sūte samantataḥ || 164
9.165 . daṇḍāhatevāmalakī phalāni kila yadyapi |
9.165 . tathāpi tu tathā citrapaurvāparyāvabhāsanāt || 165
9.166 . māyākārye'pi tattvaughe kāryakāraṇatā mithaḥ |
9.166 . sā yadyapyanyaśāstreṣu bahudhā dṛśyate sphuṭam || 166
9.167 . tathāpi mālinīśāstradṛśā tāṃ saṃpracakṣmahe |
9.167 . kalādivasudhāntaṃ yanmāyāntaḥ saṃpracakṣate || 167
9.168 . pratyātmabhinnamevaitatsukhaduḥkhādibhedataḥ |
9.168 . ekasyāmeva jagati bhogasādhanasaṃhatau || 168
9.169 . sukhādīnāṃ samaṃ vyakterbhogabhedaḥ kuto bhavet |
9.169 . na cāsau karmabhedena tasyaivānupapattitaḥ || 169
9.170 . tasmātkalādiko vargo bhinna eva kadācana |
9.170 . aikyametīśvarecchāto nṛttagītādivādane || 170
9.171 . eṣāṃ kalāditattvānāṃ sarveṣāmapi bhāvinām |
9.171 . śuddhatvamasti teṣāṃ ye śaktipātapavitritāḥ || 171
9.172 . kalā hi śuddhā tattādṛkkarmatvaṃ saṃprasūyate |
9.172 . mitamapyāśu yenāsmātsaṃsārādeṣa mucyate || 172
9.173 . rāgavidyākālayatiprakṛtyakṣārthasaṃcayaḥ |
9.173 . itthaṃ śuddha iti procya gururmānastutau vibhuḥ || 173
9.174 . evameṣā kalādīnāmutpattiḥ pravivicyate |
9.174 . māyātattvātkalā jātā kiṃcitkartṛtvalakṣaṇā || 174
9.175 . māyā hi cinmayādbhedaṃ śivādvidadhatī paśoḥ |
9.175 . suṣuptatāmivādhatte tata eva hyadṛkkriyaḥ || 175
9.176 . kalā hi kiṃcitkartṛtvaṃ sūte svāliṅganādaṇoḥ |
9.176 . tasyāścāpyaṇunānyonyaṃ hyañjane sā prasūyate || 176
9.177 . sadyonirvāṇadīkṣotthapuṃviśleṣe hi sā satī |
9.177 . śliṣyantyapi ca no sūte tathāpi svaphalaṃ kvacit || 177
9.178 . ucchūnateva prathamā sūkṣmāṅkurakaleva ca |
9.178 . bījasyāmbvagnimṛtkambutuṣayogātprasūtikṛt || 178
9.179 . kalā māyāṇusaṃyogajāpyeṣā nirvikārakam |
9.179 . nāṇuṃ kuryādupādānaṃ kiṃtu māyāṃ vikāriṇīm || 179
9.180 . malaścāvārako māyā bhāvopādānakāraṇam |
9.180 . karma syātsahakāryeva sukhaduḥkhodbhavaṃ prati || 180
9.181 . ataḥ saṃcchannacaitanyasamudbalanakāryakṛt |
9.181 . kalaivānantanāthasya śaktyā saṃpreritā jaḍā || 181
9.182 . na ceśaśaktirevāsya caitanyaṃ balayiṣyati |
9.182 . tadupodbalitaṃ taddhi na kiṃcitkartṛtāṃ vrajet || 182
9.183 . seyaṃ kalā na karaṇaṃ mukhyaṃ vidyādikaṃ yathā |
9.183 . puṃsi kartari sā kartrī prayojakatayā yataḥ || 183
9.184 . alakṣyāntarayoritthaṃ yadā puṃskalayorbhavet |
9.184 . māyāgarbheśaśaktyāderantarajñānamāntaram || 184
9.185 . tadā māyāpuṃvivekaḥ sarvakarmakṣayādbhavet |
9.185 . vijñānākalatā māyādhastānno yātyadhaḥ pumān || 185
9.186 . dhīpuṃviveke vijñāte pradhānapuruṣāntare |
9.186 . api na kṣīṇakarmā syātkalāyāṃ taddhi saṃbhavet || 186
9.187 . ataḥ sāṃkhyadṛśā siddhaḥ pradhānādho na saṃsaret |
9.187 . kalāpuṃsorviveke tu māyādho naiva gacchati || 187
9.188 . malādviviktamātmānaṃ paśyaṃstu śivatāṃ vrajet |
9.188 . sarvatra caiśvaraḥ śaktipāto'tra sahakāraṇam || 188
9.189 . māyāgarbhādhikārīyo dvayorantye tu nirmalaḥ |
9.189 . seyaṃ kalā kāryabhedādanyaiva hyanumīyate || 189
9.190 . anyathaikaṃ bhavedviśvaṃ kāryāyetyanyanihnavaḥ |
9.190 . iti mataṅgaśāstrādau yā proktā sā kalā svayam || 190
9.191 . kiṃcidrūpatayākṣipya kartṛtvamiti bhaṅgitaḥ |
9.191 . kiṃcidrūpaviśiṣṭaṃ yatkartṛtvaṃ tatkathaṃ bhavet || 191
9.192 . ajñasyeti tataḥ sūte kiṃcijjñatvātmikāṃ vidam |
9.192 . buddhiṃ paśyati sā vidyā buddhidarpaṇacāriṇaḥ || 192
9.193 . sukhādīn pratyayānmohaprabhṛtīn kāryakāraṇe |
9.193 . karmajālaṃ ca tatrasthaṃ vivinakti nijātmanā || 193
9.194 . buddhistu guṇasaṃkīrṇā vivekena kathaṃ sukham |
9.194 . duḥkhaṃ mohātmakaṃ vāpi viṣayaṃ darśayedapi || 194
9.195 . svacchāyāṃ dhiyi saṃkrāmanbhāvaḥ saṃvedyatāṃ katham |
9.195 . tayā vinaiti sāpyanyatkaraṇaṃ puṃsi kartari || 195
9.196 . nanu cobhayataḥ śubhrādarśadaśīyadhīgatāt |
9.196 . puṃsprakāśādbhāti bhāvaḥ maivaṃ tatpratibimbanam || 196
9.197 . jaḍameva hi mukhyo'tha puṃsprakāśo'sya bhāsanam |
9.197 . bahiḥsthasyaiva tasyāstu buddheḥ kiṃkalpanā kṛtā || 197
9.198 . abhedabhūmireṣā ca bhedaśceha vicāryate |
9.198 . tasmādbuddhigato bhāvo vidyākaraṇagocaraḥ || 198
9.199 . bhāvānāṃ pratibimbaṃ ca vedyaṃ dhīkalpanā tataḥ |
9.199 . kiṃcittu kurute tasmānnūnamastyaparaṃ tu tat || 199
9.200 . rāgatattvamiti proktaṃ yattatraivoparañjakam |
9.200 . na cāvairāgyamātraṃ tattatrāpyāsaktivṛttitaḥ || 200
9.201 . viraktāvapi tṛptasya sūkṣmarāgavyavasthiteḥ |
9.201 . kālastuṭyādibhiścaitatkartṛtvaṃ kalayatyataḥ || 201
9.202 . kāryāvacchedi kartṛtvaṃ kālo'vaśyaṃ kaliṣyati |
9.202 . niyatiryojanāṃ dhatte viśiṣṭe kāryamaṇḍale || 202
9.203 . vidyā rāgo'tha niyatiḥ kālaścaitaccatuṣṭayam |
9.203 . kalākāryaṃ bhoktṛbhāve tiṣṭhadbhoktṛtvapūritam || 203
9.204 . māyā kalā rāgavidye kālo niyatireva ca |
9.204 . kañcukāni ṣaḍuktāni saṃvidastatsthitau paśuḥ || 204
9.205 . dehapuryaṣṭakādyeṣu vedyeṣu kila vedanam |
9.205 . etatṣaṭkasasaṃkocaṃ yadavedyamasāvaṇuḥ || 205
9.206 . uktaṃ śivatanuśāstre tadidaṃ bhaṅgyantareṇa punaḥ |
9.206 . āvaraṇaṃ sarvātmagamaśuddhiranyāpyananyarūpeva || 206
9.207 . śivadahanakiraṇajālairdāhyatvātsā yato'nyarūpaiva |
9.207 . anidaṃpūrvatayā yadrañjayati nijātmanā tato'nanyā || 207
9.208 . sahajāśuddhimato'ṇorīśaguhābhyāṃ hi kañcukastrividhaḥ |
9.208 . tasya dvitīyacitiriva svacchasya niyujyate kalā ślakṣṇā || 208
9.209 . anayā vidvasya paśorupabhogasamarthatā bhavati |
9.209 . vidyā cāsya kalātaḥ śaraṇāntardīpakaprabhevābhūt || 209
9.210 . sukhaduḥkhasaṃvidaṃ yā vivinakti paśorvibhāgena |
9.210 . rāgaśca kalātattvācchucivastrakaṣāyavatsamutpannaḥ || 210
9.211 . tyaktuṃ vāñchati na yataḥ saṃsṛtisukhasaṃvidānandam |
9.211 . evamavidyāmalinaḥsamarthitastriguṇakañcukabalena || 211
9.212 . gahanopabhogagarbhe paśuravaśamadhomukhaḥ patati |
9.212 . etena malaḥ kathitaḥ kambukavadaṇoḥ kalādikaṃ tuṣavat || 212
9.213 . evaṃ kalākhyatattvasya kiṃcitkartṛtvalakṣaṇe |
9.213 . viśeṣabhāge kartṛtvaṃ carcitaṃ bhoktṛpūrvakam || 213
9.214 . viśeṣaṇatayā yo'tra kiñcidbhāgastadotthitam |
9.214 . vedyamātraṃ sphuṭaṃ bhinnaṃ pradhānaṃ sūyate kalā || 214
9.215 . samameva hi bhogyaṃ ca bhoktāraṃ ca prasūyate |
9.215 . kalā bhedābhisaṃdhānādaviyuktaṃ parasparam || 215
9.216 . bhoktṛbhogyātmatā na syādviyogācca parasparam |
9.216 . vilīnāyāṃ ca tasyāṃ syānmāyāsyāpi na kiṃcana || 216
9.217 . nanu śrīmadrauravādau rāgavidyātmakaṃ dvayam |
9.217 . sūte kalā hi yugapattato'vyaktamiti sthitiḥ || 217
9.218 . uktamatra vibhātyeṣa kramaḥ satyaṃ tathā hyalam |
9.218 . rajyamāno veda sarva vidaṃścāpyatra rajyate || 218
9.219 . tathāpi vastusatteyamihāsmābhirnirūpitā |
9.219 . tasyāṃ ca na kramaḥ ko'pi syādvā so'pi viparyayāt || 219
9.220 . tasmādvipratipattiṃ no kuryācchāstrodite vidhau |
9.220 . evaṃ saṃvedyamātraṃ yatsukhaduḥkhavimohataḥ || 220
9.221 . bhotsyate yattataḥ proktaṃ tatsāmyātmakamāditaḥ |
9.221 . sukhaṃ sattvaṃ prakāśatvātprakāśo hlāda ucyate || 221
9.222 . duḥkhaṃ rajaḥ kriyātmatvādkriyā hi tadatatkramaḥ |
9.222 . mohastamo varaṇakaḥ prakāśābhāvayogataḥ || 222
9.223 . ta ete kṣobhamāpannā guṇāḥ kārya pratanvate |
9.223 . akṣubdhasya vijātīyaṃ na syātkāryamadaḥ purā || 223
9.224 . uktameveti śāstre'smin guṇāṃstattvāntaraṃ viduḥ |
9.224 . bhuvanaṃ pṛthagevātra darśitaṃ guṇabhedataḥ || 224
9.225 . īśvarecchāvaśakṣubdhalolikaṃ puruṣaṃ prati |
9.225 . bhoktṛtvāya svatantreśaḥ prakṛtiṃ kṣobhayedbhṛśam || 225
9.226 . tena yaccodyate sāṃkhyaṃ muktāṇuṃ prati kiṃ na sā |
9.226 . sūte puṃso vikāritvāditi tannātra bādhakam || 226
9.227 . guṇebhyo buddhitattvaṃ tatsarvato nirmalaṃ tataḥ |
9.227 . puṃsprakāśaḥ sa vedyo'tra pratibimbatvamārchati || 227
9.228 . viṣayapratibimbaṃ ca tasyāmakṣakṛtaṃ bahiḥ |
9.228 . ataddvāraṃ samutprekṣāpratibhādiṣu tādṛśī || 228
9.229 . vṛttirbodho bhavedbuddheḥ sā cāpyālambanaṃ dhruvam |
9.229 . ātmasaṃvitprakāśasya bodho'sau tajjaḍo'pyalam || 229
9.230 . buddherahaṃkṛttādṛkṣe pratibimbitapuṃskṛteḥ |
9.230 . prakāśe vedyakaluṣe yadahaṃmananātmatā || 230
9.231 . tayā pañcavidhaścaiṣa vāyuḥ saṃrambharūpayā |
9.231 . prerito jīvanāya syādanyathā maraṇaṃ punaḥ || 231
9.232 . ata eva viśuddhātmasvātantryāhaṃsvabhāvataḥ |
9.232 . akṛtrimādidaṃ tvanyadityuktaṃ kṛtiśabdataḥ || 232
9.233 . ityayaṃ karaṇaskandho'haṃkārasya nirūpitaḥ |
9.233 . tridhāsya prakṛtiskandhaḥ sāttvarājasatāmasaḥ || 233
9.234 . sattvapradhānāhaṃkārādbhoktraṃśasparśinaḥ sphuṭam |
9.234 . manobuddhyakṣaṣaṭkaṃ tu jātaṃ bhedastu kathyate || 234
9.235 . mano yatsarvaviṣayaṃ tenātra pravivakṣitam |
9.235 . sarvatanmātrakartṛtvaṃ viśeṣaṇamahaṃkṛteḥ || 235
9.236 . buddhyahaṃkṛnmanaḥ prāhurbodhasaṃrabhaṇaiṣaṇe |
9.236 . karaṇaṃ bāhyadevairyannaivāpyantarmukhaiḥ kṛtam || 236
9.237 . prāṇaśca nāntaḥkaraṇaṃ jaḍatvātpreraṇātmanaḥ |
9.237 . prayatnecchāvibodhāṃśahetutvāditi niścitam || 237
9.238 . avasāyo'bhimānaśca kalpanā ceti na kriyā |
9.238 . ekarūpā tatastritvaṃ yuktamantaḥkṛtau sphuṭam || 238
9.239 . na ca buddhirasaṃvedyā karaṇatvānmano yathā |
9.239 . pradhānavadasaṃvedyabuddhivādastadujjhitaḥ || 239
9.240 . śabdatanmātrahetutvaviśiṣṭā yā tvahaṃkṛtiḥ |
9.240 . sā śrotre karaṇaṃ yāvadghrāṇe gandhatvabhoditā || 240
9.241 . bhautikatvamato'pyastu niyamādviṣayeṣvalam |
9.241 . ahaṃ śṛṇomi paśyāmi jighrāmītyādisaṃvidi || 241
9.242 . ahaṃtānugamādāhaṃkārikatvaṃ sphuṭaṃ sthitam |
9.242 . karaṇatvamato yuktaṃ kartraśaspṛktvayogataḥ || 242
9.243 . karturvibhinnaṃ karaṇaṃ preryatvātkaraṇaṃ kutaḥ |
9.243 . karaṇāntaravāñchāyāṃ bhavettatrānavasthitiḥ || 243
9.244 . tasmātsvātantryayogena kartā svaṃ bhedayan vapuḥ |
9.244 . karmāśasparśinaṃ svāṃśaṃ karaṇīkurute svayam || 244
9.245 . karaṇīkṛtatatsvāṃśatanmayībhāvanāvaśāt |
9.245 . karaṇīkurute'tyantavyatiriktaṃ kuṭhāravat || 245
9.246 . tenāśuddhaiva vidyāsya sāmānyaṃ karaṇaṃ purā |
9.246 . jñaptau kṛtau tu sāmānyaṃ kalā karaṇamucyate || 246
9.247 . nanu śrīmanmataṅgādau kalāyāḥ kartṛtoditā |
9.247 . tasyāṃ satyāṃ hi vidyādyāḥ karaṇatvārhatājuṣaḥ || 247
9.248 . ucyate kartṛtaivoktā karaṇatve prayojikā |
9.248 . tayā vinā tu nānyeṣāṃ karaṇānāṃ sthitiryataḥ || 248
9.249 . ato'sāmānyakaraṇavargāttatra pṛthakkṛtā |
9.249 . vidyāṃ vinā hi nānyeṣāṃ karaṇānāṃ nijā sthitiḥ || 249
9.250 . kalāṃ vinā na tasyāśca kartṛtve jñātṛtā yataḥ |
9.250 . kalāvidye tataḥ puṃso mukhyaṃ tatkaraṇaṃ viduḥ || 250
9.251 . ata eva vihīne'pi buddhikarmendriyaiḥ kvacit |
9.251 . andhe paṅgau rūpagatiprakāśo na na bhāsate || 251
9.252 . kiṃtu sāmānyakaraṇabalādvedye'pi tādṛśi |
9.252 . rūpasāmānya evāndhaḥ pratipattiṃ prapadyate || 252
9.253 . tata eva tvahaṃkārāttanmātrasparśino'dhikam |
9.253 . karmendriyāṇi vākpāṇipāyūpasthāṅghri jajñire || 253
9.254 . vacmyādade tyajāmyāśu visṛjāmi vrajāmi ca |
9.254 . iti yāhaṃkriyā kāryakṣamā karmendriyaṃ tu tat || 254
9.255 . tena cchinnakarasyāsti hastaḥ karmendriyātmakaḥ |
9.255 . tasya pradhānādhiṣṭhānaṃ paraṃ pañcāṅguliḥ karaḥ || 255
9.256 . mukhenāpi yadādānaṃ tatra yatkaraṇaṃ sthitam |
9.256 . sa pāṇireva karaṇaṃ vinā kiṃ saṃbhavetkriyā || 256
9.257 . tathābhāve tu buddhyakṣairapi kiṃ syātprayojanam |
9.257 . darśanaṃ karaṇāpekṣaṃ kriyātvāditi cocyate || 257
9.258 . parairgamau tu karaṇaṃ neṣyate ceti vismayaḥ |
9.258 . gamanotkṣepaṇādīni mukhyaṃ karmopalambhanam || 258
9.259 . punarguṇaḥ kriyā tveṣā vaiyākaraṇadarśane |
9.259 . kriyā karaṇapūrveti vyāptyā karaṇapūrvakam || 259
9.260 . jñānaṃ nādānamityetatsphuṭamāndhyavijṛmbhitam |
9.260 . tasmātkarmendriyāṇyāhustvagvadvyāptṝṇi mukhyataḥ || 260
9.261 . tatsthāne vṛttimantīti mataṅge guravo mama |
9.261 . nanvanyānyapi karmāṇi santi bhūyāṃsi tatkṛte || 261
9.262 . karaṇānyapi vācyāni tathā cākṣeṣvaniṣṭhitiḥ |
9.262 . nanvetatkheṭapālādyairnirākāri na karmaṇām || 262
9.263 . yatsādhanaṃ tadakṣaṃ syātkiṃtu kasyāpi karmaṇaḥ |
9.263 . etannāsmatkṛtapraśnatṛṣṇāsaṃtāpaśāntaye || 263
9.264 . nahyasvacchamitaprāyairjalaistṛpyanti barhiṇaḥ |
9.264 . ucyate śrīmatādiṣṭaṃ śaṃbhunātra mamottaram || 264
9.265 . svacchasaṃvedanodāravikalāprabalīkṛtam |
9.265 . iha karmānusaṃdhānabhedādekaṃ vibhidyate || 265
9.266 . tatrānusaṃdhiḥ pañcātmā pañca karmendriyāṇyataḥ |
9.266 . tyāgāyādānasaṃpattyai dvayāya dvitayaṃ vinā || 266
9.267 . svarūpaviśrāntikṛte caturdhā karma yadbahiḥ |
9.267 . pāyupāṇyaṅghrijananaṃ karaṇaṃ taccaturvidham || 267
9.268 . antaṃ prāṇāśrayaṃ yattu karmātra karaṇaṃ hi vāk |
9.268 . uktāḥ samāsataścaiṣāṃ citrāḥ kāryeṣu vṛttayaḥ || 268
9.269 . tadetadvyatiriktaṃ hi na karma kvāpi dṛśyate |
9.269 . tatkasyārthe prakalpyeyamindriyāṇāmaniṣṭhitiḥ || 269
9.270 . etatkartavyacakraṃ tadasāṃkaryeṇa kurvate |
9.270 . akṣāṇi sahavṛttyā tu buddhyante saṃkaraṃ jaḍāḥ || 270
9.271 . ukta indriyavargo'yamahaṃkārāttu rājasāt |
9.271 . tamaḥpradhānāhaṃkārādbhoktraṃśacchādanātmanaḥ || 271
9.272 . bhūtādināmnastanmātrapañcakaṃ bhūtakāraṇam |
9.272 . manobuddhyakṣakarmākṣavargastanmātravargakaḥ || 272
9.273 . ityatra rājasāhaṃkṛdyogaḥ saṃśleṣako dvaye |
9.273 . anye tvāhurmano jātaṃ rājasāhaṃkṛteryataḥ || 273
9.274 . samastendriyasaṃcāracaturaṃ laghu vegavat |
9.274 . anye tu sāttvikātsvāntaṃ buddhikarmendriyāṇi tu || 274
9.275 . rājasādgrāhakagrāhyabhāgasparśīni manvate |
9.275 . kheṭapālāstu manyante karmendriyagaṇaḥ sphuṭam || 275
9.276 . rājasāhaṃkṛterjāto rajasaḥ karmatā yataḥ |
9.276 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tu mano rājasātsāttvikātpunaḥ || 276
9.277 . indriyāṇi samastāni yuktaṃ caitadvibhāti naḥ |
9.277 . tathāhi bāhyavṛttīnāmakṣāṇāṃ vṛttibhāsane || 277
9.278 . ālocane śaktirantaryojane manasaḥ punaḥ |
9.278 . uktaṃ ca guruṇā kuryānmano'nuvyavasāyi sat || 278
9.279 . taddvayālambanā mātṛvyāpārātmakriyā iti |
9.279 . tānmātrastu gaṇo dhvāntapradhānāyā ahaṃkṛteḥ || 279
9.280 . atrāvivādaḥ sarvasya grāhyopakrama eva hi |
9.280 . pṛthivyāṃ saurabhānyādivicitre gandhamaṇḍale || 280
9.281 . yatsāmānyaṃ hi gandhatvaṃ gandhatanmātranāma tat |
9.281 . vyāpakaṃ tata evoktaṃ sahetutvāttu na dhruvam || 281
9.282 . svakāraṇe tirobhūtirdhvaso yattena nādhruvam |
9.282 . evaṃ rasādiśabdāntatanmātreṣvapi yojanā || 282
9.283 . viśeṣāṇāṃ yato'vaśyaṃ daśā prāgaviśeṣiṇī |
9.283 . kṣubhitaṃ śabdatanmātraṃ citrākārāḥ śratīrdadhat || 283
9.284 . nabhaḥ śabdo'vakāśātmā vācyādhyāsasaho yataḥ |
9.284 . tadetatsparśatanmātrayogātprakṣobhamāgatam || 284
9.285 . vāyutāmeti tenātra śabdasparśobhayātmatā |
9.285 . anye tvāhurdhvaniḥ khaikaguṇastadapi yujyate || 285
9.286 . yato vāyurnijaṃ rūpaṃ labhate na vināmbarāt |
9.286 . uttarottarabhūteṣu pūrvapūrvasthitiryataḥ || 286
9.287 . tata eva marudvyomnoraviyogo mithaḥ smṛtaḥ |
9.287 . śabdasparśau tu rūpeṇa samaṃ prakṣobhamāgatau || 287
9.288 . tejastattvaṃ tribhirdharmaiḥ prāhuḥ pūrvavadeva tat |
9.288 . taistribhiḥ sarasairāpaḥ sagandhairbhūriti kramaḥ || 288
9.289 . tatra pratyakṣataḥ siddho dharādiguṇasaṃcayaḥ |
9.289 . nahi gandhādidharmaughavyatiriktā vibhāti bhūḥ || 289
9.290 . yathā guṇaguṇidvaitavādināmekamapyadaḥ |
9.290 . citraṃ rūpaṃ paṭe bhāti kramāddharmāstathā bhuvi || 290
9.291 . yathā ca vistṛte vastre yugapadbhāti citratā |
9.291 . tathaiva yogināṃ dharmasāmastyenāvabhāti bhūḥ || 291
9.292 . gandhādiśabdaparyantacitrarūpā dharā tataḥ |
9.292 . upāyabhedādbhātyeṣā kramākramavibhāgataḥ || 292
9.293 . tata eva kramavyaktikṛto dhībheda ucyate |
9.293 . ṣaṣṭhīprayogo dhībhedādbhedyabhedakatā tathā || 293
9.294 . tena dharmātirikto'tra dharmī nāma na kaścana |
9.294 . tatrānekaprakārāḥ syurgandharūparasāḥ kṣitau || 294
9.295 . saṃsparśaḥ pākajo'nuṣṇāśītaḥ śabdo vicitrakaḥ |
9.295 . śauklyaṃ mādhuryaśītatve citrāḥ śabdāśca vāriṇi || 295
9.296 . śuklabhāsvaratoṣṇatvaṃ citrāḥ śabdāśca pāvake |
9.296 . apākajaścāśītoṣṇo dhvaniścitraśca mārute || 296
9.297 . varṇātmako dhvaniḥ śabdapratibimbānyathāmbare |
9.297 . yattu na sparśavaddharmaḥ śabda ityādi bhaṇyate || 297
9.298 . kāṇādaistatsvapratītiviruddhaṃ kena gṛhyatām |
9.298 . paṭahe dhvanirityeva bhātyabādhitameva yat || 298
9.299 . na ca hetutvamātreṇa tadādānatvavedanāt |
9.299 . śrotraṃ cāsmanmate'haṃkṛtkāraṇaṃ tatra tatra tat || 299
9.300 . vṛttibhāgīti taddeśaṃ śabdaṃ gṛhṇātyalaṃ tathā |
9.300 . yastvāha śrotramākāśaṃ karṇasaṃyogabheditam || 300
9.301 . śabdajaḥ śabda āgatya śabdabuddhiṃ prasūyate |
9.301 . tasya mande'pi murajadhvanāvākarṇake sati || 301
9.302 . amutra śrutireṣeti dūre saṃvedanaṃ katham |
9.302 . nahi śabdajaśabdasya dūrādūraravoditeḥ || 302
9.303 . śrotrākāśagatasyāsti dūrādūrasvabhāvatā |
9.303 . na cāsau prathamaḥ śabdastāvadvyāpīti yujyate || 303
9.304 . tatrasthaiḥ saha tīvrātmā śrūyamāṇastvanena tu |
9.304 . kathaṃ śrūyeta mandaḥsannahi dharmāntarāśrayaḥ || 304
9.305 . etaccānyairapākāri bahudheti vṛthā punaḥ |
9.305 . nāyastaṃ patitāghātadāne ko hi na paṇḍitaḥ || 305
9.306 . amīṣāṃ tu dharādīnāṃ yāvāṃstattvagaṇaḥ purā |
9.306 . guṇādhikatayā tiṣṭhan vyāptā tāvān prakāśate || 306
9.307 . vyāpyavyāpakatā yaiṣā tattvānāṃ darśitā kila |
9.307 . sā guṇādhikyataḥ siddhā na hetutvānna lāghavāt || 307
9.308 . ahetunāpi rāgo hi vyāpto vidyādinā sphuṭam |
9.308 . tadvinā na bhavedyattadvyāptamityucyate yataḥ || 308
9.309 . na lāghavaṃ ca nāmāsti kiṃcidatra svadarśane |
9.309 . guṇādhikyādato jñeyā vyāpyavyāpakatā sphuṭā || 309
9.310 . yo hi yasmādguṇotkṛṣṭaḥ sa tasmādūrdhva ucyate |
9.310 . ūrdhvatā vyāptṛtā śrīmanmālinīvijaye sphuṭā || 310
9.311 . ataḥ śivatvātprabhṛti prakāśatāsvarūpamādāya nijātmani dhruvam |
9.311 . samastatattvāvalidharmasaṃcayairvibhāti bhūrvyāptṛtayā sthitairalam || 311
9.312 . evaṃ jalāderapi śaktitattvaparyantadhāmno vapurasti tādṛk |
9.312 . kiṃ tūttaraṃ śaktitayaiva tattvaṃ pūrva tu taddharmatayeti bhedaḥ || 312
9.313 . anuttaraprakriyāyāṃ vaitatyena pradarśitam |
9.313 . etattasmāttataḥ paśyedvistarārthī vivecakaḥ || 313
9.314 . iti tattvasyarūpasya kṛtaṃ samyakprakāśanam  || 314

c10 atha śrītantrāloke daśamamāhnikam

10.1 . ucyate trikaśāstrekarahasyaṃ tattvabhedanam || 1
10.2 . teṣāmamīṣāṃ tattvānāṃ svavargeṣvanugāminām |
10.2 . bhedāntaramapi proktaṃ śāstre'tra śrītrikābhidhe || 2
10.3 . śaktimacchaktibhedena dharādyaṃ mūlapaścimam |
10.3 . bhidyate pañcadaśadhā svarūpeṇa sahānarāt || 3
10.4 . kalāntaṃ bhedayugghīnaṃ rudravatpralayākalaḥ |
10.4 . tadvanmāyā ca navadhā jñākalāḥ saptadhā punaḥ || 4
10.5 . mantrāstadīśāḥ pāñcadhye mantreśapatayastridhā |
10.5 . śivo na bhidyate svaikaprakāśaghanacinmayaḥ || 5
10.6 . śivo mantramaheśeśamantrā akalayukkalī |
10.6 . śaktimantaḥ sapta tathā śaktayastaccaturdaśa || 6
10.7 . svaṃ svarūpaṃ pañcadaśaṃ tadbhūḥ pañcadaśātmikā |
10.7 . tathāhi tisro devasya śaktayo varṇitāḥ purā || 7
10.8 . tā eva mātṛmāmeyatrairūpyeṇa vyavasthitāḥ |
10.8 . parāṃśo mātṛrūpo'tra pramāṇāṃśaḥ parāparaḥ || 8
10.9 . meyo'paraḥ śaktimāṃśca śaktiḥ svaṃ rūpamityadaḥ |
10.9 . tatra svarūpaṃ bhūmeryatpṛthagjaḍamavasthitam || 9
10.10 . mātṛmānādyupadhibhirasaṃjātoparāgakam |
10.10 . sakalādiśivāntaistu mātṛbhirvedyatāsya yā || 10
10.11 . śaktimadbhiranudbhūtaśaktibhiḥ sapta tadbhidaḥ |
10.11 . sakalādiśivāntānāṃ śaktiṣūdrecitātmasu || 11
10.12 . vedyatājanitāḥ sapta bhedā iti caturdaśa |
10.12 . sakalasya pramāṇāṃśo yo'sau vidyākalātmakaḥ || 12
10.13 . sāmānyātmā sa śaktitve gaṇito natu tadbhidaḥ |
10.13 . layākalasya mānāṃśaḥ sa eva paramasphuṭaḥ || 13
10.14 . jñānākalasya mānaṃ tu galadvidyākalāvṛti |
10.14 . aśuddhavidyākalanādhvaṃsasaṃskārasaṃgatā || 14
10.15 . prabubhutsuḥ śuddhavidyā santrāṇāṃ karaṇaṃ bhavet |
10.15 . prabuddhā śuddhavidyā tu tatsaṃskāreṇa saṃgatā || 15
10.16 . mānaṃ mantreśvarāṇāṃ syāttatsaṃskāravivarjitā |
10.16 . mānaṃ mantramaheśānāṃ karaṇaṃ śaktirucyate || 16
10.17 . svātantryamātrasadbhāvā yā tvicchā śaktiraiśvarī |
10.17 . śivasya saiva karaṇaṃ tayā vetti karoti ca || 17
10.18 . ā śivātsakalāntaṃ ye mātāraḥ sapta te dvidhā |
10.18 . nyagbhūtodriktaśaktitvāttadbhedo vedyabhedakaḥ || 18
10.19 . tathāhi vedyatā nāma bhāvasyaiva nijaṃ vapuḥ |
10.19 . caitreṇa vedyaṃ vedmīti kiṃhyatra pratibhāsatām || 19
10.20 . nanu caitrīyavijñānamātramatra prakāśate |
10.20 . vedyatākhyastu no dharmo bhāti bhāvasya nīlavat || 20
10.21 . vedyatā ca svabhāvena dharmo bhāvasya cettataḥ |
10.21 . sarvānpratyeva vedyaḥ syāddhaṭanīlādidharmavat || 21
10.22 . atha vedakasaṃvittibalādvedyatvadharmabhāk |
10.22 . bhāvastathāpi doṣo'sau kuvindakṛtavastravat || 22
10.23 . vedyatākhyastu yo dharmaḥ so'vedyaścetkhapuṣpavat |
10.23 . vedyaścedasti tatrāpi vedyetatyanavasthitiḥ || 23
10.24 . tato na kiṃcidvedyaṃ syānmūrchitaṃ tu jagadbhavet |
10.24 . nanu vijñātrupādhyaṃ śo paskṛtaṃ vapurucyatām || 24
10.25 . bhāvasyārthaprakāśātma yathā jñānamidaṃ tvasat |
10.25 . ekavijñātṛvedyatve na jñātrantaravedyatā || 25
10.26 . samastajñātṛvedyatve naikavijñātṛvedyatā |
10.26 . tasmānna vedyatā nāma bhāvadharmo'sti kaścana || 26
10.27 . bhāvasya vedyatā saiva saṃvido yaḥ samudbhavaḥ |
10.27 . arthagrahaṇarūpaṃ hi yatra vijñānamātmani || 27
10.28 . samavaiti prakāśyo'rthastaṃ pratyeṣaiva vedyatā |
10.28 . atra brūmaḥ padārthānāṃ na dharmo yadi vedyatā || 28
10.29 . avedyā eva te saṃsyurjñāne satyapi varṇite |
10.29 . yathāhi pṛthubudhnādirūpe kumbhasya satyapi || 29
10.30 . atadātmā paṭo naiti pṛthubudhnādirūpatām |
10.30 . tathā satyapi vijñāne vijñātṛsamavāyini || 30
10.31 . avedyadharmakā bhāvāḥ kathaṃ vedyatvamāpnuyuḥ |
10.31 . anarthaḥ sumahāṃścaiṣa dṛśyatāṃ vastu yatsvayam || 31
10.32 . prakāśātma na tatsaṃviccāprakāśā tadāśrayaḥ |
10.32 . aprakāśo manodīpacakṣurādi tathaiva tat || 32
10.33 . kiṃ tatprakāśatāṃ nāma supte jagati sarvataḥ |
10.33 . jñānasyārthaprakāśatvaṃ nanu rūpaṃ pradīpavat || 33
10.34 . apūrvamatra viditaṃ narīnṛtyāmahe tataḥ |
10.34 . arthaprakāśo jñānasya yadrūpaṃ tannirūpyatām || 34
10.35 . arthaḥ prakāśaścedrūpamartho vā jñānameva vā |
10.35 . athārthasya prakāśo yastadrūpamiti bhaṇyate || 35
10.36 . ṣaṣṭhī kartari cedukto doṣa eva duruddharaḥ |
10.36 . atha karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyeṣā ṇyarthastatra hṛdi sthitaḥ || 36
10.37 . tathā cedaṃ darśayāmaḥ kiṃ prakāśaḥ prakāśate |
10.37 . aprakāśo'pi naivāsau tathāpi ca na kiṃcana || 37
10.38 . tarhi loke kathaṃ ṇyarthaḥ ucyate cetanasthitau |
10.38 . mukhyo ṇyarthasya viṣayo jaḍeṣu tvaupacārikaḥ || 38
10.39 . tathāhi gantuṃ śakto'pi caitro'nyāyattatāṃ gateḥ |
10.39 . manvāna eva vaktyasmi gamitaḥ svāmineti hi || 39
10.40 . svāmyapyasya gatau śaktiṃ buddhvā svādhīnatāṃ sphuṭam |
10.40 . paśyannivṛttimāśaṃkya gamayāmīti bhāṣate || 40
10.41 . preryaprerakayorevaṃ maulikī ṇyarthasaṃgatiḥ |
10.41 . tadabhiprāyato'nyo'pi loke vyavaharettathā || 41
10.42 . śaraṃ gamayatītyatra punarvegākhyasaṃskriyām |
10.42 . vidadhatprerakammanya upacāreṇa jāyate || 42
10.43 . vāyuradriṃ pātayatītyatra dvāvapi tau jaḍau |
10.43 . draṣṭṛbhiḥ prerakapreryavapuṣā parikalpitau || 43
10.44 . itthaṃ jaḍena saṃbandhe na mukhyā ṇyarthasaṃgatiḥ |
10.44 . āstāmanyatra vitatametadvistarato mayā || 44
10.45 . arthe prakāśanā seyamupacārastato bhavet |
10.45 . astu cedbhāsate tarhi sa eva patadadrivat || 45
10.46 . upacāre nimittena kenāpi kila bhūyate |
10.46 . vāyuḥ pātayatītyatra nimittaṃ tatkṛtā kriyā || 46
10.47 . girau yenaiṣa saṃyoganāśādbhraṃśaṃ prapadyate |
10.47 . iha tu jñānamarthasya na kiṃcitkarameva tat || 47
10.48 . upacāraḥ kathaṃ nāma bhavetso'pi hyavastusan |
10.48 . aprakāśita evārthaḥ prakāśatvopacārataḥ || 48
10.49 . tādṛgeva śiśuḥ kiṃ hi dahatyagnyupacārataḥ |
10.49 . śiśau vahnyupacāre yadbījaṃ taikṣṇyādi tacca sat || 49
10.50 . prakāśatvopacāre tu kiṃ bījaṃ yatra satyatā |
10.50 . siddhe hi cetane yukta upacāraḥ sa hi sphuṭam || 50
10.51 . adhyāropātmakaḥ so'pi pratisaṃdhānajīvitaḥ |
10.51 . na cādyāpi kimapyasti cetanaṃ jñānamapyadaḥ || 51
10.52 . aprakāśaṃ tadanyena tatprakāśe'pyayaṃ vidhiḥ |
10.52 . nanu pradīpo rūpasya prakāśaḥ kathamīdṛśam || 52
10.53 . atrāpi na vahantyetāḥ kiṃ nu yuktivikalpanāḥ |
10.53 . yādṛśā svena rūpeṇa dīpo rūpaṃ prakāśayet || 53
10.54 . tādṛśā svayamapyeṣa bhāti jñānaṃ tu no tathā |
10.54 . pradīpaścaiṣa bhāvānāṃ prakāśatvaṃ dadā[dhā]tyalam || 54
10.55 . anyathā na prakāśerannabhede cedṛśo vidhiḥ |
10.55 . tasmātprakāśa evāyaṃ pūrvoktaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ || 55
10.56 . yathā yathā prakāśeta tattadbhāvavapuḥ sphuṭam |
10.56 . evaṃ ca nīlatā nāma yathā kācitprakāśate || 56
10.57 . tadvaccakāsti vedyatvaṃ tacca bhāvāṃśapṛṣṭhagam |
10.57 . phalaṃ prakaṭatārthasya saṃvidveti dvayaṃ tataḥ || 57
10.58 . vipakṣato rakṣitaṃ ca saṃdhānaṃ cāpi tanmithaḥ |
10.58 . tathāhi nibhṛtaścauraścaitravedyamiti sphuṭam || 58
10.59 . buddhvā nādatta evāśu parīpsāvivaśo'pi san |
10.59 . seyaṃ paśyati māṃ netratribhāgeneti sādaram || 59
10.60 . svaṃ dehamamṛteneva siktaṃ paśyati kāmukaḥ |
10.60 . na caitajjñānasaṃvittimātraṃ bhāvāṃśapṛṣṭhagam || 60
10.61 . arthakriyākaraṃ taccenna dharmaḥ konvasau bhavet |
10.61 . yaccoktaṃ vedyatādharmā bhāvaḥ sarvānapi prati || 61
10.62 . syādityetatsvapakṣaghnaṃ duṣprayogāstravattava |
10.62 . asmākaṃ tu svaprakāśaśivatāmātravādinām || 62
10.63 . anyaṃ prati cakāstīti vaca eva na vidyate |
10.63 . sarvānprati ca tannīlaṃ sa ghaṭaśceti yadvacaḥ || 63
10.64 . tadapyaviditaprāyaṃ gṛhītaṃ mugdhabuddhibhiḥ |
10.64 . nahi kālāgnirudrīyakāyāvagatanīlimā || 64
10.65 . tava nīlaḥ kiṃ nu pīto maivaṃ bhūnnatu nīlakaḥ |
10.65 . na kaṃcitprati nīlo'sau nīlo vā yaṃ prati sthitaḥ || 65
10.66 . taṃ pratyeva sa vedyaḥ syātsaṃkalpadvārako'ntataḥ |
10.66 . yathā cārthaprakāśātma jñānaṃ saṃgīryate tvayā || 66
10.67 . tathā tajjñātṛvedyatvaṃ bhāvīyaṃ rūpamucyatām |
10.67 . na ca jñātātra niyataḥ kaścijjñāne yathā tava || 67
10.68 . arthe jñātā yadā yo yastadvedyaṃ vapurucyatām |
10.68 . tattadvijñātṛvedyatvaṃ sarvānpratyeva bhāsatām || 68
10.69 . ityevaṃ codayanmanye vrajedbadhiradhuryatām |
10.69 . nahyanyaṃ prati vai kaṃcidbhāti sā vedyatā tathā || 69
10.70 . bhāvasya rūpamityukte keyamasthānavaidhurī |
10.70 . anena nītimārgeṇa nirmūlamapasāritā || 70
10.71 . anavasthā tathā hyanyairnīlādyaiḥ sadṛśī na sā |
10.71 . vedyatā kiṃtu dharmo'sau yadyogātsarvadharmavān || 71
10.72 . dharmī vedyatvamabhyeti sa sattāsamavāyavat |
10.72 . brūṣe yathā hi kurute sattā satyasataḥ sataḥ || 72
10.73 . samavāyo'pi saṃśliṣṭaḥ śliṣṭānaśliṣṭatājuṣaḥ |
10.73 . antyo viśeṣo vyāvṛttirūpo vyāvṛttivarjitān || 73
10.74 . vyāvṛttān śvetimā śuklamaśuklaṃ gamanaṃ tathā |
10.74 . tadvannīlādidharmāṃśayukto dharmī svayaṃ sthitaḥ || 74
10.75 . avedyo vedyatārūpāddharmādvedyatvamāgataḥ |
10.75 . vedyatā bhāsamānā ca svayaṃ nīlādidharmavat || 75
10.76 . aprakāśā svaprakāśāddharmādeti prakāśatām |
10.76 . prakāśe khalu viśrāntiṃ viśvaṃ śrayati cettataḥ || 76
10.77 . nānyā kācidapekṣāsya kṛtakṛtyasya sarvataḥ |
10.77 . yathā ca śivanāthena svātantryādbhāsyate bhidā || 77
10.78 . nīlādivattathaivāyaṃ vedyatā dharma ucyate |
10.78 . evaṃ siddhaṃ hi vedyatvaṃ bhāvadharmo'stu kā ghṛṇā || 78
10.79 . idaṃ tu cintyaṃ sakalaparyantoktapramātṛbhiḥ |
10.79 . vedyatvamekarūpaṃ syāccāturdaśyamataḥ kutaḥ || 79
10.80 . ucyate paripūrṇaṃ cedbhāvīyaṃ rūpamucyate |
10.80 . tadvibhurbhairavo devo bhagavāneva bhaṇyate || 80
10.81 . atha tannijamāhātmyakalpitoṃ'śāṃśikākramaḥ |
10.81 . sahyate kiṃ kṛtaṃ tarhi proktakalpanayānayā || 81
10.82 . ata eva yadā yena vapuṣā bhāti yadyathā |
10.82 . tadā tathā tattadrūpamityeṣopaniṣatparā || 82
10.83 . caitreṇa vedyaṃ jānāmi dvābhyāṃ bahubhirapyatha |
10.83 . mantreṇa tanmaheśena śivenodriktaśaktinā || 83
10.84 . anyādṛśena vetyevaṃ bhāvo bhāti yathā tathā |
10.84 . arthakriyādivaicitryamabhyetyaparisaṃkhyayā || 84
10.85 . tathā hyekāgrasakalasāmājikajanaḥ khalu |
10.85 . nṛttaṃ gītaṃ sudhāsārasāgaratvena manyate || 85
10.86 . tata evocyate mallanaṭaprekṣopadeśane |
10.86 . sarvapramātṛtādātmyaṃ pūrṇarūpānubhāvakam || 86
10.87 . tāvanmātrārthasaṃvittituṣṭāḥ pratyekaśo yadi |
10.87 . kaḥ saṃbhūya guṇasteṣāṃ pramātraikyaṃ bhavecca kim || 87
10.88 . yadā tu tattadvedyatvadharmasaṃdarbhagarbhitam |
10.88 . tadvastu śuṣkātprāgrūpādanyadyuktamidaṃ tadā || 88
10.89 . śāstre'pi tattadvedyatvaṃ viśiṣṭārthakriyākaram |
10.89 . bhūyasaiva tathāca śrīmālinīvijayottare || 89
10.90 . tathā ṣaḍvidhamadhvānamanenādhiṣṭhitaṃ smaret |
10.90 . adhiṣṭhānaṃ hi devena yadviśvasya pravedanam || 90
10.91 . tadīśavedyatvenetthaṃ jñātaṃ prakṛtakāryakṛt |
10.91 . evaṃ siddhaṃ vedyatākhyo dharmo bhāvasya bhāsate || 91
10.92 . tadanābhāsayoge tu svarūpamiti bhaṇyate |
10.92 . upādhiyogitāśaṅkāmapahastayato'sphuṭam || 92
10.93 . svātmano yena vapuṣā bhātyarthastatsvakaṃ vapuḥ |
10.93 . jānāmi ghaṭamityatra vedyatānuparāgavān || 93
10.94 . ghaṭa eva svarūpeṇa bhāta ityapadiśyate |
10.94 . nanu tatra svayaṃvedyabhāvo mantrādyapekṣayā || 94
10.95 . api cāstyeva nanvastu natu sanpratibhāsate |
10.95 . avedyameva kālāgnivapurmeroḥ parā diśaḥ || 95
10.96 . mameti saṃvidi paraṃ śuddhaṃ vastu prakāśate |
10.96 . bhātatvādvedyamapi tanna vedyatvena bhāsanāt || 96
10.97 . avedyameva bhānaṃ hi tathā kamanuyuñjmahe |
10.97 . evaṃ pañcadaśātmeyaṃ dharā tadvajjalādayaḥ || 97
10.98 . avyaktāntā yato'styeṣāṃ sakalaṃ prati vedyatā |
10.98 . yattūcyate kalādyena dharāntena samanvitāḥ || 98
10.99 . sakalā iti tatkośaṣaṭkodrekopalakṣaṇam |
10.99 . udbhūtāśuddhacidrāgakalādirasakañcukāḥ || 99
10.100 . sakalālayasaṃjñāstu nyagbhūtākhilakañcukāḥ |
10.100 . jñānākalāstu dhvastaitatkañcukā iti nirṇayaḥ || 100
10.101 . tena pradhāne vedye'pi pumānudbhūtakañcukaḥ |
10.101 . pramātāstyeva sakalaḥ pāñcadaśyamataḥ sthitam || 101
10.102 . pāñcadaśyaṃ dharādhantarniviṣṭe sakale'pi ca |
10.102 . sakalāntaramastyeva prameye'trāpi mātṛ hi || 102
10.103 . sthūlāvṛtādisaṃkocatadanyavyāptṛtājuṣaḥ |
10.103 . pītādyāḥ sthirakampratvāccaturdaśa dharādiṣu || 103
10.104 . svarūpībhūtajaḍatāḥ prāṇadehapathe tataḥ |
10.104 . pramātṛtājuṣaḥ proktā dhāraṇā vijayottare || 104
10.105 . yadā tu meyatā puṃsaḥ kalāntasya prakalpyate |
10.105 . tadudbhūtaḥ kañcukāṃśo meyo nāsya pramātṛtā || 105
10.106 . ataḥ sakalasaṃjñasya pramātṛtvaṃ na vidyate |
10.106 . trayodaśatvaṃ tacchaktiśaktimaddvayavarjanāt || 106
10.107 . nyagbhūtakañcuko mātā yukta[yata]statra layākalaḥ |
10.107 . māyāniviṣṭo vijñānākalādyāḥ prāgvadeva tu || 107
10.108 . māyātattve jñeyarūpe kañcukanyagbhavo'pi yaḥ |
10.108 . so'pi meyaḥ kañcukaikyaṃ yato māyā susūkṣmikā || 108
10.109 . vijñānākala evātra tato mātāpakañcukaḥ |
10.109 . māyāniviṣṭe'pyakale tathetyekādaśātmatā || 109
10.110 . vijñānakevale vedye kañcukadhvaṃsasusthite |
10.110 . udbubhūṣuprabodhānāṃ mantrāṇāmeva mātṛtā || 110
10.111 . te'pi mantrā yadā meyāstadā mātā tadīśvaraḥ |
10.111 . sa hyudbhavātpūrṇabodhastasminprāpte tu meyatām || 111
10.112 . udbhūtapūrṇarūpo'sau mātā mantramaheśvaraḥ |
10.112 . tasminvijñeyatāṃ prāpte svaprakāśaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ || 112
10.113 . pramātā svakatādātmyabhāsitākhilavedyakaḥ |
10.113 . śivaḥ pramātā no meyo hyanyādhīnaprakāśatā || 113
10.114 . meyatā sā na tatrāsti svaprakāśo hyasau prabhuḥ |
10.114 . svaprakāśe'tra kasmiṃścidanabhyupagate sati || 114
10.115 . aprakāśātprakāśatve hyanavasthā duruttarā |
10.115 . tataśca suptaṃ viśvaṃ syānna caivaṃ bhāsate hi tat || 115
10.116 . anyādhīnaprakāśaṃ hi tadbhātyanyastvasau śivaḥ |
10.116 . ityasya svaprakāśatve kimanyairyuktiḍambaraiḥ || 116
10.117 . mānānāṃ hi paro jīvaḥ sa evetyuktamāditaḥ |
10.117 . nanvasti svaprakāśe'pi śive vedyatvamīdṛśaḥ || 117
10.118 . upadeśo[śyo]padeṣṭṛtvavyavahāro'nyathā katham |
10.118 . satyaṃ sa tu tathā sṛṣṭaḥ parameśena vedyatām || 118
10.119 . nīto mantramaheśādikakṣyāṃ samadhiśāyyate |
10.119 . tathābhūtaśca vedyo'sau nānavacchinnasaṃvidaḥ || 119
10.120 . pūrṇasya vedyatā yuktā parasparavirodhataḥ |
10.120 . tathā vedyasvabhāve'pi vastuto na śivātmatām || 120
10.121 . ko'pi bhāvaḥ projjhatīti satyaṃ tadbhāvanā phalet |
10.121 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tenoktaṃ śivaḥ sākṣānna bhidyate || 121
10.122 . sākṣātpadenāyamarthaḥ samastaḥ prasphuṭīkṛtaḥ |
10.122 . nanvekarūpatāyuktaḥ śivastadvaśato bhavet || 122
10.123 . trivedatāmantramahānāthe kātra vivāditā |
10.123 . maheśvareśamantrāṇāṃ tathā kevalinordvayoḥ || 123
10.124 . anantabhedataikaikaṃ sthitā sakalavatkila |
10.124 . tato layākale meye pramātāsti layākalaḥ || 124
10.125 . atastrayodaśatvaṃ syāditthaṃ naikādaśātmatā |
10.125 . vijñānākalavedyatve'pyanyo jñānākalo bhavet || 125
10.126 . mātā tadekādaśatā syānnaiva tu navātmatā |
10.126 . evaṃ mantratadīśānāṃ mantreśāntarasaṃbhave || 126
10.127 . vedyatvānnava sapta syuḥ sapta pañca tu te katham |
10.127 . ucyate satyamastyeṣā kalanā kiṃtu susphuṭaḥ || 127
10.128 . yathātra sakale bhedo na tathā tvakalādike |
10.128 . anantāvāntaredṛkṣayonibhedavataḥ sphuṭam || 128
10.129 . caturdaśavidhasyāsya sakalasyāsti bheditā |
10.129 . layākale tu saṃskāramātrātsatyapyasau bhidā || 129
10.130 . akalena viśeṣāya sakalasyaiva yujyate |
10.130 . vijñānakevalādīnāṃ tāvatyapi na vai bhidā || 130
10.131 . śivasvācchandyamātraṃ tu bhedāyaiṣāṃ vijṛmbhate |
10.131 . ityāśayena saṃpaśyanviśeṣaṃ sakalādiha || 131
10.132 . layākalādau novāca trāyodaśyādikaṃ vibhuḥ |
10.132 . nanvastu vedyatā bhāvadharmaḥ kiṃtu layākalau || 132
10.133 . manvāte neha vai kiṃcittadapekṣā tvasau katham |
10.133 . śrūyatāṃ saṃvidaikātmyatattve'sminsaṃvyavasthite || 133
10.134 . jaḍe'pi citirastyeva bhotsyamāne tu kā kathā |
10.134 . svabodhāvasare tāvadbhotsyate layakevalī || 134
10.135 . dvividhaśca prabodho'sya mantratvāya bhavāya ca |
10.135 . bhāvanādibalādanyavaiṣṇavādinayoditāt || 135
10.136 . yathāsvamādharauttaryavicitrātsaṃskṛtastathā |
10.136 . līnaḥ prabuddho mantratvaṃ tadīśatvamathaiti vā || 136
10.137 . svātantryavarjitā ye tu balānmohavaśīkṛtāḥ |
10.137 . layākalātsvasaṃskārātprabuddhyante bhavāya te || 137
10.138 . jñānākalo'pi mantreśamaheśatvāya budhyate |
10.138 . mantrāditvāya vā jātu jātu saṃsṛtaye'pi vā || 138
10.139 . avatāro hi vijñāniyogibhāve'sya bhidyate |
10.139 . uktaṃ ca bodhayāmāsa sa sisṛkṣurjagatprabhuḥ || 139
10.140 . vijñānakevalānaṣṭāviti śrīpūrvaśāsane |
10.140 . ataḥ prabhotsyamānatve yānayorbodhayogyatā || 140
10.141 . tadbalādvedyatāyogyabhāvenaivātra vedyatā |
10.141 . tathāhi gāḍhanidre'pi priye'nāśaṅkitāgatām || 141
10.142 . māṃ drakṣyatīti nāṅgeṣu sveṣu mātyabhisārikā |
10.142 . evaṃ śivo'pi manute etasyaitatpravedyatām || 142
10.143 . yāsyatīti sṛjāmīti tadānīṃ yogyataiva sā |
10.143 . vedyatā tasya bhāvasya bhoktṛtā tāvatī ca sā || 143
10.144 . layākalasya citro hi bhogaḥ kena vikalpyate |
10.144 . yathā yathā hi samvittiḥ sa hi bhogaḥ sphuṭo'sphuṭaḥ || 144
10.145 . smṛtiyogyo'pyanyathā vā bhogyabhāvaṃ na tūjjhati |
10.145 . gāḍhanidrāvimūḍho'pi kāntāliṅgitavigrahaḥ || 145
10.146 . bhoktaiva bhaṇyate so'pi manute bhoktṛtāṃ purā |
10.146 . utprekṣāmātrahīno'pi kāṃcitkulavadhūṃ puraḥ || 146
10.147 . saṃbhokṣyamāṇāṃ dṛṣṭvaiva rabhasādyāti saṃmadam |
10.147 . tāmeva dṛṣṭvā ca tadā samānāśayabhāgapi || 147
10.148 . anyastathā na saṃvitte kamatropalabhāmahe |
10.148 . loke rūḍhamidaṃ dṛṣṭirasminkāraṇamantarā || 148
10.149 . prasīdatīva magneva nirvātīvetivādini |
10.149 . itthaṃ vistaratastattvabhedo'yaṃ samudāhṛtaḥ || 149
10.150 . śaktiśaktimatāṃ bhedādanyonyaṃ tatkṛteṣvapi |
10.150 . bhedeṣvanyonyato bhedāttathā tattvāntaraiḥ saha || 150
10.151 . bhedopabhedagaṇanāṃ karvato nāvadhiḥ kvacit |
10.151 . tata eva vicitro'yaṃ bhuvanādividhiḥ sthitaḥ || 151
10.152 . pārthivatve'pi no sāmyaṃ rudravaiṣṇavalokayoḥ |
10.152 . kā kathānyatra tu bhavedbhoge vāpi svarūpake || 152
10.153 . sa ca no vistaraḥ sākṣācchakyo yadyapi bhāṣitum |
10.153 . tathāpi mārgamātreṇa kathyamāno vivicyatām || 153
10.154 . saptānāṃ mātṛśaktīnāmanyonyaṃ bhedane sati |
10.154 . rūpamekānnapañcāśatsvarūpaṃ cādhikaṃ tataḥ || 154
10.155 . sarvaṃ sarvātmakaṃ yasmāttasmātsakalamātari |
10.155 . layākalādiśaktīnāṃ saṃbhavo'styeva tattvataḥ || 155
10.156 . sa tvasphuṭo'stu bhedāṃśaṃ dātuṃ tāvatprabhurbhavet |
10.156 . teṣāmapi ca bhedānāmanyonyaṃ bahubhedatā || 156
10.157 . mukhyānāṃ bhedabhedānāṃ jalādyairbhedane sati |
10.157 . mukhyabhedaprakāreṇa vidherānantyamucyate || 157
10.158 . sakalasya samudbhūtāścakṣurādisvaśaktayaḥ |
10.158 . nyagbhūtāśca pratanvanti bhedāntaramapi sphuṭam || 158
10.159 . evaṃ layākalādīnāṃ tatsaṃskārapadoditāt |
10.159 . pāṭavātprakṣayādvāpi bhedāntaramudīyate || 159
10.160 . nyakkṛtāṃ śaktimāsthāyāpyudāsīnatayā sthitim |
10.160 . anāviśyeva yadvetti tatrānyā vedyatā khalu || 160
10.161 . āviśyeva nimajjyeva vikāsyeva vighūrṇya ca |
10.161 . vidato vedyatānyaiva bhedo'trārthakriyocitaḥ || 161
10.162 . anyaśaktitirobhāve kasyāścitsusphuṭodaye |
10.162 . bhedāntaramapi jñeyaṃ vīṇāvādakadṛṣṭivat || 162
10.163 . tirobhāvodbhavau śakteḥ svaśaktyantarato'nyataḥ |
10.163 . cetyamānādacetyādvā tanvāte bahubhedatām || 163
10.164 . evametaddharādīnāṃ tattvānāṃ yāvatī daśā |
10.164 . kācidasti ghaṭākhyāpi tatra saṃdarśitā bhidaḥ || 164
10.165 . atrāpi vedyatā nāma tādātmyaṃ vedakaiḥ saha |
10.165 . tataḥ sakalavedyo'sau ghaṭaḥ sakala eva hi || 165
10.166 . yāvacchivaikavedyo'sau śiva evāvabhāsate |
10.166 . tāvadekaśarīro hi bodho bhātyeva yāvatā || 166
10.167 . adhunātra samastasya dharātattvasya darśyate |
10.167 . sāmastya evābhihitaṃ pāñcadaśyaṃ puroditam || 167
10.168 . dharātattvāvibhedena yaḥ prakāśaḥ prakāśate |
10.168 . sa eva śivanātho'tra pṛthivī brahma tanmatam || 168
10.169 . dharātattvagatāḥ siddhīrvitarītuṃ samudyatān |
10.169 . prerayanti śivecchāto ye te mantramaheśvarāḥ || 169
10.170 . preryamāṇāstu mantreśā mantrāstadvācakāḥ sphuṭam |
10.170 . dharātattvagataṃ yogamabhyasya śivavidyayā || 170
10.171 . na tu pāśavasāṃkhyīyavaiṣṇavādidvitādṛśā |
10.171 . aprāptadhruvadhāmāno vijñānākalatājuṣaḥ || 171
10.172 . tāvattattvopabhogena ye kalpānte layaṃ gatāḥ |
10.172 . sauṣuptāvasthayopetāste'tra pralayakevalāḥ || 172
10.173 . sauṣupte tattvalīnatvaṃ sphuṭameva hi lakṣyate |
10.173 . anyathā niyatasvapnasaṃdṛṣṭirjāyate kutaḥ || 173
10.174 . sauṣuptamapi citraṃ ca svacchāsvacchādi bhāsate |
10.174 . asvāpsaṃ sukhamityādismṛtivaicitryadarśanāt || 174
10.175 . yadaiva sa kṣaṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ nidrāyaiva prabuddhyate |
10.175 . tadaiva smṛtireṣeti nārthajajñānajā smṛtiḥ || 175
10.176 . tena mūḍhairyaducyeta prabuddhasyāntarāntarā |
10.176 . tūlikādisukhasparśasmṛtireṣeti tatkutaḥ || 176
10.177 . māhākarmasamullāsasaṃmiśritamalābilāḥ |
10.177 . dharādhirohiṇo jñeyāḥ sakalā iha pudgalāḥ || 177
10.178 . asyaiva saptakasya svasvavyāpāraprakalpane |
10.178 . prakṣobho yastadevoktaṃ śaktīnāṃ saptakaṃ sphuṭam || 178
10.179 . śivo hyacyutacidrūpastisrastacchaktayastu yāḥ |
10.179 . tāḥ svātantryavaśopāttagrahītrākāratāvaśāt || 179
10.180 . tridhā mantrāvasānāḥ syurudāsīnā iva sthitāḥ |
10.180 . grāhyākāroparāgāttu grahītrākāratāvaśāt || 180
10.181 . sakalāntāstu tāstisra icchājñānakriyā matāḥ |
10.181 . saptadhetthaṃ pramātṛtvaṃ tatkṣobho mānatā tathā || 181

10.201 . yathā hi ciraduḥkhārtaḥ paścādāttasukhasthitiḥ |

10.202 . tathā gatavikalpe'pi rūḍhāḥ saṃvedane janāḥ |

10.217 . ata eva hi manyante saṃpradāyadhanā janāḥ |

10.222 . saṃvittattvaṃ bhāsamānaṃ paripūrṇaṃ hi sarvataḥ |

10.236 . tatra caitre bhāsamāne yo dehāṃśaḥ sa kathyate |

10.242 . laukikī jāgradityeṣā saṃjñā piṇḍasthamityapi |

10.293 . yāvadbhairavabodhāntaḥpraveśanasahiṣṇavaḥ |

10.294 . turyātītapade saṃsyuriti pañcadaśātmake |

10.295 . yadevāsthiramābhāti sapūrvaṃ svapna īdṛśaḥ |

10.296 . trayasyāsyānusaṃdhistu yadvaśādupajāyate |

10.299 . ekatrāpi prabhau pūrṇe citturyātītamucyate |

10.300 . jñānaśaktiḥ svapna uktaḥ kriyāśaktistu jāgṛtiḥ |
