10.301 . na cenna kvāpi mukhyatvaṃ nopacāro'pi tatkvacit |
10.301 . etacchrīpūrvaśāstre ca sphuṭamuktaṃ maheśinā || 301
10.302 . tatra svarūpaṃ śaktiśca sakalaśceti tattrayam |
10.302 . iti jāgradavastheyaṃ bhede pañcadaśātmake || 302
10.303 . akalau svapnasauṣupte turyaṃ mantrādivargabhāk |
10.303 . turyātītaṃ śaktiśaṃbhū trayodaśābhidhe punaḥ || 303
10.304 . svarūpaṃ jāgradanyattu prāgvatpralayakevale |
10.304 . svaṃ jāgratsvapnasupte dve turyādyatra ca pūrvavat || 304
10.305 . vijñānākalabhede'pi svaṃ mantrā mantranāyakāḥ |
10.305 . tadīśāḥ śaktiśaṃbhvitthaṃ pañca syurjāgradādayaḥ || 305
10.306 . saptabhede tu mantrākhye svaṃ mantreśā maheśvarāḥ |
10.306 . śaktiḥ śaṃbhuśca pañcoktā avasthā jāgradādayaḥ || 306
10.307 . svarūpaṃ mantramāheśī śaktirmantramaheśvaraḥ |
10.307 . śaktiḥ śaṃbhurimāḥ pañca mantreśe pañcabhedake || 307
10.308 . svaṃ kriyā jñānamicchā ca śaṃbhuratra ca pañcamī |
10.308 . maheśabhede trividhe jāgradādi nirūpitam || 308
10.309 . vyāpārādādhipatyācca taddhānyā prerakatvataḥ |
10.309 . icchānivṛtteḥ svasthatvācchiva eko'pi pañcadhā || 309
10.310 . ityeṣa darśito'smābhistattvādhvā vistarādatha |



c11 atha śrītantrāloke ekādaśamāhnikam

11.1 . kalādhvā vakṣyate śrīmacchāṃbhavājñānusārataḥ || 1
11.2 . yathā pūrvoktabhuvanamadhye nijanijaṃ gaṇam |
11.2 . anuyatparato bhinnaṃ tattvaṃ nāmeti bhaṇyate || 2
11.3 . tathā teṣvapi tattveṣu svavarge'nugamātmakam |
11.3 . vyāvṛttaṃ paravargācca kaleti śivaśāsane || 3
11.4 . kecidāhuḥ punaryāsau śaktirantaḥ susūkṣmikā |
11.4 . tattvānāṃ sā kaletyuktā dharaṇyāṃ dhārikā yathā || 4
11.5 . atra pakṣadvaye vastu na bhinnaṃ bhāsate yataḥ |
11.5 . anugāmi na sāmānyamiṣṭaṃ naiyāyikādivat || 5
11.6 . anye vadanti dīkṣādau sukhasaṃgrahaṇārthataḥ |
11.6 . śivena kalpito vargaḥ kaleti samayāśrayaḥ || 6
11.7 . kṛtaśca devadevena samayo'paramārthatām |
11.7 . na gacchatīti nāsatyo na cānyasamayodayaḥ || 7
11.8 . nivṛttiḥ pṛthivītattve pratiṣṭhāvyaktagocare |
11.8 . vidyā niśānte śāntā ca śaktyante'ṇḍamidaṃ catuḥ || 8
11.9 . śāntātītā śive tattve kalātītaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ |
11.9 . nahyatra vargīkaraṇaṃ samayaḥ kalanāpi vā || 9
11.10 . yujyate sarvatodikkaṃ svātantryollāsadhāmani |
11.10 . svātantryāttu nijaṃ rūpaṃ boddhṛdharmādavicyutam || 10
11.11 . upadeśatadāveśaparamārthatvasiddhaye |
11.11 . bodhyatāmānayandevaḥ sphuṭameva vibhāvyate || 11
11.12 . yato'taḥ śivatattve'pi kalāsaṃgatirucyate |
11.12 . aṇḍaṃ ca nāma bhuvanavibhāgasthitikāraṇam || 12
11.13 . prāhurāvaraṇaṃ tacca śaktyantaṃ yāvadasti hi |
11.13 . yadyapi prākśivākhye'pi tattve bhuvanapaddhatiḥ || 13
11.14 . uktā tathāpyapratighe nāsminnāvṛtisaṃbhavaḥ |
11.14 . nanvevaṃ dharaṇīṃ muktvā śaktau prakṛtimāyayoḥ || 14
11.15 . api cāpratighatve'pi kathamaṇḍasya saṃbhavaḥ |
11.15 . atrāsmadguravaḥ prāhuryatpṛthivyādipañcakam || 15
11.16 . pratyakṣamidamābhāti tato'nyannāsti kiṃcana |
11.16 . meyatve sthūlasūkṣmatvānmānatve karaṇatvataḥ || 16
11.17 . kartṛtollāsataḥ kartṛbhāve sphuṭatayoditam |
11.17 . triṃśattattvaṃ vibhedātma tadabhedo niśā matā || 17
11.18 . kāryatvakaraṇatvādivibhāgagalane sati |
11.18 . vikāsotkasvatantratve śivāntaṃ pañcakaṃ jaguḥ || 18
11.19 . śrīmatkālottarādau ca kathitaṃ bhūyasā tathā |
11.19 . pañcaitāni tu tattvāni yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat || 19
11.20 . pañcamantratanau tena sadyojātādi bhaṇyate |
11.20 . īśānāntaṃ tatra tatra dharādigaganāntakam || 20
11.21 . śivatattvamataḥ śūnyātiśūnyaṃ syādanāśri[vṛ]tam |
11.21 . yattu sarvāvibhāgātma svatantraṃ bodhasundaram || 21
11.22 . saptatriṃśaṃ tu tatprāhustattvaṃ paraśivābhidham |
11.22 . tasyāpyuktanayādvedyabhāve'tra parikalpite || 22
11.23 . yadāste hyanavacchinnaṃ tadaṣṭātriṃśamucyate |
11.23 . na cānavasthā hyevaṃ syāddṛśyatāṃ hi mahātmabhiḥ || 23
11.24 . yadvedyaṃ kiṃcidābhāti tatkṣaye yatprakāśate |
11.24 . tattattvamiti nirṇītaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaṃ hṛdi bhāsate || 24
11.25 . tatkiṃ na kiṃcidvā kiṃcidityākāṅkṣāvaśe vapuḥ |
11.25 . cidānandasvatantraikarūpaṃ taditi deśane || 25
11.26 . saptatriṃśaṃ samābhāti tatrākāṅkṣā ca nāparā |
11.26 . taccāpi klṛptavedyatvaṃ yatra bhāti sa cinmayaḥ || 26
11.27 . aṣṭātriṃśattamaḥ so'pi bhāvanāyopadiśyate |
11.27 . yadi nāma tataḥ saptatriṃśa eva punarbhavet || 27
11.28 . avibhāgasvatantratvacinmayatvādidharmatā |
11.28 . samaiva vedyīkaraṇaṃ kevalaṃ tvadhikaṃ yataḥ || 28
11.29 . dharāyāṃ guṇatattvānte māyānte kramaśaḥ sthitāḥ |
11.29 . gandho raso rūpamantaḥ sūkṣmabhāvakrameṇa tu || 29
11.30 . iti sthite naye śaktitattvānte'pyasti saukṣmyabhāk |
11.30 . sparśaḥ ko'pi sadā yasmai yoginaḥ spṛhayālavaḥ || 30
11.31 . tatsparśānte tu saṃvittiḥ śuddhacidvyomarūpiṇī |
11.31 . yasyāṃ rūḍhaḥ samabhyeti svaprakāśātmikāṃ parām || 31
11.32 . ato vindurato nādo rūpamasmādato rasaḥ |
11.32 . ityuktaṃ kṣobhakatvena spande sparśastu no tathā || 32
11.33 . mataṃ caitanmaheśasya śrīpūrve yadabhāṣata |
11.33 . dhārikāpyāyinī boddhrī pavitrī cāvakāśadā || 33
11.34 . ebhiḥ śabdairvyavaharannivṛttyādernijaṃ vapuḥ |
11.34 . pañcatattvavidhiḥ proktastritattvamadhunocyate || 34
11.35 . vijñānākalaparyantamātmā vidyeśvarāntakam |
11.35 . śeṣe śivastritattve syādekatattve śivaḥ param || 35
11.36 . imau bhedāvubhau tattvabhedamātrakṛtāviti |
11.36 . tattvādhvaivāyamitthaṃ ca na ṣaḍadhvasthiteḥ kṣatiḥ || 36
11.37 . prakṛtpumānyatiḥ kālo māyā vidyeśasauśivau |
11.37 . śivaśca navatattve'pi vidhau tattvādhvarūpatā || 37
11.38 . evamaṣṭādaśākhye'pi vidhau nyāyaṃ vadetsudhīḥ |
11.38 . yatra yatra hi bhogecchā tatprādhānyopayogataḥ || 38
11.39 . anyāntarbhāvanātaśca dīkṣānantavibhedabhāk |
11.39 . tena ṣaṭtriṃśato yāvadekatattvavidhirbhavet || 39
11.40 . tattvādhvaiva sa devena prokto vyāsasamāsataḥ |
11.40 . ekatattvavidhiścaiṣa suprabuddhaṃ guruṃ prati || 40
11.41 . śiṣyaṃ ca gatabhogāśamuditaḥ śaṃbhunā yataḥ |
11.41 . bhedaṃ visphārya visphārya śaktyā svacchandarūpayā || 41
11.42 . svātmanyabhinne bhagavānnityaṃ viśramayan sthitaḥ |
11.42 . itthaṃ tryātmādhvano bhedaḥ sthūlasūkṣmaparatvataḥ || 42
11.43 . meyabhāgagataḥ proktaḥ puratattvakalātmakaḥ |
11.43 . adhunā mātṛbhāgasthaṃ rūpaṃ tredhā nirūpyate || 43
11.44 . yatpramāṇātmakaṃ rūpamadhvano mātṛbhāgagam |
11.44 . padaṃ hyavagamātmatvasamāveśāttaducyate || 44
11.45 . tadeva ca padaṃ mantraḥ prakṣobhātpracyutaṃ yadā |
11.45 . guptabhāṣī yato mātā tūṣṇīṃbhūto vyavasthitaḥ || 45
11.46 . tathāpi na vimarśātma rūpaṃ tyajati tena saḥ |
11.46 . pramāṇātmavimarśātmā mānavatkṣobhabhāṅnatu || 46
11.47 . mantrāṇāṃ ca padānāṃ ca tenoktaṃ trikaśāsane |
11.47 . abhinnameva svaṃ rūpaṃ niḥspandakṣobhite param || 47
11.48 . audāsīnyaparityāge prakṣobhānavarohaṇe |
11.48 . varṇādhvā mātṛbhāge syātpūrvaṃ yā kathitā pramā || 48
11.49 . sā tu pūrṇasvarūpatvādavibhāgamayī yataḥ |
11.49 . tata ekaikavarṇatvaṃ tattve tattve kṣamāditaḥ || 49
11.50 . kṛtvā śaive pare proktāḥ ṣoḍaśārṇā visargataḥ |
11.50 . tatra śaktiparispandastāvān prākca nirūpitaḥ || 50
11.51 . saṃkalayyocyate sarvamadhunā sukhasaṃvide |
11.51 . padamantravarṇamekaṃ puraṣoḍaśakaṃ dhareti ca nivṛttiḥ |
11.51 . tattvārṇamagninayanaṃ rasaśarapuramastramantrapadamanyā || 51
11.52 . munitattvārṇaṃ dvikapadamantraṃ vasvakṣibhuvanamaparakalā |
11.52 . agnyarṇatattvamekakapadamantraṃ sainyabhuvanamiti turyā || 52
11.53 . ṣoḍaśa varṇāḥ padamantratattvamekaṃ ca śāntyatīteyam |
11.53 . abhinavaguptenāryātrayamuktaṃ saṃgrahāya śiṣyebhyaḥ || 53
11.54 . so'yaṃ samasta evādhvā bhairavābhedavṛttimān |
11.54 . tatsvātantryātsvatantratvamaśnuvāno'vabhāsate || 54
11.55 . tathāhi mātṛrūpastho mantrādhveti nirūpitaḥ |
11.55 . tathāhi cidvimarśena grastā vācyadaśā yadā || 55
11.56 . śivajñānakriyāyattamananatrāṇatatparā |
11.56 . aśeṣaśaktipaṭalīlīlālāmpaṭyapāṭavāt || 56
11.57 . cyutā mānamayādrūpātsaṃvinmantrādhvatāṃ gatā |
11.57 . pramāṇarūpatāmetya prayātyadhvā padātmatām || 57
11.58 . tathā hi māturviśrāntirvarṇānsaṃghaṭya tānbahūn |
11.58 . saṃghaṭṭanaṃ ca kramikaṃ saṃjalpātmakameva tat || 58
11.59 . vikalpasya svakaṃ rūpaṃ bhogāveśamayaṃ sphuṭam |
11.59 . ataḥ pramāṇatārūpaṃ padamasmadgururjagau || 59
11.60 . pramāṇarūpatāveśamaparityajya meyatām || 60
11.61 . gacchankalanayā yogādadhvā proktaḥ kalātmakaḥ |
11.61 . śuddhe prameyatāyoge sūkṣmasthūlatvabhāgini || 61
11.62 . tattvādhvabhuvanādhvatve krameṇānusaredguruḥ |
11.62 . prameyamānamātṝṇāṃ yadrūpamupari sthitam || 62
11.63 . pramātmātra sthito'dhvāyaṃ varṇātmā dṛśyatāṃ kila |
11.63 . ucchalatsaṃvidāmātraviśrāntyāsvādayoginaḥ || 63
11.64 . sarvābhidhānasāmarthyādaniyantritaśaktayaḥ |
11.64 . sṛṣṭāḥ svātmasahotthe'rthe dharāparyantabhāgini || 64
11.65 . āmṛśantaḥ svacidbhūmau tāvato'rthānabhedataḥ |
11.65 . varṇaughāste pramārūpāṃ satyāṃ bibhrati saṃvidam || 65
11.66 . bālāstiryakpramātāro ye'pyasaṃketabhāginaḥ |
11.66 . te'pyakṛtrimasaṃskārasārāmenāṃ svasaṃvidam || 66
11.67 . bhinnabhinnāmupāśritya yānti citrāṃ pramātṛtām |
11.67 . asyā cākṛtrimānantavarṇasaṃvidi rūḍhatām || 67
11.68 . saṃketā yānti cette'pi yāntyasaṃketavṛttitām |
11.68 . anayā tu vinā sarve saṃketā bahuśaḥ kṛtāḥ || 68
11.69 . aviśrāntatayā kuryuranavasthāṃ duruttarām |
11.69 . bālo vyutpādyate yena tatra saṃketamārgaṇāt || 69
11.70 . aṅgulyādeśane'pyasya nāvikalpā tathā matiḥ |
11.70 . vikalpaḥ śabdamūlaśca śabdaḥ saṃketajīvitaḥ || 70
11.71 . tenānanto hyamāyīyo yo varṇagrāma īdṛśaḥ |
11.71 . saṃvidvimarśasacivaḥ sadaiva sa hi jṛmbhate || 71
11.72 . yata eva ca māyīyā varṇāḥ sūtiṃ vitenire |
11.72 . ye ca māyīyavarṇeṣu vīryatvena nirūpitāḥ || 72
11.73 . saṃketanirapekṣāste prameti parigṛhyatām |
11.73 . tathā hi paravākyeṣu śruteṣvāvriyate nijā || 73
11.74 . pramā yasya jaḍo'sau no tatrārthe'bhyeti mātṛtām |
11.74 . śukavatsa paṭhatyeva paraṃ tatkramitaikabhāk || 74
11.75 . svātantryalābhataḥ svākyapramālābhe tu boddhṛtā |
11.75 . yasya hi svapramābodho vipakṣodbhedanigrahāt || 75
11.76 . vākyādivarṇapuñje sve sa pramātā vaśībhavet |
11.76 . yathā yathā cākṛtakaṃ tadrūpamatiricyate || 76
11.77 . tathā tathā camatkāratāratamyaṃ vibhāvyate |
11.77 . ādyāmāyīyavarṇāntarnimagne cottarottare || 77
11.78 . sakete pūrvapūrvāṃśamajjane pratibhābhidaḥ |
11.78 . ādyodrekamahattve'pi pratibhātmani niṣṭhitāḥ || 78
11.79 . dhruvaṃ kavitvavaktṛtvaśālitāṃ yānti sarvataḥ |
11.79 . yāvaddhāmani saṃketanikārakalanojjhite || 79
11.80 . viśrāntaścinmaye kiṃ kiṃ na vetti kurute na vā |
11.80 . ata eva hi vāksiddhau varṇānāṃ samupāsyatā || 80
11.81 . sarvajñatvādisiddhau vā kā siddhiryā na tanmayī |
11.81 . taduktaṃ varadena śrīsiddhayogīśvarīmate || 81
11.82 . tena guptena guptāste śeṣā varṇāstviti sphuṭam |
11.82 . evaṃ māmātṛmānatvameyatvairyo'vabhāsate || 82
11.83 . ṣaḍvidhaḥ svavapuḥśuddhau śuddhiṃ so'dhvādhigacchati |
11.83 . ekena vapuṣā śuddhau tatraivānyaprakāratām || 83
11.84 . antarbhāvyācarecchuddhimanusaṃdhānavān guruḥ |
11.84 . anantarbhāvaśaktau tu sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tu śodhayet || 84
11.85 . tadviśuddhaṃ bījabhāvātsūte nottarasaṃtatim |
11.85 . śodhanaṃ bahudhā tattadbhogaprāptyekatānatā || 85
11.86 . tadādhipatyaṃ tattyāgastacchivātmatvavedanam |
11.86 . tallīnatā tannirāsaḥ sarvaṃ caitatkramākramāt || 86
11.87 . ata eva ca te mantrāḥ śodhakāścitrarūpiṇaḥ |
11.87 . siddhāntavāmadakṣādau citrāṃ śuddhiṃ vitanvate || 87
11.88 . anuttaratrikānāmakramamantrāstu ye kila |
11.88 . te sarve sarvadāḥ kintu kasyācitkvāpi mukhyatā || 88
11.89 . ataḥ śodhakabhāvena śāstre śrīpūrvasaṃjñite |
11.89 . parāparādimantrāṇāmadhvanyuktā vyavasthitiḥ || 89
11.90 . śodhakatvaṃ ca mālinyā devīnāṃ tritayasya ca |
11.90 . devatrayasya vaktrāṇāmaṅgānāmaṣṭakasya ca || 90
11.91 . kiṃ vātibahunā dvāravāstvādhāragurukrame |
11.91 . lokapāstravidhau mantrānmuktvā sarvaṃ viśodhakam || 91
11.92 . yaccaitadadhvanaḥ proktaṃ śodhyatvaṃ śoddhṛtā ca yā |
11.92 . sā svātantryācchivābhede yuktetyuktaṃ ca śāsane || 92
11.93 . sarvametadvibhātyeva parameśitari dhruve |
11.93 . pratibimbasvarūpeṇa na tu bāhyatayā yataḥ || 93
11.94 . cidvyomnyeva śive tattaddehādimatirīdṛśī |
11.94 . bhinnā saṃsāriṇāṃ rajjau sarpasragvīcibuddhivat || 94
11.95 . yataḥ prāgdehamaraṇasiddhāntaḥ svapnagocaraḥ |
11.95 . dehāntarādirmaraṇe kīdṛgvā dehasaṃbhavaḥ || 95
11.96 . svapne'pi pratibhāmātrasāmānyaprathanābalāt |
11.96 . viśeṣāḥ pratibhāsante na bhāvyante'pi te yathā || 96
11.97 . śālagrāmopalāḥ keciccitrākṛtibhṛto yathā |
11.97 . tathā māyādibhūmyantalekhācitrahṛdaścitaḥ || 97
11.98 . nagarārṇavaśailādyāstadicchānuvidhāyinaḥ |
11.98 . na svayaṃ sadasanto no kāraṇākāraṇātmakāḥ || 98
11.99 . niyateścirarūḍhāyāḥ samucchedātpravartanāt |
11.99 . arūḍhāyāḥ svatantro'yaṃ sthitaścidvyomabhairavaḥ || 99
11.100 . ekacinmātrasaṃpūrṇabhairavābhedabhāgini |
11.100 . evamasmītyanāmarśo bhedako bhāvamaṇḍale || 100
11.101 . sarvapramāṇairno siddhaṃ svapne kartrantaraṃ yathā |
11.101 . svasaṃvidaḥ svasiddhāyāstathā sarvatra buddhyatām || 101
11.102 . cittacitrapurodyāne krīḍedevaṃ hi vetti yaḥ |
11.102 . ahameva sthito bhūtabhāvatattvapurairiti || 102
11.103 . evaṃ jāto mṛto'smīti janmamṛtyuvicitratāḥ |
11.103 . ajanmanyamṛtau bhānti cittabhittau svanirmitāḥ || 103
11.104 . parehasaṃvidāmātraṃ paralokehalokate |
11.104 . vastutaḥ saṃvido deśaḥ kālo vā naiva kiṃcana || 104
11.105 . abhaviṣyadayaṃ sargo mūrtaścenna tu cinmayaḥ |
11.105 . tadavekṣyata tanmadhyātkenaiko'pi dharādharaḥ || 105
11.106 . bhūtatanmātravargāderādhārādheyatākrame |
11.106 . ante saṃvinmayī śaktiḥ śivarūpaiva dhāriṇī || 106
11.107 . tasmātpratītirevetthaṃ kartrī dhartrī ca sā śivaḥ |
11.107 . tato bhāvāstatra bhāvāḥ śaktirādhārikā tataḥ || 107
11.108 . sāṃkalpikaṃ nirādhāramapi naiva patatyadhaḥ |
11.108 . svādhāraśaktau viśrāntaṃ viśvamitthaṃ vimṛśyatām || 108
11.109 . asyā ghanāhamityādirūḍhireva dharāditā |
11.109 . yāvadante cidasmīti nirvṛttā bhairavātmatā || 109
11.110 . maṇāvindrāyudhe bhāsa iva nīlādayaḥ śive |
11.110 . paramārthata eṣāṃ tu nodayo na vyayaḥ kvacit || 110
11.111 . deśe kāle'tra vā sṛṣṭirityetadasamañjasam |
11.111 . cidātmanā hi devena sṛṣṭirdikkālayorapi || 111
11.112 . jāgarābhimate sārdhahastatritayagocare |
11.112 . prahare ca pṛthaksvapnāścitradikkālamāninaḥ || 112
11.113 . ata eva kṣaṇaṃ nāma na kiṃcidapi manmahe |
11.113 . kriyākṣaṇe vāpyekasmin bahvyaḥ saṃsyurdrutāḥ kriyāḥ || 113
11.114 . tena ye bhāvasaṃkocaṃ kṣaṇāntaṃ pratipedire |
11.114 . te nūnamenayā nāḍyā śūnyadṛṣṭyavalambinaḥ || 114
11.115 . tadya eṣa sato bhāvāñśūnyīkartuṃ tathāsataḥ |
11.115 . sphuṭīkartuṃ svatantratvādīśaḥ so'smatprabhuḥ śivaḥ || 115
11.116 . taditthaṃ parameśāno viśvarūpaḥ pragīyate |
11.116 . na tu bhinnasya kasyāpi dharāderupapannatā || 116
11.117 . uktaṃ caitatpuraiveti na bhūyaḥ pravivicyate |
11.117 . bhūyobhiścāpi bāhyārthadūṣaṇaiḥ pravyaramyata || 117
11.118 . taditthameṣa nirṇītaḥ kalādervistaro'dhvanaḥ || 118

c12 atha śrītantrāloke dvādaśamāhnikam

12.1 . athādhvano'sya prakṛta upayogaḥ prakāśyate || 1
12.2 . itthamadhvā samasto'yaṃ yathā saṃvidi saṃsthitaḥ |
12.2 . taddvārā śūnyadhīprāṇanāḍīcakratanuṣvatho || 2
12.3 . bahiśca liṅgamūrtyagnisthaṇḍilādiṣu sarvataḥ |
12.3 . tathā sthitaḥ samastaśca vyastaścaiṣa kramākramāt || 3
12.4 . āsaṃvittattvamābāhyaṃ yo'yamadhvā vyavasthitaḥ |
12.4 . tatra tatrocitaṃ rūpaṃ svaṃ svātantryeṇa bhāsayet || 4
12.5 . sarvaṃ sarvatra rūpaṃ ca tasyāpi na na bhāsate |
12.5 . nahyavaccheditāṃ kvāpi svapne'pi viṣahāmahe || 5
12.6 . evaṃ viśvādhvasaṃpūrṇaṃ kālavyāpāracitritam |
12.6 . deśakālamayaspandasadma dehaṃ vilokayet || 6
12.7 . tathā vilokyamāno'sau viśvāntardevatāmayaḥ |
12.7 . dhyeyaḥ pūjyaśca tarpyaśca tadāviṣṭo vimucyate || 7
12.8 . itthaṃ ghaṭaṃ paṭaṃ liṅgaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ pustakaṃ jalam |
12.8 . yadvā kiṃcitkvacitpaśyettatra tanmayatāṃ vrajet || 8
12.9 . tatrārpaṇaṃ hi vastūnāmabhedenārcanaṃ matam |
12.9 . tathā saṃpūrṇarūpatvānusaṃdhirdhyānamucyate || 9
12.10 . saṃpūrṇatvānusaṃdhānamakampaṃ dārḍhyamānayan |
12.10 . tathāntarjalpayogena vimṛśañjapabhājanam || 10
12.11 . tatrārpitānāṃ bhāvānāṃ svakabhedavilāpanam |
12.11 . kurvaṃstadraśmisadbhāvaṃ dadyāddhomakriyāparaḥ || 11
12.12 . tathaivaṃkurvataḥ sarvaṃ samabhāvena paśyataḥ |
12.12 . niṣkampatā vrataṃ śuddhaṃ sāmyaṃ nandiśikhoditam || 12
12.13 . tathārcanajapadhyānahomavratavidhikramāt |
12.13 . paripūrṇāṃ sthitiṃ prāhuḥ samādhiṃ guravaḥ purā || 13
12.14 . atra pūjājapādyeṣu bahirantardvayasthitau |
12.14 . dravyaughe na vidhiḥ ko'pi na kāpi pratiṣiddhatā || 14
12.15 . kalpanāśuddhisaṃdhyādernopayogo'tra kaścana |
12.15 . uktaṃ śrītrikasūtre ca jāyate yajanaṃ prati || 15
12.16 . avidhijño vidhijñaścetyevamādi suvistaram |
12.16 . yadā yathā yena yatra svā samvittiḥ prasīdati || 16
12.17 . tadā tathā tena tatra tattadbhogyaṃ vidhiśca saḥ |
12.17 . laukikālaukikaṃ sarvaṃ tenātra viniyojayet || 17
12.18 . niṣkampatve sakampastu kampaṃ nirhrāsayedbalāt |
12.18 . yathā yenābhyupāyena kramādakramato'pi vā || 18
12.19 . vicikitsā galatyantastathāsau yatnavānbhavet |
12.19 . dhīkarmākṣagatā devīrniṣiddhaireva tarpayet || 19
12.20 . vīravrataṃ cābhinandediti bhargaśikhāvacaḥ |
12.20 . tathāhi śaṅkā mālinyaṃ glāniḥ saṃkoca ityadaḥ || 20
12.21 . saṃsārakārāgārāntaḥ sthūlasthūṇāghaṭāyate |
12.21 . mantrā varṇasvabhāvā ye dravyaṃ yatpāñcabhautikam || 21
12.22 . yaccidātma prāṇijātaṃ tatra kaḥ saṃkaraḥ katham |
12.22 . saṃkarābhāvataḥ keyaṃ śaṅkā tasyāmapi sphuṭam || 22
12.23 . na śaṅketa tathā śaṅkā vilīyetāvahelayā |
12.23 . śrīsarvācāravīrālīniśācarakramādiṣu || 23
12.24 . śāstreṣu vitataṃ caitattatra tatrocyate yataḥ |
12.24 . śaṅkayā jāyate glāniḥ śaṅkayā vighnabhājanam || 24
12.25 . uvācotpaladevaśca śrīmānasmadgurorguruḥ |
12.25 . sarvāśaṅkāśaniṃ mārgaṃ numo māheśvaraṃ tviti || 25
12.26 . anuttarapadāptaye tadidamāṇavaṃ darśitābhyupāyamativistarānnanu vidāṃkurudhvaṃ budhāḥ |

c13 atha śrītantrāloke trayodaśamāhnikam

13.1 . athādhikṛtibhāhanaṃ ka iha vā kathaṃ vetyalaṃ vivecayitumucyate vividhaśaktipātakramaḥ || 1
13.2 . tatra kecidiha prāhuḥ śaktipāta imaṃ vidhim |
13.2 . taṃ pradarśya nirākṛtya svamataṃ darśayiṣyate || 2
13.3 . tatredaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ satsukhaduḥkhavimohabhāk |
13.3 . viṣamaṃ sattathābhūtaṃ samaṃ hetuṃ prakalpayet || 3
13.4 . so'vyaktaṃ tacca sattvādinānārūpamacetanam |
13.4 . ghaṭādivatkāryamiti hetureko'sya sā niśā || 4
13.5 . sā jaḍā kāryatādrūpyātkāryaṃ cāsyāṃ sadeva hi |
13.5 . kalādidharaṇīprāntaṃ jāḍyātsā sūtaye'kṣamā || 5
13.6 . teneśaḥ kṣobhayedenāṃ kṣobho'syāḥ sūtiyogyatā |
13.6 . puṃsaḥ prati ca sā bhogyaṃ sūte'nādīn pṛthagvidhān || 6
13.7 . puṃsaśca nirviśeṣatve muktāṇūn prati kiṃ na tat |
13.7 . nimittaṃ karmasaṃskāraḥ sa ca teṣu na vidyate || 7
13.8 . iti cetkarmasaṃskārābhāvasteṣāṃ kutaḥ kila |
13.8 . na bhogādanyakarmāṃśaprasaṅgo hi duratyayaḥ || 8
13.9 . yugapatkarmaṇāṃ bhogo naca yuktaḥ krameṇa hi |
13.9 . phaledyatkarma tatkasmātsvaṃ rūpaṃ saṃtyajetkvacit || 9
13.10 . jñānātkarmakṣayaścettatkuta īśvaracoditāt |
13.10 . dharmādyadi kutaḥ so'pi karmataścettaducyatām || 10
13.11 . nahi karmāsti tādṛkṣaṃ yena jñānaṃ pravartate |
13.11 . karmajatve ca tajjñānaṃ phalarāśau pateddhruvam || 11
13.12 . anyakarmaphalaṃ prācyaṃ karmarāśiṃ ca kiṃ dahet |
13.12 . īśasya dveṣarāgādiśūnyasyāpi kathaṃ kvacit || 12
13.13 . tathābhisaṃdhirnānyatra bhedahetorabhāvataḥ |
13.13 . nanvitthaṃ pradahejjñānaṃ karmajālāni karma hi || 13
13.14 . ajñānasahakārīdaṃ sūte svargādikaṃ phalam |
13.14 . ajñānaṃ jñānato naśyedanyakarmaphalādapi || 14
13.15 . upavāsādikaṃ cānyadduṣṭakarmaphalaṃ bhavet |
13.15 . niṣphalīkurute duṣṭaṃ karmetyaṅgīkṛtaṃ kila || 15
13.16 . ajñānamiti yatproktaṃ jñānābhāvaḥ sa cetsa kim |
13.16 . prāgabhāvo'thavā dhvaṃsa ādye kiṃ sarvasaṃvidām || 16
13.17 . kasyāpi vātha jñānasya prācyaḥ pakṣastvasaṃbhavī |
13.17 . na kiṃcidyasya vijñānamudapādi tathāvidhaḥ || 17
13.18 . nāṇurasti bhave hyasminnanādau ko'nvayaṃ kramaḥ |
13.18 . bhāvinaḥ prāgabhāvaśca jñānasyeti sthite sati || 18
13.19 . muktāṇorapi so'styeva janmataḥ prāgasau naca |
13.19 . jñānaṃ bhāvi vimukte'sminniti ceccarcyatāmidam || 19
13.20 . kasmājjñānaṃ na bhāvyatra nanu dehādyajanmataḥ |
13.20 . tatkasmātkarmaṇaḥ kṣaiṇyāttatkuto'jñānahānitaḥ || 20
13.21 . ajñānasya kathaṃ hāniḥ prāgabhāve hi saṃvidaḥ |
13.21 . ajñānaṃ prāgabhāvo'sau na bhāvyutpattyasaṃbhavāt || 21
13.22 . kasmānna bhāvi tajjñānaṃ nanu dehādyajanmataḥ |
13.22 . ityeṣa sarvapakṣaghno niśitaścakrakabhramaḥ || 22
13.23 . atha pradhvaṃsa evedamajñānaṃ tatsadā sthitam |
13.23 . muktāṇuṣviti teṣvastu māyākāryavijṛmbhitam || 23
13.24 . athājñānaṃ nahyabhāvo mithyājñānaṃ tu tanmatam |
13.24 . tadeva karmaṇāṃ svasminkartavye sahakāraṇam || 24
13.25 . vaktavyaṃ tarhi kiṃ karma yadā sūte svakaṃ phalam |
13.25 . tadaiva mithyājñānena satā hetutvamāpyate || 25
13.26 . atha yasminkṣaṇe karma kṛtaṃ tatra svarūpasat |
13.26 . mithyājñānaṃ yadi tatastādṛśātkamarṇaḥ phalam || 26
13.27 . prākpakṣe pralaye vṛtte prācyasṛṣṭipravartane |
13.27 . dehādyabhāvānno mithyājñānasya kvāpi saṃbhavaḥ || 27
13.28 . uttarasminpunaḥ pakṣe yadā yadyena yatra vā |
13.28 . kriyate karma tatsarvamajñānasacivaṃ tadā || 28
13.29 . avaśyamiti kasyāpi na karmaprakṣayo bhavet |
13.29 . yadyapi jñānavānbhūtvā vidhatte karma kiṃcana || 29
13.30 . viphalaṃ syāttu tatpūrvakarmarāśau tu kā gatiḥ |
13.30 . atha pralayakāle 'pi citsvabhāvatvayogataḥ || 30
13.31 . aṇūnā saṃbhavatyeva jñānaṃ mithyeti tatkutaḥ |
13.31 . svabhāvāditi cenmukte śive vā kiṃ tathā nahi || 31
13.32 . yaccādarśanamākhyātaṃ nimittaṃ pariṇāmini |
13.32 . pradhāne taddhi saṃkīrṇavaivittayobhayayogataḥ || 32
13.33 . darśanāya pumarthaikayogyatāsacivaṃ dhiyaḥ |
13.33 . ārabhya sate dharaṇīparyantaṃ tatra yaccitaḥ || 33
13.34 . buddhivṛttyaviśiṣṭatvaṃ puṃsprayāśarprasādataḥ |
13.34 . prakāśanāddhiyo'rthena saha bhogaḥ sa bhaṇyate || 34
13.35 . buddhirevāsmi vikṛtidharmikānyastu ko'pyasau |
13.35 . madvilakṣaṇa ekātmetyevaṃ vaivi yasaṃvidi || 35
13.36 . pumarthasya kṛtatvena sahakāriviyogataḥ |
13.36 . taṃ pumāṃsaṃ prati naiva sūte kiṃtvanyameva hi || 36
13.37 . atra puṃso'tha mūlasya dharmo'darśanatā dvayoḥ |
13.37 . athaveti vikalpo'yamāstāmetattu bhaṇyatām || 37
13.38 . bhogo vivekaparyanta iti yattatra ko'vadhiḥ |
13.38 . vivekalābhe nikhilasūtidṛgyadi sāpi kim || 38
13.39 . sāmānyena viśeṣairvā prācye syādekajanmataḥ |
13.39 . uttare na kadācitsyādbhāvikālasya yogataḥ || 39
13.40 . kaiścideva viśeṣaiścetsarveṣāṃ yugapadbhavet |
13.40 . viveko'nādisaṃyogātkā hyanyonyaṃ vicitratā || 40
13.41 . tasmātsāṃkhyadṛśāpīdamajñānaṃ naiva yujyate |
13.41 . ajñānena vinā bandhamokṣau naiva vyavasthayā || 41
13.42 . yujyete tacca kathitayuktibhirnopapadyate |
13.42 . bhāyākarmāṇudevecchāsadbhāve'pi sthite tataḥ || 42
13.43 . na bandhamokṣayoryogo bhedahetorasaṃbhavāt |
13.43 . tasmādajñānaśabdena jñatvakartṛtvadharmaṇām || 43
13.44 . cidaṇūnāmāvaraṇaṃ kiṃcidvācyaṃ vipaścitā |
13.44 . āvāraṇātmanā siddhaṃ tatsvarūpādabhedavat || 44
13.45 . bhede pramāṇābhāvācca tadekaṃ nikhilātmasu |
13.45 . tacca kasmātprasūtaṃ syānmāyātaścetkathaṃ nu sā || 45
13.46 . kvacideva suvītaitanna tu muktātmanītyayam |
13.46 . prācyaḥ paryanuyogaḥ syānnimittaṃ cenna labhyate || 46
13.47 . utpattyabhāvatastena nityaṃ naca vinaśyati |
13.47 . tata evaikatāyāṃ cānyātmasādhāraṇatvataḥ || 47
13.48 . na vāvastvarthakāritvānna cittatsaṃvṛtitvataḥ |
13.48 . na caitenātmanāṃ yogo hetumāṃstadasaṃbhavāt || 48
13.49 . tenaikaṃ vastu sannityaṃ nityasaṃbaddhamātmabhiḥ |
13.49 . jaḍaṃ malaṃ tadajñānaṃ saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam || 49
13.50 . tasya roddhrī yadā śaktirudāste śivaraśmibhiḥ |
13.50 . tadāṇuḥ spṛśyate spṛṣṭaḥ svake jñānakriye sphuṭe || 50
13.51 . samāviśedayaṃ sūryakānto'rkeṇeva coditaḥ |
13.51 . roddhryāśca śaktermādhyasthyatāratamyavaśakramāat || 51
13.52 . vicitratvamataḥ prāhurabhivyaktau svasaṃvidaḥ |
13.52 . sa eṣa śaktipātākhyaḥ śāstreṣu paribhāṣyate || 52
13.53 . atrocyate malastāvaditthameṣa na yujyate |
13.53 . iti pūrvāhṇike proktaṃ punaruktau tu kiṃ phalam || 53
13.54 . malasya pākaḥ ko 'yaṃ syānnāśaśceditarātmanām |
13.54 . sa eko mala ityukternairmalyamanuṣajyate || 54
13.55 . atha pratyātmaniyato'nādiśca prāgabhāvavat |
13.55 . malo naśyettathāpyeṣa nāśo yadi sahetukaḥ || 55
13.56 . hetuḥ karmeśvarecchā vā karma tāvanna tādṛśam |
13.56 . īśvarecchā svatantrā ca kvacideva tathaiva kim || 56
13.57 . ahetuko'sya nāśaścetprāgevaiṣa vinaśyatu |
13.57 . kṣaṇāntaraṃ sadṛksūte iti cetsthirataiva sā || 57
13.58 . na ca nityasya bhāvasya hetvanāyattajanmanaḥ |
13.58 . nāśo dṛṣṭaḥ prāgabhāvastvavastviti tathāstu saḥ || 58
13.59 . athāsya pāko nāmaiṣa svaśaktipratibaddhatā |
13.59 . sarvānprati tathaiṣa syādruddhaśaktirviṣāgnivat || 59
13.60 . punarudbhūtaśaktau ca svakāryaṃ syādviṣāgnivat |
13.60 . muktā api na muktāḥ syuḥ śaktiṃ cāsya na manmahe || 60
13.61 . roddhrīti cetkasya nṛṇāṃ jñatvakartṛtvayoryadi |
13.61 . sadbhāvamātrādroddhṛtve śivamuktāṇvasaṃbhavaḥ || 61
13.62 . saṃnidhānātiriktaṃ ca na kiṃcitkurute malaḥ |
13.62 . ātmanā pariṇāmitvādanityatvaprasaṅgataḥ || 62
13.63 . jñatvakartṛtvamātraṃ ca pudgalā na tadāśrayāḥ |
13.63 . taccedāvāritaṃ hanta rūpanāśaḥ prasajyate || 63
13.64 . āvāraṇaṃ cādṛśyatvaṃ na ca tadvastuno'nyatām |
13.64 . karoti ghaṭavajjñānaṃ nāvarītuṃ ca śakyate || 64
13.65 . jñānenāvaraṇīyena tadevāvaraṇaṃ katham |
13.65 . na jñāyate tathā ca syādāvṛtirnāmamātrataḥ || 65
13.66 . roddhryāśca śakteḥ kastasya pratibandhaka īśvaraḥ |
13.66 . yadyapekṣāvirahitastatra prāgdattamuttaram || 66
13.67 . karmasāmyamapekṣyātha tasyecchā saṃpravartate |
13.67 . tasyāpi rūpaṃ vaktavyaṃ samatā karmaṇāṃ hi kā || 67
13.68 . bhogaparyāyamāhātmyātkāle kvāpi phalaṃ prati |
13.68 . virodhātkarmaṇī ruddhe tiṣṭhataḥ sāmyamīdṛśam || 68
13.69 . taṃ ca kālāṃśakaṃ devaḥ sarvajño vīkṣya taṃ malam |
13.69 . runddhe lakṣyaḥ sa kālaśca sukhaduḥkhādivarjanaiḥ || 69
13.70 . naitatkramikasaṃśuddhavyāmiśrākārakarmabhiḥ |
13.70 . tathaiva deye svaphale keyamanyonyaroddhṛtā || 70
13.71 . rodhe tayośca jātyāyurapi na syādataḥ patet |
13.71 . deho bhogadayoreva nirodha iti cennanu || 71
13.72 . jātyāyuṣpradakarmāṃśasaṃnidhau yadi śaṃkaraḥ |
13.72 . malaṃ runddhe bhogadātuḥ karmaṇaḥ kiṃ bibheti saḥ || 72
13.73 . śataśo'pi hlādatāpaśūnyāṃ saṃcinvate daśām |
13.73 . na ca bhaktirasāveśamiti bhūmnā vilokitam || 73
13.74 . athāpi kālamāhātmyamapekṣya parameśvaraḥ |
13.74 . tathā karoti vaktavyaṃ kālo'sau kīdṛśastviti || 74
13.75 . kiṃ cānādirayaṃ bhogaḥ karmānādi sapudgalam |
13.75 . tataśca bhogaparyāyakālaḥ sarvasya niḥsamaḥ || 75
13.76 . ādimattve hi kasyāpi vargādasmādbhavediyam |
13.76 . vaicitrī bhuktametena kalpametena tu dvayam || 76
13.77 . iyato bhogaparyāyātsyātsāmyaṃ karmaṇāmiti |
13.77 . anena nayabījena manye vaicitryakāraṇam || 77
13.78 . jagataḥ karma yatklaptaṃ tattathā nāvakalpate |
13.78 . anādimalasaṃcchannā aṇavo dṛkkriyātmanā || 78
13.79 . sarve tulyāḥ kathaṃ citrāṃ śritāḥ karmaparamparām |
13.79 . bhogalolikayā cetsā vicitreti kuto nanu || 79
13.80 . anādi karmasaṃskāravaicitryāditi cetpunaḥ |
13.80 . vācyaṃ tadeva vaicitryaṃ kuto niyatirāgayoḥ || 80
13.81 . mahimā cedayaṃ tau kiṃ nāsamañjasyabhāginau |
13.81 . īśvarecchānapekṣā tu bhedaheturna kalpate || 81
13.82 . athānāditvamātreṇa yuktihīnena sādhyate |
13.82 . vyavastheyamalaṃ tarhi malenāstu vṛthāmunā || 82
13.83 . tathāhi karma tāvanno yāvanmāyā na pudgale |
13.83 . vyāpriyeta na cāhetustadvṛttistanmito malaḥ || 83
13.84 . itthaṃ ca kalpite māyākārye karmaṇi hetutām |
13.84 . anādi karma cedgacchetkiṃ malasyopakalpanam || 84
13.85 . nanu mābhūnmalastarhi citrākāreṣu karmasu |
13.85 . santatyāvartamāneṣu vyavasthā na prakalpate || 85
13.86 . ādau madhye ca citratvātkarmaṇāṃ na yathā samaḥ |
13.86 . ātmākāro'pi ko'pyeṣa bhāvikāle tathā bhavet || 86
13.87 . itthamucchinna evāyaṃ bandhamokṣādikaḥ kramaḥ |
13.87 . ajñānādbandhanaṃ mokṣo jñānāditi parīkṣitam || 87
13.88 . virodhe svaphale caite karmaṇī samaye kvacit |
13.88 . udāsāte yadi tataḥ karmaitatpratibudhyatām || 88
13.89 . śivaśaktinipātasya ko'vakāśastu tāvatā |
13.89 . kvāpi kāle tayoretadaudāsīnyaṃ yadā tataḥ || 89
13.90 . kālāntare tayostadvadvirodhasyānivṛttitaḥ |
13.90 . ataśca na phaletānte tābhyāṃ karmāntaraṇi ca || 90
13.91 . ruddhāni prāptakālatvādgatābhyāmupabhogyatām |
13.91 . evaṃ sadaiva vārtāyāṃ dehapāte tathaiva ca || 91
13.92 . jāte vimokṣa ityāstāṃ śaktipātādikalpanā |
13.92 . athodāsīnatatkarmadvayayogakṣaṇāntare || 92
13.93 . karmāntaraṃ phalaṃ sūte tatkṣaṇe'pi tathā na kim |
13.93 . kṣaṇāntare'tha te eva pratibandhavivarjite || 93
13.94 . phalataḥ pratibandhasya varjanaṃ kiṃkṛtaṃ tayoḥ |

13.97 . etenānye'pi ye'pekṣyā īśecchāyāṃ prakalpitāḥ |

13.98 . vairāgyaṃ bhogavairasyaṃ dharmaḥ ko'pi vivekitā |

13.99 . āpatprāptistannirīkṣā dehe kiṃcicca lakṣaṇam |

13.100 . ityapekṣyaṃ yadīśasya dūṣyametacca pūrvavat |

13.102 . itthaṃ bhrāntiviṣāveśamūrcchānirmokadāyinīm |

13.103 . devaḥ svatantraścidrūpaḥ prakāśātmā svabhāvataḥ |
13.103 . rūpapracchādanakrīḍāyogādaṇuranekakaḥ || 103
13.104 . sa svayaṃ kalpitākāravikalpātmakakarmabhiḥ |
13.104 . badhnātyātmānameveha svātantryāditi varṇitam || 104
13.105 . svātantryamahimaivāyaṃ devasya yadasau punaḥ |

13.106 . na vācyaṃ tu kathaṃ nāma kasmiṃścitpuṃsyasau tathā |
13.106 . nahi nāma pumānkaścidyasminparyanuyujyate || 106
13.107 . deva eva tathāsau cetsvarūpaṃ cāsya tādṛśam |

13.108 . āhāsmatparameṣṭhī ca śivadṛṣṭau gurūttamaḥ |
13.108 . pañcaprakārakṛtyoktiśivatvānnijakarmaṇe || 108
13.109 . pravṛttasya nimittānāmapareṣāṃ kva mārgaṇam |
13.109 . channasvarūpatābhāse puṃsi yadyādṛśaṃ phalam || 109
13.110 . tatrāṇoḥ sata evāsti svātantryaṃ karmatohi tat |

13.119 . tato'tra syātphale bhedo nāpavarge tvasau tathā |

13.121 . sthitvā mantrādi saṃgṛhya tyajetso'sya tirobhavaḥ |

13.122 . dharmādharmātmakairbhāvairanekairveṣṭayetsvayam |
13.122 . asandehaṃ svamātmānamavīcyādiśivāntake || 122
13.123 . tadvacchaktisamūhena sa eva tu viveṣṭayet |

13.124 . svayaṃ bhoktā svayaṃ jñātā svayaṃ caivopalakṣayet |
13.124 . svayaṃ bhuktiśca muktiśca svayaṃ devī svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 124
13.125 . svayamekākṣarā caiva yathoṣmā kṛṣṇavartmanaḥ |

13.126 . malamāyāvicāreṇa kliśyante svalpabuddhayaḥ || 126
13.127 . sphaṭikopalago reṇuḥ kiṃ tasya kurutāṃ priye |
13.127 . vyomnīva nīlaṃ hi malaṃ malaśaṃkāṃ tatastyajet || 127
13.128 . śrīmānvidyāguruścāha pramāṇastutidarśane |

13.129 . taṃ svecchātaḥ saṃgiramāṇāḥ stavakādyāḥ svātantryaṃ tattvayyanapekṣaṃ kathayeyuḥ |
13.129 . tāratamyaprakāśo yastīvramadhyamamandatāḥ || 129
13.130 . tā eva śaktipātasya pratyekaṃ traidhamāsthitāḥ |

13.134 . sa śiṣṭaḥ karmakartṛtvācchiṣyo'nyaḥ karmabhāvataḥ |

13.135 . uktaḥ svayaṃbhūḥ śāstrārthapratibhāpariniṣṭhitaḥ |

13.136 . tatrāpi tāratamyotthamānantyaṃ dārḍhyakamprate |

13.137 . kampamānaṃ hi vijñānaṃ svayameva punarvrajet |
13.137 . kasyāpi dārḍhyamanyasya yuktyādyaiḥ kevaletaraiḥ || 137
13.138 . yathā yathā parāpekṣātānavaṃ prātibhe bhavet |

13.139 . anyataḥ śikṣitānantajñāno'pi pratibhābalāt |
13.139 . yadvetti tatra tatrāsya śivatā jyāyasī ca sā || 139
13.140 . na cāsya samayitvādikramo nāpyabhiṣecanam |

13.141 . ādividvānmahādevastenaiṣo'dhiṣṭhito yataḥ |
13.141 . saṃskārāstadadhiṣṭhānasiddhyai tattasya tu svataḥ || 141
13.142 . devībhirdīkṣitastena sabhaktiḥ śivaśāsane |
13.142 . dṛḍhatākampratābhedaiḥ so'pi svayamatha vratāt || 142
13.143 . tapojapādergurutaḥ svasaṃskāraṃ prakalpayet |
13.143 . yato vājasineyākhya uktaṃ siñcetsvayaṃ tanum || 143
13.144 . ityādyupakramaṃ yāvadante tatpariniṣṭhitam |
13.144 . abhiṣikto bhavedevaṃ na bāhyakalaśāmbubhiḥ || 144
13.145 . śrīsarvavīraśrībrahmayāmalādau ca tattathā |
13.145 . nirūpitaṃ maheśena kiyadvā likhyatāmidam || 145
13.146 . itthaṃ prātibhavijñānaṃ kiṃ kiṃ kasya na sādhayet |
13.146 . yatprātibhādvā sarvaṃ cetyūce śeṣamahāmuniḥ || 146
13.147 . anye tvāhurakāmasya prātibho gururīdṛśaḥ |
13.147 . sāmagrījanyatā kāmye tenārimansaṃskṛto guruḥ || 147
13.148 . niyatermahimā naiva phale sādhye nivartate |
13.148 . abhiṣiktaścīrṇavidyāvratastena phalapradaḥ || 148
13.149 . asadetaditi prāhurguravastattvadarśinaḥ |

13.150 . tathāhi trīśikāśāstravivṛtau te'bhyadhurbudhāḥ |

13.151 . tadabhāve tadarthaṃ tadāhṛtaṃ jñānamādṛtam |

13.152 . dīkṣā bhavatyasandigdhā tilājyāhutivarjitā |

13.153 . sa siddhibhāgbhavennityaṃ sa yogī sa ca dīkṣitaḥ |

13.154 . ityādibhistrīśikoktairvākyairmāheśvaraiḥ sphuṭam |
13.154 . jñānaṃ dīkṣādisaṃskārasatattvamiti varṇitam || 154
13.155 . jñānopāyastu dīkṣādikriyā jñānaviyoginām |
13.155 . ityetadadhunaivāstā svaprastāve bhaviṣyati || 155
13.156 . guruśāstrapramāṇāderapyupāyatvamaṃjasā |

13.157 . upāyayogakramato nirupāyamathākramam |

13.158 . yastu prātibhabāhyātmasaṃskāradvayasundaraḥ |

13.159 . svamuktimātre kasyāpi yāvadviśvavimocane |

13.160 . tataḥ prātibhasaṃvittyai śāstramasmatkṛtaṃ tvidam |

13.161 . paropajīvitābuddhyā sarva itthaṃ na bhāsate |

13.162 . sphuṭametacca śāstreṣu teṣu teṣu nirūpyate |

13.163 . jñānayogyāstathā keciccaryāyogyāstathāpare |

13.172 . punarvivekāduktaṃ taduttarottaramucyatām |

13.175 . vikāsaṃ tattvamāyāti prātibhaṃ tadudāhṛtam |

13.178 . prātibhe tu samāyāte jñānamanyattu sendriyam |

13.182 . svacchāyādarśavatpaśyedbahirantargataṃ śivam |

13.183 . siddhijālaṃ hi kathitaṃ parapratyayakāraṇam |

13.187 . tatpātāveśato muktaḥ śiva eva bhavārṇavāt |

13.188 . vivekajaṃ ca tadbuddhyā tatkathaṃ syānnirindriyam |

13.189 . akṣatvaṃ pravivekena tacchittau bhāsakaḥ śivaḥ |

13.190 . manobuddhī na bhinne tu kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare |
13.190 . viveke kāraṇe hyete prabhuśaktyupavṛṃhite || 190
13.191 . na manobuddhihīnastu jñānasyādhigamaḥ priye |

13.192 . vivekaḥ sarvabhāvānāṃ śuddhabhāvānmahāśayaḥ |

13.193 . aṇimādigataṃ cāpi bandhakaṃ jaḍamindriyam |
13.193 . nanu prātibhato muktau dīkṣayā kiṃ śivādhvare || 193
13.194 . ūce'jñānā hi dīkṣāyāṃ bālavāliśayoṣitaḥ |
13.194 . pāśacchedādvimucyante prabuddhyante śivādhvare || 194
13.195 . tasmāddīkṣā bhavatyeṣu kāraṇatvena sundari |
13.195 . dīkṣayā pāśamokṣe tu śuddhabhāvādvivekajam || 195
13.196 . ityeṣa paṭhito granthaḥ svayaṃ ye boddhumakṣamāḥ |
13.196 . teṣāṃ śivoktisaṃvādādbodho dārḍhyaṃ vrajediti || 196
13.197 . śrīmanniśāṭane cātmaguruśāstravaśāttridhā |
13.197 . jñānaṃ mukhyaṃ svopalabdhi vikalpārṇavatāraṇam || 197
13.198 . mantrātmabhūtadravyāśadivyatattvādigocarā |
13.198 . śaṃkā vikalpamūlā hi śāmyetsvapratyayāditi || 198
13.199 . enamevārthamantaḥsthaṃ gṛhītvā mālinīmate |
13.199 . evamasyātmanaḥ kāle kasmiṃścidyogyatāvaśāt || 199
13.200 . śaivī saṃbadhyate śaktiḥ śāntā muktiphalapradā |

13.201 . ajñānena sahaikatvaṃ kasyacidvinivartate || 201

13.203 . tamārādhya tatastuṣṭāddīkṣāmāsādya śāṅkarīm |

13.204 . asyārthā ātmanaḥ kācitkalanāmarśanātmikā |
13.204 . svaṃ rūpaṃ prati yā saiva ko'pi kāla ihoditaḥ || 204
13.205 . yogyatā śivatādātmyayogārhatvamihocyate |
13.205 . pūrvaṃ kiṃ na tathā kasmāttadaiveti na saṃgatam || 205
13.206 . tathābhāsanamujjhitvā na hi kālo'sti kaścana |
13.206 . svātantryāttu tathābhāse kālaśaktirvijṛmbhatām || 206
13.207 . natu paryanuyuktyai sā śive tanmahimoditā |

13.207 . satyaṃ sācchādanātmā tu śāntā tveṣā svarūpadṛk || 207
13.208 . kṣobho hi bheda evaikyaṃ praśamastanmayī tataḥ || 208
13.209 . tayā śāntyā tu saṃbaddhaḥ sthitaḥ śaktisvarūpabhāk |

13.210 . tatrāpi tāratamyādivaśācchīghracirāditaḥ |
13.210 . dehapāto bhavedasya yadvā kāṣṭhāditulyatā || 210
13.211 . samastavyavahāreṣu parācīnitacetanaḥ |

13.212 . evaṃ prāgviṣayo grantha iyānanyatra tu sphuṭam |
13.212 . granthāntaraṃ madhyatīvraśaktipātāṃśasūcakam || 212
13.213 . ajñānarūpatā puṃsi bodhaḥ saṃkocite hṛdi |
13.213 . saṃkoce vinivṛtte tu svasvabhāvaḥ prakāśate || 213
13.214 . rudraśaktisamāviṣṭa ityanenāsya varṇyate |
13.214 . cihnavargo ya ukto'tra rudre bhaktiḥ suniścalā || 214
13.215 . mantrasiddhiḥ sarvatattvavaśitvaṃ kṛtyasaṃpadaḥ |
13.215 . kavitvaṃ sarvaśāstrārthaboddhṛtvamiti tatkramāt || 215
13.216 . svatāratamyayogātsyādeṣāṃ vyastasamastatā |
13.216 . tatrāpi bhuktau muktau ca prādhānyaṃ carcayedbudhaḥ || 216
13.217 . sa ityanto grantha eṣa dvitīyaviṣayaḥ sphuṭaḥ |
13.217 . anyastu mandatīvrākhyaśaktipātavidhiṃ prati || 217
13.218 . mandatīvrācchaktibalādyiyāsāsyopajāyate |
13.218 . śivecchāvaśayogena sadguruṃ prati so'pi ca || 218
13.219 . atraiva lakṣitaḥ śāstre yaduktaṃ parameṣṭhinā |
13.219 . yaḥ punaḥ sarvatattvāni vettyetāni yathārthataḥ || 219
13.220 . sa gururmatsamaḥ prokto mantravīryaprakāśakaḥ |
13.220 . dṛṣṭāḥ saṃbhāvitāstena spṛṣṭāśca prītacetasā || 220
13.221 . narāḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyante saptajanmakṛtairapi |
13.221 . ye punardīkṣitāstena prāṇinaḥ śivacoditāḥ || 221
13.222 . te yatheṣṭaṃ phalaṃ prāpya padaṃ gacchantyanāmayam |
13.222 . kiṃ tattvaṃ tattvavedī ka ityāmarśanayogataḥ || 222
13.223 . pratibhānātsuḥṛtsaṅgādgurau jigamiṣurbhavet |
13.223 . evaṃ jigamiṣāyogādācāryaḥ prāpyate sa ca || 223
13.224 . tāratamyādiyogena saṃsiddhaḥ saṃskṛto'pi ca |
13.224 . prāgbhedabhāgī jhaṭiti kramātsāmastyatoṃśataḥ || 224
13.225 . ityādibhedabhinno hi gurorlābha ihoditaḥ |
13.225 . tasmāddīkṣāṃ sa labhate sadya eva śivapradām || 225
13.226 . jñānarūpāṃ yathā vetti sarvameva yathārthataḥ |
13.226 . jīvanmuktaḥ śivībhūtastadaivāsau nigadyate || 226
13.227 . dehasaṃbandhitāpyasya śivatāyai yataḥ sphuṭā |
13.227 . asyāṃ bhedo hi kathanātsaṅgamādavalokanāt || 227
13.228 . śāstrātsaṃkramaṇātsāmyacaryāsaṃdarśanāccaroḥ |
13.228 . mantramudrādimāhātmyātsamastavyastabhedataḥ || 228
13.229 . kriyayā vāntarākārarūpaprāṇapraveśataḥ |
13.229 . tadā ca dehasaṃstho'pi sa mukta iti bhaṇyate || 229
13.230 . uktaṃ ca śāstrayoḥ śrīmadratnamālāgamākhyayoḥ |
13.230 . yasminkāle tu guruṇā nirvikalpaṃ prakāśitam || 230
13.231 . tadaiva kila mukto'sau yantraṃ tiṣṭhati kevalam |
13.231 . prārabdhṛkarmasaṃbandhāddehasya sukhiduḥkhite || 231
13.232 . na viśaṅketa tacca śrīgamaśāstre nirūpitam |
13.232 . avidyopāsito deho hyanyajanmasamudbhuvā || 232
13.233 . karmaṇā tena bādhyante jñānino'pi kalevare |
13.233 . jātyāyurbhogadasyaikapraghaṭṭakatayā sthitiḥ || 233
13.234 . uktaikavacanāddhiśca yatastenetisaṃgatiḥ |
13.234 . abhyāsayuktisaṃkrāntivedhaghaṭṭanarodhataḥ || 234
13.235 . hutervā mantrasāmarthyātpāśacchedaprayogataḥ |
13.235 . sadyonirvāṇadāṃ kuryātsadyaḥprāṇaviyojikām || 235
13.236 . tatra tveṣo'sti niyama āsanne maraṇakṣaṇe |
13.236 . tāṃ kuryānnānyathārabdhṛ karma yasmānna śuddhyati || 236
13.237 . uktaṃ ca pūrvamevaitanmaṃtrasāmarthyayogataḥ |
13.237 . prāṇairviyojito'pyeṣa bhuṅkte śeṣaphalaṃ yataḥ || 237
13.238 . tajjanmaśeṣaṃ vividhamativāhya tataḥ sphuṭam |
13.238 . karmāntaranirodhena śīghramevāpavṛjyate || 238
13.239 . tasmātprāṇaharīṃ dīkṣāṃ nājñātvā maraṇakṣaṇam |
13.239 . vidadhyātparameśājñālaṅghanaikaphalā hi sā || 239
13.240 . ekastriko'yaṃ nirṇītaḥ śaktipāte'pyathāparaḥ |
13.240 . tīvramadhye tu dīkṣāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ na tathā dṛḍhām || 240
13.241 . svātmano vetti śivatāṃ dehānte tu śivo bhavet |
13.241 . uktaṃ ca niśisaṃcārayogasaṃcāraśāstrayoḥ || 241
13.242 . vikalpāttu tanau sthitvā dahānte śivatāṃ vrajet |
13.242 . madhyamadhye śaktipāte śivalābhotsuko'pi san || 242
13.243 . bubhukṣuryatra yuktastadbhuktvā dehakṣaye śivaḥ |
13.243 . mandamadhye tu tatraiva tattve kvāpi niyojitaḥ || 243
13.244 . dehānte tattvagaṃ bhogaṃ bhuktvā paścācchivaṃ vrajet |
13.244 . tatrāpi tāratamyasya saṃbhavācciraśīghratā || 244
13.245 . bahvalpabhogayogaśca dehabhūmālpatākramaḥ |
13.245 . tīvramande madhyamande mandamande bubhukṣutā || 245
13.246 . kramānmukhyātimātreṇa vidhinaityantataḥ śivam |
13.246 . anye yiyāsurityādigranthaṃ prāggranthasaṃgatam || 246
13.247 . kurvanti madhyatīvrākhyaśaktisaṃpātagocaram |
13.247 . yadā pratibhayāviṣṭo'pyeṣa saṃvādayojanām || 247
13.248 . icchanyiyāsurbhavati tadā nīyeta sadgurum |
13.248 . na sarvaḥ pratibhāviṣṭaḥ śaktyā nīyeta sadgurum || 248
13.249 . iti brūte yiyāsutvaṃ vaktavyaṃ nānyathā dhruvam |
13.249 . rudraśaktisamāviṣṭo nīyate sadguruṃ prati || 249
13.250 . tena prāptavivekotthajñānasaṃpūrṇamānasaḥ |
13.250 . dārḍhyasaṃvādarūḍhyāderyiyāsurbhavati sphuṭam || 250
13.251 . uktaṃ nandiśikhātantre prācyaṣaṭke maheśinā |
13.251 . abhilāṣaḥ śive deve paśūnāṃ bhavate tadā || 251
13.252 . yadā śaivābhimānena yuktā vai paramāṇavaḥ |
13.252 . tadaiva te vimuktāstu dīkṣitā guruṇā yataḥ || 252
13.253 . prāptimātrācca te siddhasādhyā iti hi gamyate |
13.253 . tamārādhyeti tu grantho mandatīvraikagocaraḥ || 253
13.254 . navadhā śaktipāto'yaṃ śaṃbhunāthena varṇitaḥ |
13.254 . idaṃ sāramiha jñeyaṃ paripūrṇacidātmanaḥ || 254
13.255 . prakāśaḥ paramaḥ śaktipāto'vacchedavarjitaḥ |
13.255 . tathāvidho'pi bhogāṃśāvacchedenopalakṣitaḥ || 255
13.256 . aparaḥ śaktipāto'sau paryante śivatāpradaḥ |
13.256 . ubhayatrāpi karmādermāyāntarvartino yataḥ || 256
13.257 . nāsti vyāpāra ityevaṃ nirapekṣaḥ sa sarvataḥ |
13.257 . tena māyāntarāle ye rudrā ye ca tadūrdhvataḥ || 257
13.258 . svādhikārakṣaye taistairbhairavībhūyate haṭhāt |
13.258 . ye māyayā hyanākrāntāste karmādyanapekṣiṇaḥ || 258
13.259 . śaktipātavaśādeva tāṃ tāṃ siddhimupāśritāḥ |
13.259 . nanu pūjājapadhyānaśaṃkarāsevanādibhiḥ || 259
13.260 . te mantrāditvamāpannāḥ kathaṃ karmānapekṣiṇaḥ |
13.260 . maivaṃ tathāvidhottīrṇaśivadhyānajapādiṣu || 260
13.261 . pravṛttireva prathamameṣāṃ kasmādvivicyatām |
13.261 . karmatatsāmyavairāgyamalapākādi dūṣitam || 261
13.262 . īśvarecchā nimittaṃ cecchaktipātaikahetutā |
13.262 . japādikā kriyāśaktirevetthaṃ natu karma tat || 262
13.263 . karma tallokarūḍhaṃ hi yadbhogamavaraṃ dadat |
13.263 . tirodhatte bhoktṛrūpaṃ saṃjñāyā tu na no bharaḥ || 263
13.264 . teṣāṃ bhogotkatā kasmāditi ceddattamuttaram |
13.264 . citrākāraprakāśo'yaṃ svatantraḥ parameśvaraḥ || 264
13.265 . svātantryāttu tirobhāvabandho bhoge'sya bhoktṛtām |
13.265 . puṣṇansvaṃ rūpameva syānmalakarmādivarjitam || 265
13.266 . uktaṃ seyaṃ kriyāśaktiḥ śivasya paśuvartinī |
13.266 . bandhayitrīti tatkarma kathyate rūpalopakṛt || 266
13.267 . jñātā sā ca kriyāśaktiḥ sadyaḥ siddhyupapādikā |
13.267 . avicchinnasvātmasaṃvitprathā siddhirihocyate || 267
13.268 . sā bhogamokṣasvātantryamahālakṣmīrihākṣayā |
13.268 . viṣṇvādirūpatā deve yā kācitsā nijātmanā || 268
13.269 . bhedayogavaśānmāyāpadamadhyavyavasthitā |
13.269 . tena tadrūpatāyogācchaktipātaḥ sthito'pi san || 269
13.270 . tāvantaṃ bhogamādhatte paryante śivatāṃ natu |
13.270 . yathā svātantryato rājāpyanugṛhṇāti kaṃcana || 270
13.271 . īśaśaktisamāveśāttathā viṣṇvādayo'pyalam |
13.271 . māyāgarbhādhikārīyaśaktipātavaśāttataḥ || 271
13.272 . ko'pi pradhānapuruṣavivekī prakṛtergataḥ |
13.272 . utkṛṣṭāttata evāśu ko'pi buddhā vivekitām || 272
13.273 . kṣaṇātpuṃsaḥ kalāyāśca puṃmāyāntaravedakaḥ |
13.273 . kalāśrayasyāpyatyantaṃ karmaṇo vinivartanāt || 273
13.274 . jñānākalaḥ prāktanastu karmī tasyāśrayasthiteḥ |
13.274 . sa paraṃ prakṛterbudhne sṛṣṭiṃ nāyāti jātucit || 274
13.275 . māyādhare tu sṛjyetānanteśena pracodanāt |
13.275 . vijñānākalatāṃ prāptaḥ kevalādadhikārataḥ || 275
13.276 . malānmantratadīśādibhāvameti sadā śivāt |
13.276 . patyuḥ parasmādyastveṣa śaktipātaḥ sa vai malāt || 276
13.277 . ajñānākhyādviyokteti śivabhāvaprakāśakaḥ |
13.277 . nānyena śivabhāvo hi kenacitsaṃprakāśate || 277
13.278 . svacchandaśāstre tenoktaṃ vādināṃ tu śatatrayam |
13.278 . triṣaṣṭyabhyadhikaṃ bhrāntaṃ vaiṣṇavādyaṃ niśāntare || 278
13.279 . śivajñānaṃ kevalaṃ ca śivatāpattidāyakam |
13.279 . śivatāpattiparyantaḥ śaktipātaśca carcyate || 279
13.280 . anyathā kiṃ hi tatsyādyacchaivyā śaktyānadhiṣṭhitam |
13.280 . teneha vaiṣṇavādīnāṃ nādhikāraḥ kathaṃcana || 280
13.281 . te hi bhedaikavṛttitvādabhede dūravarjitāḥ |
13.281 . svātantryāttu maheśasya te'pi cecchivatonmukhāḥ || 281
13.282 . dviguṇā saṃskriyāstyeṣāṃ liṅgoddhṛtyātha dīkṣayā |
13.282 . duṣṭādhivāsavigame puṣpaiḥ kumbho'dhivāsyate || 282
13.283 . dviguṇo'sya sa saṃskāro netthaṃ śuddhe ghaṭe vidhiḥ |
13.283 . itthaṃ śrīśaktipāto'yaṃ nirapekṣa ihoditaḥ || 283
13.284 . anayaiva diśā neyaṃ mataṅgakiraṇādikam |
13.284 . granthagauravabhītyā tu tallikhitvā na yojitam || 284
13.285 . purāṇe'pi ca tasyaiva prasādādbhaktiriṣyate |
13.285 . yayā yānti parāṃ siddhiṃ tadbhāvagatamānasāḥ || 285
13.286 . evakāreṇa karmādisāpekṣatvaṃ niṣidhyate |
13.286 . prasādo nirmalībhāvastena saṃpūrṇarūpatā || 286
13.287 . ātmanā tena hi śivaḥ svayaṃ pūrṇaḥ prakāśate |
13.287 . śivībhāvamahāsiddhisparśavandhye tu kutracit || 287
13.288 . vaiṣṇavādau hi yā bhaktirnāsau kevalataḥ śivāt |
13.288 . śivo bhavati tatraiṣa kāraṇaṃ na tu kevalaḥ || 288
13.289 . nirmalaścāpi tu prāptāvacchitkarmādyapekṣakaḥ |
13.289 . yayā yānti parāṃ siddhimityasyedaṃ tu jīvitam || 289
13.290 . śrīmānutpaladevaścāpyasmākaṃ paramo guruḥ |
13.290 . śaktipātasamaye vicāraṇaṃ prāptamīśa na karoṣi karhicit || 290
13.291 . adya māṃ prati kimāgataṃ yataḥ svaprakāśanavidhau vilambase |
13.291 . karhicitprāptaśabdābhyāmanapekṣitvamūcivān || 291
13.292 . durlabhatvamarāgitvaṃ śaktipātavidhau vibhoḥ |
13.292 . aparārdhena tasyaiva śaktipātasya citratām || 292
13.293 . vyavadhānacirakṣiprabhedādyairupavarṇitaiḥ |
13.293 . śrīmatāpyaniruddhena śaktimunmīlinīṃ vibhoḥ || 293
13.294 . vyācakṣāṇena mātaṅge varṇitā nirapekṣatā |
13.294 . sthāvarānte'pi devasya svarūponmīlanātmikā || 294
13.295 . śaktiḥ patantī sāpekṣā na kvāpīti suvistarāt |
13.295 . evaṃ vicitre'pyetasmiñchaktipāte sthite sati || 295
13.296 . tāratamyādibhirbhedaiḥ samayyādivicitratā |
13.296 . kaścidrudrāśatāmātrāpādanāttatprasādataḥ || 296
13.297 . śivatvaṃ kramaśo gacchetsamayī yo nirūpyate |
13.297 . kaścicchuddhādhvabandhaḥ san putrakaḥ śīghramakramāt || 297
13.298 . bhogavyavadhinā ko'pi sādhakaściraśīghrataḥ |
13.298 . kaścitsaṃpūrṇakartavyaḥ kṛtyapañcakabhāgini || 298
13.299 . rūpe sthito guruḥ so'pi bhogamokṣādibhedabhāk |
13.299 . samayyādicatuṣkasya samāsavyāsayogataḥ || 299
13.300 . kramākramādibhirbhedaiḥ śaktipātasya citratā |
13.300 . kramikaḥ śaktipātaśca siddhānte vāmake tataḥ || 300
13.301 . dakṣe mate kule kaule ṣaḍardhe hṛdaye tataḥ |
13.301 . ullaṅghanavaśādvāpi jhaṭityakramameva vā || 301
13.302 . uktaṃ śrībhairavakule pañcadīkṣāsusaṃskṛtaḥ |
13.302 . gururullaṅghitādhaḥsthasrotā vai trikaśāstragaḥ || 302
13.303 . jñānācārādibhedena hyuttarādharatā vibhuḥ |
13.303 . śāstreṣvadīdṛśacchrīmatsarvācārahṛdādiṣu || 303
13.304 . vāmamārgābhiṣiktastu daiśikaḥ paratattvavit |
13.304 . tathāpi bhairave tantre punaḥ saṃskāramarhati || 304
13.305 . śaivavaimalasiddhāntā ārhatāḥ kārukāśca ye |
13.305 . sarve te paśavo jñeyā bhairave mātṛmaṇḍale || 305
13.306 . kulakālīvidhau coktaṃ vaiṣṇavānā viśeṣataḥ |
13.306 . bhasmaniṣṭhāprapannānāmityādau naiva yogyatā || 306
13.307 . svacchandaśāstre saṃkṣepāduktaṃ ca śrīmaheśinā |
13.307 . anyaśāstrarato yastu nāsau siddhiphalapradaḥ || 307
13.308 . samayyādikramāllabdhābhiṣeko hi gururmataḥ |
13.308 . sa ca śaktivaśāditthaṃ vaiṣṇavādiṣu ko'nvayaḥ || 308
13.309 . chadmāpaśravaṇādyaistu tajjñānaṃ gṛhṇato bhavet |
13.309 . prāyaścittamatastādṛgadhikāryatra kiṃ bhavet || 309
13.310 . phalākāṅkṣāyutaḥ śiṣyastadekāyattasiddhikaḥ |
13.310 . dhruvaṃ pacyeta narake prāyaścittyupasevanāt || 310
13.311 . tirobhāvaprakāro'yaṃ yattādṛśi niyojitaḥ |
13.311 . gurau śivena tadbhaktiḥ śaktipāto'sya nocyate || 311
13.312 . yadātu vaicitryavaśājjānīyāttasya tādṛśam |
13.312 . viparītapravṛttatvaṃ jñānaṃ tasmādupāharet || 312
13.313 . taṃ ca tyajetpāpavṛttiṃ bhavettu jñānatatparaḥ |
13.313 . yathā caurādgṛhītvārthaṃ taṃ nigṛhṇāti bhūpatiḥ || 313
13.314 . vaiṣṇavādestathā śaivaṃ jñānamāhṛtya sanmatiḥ |
13.314 . sa hi bhedaikavṛttitvaṃ śivajñāne śrute'pyalam || 314
13.315 . nojjhatīti dṛḍhaṃ vāmādhiṣṭhitastatpaśūttamaḥ |
13.315 . śivenaiva tirobhāvya sthāpito niyaterbalāt || 315
13.316 . kathaṅkāraṃ patipadaṃ prayātu paratantritaḥ |
13.316 . svacchandaśāstre proktaṃ ca vaiṣṇavādiṣu ye ratāḥ || 316
13.317 . bhramayatyeva tānmāyā hyamokṣe mokṣalipsayā |
13.317 . vaiṣṇavādiḥ śaivaśāstraṃ melayannijaśāsane || 317
13.318 . dhruvaṃ saṃśayamāpanna ubhayabhraṣṭatāṃ vrajet |
13.318 . svadṛṣṭau paradṛṣṭau ca samayollaṅghanādasau || 318
13.319 . pratyavāyaṃ yato'bhyeti carettannedṛśaṃ kramam |
13.319 . uktaṃ śrīmadgahvare ca parameśena tādṛśam || 319
13.320 . nānyaśāstrābhiyuktaṣu śivajñānaṃ prakāśate |
13.320 . tanna saiddhāntiko vāme nāsau dakṣe sa no mate || 320
13.321 . kulakaule trike nāsau pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ paratra tu |
13.321 . avacchinno'navacchedaṃ no vettyānantyasaṃsthitaḥ || 321
13.322 . sarvaṃsahastato'dhaḥstha ūrdhvastho'dhikṛto guruḥ |
13.322 . svātmīyādharasaṃsparśātprāṇayannadharāḥ kriyāḥ || 322
13.323 . saphalīkurute yattadūrdhvastho gururuttamaḥ |
13.323 . adhaḥsthadṛkstho'pyetādṛggurusevī bhavetsa yaḥ || 323
13.324 . tādṛkśaktinipāteddho yo drāgūrdhvamimaṃ nayet |
13.324 . tattadgirinadīprāyāvacchinne kṣetrapīṭhake || 324
13.325 . uttarottaravijñāne nādhikāryadharo'dharaḥ |
13.325 . uttarottaramācāryaṃ vidannapyadharo'dharaḥ || 325
13.326 . kurvannadhikriyāṃ śāstralaṅghī nigrahabhājanam |
13.326 . śaktipātabalādeva jñānayogyavicitratā || 326
13.327 . śrautaṃ cintāmayaṃ dvyātma bhāvanāmayameva ca |
13.327 . jñānaṃ taduttaraṃ jyāyo yato mokṣaikakāraṇam || 327
13.328 . tattvebhya uddhṛtiṃ kvāpi yojanaṃ sakale'kale |
13.328 . kathaṃ kuryādvinā jñānaṃ bhāvanāmayamuttamam || 328
13.329 . yogī tu prāptatattattvasiddhirapyuttame pade |
13.329 . sadāśivādye svabhyastajñānitvādeva yojakaḥ || 329
13.330 . adhareṣu ca tattveṣu yā siddhiryogajāsya sā |
13.330 . vimocanāyāṃ nopāyaḥ sthitāpi dhanadāravat || 330
13.331 . yastūtpannasamastādhvasiddhiḥ sahi sadāśivaḥ |
13.331 . sākṣādeṣa kathaṃ martyānmocayedgurutāṃ vrajan || 331
13.332 . tenoktaṃ mālinītantre vicārya jñānayogite |
13.332 . yataśca mokṣadaḥ proktaḥ svabhyastajñānavānbudhaiḥ || 332
13.333 . tasmātsvabhyastavijñānataivaikaṃ gurulakṣaṇam |
13.333 . vibhāgastveṣa me proktaḥ śaṃbhunāthena darśyate || 333
13.334 . mokṣajñānaparaḥ kuryādguruṃ svabhyastavedanam |
13.334 . anyaṃ tyajetprāptamapi tathācoktaṃ śivena tat || 334
13.335 . āmodārthī yathā bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpātpuṣpāntaraṃ vrajet |
13.335 . vijñānārthī tathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ vrajet || 335
13.336 . śaktihīnaṃ guruṃ prāpya mokṣajñāne kathaṃ śrayet |
13.336 . naṣṭamūle drume devi kutaḥ puṣpaphalādikam || 336
13.337 . uttarottaramutkarṣalakṣmīṃ paśyannapi sthitaḥ |
13.337 . adhame yaḥ pade tasmātko'nyaḥ syāddaivadagdhakaḥ || 337
13.338 . yastu bhogaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca vāñchedvijñānameva ca |
13.338 . svabhyastajñāninaṃ yogasiddhaṃ sa gurumāśrayet || 338
13.339 . tadabhāve tu vijñānamokṣayorjñāninaṃ śrayet |
13.339 . bhuktyaṃśe yoginaṃ yastatphalaṃ dātuṃ bhavetkṣamaḥ || 339
13.340 . phaladānākṣame yoginyapāyaikopadeśini |
13.340 . varaṃ jñānī yo'bhyupāyaṃ diśedapica mocayet || 340
13.341 . jñānī na pūrṇa evaiko yadi hyaṃśāṃśikākramāt |
13.341 . jñānānyādāya vijñānaṃ kurvītākhaṇḍamaṇḍalam || 341
13.342 . tenāsaṃkhyāngurūnkuryātpūraṇāya svasaṃvidaḥ |
13.342 . dhanyastu pūrṇavijñānaṃ jñānārthī labhate gurum || 342
13.343 . nānāgurvāgamasrotaḥpratibhāmātramiśritam |
13.343 . kṛtvā jñānārṇavaṃ svābhirvipruṅgiḥ plāvayenna kim || 343
13.344 . ā tapanānmoṭakāntaṃ yasya me'sti gurukramaḥ |
13.344 . tasya me sarvaśiṣyasya nopadeśadaridratā || 344
13.345 . śrīmatā kallaṭenetthaṃ guruṇā tu nyarūpyata |
13.345 . ahamapyata evādhaḥśāstradṛṣṭikutūhalāt || 345
13.346 . tārkikaśrautabauddhārhadvaiṣṇavādīnnaseviṣi |
13.346 . lokādhyātmātimārgādikarmayogavidhānataḥ || 346
13.347 . saṃbodhotkarṣabāhulyātkramotkṛṣṭānvibhāvayet |
13.347 . śrīpūrvaśāstre praṣṭāro munayo nāradādayaḥ || 347
13.348 . prāgvaiṣṇavāḥ saugatāśca siddhāntādividastataḥ |
13.348 . kramāttrikārthavijñānacandrotsukitadṛṣṭayaḥ || 348
13.349 . tasmānna gurubhūyastve viśaṅketa kadācana |
13.349 . gurvantararate mūḍhe āgamāntarasevake || 349
13.350 . pratyavāyo ya āmnātaḥ sa itthamiti gṛhyatām |
13.350 . yo yatra śāstre'dhikṛtaḥ sa tatra gururucyate || 350
13.351 . tatrānadhikṛto yastu tadgurvantaramucyate |
13.351 . yathā tanmaṇḍalāsīno maṇḍalāntarabhūpatim || 351
13.352 . svamaṇḍalajigīṣuḥ sansevamāno vinaśyati |
13.352 . tathottarottarajñānasiddhiprepsuḥ samāśrayan || 352
13.353 . adharādharamācāryaṃ vināśamadhigacchati |
13.353 . evamevordhvavartiṣṇorāgamātsiddhivāñchakaḥ || 353
13.354 . māyīyaśāstranirato vināśaṃ pratipadyate |
13.354 . uktaṃ ca śrīmadānande karma saṃśritya bhāvataḥ || 354
13.355 . jugupsate tattasmiṃśca viphale'nyatsamāśrayet |
13.355 . dināddinaṃ hrasaṃstvevaṃ pacyate rauravādiṣu || 355
13.356 . yastūrdhvordhvapathaprepsuradharaṃ gurumāgamam |
13.356 . jihāsecchaktipātena sa dhanyaḥ pronmukhīkṛtaḥ || 356
13.357 . ata eveha śāstreṣu śaiveṣveva nirūpyate |
13.357 . śāstrāntarārthānāśvastānprati saṃskārako vidhiḥ || 357
13.358 . ataścāpyuttamaṃ śaivaṃ yo'nyatra patitaḥ sahi |
13.358 . ihānugrāhya ūrdhvordhvaṃ netastu patitaḥ kvacit || 358
13.359 . ata eva hi sarvajñairbrahmaviṣṇvādibhirnije |
13.359 . na śāsane samāmnātaṃ liṅgoddhārādi kiṃcana || 359
13.360 . itthaṃ viṣṇvādayaḥ śaivaparamārthaikavedinaḥ |
13.360 . kāṃścitprati tathādikṣuste mohādvimatiṃ śritāḥ || 360
13.361 . tathāvidhāmeva matiṃ satyasaṃsparśanākṣamām |
13.361 . dṛṣṭvaiṣāṃ brahmaviṣṇvādyairbuddhairapi tathoditam || 361
13.362 . ityeṣa yuktyāgamataḥ śaktipāto vivecitaḥ |

c14 atha śrītantrāloke caturdaśamāhnikam

14.1 . tirobhāvasvarūpaṃ tu kathyamānaṃ vivicyatām |
14.1 . svabhāvātparameśāno niyatyaniyatikramam || 1
14.2 . spṛśanprakāśate yena tataḥ svacchanda ucyate |
14.2 . niyatiṃ karmaphalayorāśrityaiṣa maheśvaraḥ || 2
14.3 . sṛṣṭisaṃsthitisaṃhārānvidhatte'vāntarasthitīn |
14.3 . mahāsarge punaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārānantyaśālini || 3
14.4 . ekaḥ sa devo viśvātmā niyatityāgataḥ prabhuḥ |
14.4 . avāntare yā ca sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiścātrāpyayantritam || 4
14.5 . nojjhatyeṣa vapustyaktaniyatiśca sthito'tra tat |
14.5 . niyatyaiva yadā caiṣa svarūpācchādanakramāt || 5
14.6 . bhuṅkte duḥkhavimohādi tadā karmaphalakramaḥ |
14.6 . tyaktvā tu niyamaṃ kārmaṃ duḥkhamohaparītatām || 6
14.7 . bibhāsayiṣurāste'yaṃ tirodhāne'napekṣakaḥ |
14.7 . yathā prakāśasvātantryātpratibuddho'pyabuddhavat || 7
14.8 . āste tadvadanuttīrṇo'pyuttīrṇa iva ceṣṭate |
14.8 . yathā ca buddhastāṃ mūḍhaceṣṭāṃ kurvannapi dviṣan || 8
14.9 . hṛdyāste mūḍha evaṃ hi prabuddhānāṃ viceṣṭitam |
14.9 . śrīvidyādhipatiścāha mānastotre tadīdṛśam || 9
14.10 . ye yauṣmāke śāsanamārge kṛtadīkṣāḥ saṃgacchanto mohavaśādvipratipattim |
14.10 . nūnaṃ teṣā nāsti bhavadbhānuniyogaḥ saṅkocaḥ kiṃ sūryakaraistāmarasānām || 10
14.11 . jñātajñeyā dhātṛpadasthā api santo ye tvanmārgātkāpathagāste'pi na samyaka |
14.11 . prāyasteṣāṃ laiṅgikabuddhyādisamuttho mithyābodhaḥ sarpavasādīpajakalpaḥ || 11
14.12 . yasmādviddhaṃ sūtakamukhyena nu tāmraṃ tadyadbhūyaḥ svāṃ prakṛtiṃ no samupeyāt |
14.12 . no taiḥ pītaṃ bhūtalasaṃsthairamṛtaṃ tadyeṣāṃ tṛṭkṣudduḥkhavibādhāḥ punarasmin || 12
14.13 . tataḥ prabuddhaceṣṭāsau mantracaryārcanādikā |
14.13 . dveṣeddhāntardahatyenaṃ dāhaḥ śaṅkaiva sā yataḥ || 13
14.14 . na cāsya karmamahimā tādṛgyenetthamāsta saḥ |
14.14 . kiṃ hi tatkarma kasmādvā pūrveṇātra samo vidhiḥ || 14
14.15 . tasmātsā parameśecchā yayāyaṃ mohitastathā |
14.15 . anantakālasaṃvedyaduḥkhapātratvamīhate || 15
14.16 . tatrāpi cecchāvaicitryādihāmutrobhayātmakaḥ |
14.16 . duḥkhasyāpi vibhedo'sti ciraśaighryakṛtastathā || 16
14.17 . kālakāmāndhakādīnāṃ paulastyapuravāsinām |
14.17 . tathānyeṣāṃ tirobhāvastāvadduḥkho hyamutra ca || 17
14.18 . anyo'pi ca tirobhāvaḥ samayollaṅghanātmakaḥ |
14.18 . yaduktaṃ parameśena śrīmadānandagahvare || 18
14.19 . samayollaṅghanāddevi kravyādatvaṃ śataṃ samāḥ |
14.19 . tatrāpi mandatīvrādibhedādbahuvidhaḥ kramaḥ || 19
14.20 . svātantryācca maheśasya tirobhūto'pyasau svayam |
14.20 . paradvāreṇa vābhyeti bhūyo'nugrahamapyalam || 20
14.21 . bhūyo'nugrahataḥ prāyaścittādyācaraṇe sati |
14.21 . anusāreṇa dīkṣādau kṛte syācchivatāmayaḥ || 21
14.22 . tirobhūtaḥ paretāsurapi bandhusuhṛdgurūn |
14.22 . ālambya śaktipātena dīkṣādyairanugṛhyate || 22
14.23 . tatrāpi kālaśīghratvaciratvādivibhedatām |
14.23 . tathaiti śaktipāto'sau yenāyāti śivātmatām || 23
14.24 . itthaṃ sṛṣṭisthitidhvaṃsatirobhāvamanugrahaḥ |
14.24 . iti pañcasu kartṛtvaṃ śivatvaṃ saṃvidātmanaḥ || 24
14.25 . pañcakṛtyasvatantratvasaṃpūrṇasvātmamāninaḥ |
14.25 . yogino'rcājapadhyānayogāḥ saṃsyuḥ sadoditāḥ || 25
14.26 . aindrajālikavṛttānte na rajyeta kadācana |
14.26 . sādāśivo'pi yo bhogo bandhaḥ so'pyucitātmanām || 26
14.27 . jñātṛtvameva śivatā svātantryaṃ tadihocyate |
14.27 . kulālavattu kartṛtvaṃ na mukhyaṃ tadadhiṣṭhiteḥ || 27
14.28 . iti jñātvā grahītavyā naiva jātvapi khaṇḍanā |
14.28 . śivo'haṃ cenmadicchānuvarti kiṃ na jagattviti || 28
14.29 . mamecchāmanuvartantāmityatrāhaṃvidi sphuret |
14.29 . śivo vā parameśāno dehādiratha nirmitaḥ || 29
14.30 . śivasya tāvadastyetaddehastveṣa tathā tvayā |
14.30 . kṛtaḥ kānyā dehatāsya tatkiṃ syādvācyatāpadam || 30
14.31 . uktaṃ ca siddhasantānaśrīmadūrmimahākule |
14.31 . pavanabhramaṇaprāṇavikṣepādikṛtaśramāḥ || 31
14.32 . kuhakādiṣu ye bhrāntāste bhrāntāḥ parame pade |
14.32 . sarvatra bahumānena yāpyutkrāntirvimuktaye || 32
14.33 . proktā sā sāraśāstreṣu bhogopāyatayoditā |
14.33 . yadi sarvagatā devo vadotkramya kva yāsyati || 33
14.34 . athāsarvagatastarhi ghaṭatulyastadā bhavet |
14.34 . utkrāntividhiyogo'yamekadeśena kathyate || 34
14.35 . niraṃśe śivatattve tu kathamutkrāntisaṃgatiḥ |
14.35 . yathā dharādau vāyvante bhṛgvambvagnyupavāsakaiḥ || 35
14.36 . ātmano yojanaṃ vyomni tadvadutkrāntivartanā |
14.36 . tasmānnotkramayejjīvaṃ paratattvasamīhayā || 36
14.37 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tūktaṃ yadutkrānterlakṣaṇaṃ na tat |
14.37 . muktyupāyatayā kiṃtu bhogahānyai tathaiṣaṇāt || 37
14.38 . japadhyānādisaṃsiddhaḥ svātantryācchaktipātataḥ |
14.38 . bhogaṃ prati viraktaśceditthaṃ dehaṃ tyajediti || 38
14.39 . svacchandamṛtyorapi yadbhīṣmādeḥ śrūyate kila |
14.39 . bhogavairasyasaṃprāptau jīvitāntopasarpaṇam || 39
14.40 . yogamantrāmṛtadravyavarādyaiḥ siddhibhāktanuḥ |
14.40 . hātuṃ nahyanyathā śakyā vinoktakramayogataḥ || 40
14.41 . uktaṃ ca mālinītantre parameśena tādṛśam |
14.41 . sarvamapyathavā bhogaṃ manyamāno virūpakam || 41
14.42 . ityādi vadatā sarvairalakṣyāntaḥsatattvakam |
14.42 . evaṃ sṛṣṭyādikartavyasvasvātantryopadeśanam || 42
14.43 . yatsaiva mukhyadīkṣā syācchiṣyasya śivadāyinī |
14.43 . uktaṃ śrīniśicāre ca bhairavīyeṇa tejasā || 43
14.44 . vyāptaṃ viśvaṃ prapaśyanti vikalpojjhitacetasaḥ |
14.44 . vikalpayuktacittastu piṇḍapātācchivaṃ vrajet || 44
14.45 . bāhyadīkṣādiyogena caryāsamayakalpanaiḥ |
14.45 . avikalpastathādyaiva jīvanmukto na saṃśayaḥ || 45
14.46 . saṃsārajīrṇatarumūlakalāpakalpasaṃkalpasāntaratayā paramārthavahneḥ |
14.46 . syurvisphuliṅgakaṇikā api cettadante dedīpyate vimalabodhahutāśarāśiḥ || 46
14.47 . itthaṃ dīkṣopakramo'yaṃ darśitaḥ śāstrasaṃmataḥ |


c15 atha śrītantrāloke pañcadaśamāhnikam

15.1 . athaitadupayogāya yāgastāvannirūpyate |
15.1 . tatra dīkṣaiva bhoge ca muktau cāyātyupāyatām || 1
15.2 . svayaṃ saṃskārayogādvā tadaṅgaṃ tatpradarśyate |
15.2 . yo yatrābhilaṣedbhogān sa tatraiva niyojitaḥ || 2
15.3 . siddhibhāṅmantraśaktyeti śrīmatsvāyaṃbhuve vibhuḥ |
15.3 . yogyatāvaśato yatra vāsanā yasya tatra saḥ || 3
15.4 . yojyo na cyavate tasmāditi śrīmālinīmate |
15.4 . vadanbhogādyupāyatvaṃ dīkṣāyāḥ prāha no guruḥ || 4
15.5 . na cādhikāritā dīkṣāṃ vinā yoge'sti śāṅkare |
15.5 . na ca yogādhikāritvamekamevānayā bhavet || 5
15.6 . api mantrādhikāritvaṃ muktiśca śivadīkṣayā |
15.6 . ityasminmālinīvākye sākṣānmokṣābhyupāyatā || 6
15.7 . dīkṣāyāḥ kathitā prācyagranthena punarucyate |
15.7 . pāramparyeṇa saṃskṛtyā mokṣabhogābhyupāyatā || 7
15.8 . yeṣāmadhyavasāyo'sti na vidyāṃ pratyaśaktitaḥ |
15.8 . sukhopāyamidaṃ teṣāṃ vidhānamuditaṃ guroḥ || 8
15.9 . iti śrīmanmataṅgākhye hyuktā mokṣābhyupāyatā |
15.9 . samyagjñānasvabhāvā hi vidyā sākṣādvimocikā || 9
15.10 . uktaṃ tatraiva tattvānāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvataḥ |
15.10 . heyādeyatvakathane vidyāpāda iti sphuṭam || 10
15.11 . tatrāśaktāstu ye teṣāṃ dīkṣācaryāsamādhayaḥ |
15.11 . te vidyāpūrvakā yasmāttasmājjñānyuttamottamaḥ || 11
15.12 . jñānaṃ ca śāstrāttaccāpi śrāvyo nādīkṣito yataḥ |
15.12 . ato'sya saṃskriyāmātropayogo dīkṣayā kṛtaḥ || 12
15.13 . yatra tatrāstu guruṇā yojito'sau phalaṃ punaḥ |
15.13 . svavijñānocitaṃ yāti jñānītyuktaṃ purā kila || 13
15.14 . yasya tvīśaprasādena divyā kācana yogyatā |
15.14 . guroḥ śiśośca tau naiva prati dīkṣopayogitā || 14
15.15 . jñānameva tadā dīkṣā śrītraiśikanirūpaṇāt |
15.15 . sarvaśāstrārthavettṛtvamakasmāccāsya jāyate || 15
15.16 . iti śrīmālinīnītyā yaḥ sāṃsiddhikasaṃvidaḥ |
15.16 . sa uttamādhikārī syājjñānavānhi gururmataḥ || 16
15.17 . ātmane vā parebhyo vā hitārthī cetayedidam |
15.17 . ityuktyā mālinīśāstre tatsarvaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam || 17
15.18 . jñānayogyāstathā keciccaryāyogyāstathāpare |
15.18 . dīkṣāyogyā yogayogyā iti śrīkairaṇe vidhau || 18
15.19 . tatroktalakṣaṇaḥ karmayogajñānaviśāradaḥ |
15.19 . uttarottaratābhūmyutkṛṣṭo gururudīritaḥ || 19
15.20 . sa ca prāguktaśaktyanyatamapātapavitritam |
15.20 . parīkṣya pṛṣṭvā vā śiṣyaṃ dīkṣākarma samācaret || 20
15.21 . uktaṃ svacchandaśāstre ca śiṣyaṃ pṛcchedguruḥ svayam |
15.21 . phalaṃ prārthayase yādṛktādṛksādhanamārabhe || 21
15.22 . vāsanābhedataḥ sādhyaprāptirmantrapracoditā |
15.22 . mantramudrādhvadravyāṇāṃ home sādhāraṇā sthitiḥ || 22
15.23 . vāsanābhedato bhinnaṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ ca guroḥ phalam |
15.23 . sādhako dvividhaḥ śaivadharmā lokojjhitasthitiḥ || 23
15.24 . lokadharmī phalākāṃkṣī śubhasthaścāśubhojjhitaḥ |
15.24 . dvidhā mumukṣurnirbījaḥ samayādivivarjitaḥ || 24
15.25 . bālabāliśavṛddhastrībhogabhugvyādhitādikaḥ |
15.25 . anyaḥ sabījo yasyetthaṃ dīkṣoktā śivaśāsane || 25
15.26 . vidvaddvandvasahānāṃ tu sabījā samayātmikā |
15.26 . dīkṣānugrāhikā pālyā viśeṣasamayāstu taiḥ || 26
15.27 . abhāvaṃ bhāvayetsamyakkarmaṇāṃ prācyabhāvinām |
15.27 . mumukṣornirapekṣasya prārabdhrekaṃ na śādhayet || 27
15.28 . sādhakasya tu bhūtyarthamitthameva viśodhayet |
15.28 . śivadharmiṇyasau dīkṣā lokadharmāpahāriṇī || 28
15.29 . adharmarūpiṇāmeva na śubhānāṃ tu śodhanam |
15.29 . lokadharmiṇyasau dīkṣā mantrārādhanavarjitā || 29
15.30 . prārabdhadehabhede tu bhuṅkte'sāvaṇimādikam |
15.30 . bhuktvordhvaṃ yāti yatraiṣa yukto'tha sakale'kale || 30
15.31 . samayācārapāśaṃ tu nirbījāyāṃ viśodhayet |
15.31 . dīkṣāmātreṇa muktiḥ syādbhaktyā deve gurau sadā || 31
15.32 . sadyonirvāṇadā seyaṃ nirbījā yeti bhaṇyate |
15.32 . atītānāgatārabdhapāśatrayaviyojikā || 32
15.33 . dīkṣāvasāne śuddhasya dehatyāge paraṃ padam |
15.33 . dehatyāge sabījāyāṃ karmābhāvādvipadyate || 33
15.34 . samayācārapāśaṃ tu dīkṣitaḥ pālayetsadā |
15.34 . evaṃ pṛṣṭvā parijñāya vicārya ca guruḥ svayam || 34
15.35 . ucitāṃ saṃvidhitsustāṃ vāsanāṃ tādṛśīṃ śrayet |
15.35 . āyātaśaktipātasya dīkṣāṃ prati na daiśikaḥ || 35
15.36 . avajñāṃ vidadhīteti śaṃbhunājñā nirūpitā |
15.36 . svadhanena daridrasya kuryāddīkṣāṃ guruḥ svayam || 36
15.37 . api dūrvāmbubhiryadvā dīkṣāyai bhikṣate śiśuḥ |
15.37 . bhikṣopāttaṃ nijaṃ vātha dhanaṃ prāggurave śiśuḥ || 37
15.38 . dadyādyena viśuddhaṃ tadyāgayogyatvamaśnute |
15.38 . tatrādau śivatāpattisvātantryāveśa eva yaḥ || 38
15.39 . sa eva hi guruḥ kāryastato'sau dīkṣaṇe kṣamaḥ |
15.39 . śivatāveśitā cāsya bahūpāyā pradarśitā || 39
15.40 . kramikā bāhyarūpā tu snānanyāsārcanādibhiḥ |
15.40 . bahvīṣu tāsu tāsveṣa kriyāsu śivatāṃ hṛdi || 40
15.41 . saṃdadhaddṛḍhamabhyeti śivabhāvaṃ prasannadhīḥ |
15.41 . śivībhūto yadyadicchettattatkartuṃ samīhate || 41
15.42 . śivābhimānitopāyo bāhyo heturna mokṣadaḥ |
15.42 . śivo'yaṃ śiva evāsmītyevamācāryaśiṣyayoḥ || 42
15.43 . hetutadvattayā dārḍhyābhimāno mocako hyaṇoḥ |
15.43 . nādhyātmena vinā bāhyaṃ nādhyātmaṃ bāhyavarjitam || 43
15.44 . siddhyejjñānakriyābhyāṃ taddvitīyaṃ saṃprakāśate |
15.44 . śrībrahmayāmale deva iti tena nyarūpayat || 44
15.45 . śrīmadānandaśāstre ca nāśuddhiḥ syādvipaścitaḥ |
15.45 . kintu snānaṃ suvastratvaṃ tuṣṭisaṃjananaṃ bhavet || 45
15.46 . tatra prasiddhadehādimātṛnirmalatākramāt |
15.46 . ayatnato'ntarantaḥ syānnairmalya snāyatāṃ tataḥ || 46
15.47 . snānaṃ ca devadevasya yanmūrtyaṣṭakamucyate |
15.47 . tatraivaṃ mantradīpte'ntarmaladāhe nimajjanam || 47
15.48 . tatreṣṭamantrahṛdayo gorajo'ntaḥ padatrayam |
15.48 . gatvāgatya bhajetsnānaṃ pārthivaṃ dhṛtidāyakam || 48

15.184 . triśiraḥśāsanādau ca sa dṛṣṭo vidhirucyate |

15.192 . praviśya śivaraśmīddhadṛśā veśmāvalokayet |

15.193 . tatrottarāśābhimukho mumukṣustādṛśāya vā |

15.194 . yadyapyasti na diṅnāma kācitpūrvāparādikā |

15.195 . upādhiḥ pūrvatādiṣṭa iti cettatkṛtaṃ diśā |

15.196 . tasmātsaṃvitprakāśo'yaṃ mūrtyābhāsanabhāgataḥ |

15.197 . tatra yadyatprakāśena sadā svīkaraṇe kṣamam |

15.198 . kiṃcitprakāśatā madhyaṃ tato vai diksamudbhavaḥ |

15.199 . parāṅmukhaṃ tu tatpaścāditi digdvayamāgatam |

15.200 . yatra tiṣṭhoddakṣiṇaṃ tatprakāśasyānukūlyataḥ |
15.200 . dakṣiṇasya puraḥsaṃsthaṃ vāmamityupadiśyate || 200
15.201 . tatprakāśitameyendusparśasaumyaṃ tadeva hi |

15.202 . evaṃ prakāśamātre'sminvarade parame śive || 202

15.204 . īśanraghoravāmākhyasadyo'dhobhedato diśaḥ |

15.205 . puruṣo dakṣiṇācaṇḍo vāmā vāmastu saumyakaḥ |

15.206 . pātālavaktramadharamaprakāśatayā sthiteḥ |
15.206 . khamarudvahnijalabhūkhāni vaktrāṇyamuṣya hi || 206

15.208 . madhye tu yatprakāśaṃ tanna prakāśyaṃ na cetarat |
15.208 . prakāśatvāddiśyamānamato'smindikcatuṣṭayam || 208
15.209 . pañcamantratanurnātha itthaṃ viśvadigīśvaraḥ |

15.210 . ūrdhvābhivyaktyayogyatvādviṣṇordhātuśca pañcamam |

15.211 . digvibhāgastu tajjo'sti vadanānāṃ catuṣṭayāt |
15.211 . pañcamasya yujitve tau parityaktanijātmakau || 211
15.212 . tato brahmāṇḍamadhye'pi jñānaśaktirvibho raviḥ |
15.212 . diśāṃ vibhāgaṃ kurute prakāśaghanavṛttimān || 212
15.213 . tathāhi viṣuvadyoge yataḥ pūrvaṃ pradṛśyate |

15.214 . tasmiñjigamiṣorasya yatsavyaṃ tattu dakṣiṇam |

15.215 . tatpurovarti vāmaṃ tu tadbhāsā khacitaṃ manāk |

15.216 . yatrāsāvastamabhyeti tatpaścimamiti sthitiḥ |

15.217 . tadeva pūrvameteṣāṃ yathādhvani nirūpitam |

15.219 . adhiṣṭhitā maheśena citratadrūpadhāriṇā |
15.219 . kiṃ vātibahunā yo'sau yaṣṭā tatsaṃmukhāditaḥ || 219
15.220 . diśo'pi pravibhajyante prāksavyottarapaścimāḥ |

15.221 . adhitiṣṭhatyarkamiva sa vicitravapuryataḥ |

15.222 . te hi prakāśaśaktyaṃśāḥ prakāśānuvidhāyinaḥ |
15.222 . prakāśasya yadaiśvaryaṃ sa indro yattu tanmahaḥ || 222
15.223 . so'gniryantṛtvabhīmatve yamo rakṣastadūnimā |
15.223 . prakāśyaṃ varuṇastacca cāñcalyādvāyurucyate || 223
15.224 . bhāvasañcayayogena vitteśastatkṣaye vibhuḥ |

15.230 . sthitastadanusāreṇa madhyībhavati śaṃkaraḥ |

15.256 . guravastvāhuritthaṃ yannyāsadvayamudāhṛtam |

15.258 . kiṃtvāvāhyastu yo mantraḥ sa tatrāṅgasamanvitaḥ |

15.259 . mudrāpradarśanaṃ paścātkāyena manasā girā |

15.262 . abhedamānīya kṛtā śuddhā nyāsabalakramāt |
15.262 . tena ye'codayanmūḍhāḥ pāśadāhavidhūnane || 262
15.263 . kṛte śānte śive rūḍhaḥ punaḥ kimavarohati |

15.264 . na vidusta svasaṃvittisphurattāsāravarjitāḥ |

15.265 . sarvetarādhvavyāvṛtto ghaṭatulyo'sti kutracit |
15.265 . mahāprakāśarūpā hi yeyaṃ saṃvidvijṛmbhate || 265
15.266 . sa śivaḥ śivataivāsya vaiśvarūpyāvabhāsitā |
15.266 . tathābhāsanayogo'taḥ svarasenāsya jṛmbhate || 266
15.267 . bhāsyamāno'tra cābhedaḥ svātmano bheda eva ca |
15.267 . bhede vijṛmbhite māyā māyāmāturvijṛmbhate || 267
15.268 . abhede jṛmbhate'syaiva māyāmātuḥ śivātmatā |
15.268 . māyāpramātā tadrūpavikalpābhyāsapāṭavāt || 268
15.269 . śiva eva tadabhyāsaphalaṃ nyāsādi kīrtitam |
15.269 . yathāhi duṣṭakarmāsmītyevaṃ bhāvayatastathā || 269
15.270 . tathā śivo'haṃ nānyo'smītyevaṃ bhāvayatastathā |
15.270 . etadevocyate dārḍhyaṃ vimarśahṛdayaṅgamam || 270
15.271 . śivaikātmyavikalpaughadvārikā nirvikalpatā |
15.271 . anyathā tasya śuddhasya vimarśaprāṇavartinaḥ || 271
15.272 . kathaṃ nāmāvimṛṣṭaṃ syādrūpaṃ bhāsanadharmaṇaḥ |
15.272 . tenātidurghaṭaghaṭāsvatantrecchāvaśādayam || 272
15.273 . bhānapi prāṇabuddhyādiḥ svaṃ tathā na vikalpayet |
15.273 . pratyutātisvatantrātmaviparītasvadharmatām || 273
15.274 . vināśyanīśāyattatvarūpāṃ niścitya majjati |
15.274 . tataḥ saṃsārabhāgīyatathāniścayaśātinīm || 274
15.275 . nityādiniścayadvārāmavikalpāṃ sthitiṃ śrayet |

15.276 . na te dīkṣāmanunyāsakāriṇaśceti varṇitam |
15.276 . evaṃ viśvaśarīraḥ sanviśvātmatvaṃ gataḥ sphuṭam || 276
15.277 . nyāsamātrāttathābhūtaṃ dehaṃ puṣpādinārcayet |
15.277 . pṛthaṅmantrairvistareṇa saṃkṣepānmūlamantrataḥ || 277
15.278 . dhūpanaivedyatṛptyādyaistathā vyāsasamāsataḥ |
15.278 . saṃsāravāmācāratvātsarvaṃ vāmakareṇa tu || 278
15.279 . kuryāttarpaṇayogaṃ ca daiśikastadanāmayā |

15.280 . vāmācāraparo mantrī yāgaṃ kuryāditi sphuṭam |

15.281 . sarvatīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam |
15.281 . tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitamanāmātarpaṇātpriye || 281
15.282 . śrīmannandiśikhāyāṃ ca śrīmadānandaśāsane |
15.282 . taduktaṃ srukca pūrṇāyāṃ sruvaśvājyāhutau bhavet || 282
15.283 . śeṣaṃ vāmakareṇaiva pūjāhomajapādikam |
15.283 . evamānandasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvaunmukhyavivarjitam || 283
15.284 . yāgena dehaṃ miṣpādya bhāvayeta śivātmakam |
15.284 . galite viṣayaunmukhye pārimitye vilāpite || 284
15.285 . dehe kimavaśiṣyeta śivānandarasādṛte |
15.285 . śivānandarasāpūrṇaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśattattvanirbharam || 285
15.286 . dehaṃ divāniśaṃ paśyannarcayansyācchivātmakaḥ |
15.286 . viśvātmadehaviśrāntitṛptastalliṅganiṣṭhitaḥ || 286
15.287 . bāhyaṃ liṅgavratakṣetracaryādi nahi vāñchati |
