15.523 . hananaṃ mohanaṃ ceti samayāṣṭakamaṣṭadhā |
15.523 . svabhāvaṃ mantratantrāṇāṃ samayācāramelakam || 523
15.524 . asatpralāpaṃ paruṣamanṛtaṃ nāṣṭadhā vadet |
15.524 . aphalaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ hiṃsāṃ paradārābhimarśanam || 524
15.525 . garvaṃ dambhaṃ bhūtaviṣavyādhitantraṃ nacācaret |
15.525 . svaṃ mantramakṣasūtraṃ ca vidyāṃ jñānasvarūpakam || 525
15.526 . samācārānguṇānkleśānsiddhiliṅgāni gūhayet |
15.526 . guruṃ śāstraṃ devavahnī jñānavṛddhāṃstriyo vratam || 526
15.527 . guruvargaṃ yathāśaktyā pūjayedaṣṭakaṃ tvidam |
15.527 . dīnānkliṣṭānpitṝnkṣetrapālānprāṇigaṇān khagān || 527
15.528 . śmāśānikaṃ bhūtagaṇaṃ dehadevīśca tarpayet |
15.528 . śivaṃ śaktiṃ tathātmānaṃ mudrāṃ mantrasvarūpakam || 528
15.529 . saṃsārabhuktimuktīśca guruvaktrāttu bhāvayet |
15.529 . rāgaṃ dveṣamasūyāṃ ca saṃkocerṣyābhimānitāḥ || 529
15.530 . samayapratibhettṝṃstadanācārāṃśca ghātayet |
15.530 . paśumārgasthitānkrūrāndveṣiṇaḥ piśunāñjaḍān || 530
15.531 . rājñaścānucarānpāpānvighnakartṝṃśca mohayet |
15.531 . śākinyaḥ pūjanīyāśca tāścetthaṃ śrīgamoditāḥ || 531
15.532 . sāhasaṃ dviguṇaṃ yāsāṃ kāmaścaiva caturguṇaḥ |
15.532 . lobhaścāṣṭaguṇastāsāṃ śaṅkyaṃ śākinya ityalam || 532
15.533 . kulāmnāyasthitā vīradravyabāhyāstu ye na taiḥ |
15.533 . paśubhiḥ saha vastavyamiti śrīmādhave kule || 533
15.534 . devatācakragurvagniśāstraṃ sāmyātsadārcayet |
15.534 . aniveditametebhyo na kiṃcidapi bhakṣayet || 534
15.535 . etaddravyaṃ nāpaharedguruvargaṃ prapūjayet |
15.535 . sa ca tadbhrātṛbhāryātukprāyo vidyākṛto bhavet || 535
15.536 . na yonisaṃbandhakṛto laukikaḥ sa paśuryataḥ |
15.536 . tasyābhiṣvaṅgabhūmistu gurvārādhanasiddhaye || 536
15.537 . arcyo na svamahimnā tu tadvargo guruvatpunaḥ |
15.537 . gurornindāṃ na kurvīta tasyai hetuṃ nacācaret || 537
15.538 . naca tāṃ śṛṇuyānnainaṃ kopayennāgrato'sya ca |
15.538 . vinājñayā prakurvīta kiṃcittatsevanādṛte || 538
15.539 . laukikālaukikaṃ kṛtyaṃ krodhaṃ krīḍāṃ tapo japam |
15.539 . gurūpabhuktaṃ yatkiṃcicchayyāvastrāsanādikam || 539
15.540 . nopabhuñjīta tatpadbhyāṃ na spṛśetkiṃtu vandayet |
15.540 . śrīmattraiśirase'pyuktaṃ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ || 540
15.541 . araṇye kāṣṭhavattiṣṭhedasidhārāvrato'pi san |
15.541 . niyamastho yamastho'pi tatpadaṃ nāśnute param || 541
15.542 . gurvārādhanasaktastu manasā karmaṇā girā |
15.542 . prāpnoti gurutastuṣṭātpūrṇaṃ śreyo mahādbhutam || 542
15.543 . himapātairyathā bhūmiśchāditā sā samantataḥ |
15.543 . mārutaśleṣasaṃyogādaśmavattiṣṭhate sadā || 543
15.544 . yamādau niścale tadvadbhāva ekastu gṛhyate |
15.544 . gurostvārādhitātpūrṇaṃ prasarajjñānamāpyate || 544
15.545 . sarvato'vasthitaṃ cittvaṃ jñeyasthaṃ yasya tatkathā |
15.545 . sadya eva nayedūrdhvaṃ tasmādārādhayedgurum || 545
15.546 . śrīsāre'pyasya saṃbhāṣātpātakaṃ naśyati kṣaṇāt |
15.546 . tasmātparīkṣya yatnena śāstroktyā jñānalakṣaṇaiḥ || 546
15.547 . śāstrācāreṇa varteta tena saṅgaṃ tathā kuru |
15.547 . snehājjātu vadejjñānaṃ lobhānna hriyate hi saḥ || 547
15.548 . tena tuṣṭena tṛpyanti devāḥ pitara evaca |
15.548 . uttīrya narakādyānti sadyaḥ śivapuraṃ mahat || 548
15.549 . bhuṅkte tiṣṭhedyatra gṛhe vrajecchivapuraṃ tu saḥ |
15.549 . iti jñātvā sadā pitrye śrāddhe svaṃ gurumarcayet || 549
15.550 . bhuñjīta sa svayaṃ cānyānādiśettatkṛte guruḥ |
15.550 . yo dīkṣitastu śrāddhādau svatantraṃ vidhimācaret || 550
15.551 . tasya tanniṣphalaṃ sarvaṃ samayena ca laṅghyate |
15.551 . saiddhāntikārpitaṃ caṇḍīyogyaṃ dravyaṃ vivarjayet || 551
15.552 . śākinīvācakaṃ śabdaṃ na kadācitsamuccaret |
15.552 . striyaḥ pūjyā virūpāstu vṛddhāḥ śilpopajīvikāḥ || 552
15.553 . antyā vikāritāṅgyaśca veśyāḥ svacchandaceṣṭitāḥ |
15.553 . tathāca śrīgame proktaṃ pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 553
15.554 . nirācārāḥ sarvabhakṣyā dharmādharmavivarjitāḥ |
15.554 . svacchandagāḥ palāśinyo lampaṭā devatā iva || 554
15.555 . veśyāḥ pūjyāstadgṛhaṃ ca prayāgo'tra yajetkramam |
15.555 . strīṣu tannācaretkicidyena tābhyo jugupsate || 555
15.556 . ato na nagnāstāḥ paśyennacāpi prakaṭastanīḥ |
15.556 . vṛddhāyāḥ saṃsthitāyā vā na jugupseta mudrikām || 556
15.557 . vaikṛtyaṃ tatra saurūpyaṃ melakaṃ na prakāśayet |
15.557 . devamūrtiṃ śūnyatanuṃ pūjayettripathādiṣu || 557
15.558 . sarvaparvasu sāmānyaviśeṣeṣu viśeṣataḥ |
15.558 . pūjā guroranadhyāyo melake lobhavarjanam || 558
15.559 . na jugupseta madyādi vīradravyaṃ kadācana |
15.559 . na nindedatha vandeta nityaṃ tajjoṣiṇastathā || 559
15.560 . upadeśāya na doṣā hṛdayaṃ cenna vidviṣet |
15.560 . vijātīyavikalpāṃśotpuṃsanāya yateta ca || 560
15.561 . guroḥ śāstrasya devīnāṃ nāma mantre yatastataḥ |
15.561 . arcāto'nyatra noccāryamāhūtaṃ tarpayettataḥ || 561
15.562 . āgatasya ca mantrasya na kuryāttarpaṇaṃ yadi |
15.562 . haratyardhaśarīraṃ tadityūce bhagavānyataḥ || 562
15.563 . śrīmadūrmau ca devīnāṃ vīrāṇāṃ ceṣṭitaṃ na vai |
15.563 . prathayenna jugupseta vadennādravyapāṇikaḥ || 563
15.564 . śrīpūrvaṃ nāma vaktavyaṃ gurordravyakareṇa ca |
15.564 . gurvādīnāṃ na laṅghyā ca chāyā na tairthikaiḥ saha || 564
15.565 . jalpaṃ kurvansvaśāstrārthaṃ vadennāpica sūcayet |
15.565 . nityādviśeṣapūjāṃ ca kuryānnaimittike vidhau || 565
15.566 . tato'pi madhye varṣasya tato'pi hi pavitrake |
15.566 . anyastamantro nāsīta sevyaṃ śāstrāntaraṃ ca no || 566
15.567 . aprarūḍhaṃ hi vijñānaṃ kampetetarabhāvanāt |
15.567 . gṛhopaskaraṇāstrāṇi davatāyāgayogataḥ || 567
15.568 . arcyānīti na padbhyāṃ vai spṛśennāpi vilaṅghyet |
15.568 . guruvarge gṛhāyāte viśeṣaṃ kaṃcidācaret || 568
15.569 . dīkṣitānāṃ na nindādi kuryādvidveṣapūrvakam |
15.569 . upadeśāya no doṣaḥ sa hyavidveṣapūrvakaḥ || 569
15.570 . na vaṣṇavādikādhaḥsthadṛṣṭibhiḥ saṃvasedalam |
15.570 . sahabhojanaśayyādyarnaiṣāṃ prakaṭayetsthitim || 570
15.571 . uktaṃ śrīṃmādhavakule śāsanāntarasaṃsthitān |
15.571 . vedoktiṃ vaiṣṇavoktiṃ ca tairuktaṃ varjayetsadā || 571
15.572 . akulīneṣu saṃparkāttatkulātpatanādbhayam |
15.572 . ekapātre kulāmnāye tasmāttānparivarjayet || 572
15.573 . pramādācca kṛte sakhye goṣṭhyāṃ cakraṃ tu pūjayet |
15.573 . śrīmadūrmau ca kathitamāgamāntarasevake || 573
15.574 . gurvantararate mūḍhe devadravyopajīvake |
15.574 . śaktihiṃsākare duṣṭe saṃparkaṃ naiva kārayet || 574
15.575 . na vikalpena dīkṣādau vrajedāyatanādikam |
15.575 . uktāsthāśithilatve yannimittaṃ naiva taccaret || 575
15.576 . śāsanasthānpurājātyā na paśyennāpyudīrayet |
15.576 . naca vyavaharetsarvāñchivābhedena kevalam || 576
15.577 . sadvidyaiḥ sākamāsīta jñānadīptyai yateta ca |
15.577 . nāsaṃskṛtāṃ vrajettajjaṃ viphalatvaṃ nacānayet || 577
15.578 . melakārdhaniśācaryā janavarjaṃ ca tannahi |
15.578 . māṃsādidāhagandhaṃ ca jighreddevīpriyo hyasau || 578
15.579 . gurvājñāṃ pālayansarvaṃ tyajenmantramayo bhavet |
15.579 . śāstrapūjājapadhyānavivekatadupakriyāḥ || 579
15.580 . akurvanniṣphalāṃ naiva ceṣṭeta trividhāṃ kriyām |
15.580 . mantratantrairna vādaṃ ca kuryānno bhakṣayedviṣam || 580
15.581 . samayānāṃ vilope ca guruṃ pṛcchedasannidhau |
15.581 . tadvargaṃ nijasantānamanyaṃ tasyāpyasaṃnidhau || 581
15.582 . tenoktamanutiṣṭhecca nirvikalpaṃ prayatnataḥ |
15.582 . yataḥ śāstrādisaṃbodhatanmayīkṛtamānasaḥ || 582
15.583 . śiva eva gururnāsya vāgasatyā viniḥsaret |
15.583 . śivasya svātmasaṃskṛtyai prahvībhāvo guroḥ punaḥ || 583
15.584 . hlādāyetyubhayārthāya tattuṣṭiḥ phaladā śiśoḥ |
15.584 . gurvāyattaikasiddhirhisamayyapi vibodhabhāk || 584
15.585 . tadbodhabahumānena vidyādgurutamaṃ gurum |
15.585 . ataḥ saṃprāpya vijñānaṃ yo gurau bāhyamānavān || 585
15.586 . nāsau vijñānaviśvasto nāsatyaṃ bhraṣṭa eva saḥ |
15.586 . jñānānāśvastacittaṃ taṃ vacomātreṇa śāstritam || 586
15.587 . bhaktaṃ ca nārcayejjātu hṛdā vijñānadūṣakam |
15.587 . tādṛkca na guruḥ kāryastaṃ kṛtvāpi parityajet || 587
15.588 . mukhyabuddhyā na saṃpaśyedvaiṣṇavādigatāngurūn |
15.588 . tathāca śrīmadūrmyākhye guroruktaṃ viśeṣaṇam || 588
15.589 . gurvājñā prāṇasaṃdehe nopekṣyā no vikalpyate |
15.589 . kauladīkṣā kaulaśāstraṃ tattvajñānaṃ prakāśitam || 589
15.590 . yenāsau gururityukto hyanye vai nāmadhāriṇaḥ |
15.590 . śrīmadānandaśāstre ca tathaivoktaṃ viśeṣaṇam || 590
15.591 . yasmāddīkṣā mantraśāstraṃ tattvajñānaṃ sa vai guruḥ |
15.591 . tiṣṭhedavyaktaliṅgaśca na liṅgaṃ dhārayetkvacit || 591
15.592 . na liṅgibhiḥ samaṃ kaiścitkuryādācāramelanam |
15.592 . kevalaṃ liṅginaḥ pālyā na bībhatsyā virūpakāḥ || 592
15.593 . śrīmadrātrikule coktaṃ mokṣaḥ śaṅkāpahānitaḥ |
15.593 . aśuddhavāsasnayaiṣā mokṣavārtāpi durlabhā || 593
15.594 . na likhenmantrahṛdayaṃ śrīmanmāloditaṃ kila |
15.594 . tadaṅgāduddharenmantraṃ natu lekhe vilekhayet || 594
15.595 . atattve'bhiniveśaṃ ca na kuryātpakṣapātataḥ |
15.595 . jātividyākulācāradehadeśaguṇārthajān || 595
15.596 . grahāngrahānivāṣṭau drāktyajedgahvararśitān |
15.596 . tathā śrīniśicārādau hayatvenopadarśitān || 596
15.597 . brāhmaṇo'haṃ mayā vedaśāstroktādaparaṃ katham |
15.597 . anuṣṭheyamayaṃ jātigrahaḥ paranirodhakaḥ || 597
15.598 . evamanye'pyudāhāryāḥ kulagahvaravartmanā |
15.598 . atatsvabhāve tādrūpyaṃ darśayannavaśe'pi yaḥ || 598
15.599 . svarūpācchādakaḥ so'tra graho graha ivoditaḥ |
15.599 . saṃvitsvabhāve no jātiprabhṛtiḥ kāpi kalpanā || 599
15.600 . rūpaṃ sā tvasvarūpeṇa tadrūpaṃ chādayatyalam |
15.600 . yā kācitkalpanā saṃvittattvasyākhaṇḍitātmanaḥ || 600
15.601 . saṃkocakāriṇī sarvaḥ sa grahastāṃ parityajet |
15.601 . śrīmadānandaśāstre ca kathitaṃ parameṣṭhinā || 601
15.602 . nirapekṣaḥ prabhurvāmo na śuddhyā tatra kāraṇam |
15.602 . devītṛptirmakhe raktamāṃsairno śaucayojanāt || 602
15.603 . dvijāntyajaiḥ samaṃ kāryā carcānte'pi marīcayaḥ |
15.603 . avikārakṛtastena vikalpānnirayo bhavet || 603
15.604 . sarvadevamayaḥ kāyaḥ sarvaprāṇiṣviti sphuṭam |
15.604 . śrīmadbhirnakuleśādyairapyetatsunirūpitam || 604
15.605 . śarīramevāyatanaṃ nānyadāyatanaṃ vrajet |
15.605 . tīrthamekaṃ smarenmantramanyatīrthāni varjayet || 605
15.606 . vidhimenaṃ sukhaṃ jñātvā vidhijālaṃ parityajet |
15.606 . samādhirniścayaṃ muktvā na cānyenopalabhyate || 606
15.607 . iti matvā vidhānajñaḥ saṃmohaṃ parivarjayet |
15.607 . mantrasya hṛdayaṃ muktvā na cānyatparamaṃ kvacit || 607
15.608 . iti matvā vidhānajño mantrajālaṃ parityajet |
15.608 . naivedyaṃ prāśayennadyāstaccheṣaṃ ca jale kṣipet || 608
15.609 . tairbhukte na bhaveddoṣo jalajaiḥ pūrvadīkṣitaiḥ |
15.609 . avayaśpālanīyatvātparattvena saṃgamāt || 609
15.610 . jñānaprāptyabhyupāyatvātsamayāste prakīrtitāḥ |
15.610 . evaṃ saṃśrāvya samayāndevaṃ saṃpūjya daiśikaḥ || 610
15.611 . visarjayetsvacidvyomni śānte mūrtivilāpanāt |
15.611 . yadi putrakadīkṣāsya na kāryā samanantaram || 611
15.612 . tadābhiṣiñcetsāstreṇa śivakumbhena taṃ śiśum |
15.612 . ātmānaṃ ca tato yasmājjalamūrtirmaheśvaraḥ || 612
15.613 . mantrayuṅnikhilāpyāyī kāryaṃ tadabhiṣecanam |
15.613 . iti samayadīkṣaṇamidaṃ prakāśitaṃ vistarācca saṃkṣepāt ||



c16 atha śrītantrāloke ṣoḍaśamāhnikam

16.1 . atha putrakatvasiddhyai nirūpyate śivanirūpito 'tra vidhiḥ |
16.1 . yadā tu samayasthasya putrakatve niyojanam |
16.1 . gurutve sādhakatve vā kartumicchati daiśikaḥ || 1
16.2 . tadādhivāsaṃ kṛtvāhni dvitīye maṇḍalaṃ likhet |
16.2 . sāmudāyikayāge'tha tathānyatra yathoditam || 2
16.3 . ṣaḍaṣṭataddviguṇitacaturviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
16.3 . cakrapañcakamākhyātaṃ śāstre śrīpūrvasaṃjñite || 3
16.4 . dvātriṃśattaddviguṇitaṃ śrīmattraiśirase mate |
16.4 . asaṃkhyacakrasaṃbandhaḥ śrīsiddhādau nirūpitaḥ || 4
16.5 . tasmādyathātathā yāgaṃ yāvaccakreṇa saṃmitam |
16.5 . pūjayedyena tenātra triśūlatrayamālikhet || 5
16.6 . triśūlatritaye devītrayaṃ paryāyavṛttitaḥ |
16.6 . madhyasavyānyabhedena pūrṇaṃ saṃpūjitaṃ bhavet || 6
16.7 . vartanā maṇḍalasyāgre saṃkṣepādupadekṣyate |
16.7 . ālikhya maṇḍalaṃ gandhavastreṇaivāsya mārjanam || 7
16.8 . kṛtvā snāto guruḥ prāgvanmaṇḍalāgre'tra devatāḥ |
16.8 . bāhyagāḥ pūjayeddvāradeśe ca dvāradevatāḥ || 8
16.9 . maṇḍalasya purobhāge tadaiśānadiśaḥ kramāt |
16.9 . āgneyyantaṃ gaṇeśādīn kṣetrapāntānprapūjayet || 9
16.10 . gaṇapatiguruparamākhyāḥ parameṣṭhī pūrvasiddhavākkṣetrapatiḥ |
16.10 . iti saptakamākhyātaṃ gurupaṅktividhau prapūjyamasmadgurubhiḥ || 10
16.11 . tata ājñāṃ gṛhītvā tu puṣpadhūpādipūjitam |
16.11 . pūjyamādhāraśaktyādi śūlamūlātprabhṛtyalam || 11
16.12 . śivāntaṃ sitapadmānte triśūlānāṃ traye kramāt |
16.12 . madhyaśūle madhyagaḥ syātsadbhāvaḥ parayā saha || 12
16.13 . vāme cāparayā sākaṃ navātmā dakṣagaṃ param |
16.13 . triśūle dakṣiṇe madhyaśṛṅgastho ratiśekharaḥ || 13
16.14 . syātparāparayā sākaṃ dakṣe bhairavasatpare |
16.14 . vāme triśūle madhyastho navātmāparayā saha || 14
16.15 . syātpare parayā sākaṃ vāmāre saṃśca bhairavaḥ |
16.15 . itthaṃ sarvagatatve śrīparādevyāḥ sthite sati || 15
16.16 . yāgo bhavetsusaṃpūrṇastadadhiṣṭhānamātrataḥ |
16.16 . ekaśūle'pyato yāge cintayettadadhiṣṭhitam || 16
16.17 . avidhijño vidhānajña ityevaṃ trīśikoditam |
16.17 . tato madhye tathā dakṣe vāme śṛṅge ca sarvataḥ || 17
16.18 . lokapālāstraparyantamekātmatvena pūjayet |
16.18 . paratvena ca sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ prapūjayet || 18
16.19 . śrīmantaṃ mātṛsadbhāvabhaṭṭārakamanāmayam |
16.19 . tato'pi bhogayāgena vidyāṅgaṃ bhairavāṣṭakam || 19
16.20 . yāmalaṃ cakradevīśca svasthāne pūjayedbahiḥ |
16.20 . lokapālānastrayutāngandhapuṣpāsavādibhiḥ || 20
16.21 . pūjayetparayā bhaktyā vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ |
16.21 . tataḥ kumbhāstrakalaśīmaṇḍalasthānalātmanām || 21
16.22 . pañcānāmanusandhānaṃ kuryādadvayabhāvanāt |
16.22 . ye tu tāmadvayavyāptiṃ na vindanti śivātmikām || 22
16.23 . mantranāḍīprayogeṇa te viśantyadvaye pathi |
16.23 . svadakṣiṇena niḥsṛtya maṇḍalasthasya vāmataḥ || 23
16.24 . praviśyānyena niḥsṛtya kumbhasthe karkarīgate |
16.24 . vahnisthe ca krameṇetthaṃ yāvatsvasminsvavāmataḥ || 24
16.25 . mūlānusandhānabalātprāṇatantūmbhane sati |
16.25 . itthamaikyasphurattātmā vyāptisaṃvitprakāśate || 25
16.26 . tato viśeṣapūjāṃ ca kuryādadvayabhāvitām |
16.26 . yacchivādvayapīyūṣasaṃsiktaṃ paramaṃ hi tat || 26
16.27 . tenārghapuṣpagandhāderāsavasya paśoratha |
16.27 . yā śivādvayatādṛṣṭiḥ sā śuddhiḥ paramīkṛtiḥ || 27

16.55 . śivajñānaṃ mantralokaprāptistatparivāratā |

16.59 . vyādhicchedauṣadhatapoyojanātra nidarśanam |

16.61 . tenaitanmāraṇaṃ noktaṃ dīkṣeyaṃ citrarūpiṇī |

16.62 . iyaṃ tu yojanaiva syātpaśordevāya tarpaṇe |

16.69 . bhogyīcikīrṣitaṃ naiva kuryādanyatra taṃ paśum |

16.70 . taṃ paśuṃ kiṃtu kāṅkṣā cedviśeṣe taṃ tu ḍhaukayet |

16.71 . paśorvapāmedasī ca gālite vahnimadhyataḥ |

16.76 . samāveśaya māṃ svātmaraśmibhiryadahaṃ śivaḥ |

16.79 . tejorūpeṇa mantrāṃśca śivahaste samarcayet |

16.80 . ātmānaṃ bhāvayetpaścādekakaṃ jalacandravat |

16.81 . maṇḍalastho'hamevāyaṃ sākṣī cākhilakarmaṇām |

16.82 . homādhikaraṇatvena vahnāvahamavasthitaḥ |

16.83 . sāmānyatejorūpāntarāhūtā bhuvaneśvarāḥ |

16.84 . ā yāgāntamahaṃ kumbhe saṃsthito vighnaśāntaye |
16.84 . sāmānyarūpatā yena viśeṣāpyāyakāriṇī || 84
16.85 . śiṣyadehe ca tatpāśaśithilatvaprasiddhaye |

16.86 . sākṣātsvadehasaṃstho'haṃ kartānugrahakarmaṇām |

16.87 . bhinnakāryākṛtivrātendriyacakrānusandhimān |

16.88 . evamālocya yenaiṣo'dhvanā dīkṣāṃ cikīrṣati |

16.89 . anusandhibalānte ca samāsavyāsabhedataḥ |

16.92 . kartāhamasya tannānyādhīnaṃ ca madadhiṣṭhitam |

16.93 . matsamatvaṃ gato janturmukta ityabhidhīyate |

16.97 . śodhyādhvani ca vinyaste tatraiva pariśodhakam |

16.99 . aparaṃ parāparaṃ ca paraṃ ca vidhimicchayā |

16.100 . lalāṭāntaṃ vedavasau randhrāntaṃ rasarandhrake |
16.100 . vasukhendau dvādaśāntamityeṣa trividho vidhiḥ || 100
16.101 . krameṇa kathyate dṛṣṭaḥ śāstre śrīpūrvasaṃjñite |

16.102 . dharā jalādimūlāntaṃ pratyekaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ kramāt |

16.103 . puṃsaḥ kalāntaṃ ṣaṭtattvīṃ pratyekaṃ tryaṅgule kṣipet |

16.104 . sadāśivāntaṃ māyādicatuṣkaṃ caturaṅgule |

16.106 . jalāddhyantaṃ sārdhayugmaṃ mūlaṃ tryaṅgulamityataḥ |
16.106 . dvādaśāṅgulatādhikyādvidhireṣa parāparaḥ || 106
16.107 . jalāddhyantaṃ tryaṅgule cedavyaktaṃ tu catuṣṭaye |
16.107 . taccaturviṃśatyādhikyātparo'pyaṣṭaśate vidhiḥ || 107
16.108 . trividhonmānakaṃ vyaktaṃ vasudigbhyo ravikṣayāt |
16.108 . mayatantre tathācoktaṃ tattatsvaphalavāñchayā || 108
16.109 . navapañcacatustryekatattvanyāse svayaṃ dhiyā |
16.109 . nyāsaṃ prakalpayettāvattattvāntarbhāvacintanāt || 109
16.110 . kalāpañcakavedāṇḍanyāso'nenaiva lakṣitaḥ |
16.110 . uktaṃ ca triśirastantre svādhārasthaṃ yathāsthitam || 110
16.111 . dvādaśāṅgulamutthānaṃ dehātītaṃ samaṃ tataḥ |
16.111 . dvāsaptatirdaśa dve ca dehasthaṃ śiraso'ntataḥ || 111
16.112 . pādādārabhya suśroṇi anāhatapadāvadhi |
16.112 . dehātīte'pi viśrāntyā saṃvitteḥ kalpanāvaśāt || 112
16.113 . dehatvamiti tasmātsyādutthānaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam |

16.114 . puranyāso'tha gulphāntaṃ bhūḥ purāṇyatra ṣoḍaśa |

16.115 . dviraṇḍāntaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ tu cchagalāṇḍamathābdhiṣu |
16.115 . devayogāṣṭake dve hi pratyekāṅgulapādataḥ || 115
16.116 . iti pradhānaparyantaṃ ṣaṭcatvāriṃśadaṅgulam |
16.116 . ṣaṭpañcāśatpurāṇītthaṃ prāgdharāyāṃ tu ṣoḍaśa || 116
16.117 . tato'pyardhāṅgulavyāptyā ṣaṭpurāṇyaṅgulatraye |
16.117 . catvāri yugma ekasminnekaṃ ca puramaṅgule || 117
16.118 . sarāge puṃspurāṇīśasaṃkhyānītthaṃ ṣaḍaṅgule |

16.119 . saṃvartajyotiṣorevaṃ kalātattvagayoḥ kramāt |
16.119 . śūrapañcāntapurayorniyatau caikayugmatā || 119
16.120 . śrīpūrvaśāstre taccoktaṃ parameśena śaṃbhunā |

16.121 . asāratvātkramasyādau niyatiḥ parataḥ kalā |

16.122 . ekavīraśikheśaśrīkaṇṭhāḥ kāle trayastraye |
16.122 . kālasya pūrvaṃ vinyāso niyaterabhidhīyate || 122
16.123 . athavānyonyasaṃjñābhirvyapadeśo hi dṛśyate |

16.124 . tato'pyaṅguṣṭhamātrāntaṃ māyātattvasthamaṣṭakam |
16.124 . pratyekamardhāṅgulataḥ syādaṅgulacatuṣṭaye || 124
16.125 . itthaṃ dvyakṣṇi purāṇyaṣṭāviṃśatiḥ puruṣānniśi |
16.125 . puratrayaṃ dvayostryaṃśanyūnāṅgulamiti kramāt || 125
16.126 . dvayordvayaṃ pañcapurī vaidyīye caturaṅgule |
16.126 . tata aiśapurāṇyaṣṭau catuṣke'rdhāṅgulakramāt || 126
16.127 . tatastrīṇi dvaye dve ca dvayoritthaṃ catuṣṭaye |
16.127 . sādāśivaṃ pañcakaṃ syāditthaṃ vasvekakaṃ ravau || 127
16.128 . ṣoḍaśakaṃ rasaviśikhaṃ vasudvikaṃ vasuśaśīti puravargāḥ |

16.129 . aṣṭādaśādhikaśataṃ purāṇi dehe'tra caturaśītimite |
16.129 . vinyastāni taditthaṃ śeṣe tu vyāpakaṃ śivaṃ tattvam || 129
16.130 . iti vidhiraparaḥ kathitaḥ parāparākhyo rasaśrutisthāne |

16.131 . lakulāderyogāṣṭakaparyantasyātra bhuvanapūgasya |

16.132 . aparādividhitraitādatha nyāsaḥ padādhvanaḥ |

16.133 . tayaiva dīkṣā kāryā cettadeyaṃ nyāsakalpanā |

16.134 . tattattvādyanusāreṇa tatrāntarbhāvyate tathā |
16.134 . svapradhānatvayoge tu dīkṣāyāṃ padapaddhatim || 134
16.135 . nyasyetkrameṇa tattvādivadanānavalokinīm |
16.135 . caturṣvaṣṭāsu cāṣṭāsu daśasvatha daśasvatha || 135
16.136 . daśasvatho pañcadaśasvatha vedaśarenduṣu |
16.136 . dharāpadānnavapadīṃ mātṛkāmālinīgatām || 136
16.137 . yojayedvyāptṛ daśamaṃ padaṃ tu śivasaṃjñitam |
16.137 . dharāpadaṃ varjayitvā pañca yāni padāni tu || 137
16.138 . vidhidvayaṃ syānnikṣipya dvādaśa dvādaśāṅgulān |
16.138 . mantrādhvano'pyeṣa eva vidhirvinyāsayojane || 138
16.139 . vyāptimātraṃ hi bhidyetetyuktaṃ prāgeva tattathā |
16.139 . varṇādhvano'tha vinyāsaḥ kathyate'tra vidhitraye || 139
16.140 . ekaṃ caturṣu pratyekaṃ dvayoraṅgulayoḥ kramāt |
16.140 . trayoviṃśativarṇī syātṣaḍvarṇyekaikaśastriṣu || 140
16.141 . pratyekamatha catvāraścaturṣviti vilomataḥ |
16.141 . mālinīmātṛkārṇāḥ syurvyāptṛ śaivaṃ rasendutaḥ || 141
16.142 . varjayitvādyavarṇaṃ tu tattvavatsyādravīnnavīn |
16.142 . tāṃ trayoviṃśatau varṇeṣvapyanyatsyādvidhidvayam || 142
16.143 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tenādau tattveṣūktaṃ vidhitrayam |
16.143 . atidiṣṭaṃ tu tadbhinnābhinnavarṇadvaye samam || 143
16.144 . dvividho'pi hi varṇānāṃ ṣaḍvidho bheda ucyate |
16.144 . tattvamārgavidhānena jñātavyaḥ paramārthataḥ || 144
16.145 . upadeśātideśābhyāṃ yaduktaṃ tatpadādiṣu |
16.145 . bhūyo'tidiṣṭaṃ tatraiva śāstre'smaddhṛdayeśvare || 145

16.256 . vaiṣṇavādiḥ paśuḥ prokto na yogyaḥ patiśāsane |

16.263 . karapāṇyabhijalpau tau saṃkīryetāṃ kathaṃ kila |

16.264 . vyavahārāttu sā sākṣāccitropākhyāvimarśinī |
16.264 . tadvimarśodayaḥ prācyasvavimarśamayaḥ sphuret || 264
16.265 . yāvadbālasya saṃvittirakṛtrimavimarśane |
16.265 . tena tanmantraśabdārthaviśeṣotthaṃ vikalpanam || 265
16.266 . śabdāntarotthādbhedena paśyatā mantra ādṛtaḥ |
16.266 . yaccāpi bījapiṇḍāderuktaṃ prāgbodharūpakam || 266
16.267 . tattasyaiva kuto'nyasya tatkasmādanyakalpanā |
16.267 . etadarthaṃ guroryatnāllakṣaṇe tatra tatra tat || 267
16.268 . lakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ hyeṣa mantratantraviśāradaḥ |
16.268 . tena mantrārthasaṃbodhe mantravārtikamādarāt || 268
16.269 . ūhāpohaprayogaṃ vā sarvathā gururācaret |
16.269 . mantrārthavidabhāve tu sarvathā mantratanmayam || 269
16.270 . guruṃ kuryāttadabhyāsāttatsaṃkalpamayo hyasau |
16.270 . tatsamānābhisaṃjalpo yadā mantrārthabhāvanāt || 270
16.271 . gurorbhavettadā sarvasāmye ko bheda ucyatām |
16.271 . aṃśenāpyatha vaiṣamye na tato'rthakriyā hi sā || 271
16.272 . gomayātkīṭataḥ kīṭa ityevaṃ nyāyato yadā |
16.272 . saṃjalpāntarato'pyarthakriyāṃ tāmeva paśyati || 272
16.273 . tadaiṣa satyasaṃjalpaḥ śiva eveti kathyate |
16.273 . sa yadvakti tadeva syānmantro bhogāpavargadaḥ || 273
16.274 . naiṣo'bhinavaguptasya pakṣo mantrārpitātmanaḥ |
16.274 . yo'rthakriyāmāha bhinnāṃ kīṭayorapi tādṛśoḥ || 274
16.275 . mantrārpitamanāḥ kiṃcidvadanyattu viṣaṃ haret |
16.275 . tanmantra eva śabdaḥ sa paraṃ tatra ghaṭādivat || 275
16.276 . kāntāsaṃbhogasaṃjalpasundaraḥ kāmukaḥ sadā |
16.276 . tatsaṃskṛto'pyanyadeṣa kurvansvātmani tṛpyati || 276
16.277 . tathā tanmantrasaṃjalpabhāvito'nyadapi bruvan |
16.277 . anicchurapi tadrūpastathā kāryakaro dhruvam || 277
16.278 . vikalpayannapyekārthaṃ yato'nyadapi paśyati |
16.278 . viṣāpahārimantrādītyuktaṃ śrīpūrvaśāsane || 278
16.279 . yadi vā viṣanāśe'pi hetubhedādvicitratā |
16.279 . dhātvāpyāyādikānantakāryabhedādbhaviṣyati || 279
16.280 . tadevaṃ mantrasaṃjalpavikalpābhyāsayogataḥ |
16.280 . bhāvyavastusphuṭībhāvaḥ saṃjalpahrāsayogataḥ || 280
16.281 . vastveva bhāvayatyeṣa na saṃjalpamimaṃ punaḥ |
16.281 . gṛhṇāti bhāsanopāyaṃ bhāte tatra tu tena kim || 281
16.282 . evaṃ saṃjalpanirhrāse suparisphuṭatātmakam |
16.282 . akṛttrimavimarśātma sphuredvastvavikalpakam || 282
16.283 . nirvikalpā ca sā saṃvidyadyathā paśyati sphuṭam |
16.283 . tattathaiva tathātmatvādvastuno'pi bahiḥsthiteḥ || 283
16.284 . viśeṣatastvamāyīyaśivatābhedaśālinaḥ |
16.284 . mokṣe'bhyupāyaḥ saṃjalpo bandhamokṣau tataḥ kila || 284
16.285 . vikalpe'pi guroḥ samyagabhinnaśivatājuṣaḥ |
16.285 . avikalpakaparyantapratīkṣā nopayujyate || 285
16.286 . tadvimarśasvabhāvā hi sā vācyā mantradevatā |
16.286 . mahāsaṃvitsamāsannetyuktaṃ śrīgamaśāsane || 286
16.287 . nikaṭasthā yathā rājñāmanyeṣāṃ sādhayantyalam |
16.287 . siddhiṃ rājopagāṃ śīghramevaṃ mantrādayaḥ parām || 287
16.288 . uktābhiprāyagarbhaṃ taduktaṃ śrīmālinīmate |
16.288 . mantrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kasmādityukte munibhiḥ kila || 288
16.289 . yogamekatvamicchanti vastuno'nyena vastunā |
16.289 . tadvastu jñeyamityuktaṃ heyatvādiprasiddhaye || 289
16.290 . tatprasiddhyai śivenoktaṃ jñānaṃ yadupavarṇitam |
16.290 . sabījayogasaṃsiddhyai mantralakṣaṇamapyalam || 290
16.291 . na cādhikāritā dīkṣāṃ vinā yoge'sti śāṅkare |
16.291 . kriyājñānavibhedena sā ca dvedhā nigadyate || 291
16.292 . dvividhā sā prakartavyā tena caitadudāhṛtam |
16.292 . naca yogādhikāritvamekamevānayā bhavet || 292
16.293 . api mantrādhikāritvaṃ muktiśca śivadīkṣayā |
16.293 . anenaitadapi proktaṃ yogī tattvaikyasiddhaye || 293
16.294 . mantramevāśrayenmūlaṃ nirvikalpāntamādṛtaḥ |
16.294 . mantrābhyāsena bhogaṃ vā mokṣaṃ vāpi prasādhayan || 294
16.295 . tatrādhikāritālabdhyai dīkṣāṃ gṛhṇīta daiśikāt |
16.295 . tena mantrajñānayogabalādyadyatprasādhayet || 295
16.296 . tatsyādasyānyatattve'pi yuktasya guruṇā śiśoḥ |
16.296 . dīkṣā hyasyopayujyeta saṃskriyāyāṃ sa saṃskṛtaḥ || 296
16.297 . svabalenaiva bhogaṃ vā mokṣaṃ vā labhate budhaḥ |
16.297 . tena vijñānayogādibalī prāksamayī bhavan || 297
16.298 . putrako vā na tāvānsyādapitu svabalocitaḥ |
16.298 . yastu vijñānayogādivandhyaḥ so'ndho yathā pathi || 298
16.299 . daiśikāyatta eva syādbhoge muktau ca sarvathā |
16.299 . dīkṣā ca kevalā jñānaṃ vināpi nijamāntaram || 299
16.300 . mocikaiveti kathitaṃ yuktyā cāgamataḥ purā |
16.300 . yastu dīkṣākṛtāmevāpekṣya yojanikāṃ śiśuḥ || 300
16.301 . sphuṭībhūtyai taducitaṃ jñānaṃ yogamathāśritaḥ |
16.301 . so'pi yatraiva yuktaḥ syāttanmayatvaṃ prapadyate || 301
16.302 . gurudīkṣāmantraśāstrādhīnasarvasthitistataḥ |
16.302 . duṣṭānāmeva sarveṣāṃ bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyatām || 302
16.303 . karmaṇāṃ śodhanaṃ kāryaṃ bubhukṣorna śubhātmanām |
16.303 . yaḥ punarlaukikaṃ bhogaṃ rājyasvargādikaṃ śiśuḥ || 303
16.304 . tyaktvā lokottaraṃ bhogamīpsustasya śubheṣvapi |
16.304 . tatra dravyamayīṃ dīkṣāṃ kurvannājyatilādikaiḥ || 304
16.305 . karmāsya śodhayāmīti juhuyāddaiśikottamaḥ |
16.305 . jñānamayyāṃ tu dīkṣāyāṃ tadviśuddhyati sandhitaḥ || 305
16.306 . guroḥ svasaṃvidrūḍhasya balāttatprakṣayo bhavet |
16.306 . yadāsyāśubhakarmāṇi śuddhāni syustadā śubham || 306
16.307 . svatāratamyāśrayaṇādadhvamadhye prasūtidam |
16.307 . śubhapākakramopāttaphalabhogasamāptitaḥ || 307
16.308 . yatraiṣa yojitastatstho bhāvikarmakṣaye kṛte |
16.308 . bhāvināṃ cādyadehasthadehāntaravibhedinām || 308
16.309 . aśubhāṃśaviśuddhau syādbhogasyaivānupakṣayaḥ |
16.309 . bhuñjānasyāsya satataṃ bhogānmāyālayāntataḥ || 309
16.310 . na duḥkhaphaladaṃ dehādyadhvamadhye'pi kiṃcana |
16.310 . tato māyālaye bhuktasamastasukhabhogakaḥ || 310
16.311 . niṣkale sakale vaiti layaṃ yojanikābalāt |
16.311 . iti prameyaṃ kathitaṃ dīkṣā kāle guroryathā || 311

c17 atha śrītantrāloke saptadaśamāhnikam

17.1 . atha bhairavatādātmyadāyinīṃ prakriyāṃ bruve |
17.1 . evaṃ maṇḍalakumbhāgniśiṣyasvātmasu pañcasu || 1
17.2 . gṛhītvā vyāptimaikyena nyasyādhvānaṃ ca śiṣyagam |
17.2 . karmamāyāṇumalinatrayaṃ bāhau gale tathā || 2
17.3 . śikhāyāṃ ca kṣipetsūtragranthiyogena daiśikaḥ |
17.3 . tasyātadrūpatābhānaṃ malo granthiḥ sa kīrtyate || 3
17.4 . itipratītidārḍhyārthaṃ bahirgranthyupakalpanam |
17.4 . bāhū karmāspadaṃ viṣṇurmāyātmā galasaṃśritaḥ || 4
17.5 . adhovahā śikhāṇutvaṃ tenetthaṃ kalpanā kṛtā |
17.5 . naraśaktiśivākhyasya trayasya bahubhedatām || 5
17.6 . vaktuṃ tristriguṇaṃ sūtraṃ granthaye parikalpayet |
17.6 . tejojalānnatritayaṃ tredhā pratyekamapyadaḥ || 6
17.7 . śrutyante ke'pyataḥ śuklakṛṣṇaraktaṃ prapedire |
17.7 . tato'gnau tarpitāśeṣamantre cidvyomamātrake || 7
17.8 . sāmānyarūpe tattvānāṃ kramācchuddhiṃ samācaret |
17.8 . tatra svamantrayogena dharāmāvāhayetpurā || 8
17.9 . iṣṭvā puṣpādibhiḥ sarpistilādyairatha tarpayet |
17.9 . tattattvavyāpikāṃ paścānmāyātattvādhidevatām || 9
17.10 . māyāśaktiṃ svamantreṇāvāhyābhyarcya pratarpayet |
17.10 . āvāhane mātṛkārṇaṃ mālinyarṇaṃ ca pūjane || 10
17.11 . kuryāditi guruḥ prāha svarūpāpyāyanadvayāt |
17.11 . tāro varṇo'tha saṃbuddhipadaṃ tvāmityataḥ param || 11
17.12 . uttamaikayutaṃ karmapadaṃ dīpakamapyataḥ |
17.12 . tabhyaṃ nāma caturthyantaṃ tato'pyucitadīpakam || 12
17.13 . ityūhamantrayogena tattatkarma pravartayet |
17.13 . āvāhanānantaraṃ hi karma sarvaṃ nigadyate || 13
17.14 . āvāhanaṃ ca saṃbodhaḥ svasvabhāvavyavasthiteḥ |
17.14 . bhāvasyāhaṃmayasvātmatādātmyāveśyamānatā || 14
17.15 . śāktī bhūmiśca saivoktā yasyāṃ mukhyāsti pūjyatā |
17.15 . abhātatvādabhedācca nahyasau nṛśivātmanoḥ || 15
17.16 . jaḍābhāseṣu tattveṣu saṃvitsthityai tato guruḥ |
17.16 . āvāhanavibhaktiṃ prākkṛtvā turyavibhaktitaḥ || 16
17.17 . namaskārāntatāyogātpūrṇāṃ sattāṃ prakalpayet |
17.17 . tataḥ pūrṇasvabhāvatvaṃ tadrūpodrekayogataḥ || 17
17.18 . dhyeyodreko bhaveddhyātṛprahvībhāvavaśādyataḥ |
17.18 . āvāhyeṣṭvā pratarpyeti śrīsvacchande nirūpitam || 18
17.19 . anenaiva pathāneyamityasmadguravo jaguḥ |
17.19 . paratvena tu yatpūjyaṃ tatsvatantracidātmakam || 19
17.20 . anavacchitprakāśatvānna prakāśyaṃ tu kutracit |
17.20 . tasya hyetatprapūjyatvadhyeyatvādi yadullaset || 20
17.21 . tasyaiva tatsvatantratvaṃ yātidurghaṭakāritā |
17.21 . saṃbodharūpe tattasmin kathaṃ saṃbodhanā bhavet || 21
17.22 . prakāśanāyāṃ na syātprakāśasya prakāśatā |
17.22 . saṃbodhanavibhaktyaiva vinā karmādiśaktitām || 22
17.23 . svātantryāttaṃ darśayituṃ tatrohamimamācaret |
17.23 . devamāvāhayāmīti tato devāya dīpakam || 23
17.24 . prāgyuktyā pūrṇatādāyi namaḥsvāhādikaṃ bhavet |
17.24 . nutiḥ pūrṇatvamagnīndusaṃghaṭṭāpyāyatā param || 24
17.25 . āpyāyakaṃ ca procchālaṃ vauṣaḍādi pradīpayet |
17.25 . tatra bāhye'pi tādātmyaprasiddhaṃ karma codyate || 25
17.26 . yadi karmapadaṃ tanno gururabhyūhayetkvacit |
17.26 . anābhāsitatadvastubhāsanāya niyujyate || 26
17.27 . mantraḥ kiṃ tena tatra syātsphuṭaṃ yatrāvabhāsi tat |
17.27 . tena prokṣaṇasaṃsekajapādividhiṣu dhruvam || 27
17.28 . tatkarmābhyūhanaṃ kuryātpratyuta vyavadhātṛtām |
17.28 . bahistathātmatābhāve kāryaṃ karmapadohanam || 28
17.29 . tṛptāvāhutihutabhukpāśaploṣacchidādiṣu |
17.29 . yatroddiṣṭe vidhau paścāttadanantaiḥ kriyātmakaiḥ || 29
17.30 . aṃśaiḥ sādhyaṃ na tatroho dīkṣaṇādividhiṣviva |
17.30 . tataḥ śiṣyasya tattattvasthāne'streṇa pratāḍanam || 30
17.31 . kṛtvātha śivahastena hṛdayaṃ parimarśayet |
17.31 . tataḥ svanāḍīmārgeṇa hṛdayaṃ prāpya vai śiśoḥ || 31
17.32 . śiṣyātmanā sahaikatvaṃ gatvādāya ca taṃ hṛdā |
17.32 . puṭitaṃ haṃsarūpākhyaṃ tatra saṃhāramudrayā || 32
17.33 . kuryādātmīyahṛdayasthitamapyavabhāsakam |
17.33 . śiṣyadehasya tejobhī raśmimātrāviyogataḥ || 33
17.34 . svabandhasthānacalanātsvatantrasthānalābhataḥ |
17.34 . svakarmāparatantratvātsarvatrotpattimarhati || 34
17.35 . tenātmahṛdayānītaṃ prākkṛtvā pudgalaṃ tataḥ |
17.35 . māyāyāṃ taddharātattvaśarīrāṇyasya saṃsṛjet || 35
17.36 . tatrāsya garbhādhānaṃ ca yuktaṃ puṃsavanādibhiḥ |
17.36 . garbhaniṣkrāmaparyantairekāṃ kurvīta saṃskriyām || 36
17.37 . jananaṃ bhogabhoktṛtvaṃ militvaikātha saṃskriyā |
17.37 . tato'sya teṣu bhogeṣu kuryāttanmayatāṃ layam || 37
17.38 . tatastattattvapāśānāṃ vicchedaṃ samupācaret |
17.38 . saṃskārāṇāṃ catuṣke'sminnaparāṃ ca parāparām || 38
17.39 . mantrāṇāṃ pañcadaśakaṃ parāṃ vā yojayetkramāt |
17.39 . pivanyādyaṣṭakaṃ śastrādikaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ parā tathā || 39
17.40 . iti pañcadaśaite syuḥ kramāllīnatvasaṃskṛtau |
17.40 . aparāmantramuktvā prāgamukātmana ityatha || 40
17.41 . garbhādhānaṃ karomīti punarmantraṃ tameva ca |
17.41 . svāhāntamuccarandadyādāhutitritayaṃ guruḥ || 41
17.42 . paraṃ parāparāmantramamukātmana ityatha |
17.42 . jātasya bhogabhoktṛtvaṃ karomyatha parāparām || 42
17.43 . ante svāheti proccārya vitarettisra āhutīḥ |
17.43 . uccārya pivanīmantramamukātmana ityatha || 43
17.44 . bhoge layaṃ karomīti punarmantraṃ tameva ca |
17.44 . svāhāntamāhutīstisro dadyādājyatilādibhiḥ || 44
17.45 . eṣa eva vamanyādau vidhiḥ pañcadaśāntake |
17.45 . pūrvaṃ parātmakaṃ mantramamukātmana ityatha || 45
17.46 . pāśācchedaṃ karomīti parāmantraḥ punastataḥ |
17.46 . huṃ svāhā phaṭsamuccārya dadyāttisro'pyathāhutīḥ || 46
17.47 . saṃskārāṇāṃ catuṣke'sminye mantrāḥ kathitā mayā |
17.47 . teṣu karmapadātpūrvaṃ dharātattvapadaṃ vadet || 47
17.48 . tato dharātattvapatimāmantryeṣṭvā pratarpya ca |
17.48 . śivābhimānasaṃrabdho gururevaṃ samādiśet || 48
17.49 . tattveśvara tvayā nāsya putrakasya śivājñayā |
17.49 . pratibandhaḥ prakartavyo yātuḥ padamanāmayam || 49
17.50 . tato yadi samīheta dharātattvāntarālagam |
17.50 . pṛthakśodhayituṃ mantrī bhuvanādyadhvapañcakam || 50
17.51 . aparāmantrataḥ prāgvattisrastisrastadāhutīḥ |
17.51 . dadyātpuraṃ śodhayāmītyūhayuktaṃ prasannadhīḥ || 51
17.52 . evaṃ kalāmantrapadavarṇeṣvapi vicakṣaṇaḥ |
17.52 . tisrastisro hutīrdadyātpṛthaksāmastyato'pivā || 52
17.53 . tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā parayā vauṣaḍantayā |
17.53 . aparāmantrataḥ śiṣyamuddhṛtyātmahṛdaṃ nayet || 53
17.54 . yadā tvekena śuddhena tadantarbhāvacintanāt |
17.54 . na pṛthakśodhayettattvanāthasaṃśravaṇātparam || 54
17.55 . tadā pūrṇāṃ vitīryāṇumutkṣipyātmani yojayet |
17.55 . tātsthyātmasaṃsthyayogāya tayaivāparayāhutīḥ || 55
17.56 . sakarmapadayā dadyāditi kecittu manvate |
17.56 . anye tu guravaḥ prāhurbhāvanāmayamīdṛśam || 56
17.57 . nātra bāhyāhutirdeyā daiśikasya pṛthakpunaḥ |
17.57 . dadyādvā yadi no doṣaḥ syādupāyaḥ sa bhāvane || 57
17.58 . evaṃ prāktanatātsthyātmasaṃsthatve yojayedguruḥ |
17.58 . tataḥ śiṣyahṛdaṃ neyaḥ sa ātmā tāvato'dhvanaḥ || 58
17.59 . śuddhastaddārḍhyasiddhyai ca pūrṇā syātparayā punaḥ |
17.59 . mahāpāśupataṃ pūrvaṃ vilomasya viśuddhaye || 59
17.60 . juhomi punarastreṇa vauṣaḍanta iti kṣipet |
17.60 . punaḥ pūrṇāṃ tato māyāmabhyarcyātha visarjayet || 60
17.61 . dharātattvaṃ viśuddhaṃ sajjalena śuddharūpiṇā |
17.61 . bhāvayenmiśritaṃ vāri śuddhiyogyaṃ tato bhavet || 61
17.62 . tathā tattatpurātattvamiśraṇāduttarottaram |
17.62 . sarvā śivībhavettattvāvalī śuddhānyathā pṛthak || 62
17.63 . pṛthaktvaṃ ca malo māyābhidhānastasya saṃbhave |
17.63 . karmakṣaye'pi no muktirbhavedvidyeśvarādivat || 63
17.64 . tato'pi jalatattvasya vahnau vyomni cidātmake |
17.64 . āhvānādyakhilaṃ yāvattejasyasya vimaśraṇam || 64
17.65 . evaṃ kramātkalātattve śuddhe pāśaṃ bhujāśritam |
17.65 . chindyātkalā hi sā kiṃcitkartṛtvonmīlanātmikā || 65
17.66 . karmākhyamalajṛmbhātmā taṃ ca granthiṃ srugagragam |
17.66 . pūrṇāhutyā samaṃ vahnimantratejasi nirdahet || 66
17.67 . mantro hi viśvarūpaḥ sannupāśrayavaśāttathā |
17.67 . vyaktarūpastato vahnau pāśaploṣavidhāyakaḥ || 67
17.68 . pluṣṭo līnasvabhāvo'sau pāśastaṃ prati śambhuvat |
17.68 . parameśamahātejaḥśeṣamātratvamaśnute || 68
17.69 . karmapāśe'tra hotavye pūrṇayāsya śubhāśubham |
17.69 . aśubhaṃ vā bhavadbhūtaṃ bhāvi vātha samastakam || 69
17.70 . dahāmi phaṭtrayaṃ vauṣaḍiti pūrṇāṃ vinikṣipet |
17.70 . evaṃ māyāntasaṃśuddhau kaṇṭhapāśaṃ ca homayet || 70
17.71 . pūrṇasya tasya māyākhyaṃ pāśabhedaprathātmakam |
17.71 . dahāmi phaṭtrayaṃ vauṣaḍiti pūrṇāṃ kṣipedguruḥ || 71
17.72 . nirbījā yadi kāryā tu tadātraivāparāṃ kṣipet |
17.72 . pūrṇāṃ samayapāśākhyabījadāhapadānvitām || 72
17.73 . gurau deve tathā śāstre bhaktiḥ kāryāsya nahyasau |
17.73 . samayaḥ śaktipātasya svabhāvo hyeṣa no pṛthak || 73
17.74 . māyānte śuddhimāyāte vāgīśī yā purābhavat |
17.74 . māyā śaktimayī saiva vidyāśaktitvamaśnute || 74
17.75 . tacchuddhavidyāmāhūya vidyāśaktiṃ niyojayet |
17.75 . evaṃ krameṇa saṃśuddhe sadāśivapade'pyalam || 75
17.76 . śikhāṃ granthiyutāṃ chittvā malamāṇavakaṃ dahet |
17.76 . yato'dhikārabhogākhyau dvau pāśau tu sadāśive || 76
17.77 . ityuktyāṇavapāśo'tra māyīyastu niśāvadhiḥ |
17.77 . śiṣyo yathocitaṃ snāyādācāmeddaiśikaḥ svayam || 77
17.78 . āṇavākhye vinirdagdhe hyadhovāhiśikhāmale |
17.78 . tataḥ prāguktasakalaprameyaṃ paricintayan || 78
17.79 . śiṣyadehādimātmīyadehaprāṇādiyojitam |
17.79 . kṛtvātmadehaprāṇāderviśvamantaranusmaret || 79
17.80 . uktaprakriyayā caivaṃ dṛḍhabuddhirananyadhīḥ |
17.80 . prāṇasthaṃ deśakālādhvayugaṃ prāṇaṃ ca śaktigam || 80
17.81 . tāṃ ca saṃvidgatāṃ śuddhāṃ saṃvidaṃ śivarūpiṇīm |
17.81 . śiṣyasaṃvidabhinnāṃ ca mantravahnyādyabhedinīm || 81
17.82 . dhyāyan prāgvatprayogeṇa śivaṃ sakalaniṣkalam |
17.82 . dvyātmakaṃ vā kṣipetpūrṇāṃ praśāntakaraṇena tu || 82
17.83 . uktaṃ traiśirase tantre sarvasaṃpūraṇātmakam |
17.83 . mūlādudayagatyā tu śivenduparisaṃplutam || 83
17.84 . janmāntamadhyakuharamūlasrotaḥsamutthitam |
17.84 . śivārkaraśmibhistīvraiḥ kṣubdhaṃ jñānāmṛtaṃ tu yat || 84
17.85 . tena saṃtarpayetsamyakpraśāntakaraṇena tu |
17.85 . śūnyadhāmābjamadhyasthaprabhākiraṇabhāsvaraḥ || 85
17.86 . ādheyādhāraniḥspandabodhaśāstraparigrahaḥ |
17.86 . janmādheyaprapañcaikasphoṭasaṃghaṭṭaghaṭṭanaḥ || 86
17.87 . mūlasthānātsamārabhya kṛtvā someśamantagam |
17.87 . khamivātiṣṭhate yāvatpraśāntaṃ tāvaducyate || 87
17.88 . uktaṃ śrīpūrvaśāstre ca srucamāpūrya sarpiṣā |
17.88 . kṛtvā śiṣyaṃ tathātmasthaṃ mūlamantramanusmaran || 88
17.89 . śivaṃ śaktiṃ tathātmānaṃ śiṣyaṃ sarpistathānalam |
17.89 . ekīkurvañchanairgaccheddvādaśāntamananyadhīḥ || 89
17.90 . tatra kumbhakamāsthāya dhyāyansakalaniṣkalam |
17.90 . tiṣṭhettāvadanudvigno yāvadājyakṣayo bhvet || 90
17.91 . evaṃ yuktaḥ pare tattve guruṇā śivamūrtinā |
17.91 . na bhūyaḥ paśutāmeti dagdhamāyānibandhanaḥ || 91
17.92 . dehapāte punaḥ prepsedyadi tattveṣu kutracit |
17.92 . bhogān samastavyastatvabhedairante paraṃ padam || 92
17.93 . tadā tattattvabhūmau tu tatsaṃkhyāyāmananyadhīḥ |
17.93 . punaryojanikāṃ kuryātpūrṇāhutyantareṇa tu || 93
17.94 . muktipradā bhogamokṣapradā vā yā prakīrtitā |
17.94 . dīkṣā sā syātsabījatvanirbījātmatayā dvidhā || 94
17.95 . bāle nirjñātamaraṇe tvaśakte vā jarādibhiḥ |
17.95 . kāryā nirbījikā dīkṣā śaktipātabalodaye || 95
17.96 . nirbījāyāṃ sāmayāṃstu pāśānapi viśodhayet |
17.96 . kṛtanirbījadīkṣastu devāgnigurubhaktibhāk || 96
17.97 . iyataiva śivaṃ yāyātsadyo bhogān vibhujya vā |
17.97 . śrīmaddīkṣottare coktaṃ cāre ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgule || 97
17.98 . tattvānyāpādamūrdhāntaṃ bhuvanāni tyajetkramāt |
17.98 . tuṭimātraṃ niṣkalaṃ tadadehaṃ tadahaṃparam || 98
17.99 . śaktyā tatra kṣipāmyenamiti dhyāyaṃstu dīkṣayet |
17.99 . sabījāyāṃ tu dīkṣāyāṃ samayānna viśodhayet || 99
17.100 . viśeṣastvayametasyāṃ yāvajjīvaṃ śiśorguruḥ |
17.100 . śeṣavṛttyai śuddhatattvasṛṣṭiṃ kurvīta pūrṇayā || 100
17.101 . abhinnācchivasaṃbodhajaladheryugapatsphurat |
17.101 . pūrṇāṃ kṣipaṃstattvajālaṃ dhyāyedbhārūpakaṃ sṛtam || 101
17.102 . viśuddhatattvasṛṣṭiṃ vā kuryātkumbhābhiṣecanāt |
17.102 . tathā dhyānabalādeva yadvā pūrṇābhiṣecanaiḥ || 102
17.103 . pṛthivī sthirarūpāsya śivarūpeṇa bhāvitā |
17.103 . sthirīkaroti tāmeva bhāvanāmiti śuddhyati || 103
17.104 . jalamāpyāyayatyenāṃ tejo bhāsvaratāṃ nayet |
17.104 . marudānandasaṃsparśaṃ vyoma vaitatyamāvahet || 104
17.105 . evaṃ tanmātravargo'pi śivatāmaya iṣyate |
17.105 . parānandamahāvyāptiraśeṣamalavicyutiḥ || 105
17.106 . śive gantṛtvamādānamupādeyaśivastutiḥ |
17.106 . śivāmodabharāsvādadarśanasparśanānyalam || 106
17.107 . tadākarṇanamityevamindriyāṇāṃ viśuddhatā |
17.107 . saṃkalpādhyavasāmānāḥ prakāśo raktisaṃsthitī || 107
17.108 . śivātmatvena yatseyaṃ śuddhatā mānasādike |
17.108 . niyamo rañjanaṃ kartṛbhāvaḥ kalanayā saha || 108
17.109 . vedanaṃ heyavastvaṃśaviṣaye suptakalpatā |
17.109 . itthaṃ śivaikyarūḍhasya ṣaṭkañcukagaṇo'pyayam || 109
17.110 . śuddha eva pumān prāptaśivabhāvo viśuddhyati |
17.110 . vidyeśādiṣu tattveṣu naiva kācidaśuddhatā || 110
17.111 . ityevaṃ śuddhatattvānāṃ sṛṣṭyā śiṣyo'pi tanmayaḥ |
17.111 . bhaveddhyetatsūcitaṃ śrīmālinīvijayottare || 111
17.112 . bandhamokṣāvubhāvetāvindriyāṇi jagurbudhāḥ |
17.112 . nigṛhītāni bandhāya vimuktāni vimuktaye || 112
17.113 . etāni vyāpake bhāve yadā syurmanasā saha |
17.113 . muktāni kvāpi viṣaye rodhādbandhāya tāni tu || 113
17.114 . ityevaṃ dvividho bhāvaḥ śuddhāśuddhaprabhedataḥ |
17.114 . indriyāṇāṃ samākhyātaḥ siddhayogīśvare mate || 114
17.115 . śrīmān vidyāgurustvāha pramāṇastutidarśane |
17.115 . samastamantrairdīkṣāyāṃ niyamastveṣa kathyate || 115
17.116 . māyāntaśuddhau sarvāḥ syuḥ kriyā hyaparayā sadā |
17.116 . dvyātmayā sakalānte tu niṣkale parayaiva tu || 116
17.117 . īśānte ca pivanyādi sakalānte'ṅgapañcakam |
17.117 . ityevaṃvidhimālocya karma kuryādgurūttamaḥ || 117
17.118 . purādhvani hutīnāṃ yā saṃkhyeyaṃ tattvavarṇayoḥ |
17.118 . tāmeva dviguṇīkuryātpadādhvani caturguṇām || 118
17.119 . kramānmantrakalāmārge dviguṇā dviguṇā kramāt |
17.119 . yāvattritattvasaṃśuddhau syādviṃśatiguṇā tataḥ || 119
17.120 . pratikarma bhavetṣaṣṭirāhutīnāṃ tritattvake |
17.120 . ekatattve śataṃ prāhurāhutīnāṃ tu sāṣṭakam || 120
17.121 . vilomakarmaṇā sākaṃ yāḥ pūrṇāhutayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
17.121 . tāsāṃ sarvādhvasaṃśuddhau saṃkhyānyatvaṃ na kiṃcana || 121
17.122 . ityeṣā kathitā dīkṣā jananādisamanvitā || 122

c18 atha śrītantrāloke aṣṭādaśamāhnikam

18.1 . atha saṃkṣiptadīkṣeyaṃ śivatāpattidocyate |
18.1 . na rajo nādhivāso'tra na bhūkṣetraparigrahaḥ |
18.1 . yatra tatra pradeśe tu pūjayitvā guruḥ śivam || 1
18.2 . adhvānaṃ manasā dhyātvā dīkṣayettattvapāragaḥ |
18.2 . jananādivihīnāṃ tu yena yenādhvanā guruḥ || 2
18.3 . kuryātsa ekatattvāntāṃ śivabhāvaikabhāvitaḥ |
18.3 . parāmantrastato'syeti tattvaṃ saṃśodhayāmyatha || 3
18.4 . svāheti pratitattvaṃ syācchuddhe pūrṇāhutiṃ kṣipet |
18.4 . evaṃ mantrāntaraiḥ kuryātsamastairathavoktavat || 4
18.5 . parāsaṃpuṭitaṃ nāma svāhāntaṃ prathamāntakam |
18.5 . śataṃ sahasraṃ sāṣṭaṃ vā tena śaktyaiva homayet || 5
18.6 . tataḥ pūrṇeti saṃśodhyahīnamuttamamīdṛśam |
18.6 . dīkṣākarmoditaṃ tatra tatra śāstre maheśinā || 6
18.7 . pratyekaṃ mātṛkāyugmavarṇaistattvāni śodhayet |
18.7 . yadi vā piṇḍamantreṇa sarvamantreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 7
18.8 . yathā yathā ca svabhyastajñānastanmayatātmakaḥ |
18.8 . gurustathā tathā kuryātsaṃkṣiptaṃ karma nānyathā || 8
18.9 . śrībrahmayāmale coktaṃ saṃkṣipte'pi hi bhāvayet |
18.9 . vyāptiṃ sarvādhvasāmānyāṃ kiṃtu yāge na vistaraḥ || 9
18.10 . atanmayībhūtamiti vikṣiptaṃ karma sandadhat |
18.10 . kramāttādātmyametīti vikṣiptaṃ vidhimācaret || 10
18.11 . saṃkṣipto vidhirukto'yaṃ kṛpayā yaḥ śivoditaḥ |
18.11 . dīkṣottare kairaṇe ca tatra tatrāpi śāsane || 11

c19 atha śrītantrāloke ekānnaviṃśamāhnikam

19.1 . atha sadyaḥsamutkrāntipradā dīkṣā nirūpyate |
19.1 . tatkṣaṇāccopabhogādvā dehapāte śivaṃ vrajet |
19.1 . ityuktyā mālinīśāstre sūcitāsau maheśinā || 1
19.2 . dehapāte samīpasthe śaktipātasphuṭatvataḥ |
19.2 . āsādya śāṃkarīṃ dīkṣāṃ tasmāddīkṣākṣaṇātparam || 2
19.3 . śivaṃ vrajedityartho'tra pūrvāparavivecanāt |
19.3 . vyākhyātaḥ śrīmatāsmākaṃ guruṇā śambhumūrtinā || 3
19.4 . yadā hyāsannamaraṇe śaktipātaḥ prajāyate |
19.4 . tatra mande'tha gurvādisevayāyuḥ kṣayaṃ vrajet || 4
19.5 . athavā bandhumitrādidvārā sāsya vibhoḥ patet |
19.5 . pūrvaṃ vā samayī naiva parāṃ dīkṣāmavāptavān || 5
19.6 . āptadīkṣo'pi vā prāṇāñjihāsuḥ kleśavarjitam |
19.6 . antyāngurustadā kuryātsadya+utkrāntidīkṣaṇam || 6
19.7 . natvapakvamale nāpi śeṣakārmikavigrahe |
19.7 . kuryādutkramaṇaṃ śrīmadgahvare ca nirūpitam || 7
19.8 . dṛṣṭvā śiṣyaṃ jarāgrastaṃ vyādhinā paripīḍitam |
19.8 . utkramayya tatastvenaṃ paratattve niyojayet || 8
19.9 . viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatve kāmacāravidhānataḥ |
19.9 . pūrvoktamarthajātaṃ śrīśambhunātra nirūpitam || 9
19.10 . vidhiṃ pūrvoditaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvā samayaśuddhitaḥ |
19.10 . kṣurikāmasya vinyasyejjvalantīṃ marmakartarīm || 10
19.11 . kṛtvā pūrvoditaṃ nyāsaṃ kālānalasamaprabham |
19.11 . saṃhṛtikramataḥ sārdhaṃ sṛkchindiyugalena tu || 11
19.12 . āgneyīṃ dhāraṇāṃ kṛtvā sarvamarmapratāpanīm |
19.12 . pūrayedvāyunā dehamaṅguṣṭhānmastakāntakam || 12
19.13 . tamutkṛṣya tato'ṅguṣṭhādūrdhvāntaṃ vakṣyamāṇayā |
19.13 . kṛntenmarmāṇi randhrāntātkālarātryā visarjayet || 13
19.14 . anena kramayogena yojito hutivarjitaḥ |
19.14 . samayyapyeti tāṃ dīkṣāmiti śrīmālinīmate || 14
19.15 . ṣoḍaśādhāraṣaṭcakralakṣyatrayakhapañcakāt |
19.15 . kvacidanyataratrātha prāguktapaśukarmavat || 15
19.16 . praviśya mūlaṃ kandādeśchindannaikyavibhāvanāt |
19.16 . pūrṇāhutiprayogeṇa sveṣṭe dhāmni niyojayet || 16
19.17 . jñānatriśūlaṃ saṃdīptaṃ dīptacakratrayojjvalam |
19.17 . cintayitvāmunā tasya vedanaṃ bodhanaṃ bhramam || 17
19.18 . dīpanaṃ tāḍanaṃ todaṃ calanaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ |
19.18 . kandādicakragaṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa hṛdambuje || 18
19.19 . dvādaśānte tataḥ kṛtvā binduyugmagate kṣipet |
19.19 . nirlakṣye vā pare dhāmni saṃyuktaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 19
19.20 . na tasya kuryātsaṃskāraṃ kaṃcidityāha gahvare |
19.20 . devaḥ kimasya pūrṇasya śrāddhādyairiti bhāvitaḥ || 20
19.21 . śrīmaddīkṣottare tveṣa vidhirvahnipuṭīkṛtaḥ |
19.21 . haṃsaḥ pumānadhastasya rudrabindusamanvitaḥ || 21
19.22 . śiṣyadehe niyojyaitadanudvagnaḥ śataṃ japet |
19.22 . utkramyordhvanimeṣeṇa śiṣya itthaṃ paraṃ vrajet || 22
19.23 . eṣa eva vidhiḥ śrīmatsiddhayogīśvarīmate |
19.23 . iyamutkrāmaṇī dīkṣā kartavyā yogino guroḥ || 23
19.24 . anabhyastaprāṇacāraḥ kathamenāṃ kariṣyati |
19.24 . vakṣyamāṇāṃ brahmavidyāṃ sakalāṃ niṣkalombhitām || 24
19.25 . karṇe'sya vā paṭhedbhūyo bhūyo vāpyatha pāṭhayet |
19.25 . svayaṃ ca karma kurvīta tattvaśuddhyādikaṃ guruḥ || 25
19.26 . mantrakriyābalātpūrṇāhutyetthaṃ yojayetpare |
19.26 . yogābhyāsamakṛtvāpi sadya+utkrāntidāṃ guruḥ || 26
19.27 . jñānamantrakriyādhyānabalātkartuṃ bhavetprabhuḥ |
19.27 . anayotkramyate śiṣyo balādevaikakaṃ kṣaṇam || 27
19.28 . kālasyollaṅghya bhogo hi kṣaṇiko'syāstu kiṃ tataḥ |
19.28 . sadya+utkrāntidā cānyā yasyāṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ tadā || 28
19.29 . dadyādyadāsya prāṇāḥ syurdhruvaṃ niṣkramaṇecchavaḥ |
19.29 . vināpi kriyayā bhāvibrahmavidyābalādguruḥ || 29
19.30 . karṇajāpaprayogeṇa tattvakañcukajālataḥ |
19.30 . niḥsārayanyathābhīṣṭe sakale niṣkale dvaye || 30
19.31 . tattve vā yatra kutrāpi yojayetpudgalaṃ kramāt |
19.31 . samayī putrako vāpi paṭhedvidyāmimāṃ tathā || 31
19.32 . tatpāṭhāttu samayyuktāṃ rudrāṃśāpattimaśnute |
19.32 . etau jape cādhyayane yasmādadhikṛtāvubhau || 32
19.33 . nādhyāpanopadeśe vā sa eṣo'dhyayanādṛte |
19.33 . paṭhatostvanayorvastusvabhāvāttasya sā gatiḥ || 33
19.34 . yathā niṣiddhabhūtādikarmā mantraṃ smaransvayam |
19.34 . āviṣṭe'pi kvacinnaiti lopaṃ kartṛtvavarjanāt || 34
19.35 . yathā ca vācayañśāstraṃ samayī śūnyaveśmani |
19.35 . na lupyate tadantaḥsthaprāṇivargopakārataḥ || 35
19.36 . tathā svayaṃ paṭhanneṣa vidyāṃ vastusvabhāvataḥ |
19.36 . tasminmukte na lupyeta yato kiṃcitkaro'tra saḥ || 36
19.37 . nanu cādīkṣitāgre sa noccarecchāstrapaddhatim || 37
19.38 . hanta kuḍyāgrato'pyasya niṣedhastvatha kathyate |
19.38 . paryudāsena yaḥ śrotumavadhārayituṃ kṣamaḥ || 38
19.39 . sa evātra niṣiddho no kuḍyakīṭapatatriṇaḥ |
19.39 . tarhi pāṣāṇatulyo'sau vilīnendriyavṛttikaḥ || 39
19.40 . tasyāgre paṭhatastasya niṣedhollaṅghanā katham |
19.40 . sa tu vastusvabhāvena galitākṣo'pi budhyate || 40
19.41 . akṣānapekṣayaivāntaścicchaktyā svaprakāśayā |
19.41 . prāgdehaṃ kila tityakṣurnottaraṃ cādhitaṣṭhivān || 41
19.42 . madhye prabodhakabalātpratibudhyetpudgalaḥ |
19.42 . mantrāḥ śabdamayāḥ śuddhavimarśātmatayā svayam || 42
19.43 . arthātmanā cāvabhāntastadarthapratibodhakāḥ |
19.43 . tenāsya galitākṣasya prabodho jāyate svayam || 43
19.44 . svacitsamānajātīyamantrāmarśanasaṃnidheḥ |
19.44 . yathā hyalpajavo vāyuḥ sajātīyavimiśritaḥ || 44
19.45 . javī tathātmā saṃsuptāmarśo'pyevaṃ prabudhyate |
19.45 . prabuddhaḥ sa ca saṃjāto na cādīkṣita ucyate || 45
19.46 . dīkṣā hi nāma saṃskāro na tvanyatso'sti cāsya hi |
19.46 . ata eva nijaṃ śāstraṃ paṭhati kvāpi sāmaye || 46
19.47 . tacchrutvā ko'pi dhanyaścenmucyate nāsya sā kṣatiḥ |
19.47 . śāstranindāṃ maiṣa kārṣīddvayoḥ pātityadāyinīm || 47
19.48 . ityevaṃparametannādīkṣitāgre paṭhediti |
19.48 . yathā ca samayī kāṣṭhe loṣṭe vā mantrayojanām || 48
19.49 . kurvaṃstasmiṃścalatyeti na lopaṃ tadvadatra hi |
19.49 . yato'sya pratyayaprāptiprepsoḥ samayinastathā || 49
19.50 . pravṛttasya svabhāvena tasminmukte na vai kṣatiḥ |
19.50 . sādhakastu sadā sādhye phale niyatiyantraṇāt || 50
19.51 . makṣikāśrutamantro'pi prāyaścittaucitīṃ caret |
19.51 . itthaṃ sadyaḥsamutkrāntiryoktā tāmājñayā guroḥ || 51
19.52 . samayyādirapi proktakāle proktārthasiddhaye |
19.52 . svayaṃ kuryātsamabhyastaprāṇacāragamāgamaḥ || 52
19.53 . akṛtādhikṛtirvāpi guruḥ samayaśuddhaye |
19.53 . adhastanapadāvastho natu jñāneddhacetanaḥ || 53
19.54 . itīyaṃ sadya+utkrāntiḥ sūcitā mālinīmate |
19.54 . svayaṃ vā guruṇā vātha kāryatvena maheśinā || 54
19.55 . sarvaṃ bhogaṃ virūpaṃ tu matvā dehaṃ tyajedyadi |
19.55 . tadā tena krameṇāśu yojitaḥ samayī śivaḥ || 55
19.56 . ukteyaṃ sadya+utkrāntiryā gopyā prāṇavadbudhaiḥ || 56

c20 atha śrītantrāloke viṃśatitamamāhnikam

20.1 . atha dīkṣāṃ bruve mūḍhajanāśvāsapradāyinīm || 1
20.2 . trikoṇe vahnisadane vahnivarṇojjvale'bhitaḥ |
20.2 . vāyavyapuranirdhūte kare savye sujājvale || 2
20.3 . bījaṃ kiṃcidgṛhītvaitattathaiva hṛdayāntare |
20.3 . kare ca dahyamānaṃ saccintayettajjapaikayuk || 3
20.4 . vahnidīpitaphaṭkāradhoraṇīdāhapīḍitam |
20.4 . bījaṃ nirbījatāmeti svasūtikaraṇākṣamam || 4
20.5 . taptaṃ naitatprarohāya tenaiva pratyayena tu |
20.5 . malamāyākhyakarmāṇi mantradhyānakriyābalāt || 5
20.6 . dagdhāni na svakāryāya nirbījapratyayaṃ tvimam |
20.6 . sa śrīmānsuprasanno me śaṃbhunātho nyarūpayat || 6
20.7 . bījasyāpyatra kāryā ca yojanā kṛpayā guroḥ |
20.7 . yato dīkṣā sudīptatvātsthāvarāṇyapi mocayet || 7
20.8 . yo gururjapahomārcādhyānasiddhatvamātmani |
20.8 . jñātvā dīkṣāṃ carettasya dīkṣā sapratyayā smṛtā || 8
20.9 . avadhūte nirācāre tattvajñe natvayaṃ vidhiḥ |
20.9 . sācāraiḥ kriyate dīkṣā yā dṛṣṭapratyayānvitā || 9
20.10 . nirācāreṇa dīkṣāyāṃ pratyayastu na gadyate |
20.10 . jñānaṃ svapratyayaṃ yasmānna phalāntaramarhati || 10
20.11 . dhyānādi tu phalātsādhyamiti siddhāmatoditam |
20.11 . tulāśuddhiparīkṣāṃ vā kuryātpratyayayoginīm || 11
20.12 . yathā śrītantrasadbhāve kathitā parameśinā |
20.12 . śrīpūrvaśāstre'pyeṣā ca sūcitā parameśinā || 12
20.13 . ānanda udbhavaḥ kampo nidrā ghūrṇiśca pañcamī |
20.13 . ityevaṃvadatā śaktitāratamyābhidhāyinā || 13
20.14 . udbhavo laghubhāvena dehagrahatirohiteḥ |
20.14 . deho hi pārthivo mukhyastadā mukhyatvamujjhati || 14
20.15 . bhāvilāghavamantreṇa śiṣyaṃ dhyātvā samutplutam |
20.15 . karmāṇi tatrāśeṣāṇi pūrvoktānyācaredguruḥ || 15
20.16 . uktā seyaṃ tulāśuddhidīkṣā pratyayadāyinī |



c21 atha śrītantrāloke ekaviṃśatitamamāhnikam

21.1 . parokṣasaṃsthitasyātha dīkṣākarma nigadyate || 1
21.2 . bhuktimuktiprasiddhyarthaṃ nīyate sadguruṃ prati |
21.2 . ityasminmālinīvākye pratiḥ sāṃmukhyāvācakaḥ || 2
21.3 . sāṃmukhyaṃ cāsya śiṣyasya tatkṛpāspadatātmakam |
21.3 . tamārādhyeti vacanaṃ kṛpāhetūpalakṣaṇam || 3
21.4 . tatsaṃbandhāttataḥ kaścittatkṣaṇādapavṛjyate |
21.4 . ityasyāyamapi hyartho mālinīvākyasanmaṇeḥ || 4
21.5 . tatkṣaṇāditi nāsyāsti yiyāsādikṣaṇāntaram |
21.5 . kiṃtvevameva karuṇānighnastaṃ gururuddharet || 5
21.6 . gurusevākṣīṇatanordīkṣāmaprāpya pañcatām |
21.6 . gatasyātha svayaṃ mṛtyukṣaṇoditatathāruceḥ || 6
21.7 . athavādharatantrādidīkṣāsaṃskārabhāginaḥ |
21.7 . prāptasāmayikasyātha parāṃ dīkṣāmavindataḥ || 7
21.8 . ḍimbāhatasya yogeśībhakṣitasyābhicārataḥ |
21.8 . mṛtasya guruṇā yantratantrādinihatasya vā || 8
21.9 . bhraṣṭasvasamayasyātha dīkṣāṃ prāptavato'pyalam |
21.9 . bandhubhāryāsuhṛtputragāḍhābhyarthanayogataḥ || 9
21.10 . svayaṃ tadviṣayotpannakaruṇābalato'pi vā |
21.10 . vijñātatanmukhāyātaśaktipātāṃśadharmaṇaḥ || 10
21.11 . gururdīkṣāṃ mṛtoddhārīṃ kurvīta śivadāyinīm |
21.11 . śrīmṛtyuñjayasiddhādau taduktaṃ parameśinā || 11
21.12 . adīkṣite nṛpatyādāvalase patite mṛte |
21.12 . bālāturastrīvṛddhe ca mṛtoddhāraṃ prakalpayet || 12
21.13 . vidhiḥ sarvaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ sa tu saṃkṣipta iṣyate |
21.13 . gurvādipūjārahito bāhye bhogāya sā yataḥ || 13
21.14 . adhivāsacarukṣetraṃ śayyāmaṇḍalakalpane |
21.14 . nopayogyatra tacchiṣyasaṃskriyāsvapnadṛṣṭaye || 14
21.15 . mantrasaṃnidhisaṃtṛptiyogāyātra tu maṇḍalam |
21.15 . bhūyodine ca devārcā sākṣānnāsyopakāri tat || 15
21.16 . kriyopakaraṇasthānamaṇḍalākṛtimantrataḥ |
21.16 . dhyānayogaikatadbhaktijñānatanmayabhāvataḥ || 16
21.17 . tatpraviṣṭasya kasyāpi śiṣyāṇāṃ ca gurostathā |
21.17 . ekādaśaite kathitāḥ saṃnidhānāya hetavaḥ || 17
21.18 . uttarottaramutkṛṣṭāstathā vyāmiśraṇāvaśāt |
21.18 . kriyātibhūyasī puṣpādyuttamaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam || 18
21.19 . ekaliṅgādi ca sthānaṃ yatrātmā saṃprasīdati |
21.19 . maṇḍalaṃ tritriśūlābjacakraṃ yanmantramaṇḍale || 19
21.20 . anāhūte'pi dṛṣṭaṃ satsamayitvaprasādhanam |
21.20 . taduktaṃ mālinītantre siddhaṃ samayamaṇḍalam || 20
21.21 . yena saṃdṛṣṭamātreti siddhamātrapadadvayāt |
21.21 . ākṛtirdīptarūpā yā mantrastadvatsudīptikaḥ || 21
21.22 . śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭamato neha kathitaṃ vistarātpunaḥ |
21.22 . kṛtvā maṇḍalamabhyarcya tatra devaṃ kuśairatha || 22
21.23 . gomayenākṛtiṃ kuryācchiṣyavattāṃ nidhāpayet |
21.23 . tatastasyāṃ śodhyamekamadhvānaṃ vyāptibhāvanāt || 23
21.24 . prakṛtyantaṃ vinikṣipya punarenaṃ vidhiṃ caret |
21.24 . mahājālaprayogeṇa sarvasmādadhvamadhyataḥ || 24
21.25 . cittamākṛṣya tatrasthaṃ kuryāttadvidhirucyate |
21.25 . mūlādhārādudetya prasṛtasuvitatānantanāḍyadhvadaṇḍaṃ vīryeṇākramya nāsāgaganaparigataṃ vikṣipan vyāptumīṣṭe |
21.25 . yāvaddhūmābhirāmapracitataraśikhājālakenādhvacakraṃ saṃchādyābhīṣṭajīvānayanamiti mahājālanāmā prayogaḥ || 25
21.26 . etenācchādanīyaṃ vrajati paravaśaṃ saṃmukhīnatvamādau pañcādānīyate cetsakalamatha tato'pyadhvamadhyādyatheṣṭam |
21.26 . ākṛṣṭāvuddhṛtau vā mṛtajanaviṣaye karṣaṇīye'tha jīve yogaḥ śrīśaṃbhunāthāgamaparigamito jālanāmā mayoktaḥ || 26
21.27 . ciravighaṭite senāyugmeyathāmilite punarhayagajanaraṃ svāṃ svāṃ jātiṃ rasādabhidhāvati |
21.27 . karaṇapavanairnāḍīcakraistathaiva samāgatairnijanijarasādekībhāvyaṃ svajālavaśīkṛtaiḥ || 27
21.28 . mahājālasamākṛṣṭo jīvo vijñānaśālinā |
21.28 . svaḥpretatiryaṅnirayāṃstadaivaiṣa vimuñcati || 28
21.29 . tajjñānamantrayogāptaḥ puruṣaścaiṣa kṛtrimam |
21.29 . yogīva sādhyahṛdayāttadā tādātmyamujjhati || 29
21.30 . sthāvarādidaśāścitrāstatsalokasamīpatāḥ |
21.30 . tyajecceti na citraṃ sa evaṃ yaḥ karmaṇāpi vā || 30
21.31 . adhikāriśarīratvānmānuṣye tu śarīragaḥ |
21.31 . na tadā mucyate dehāddehānte tu śivaṃ vrajet || 31
21.32 . tasmindehe tu kāpyasya jāyate śāṅkarī parā |
21.32 . bhaktirūhācca vijñānādācāryādvāpyasevitāt || 32
21.33 . taddehasaṃsthito'pyeṣa jīvo jālabalādimam |
21.33 . dārbhādidehaṃ vyāpnoti svādhiṣṭhityāpyacetayan || 33
21.34 . yogamantrakriyājñānabhūyobalavaśātpunaḥ |
21.34 . manuṣyadehamapyeṣa tadaivāśu vimuñcati || 34
21.35 . suptakalpo'pyadeho'pi yo jīvaḥ so'pi jālataḥ |
21.35 . ākṛṣṭo dārbhamāyāti dehaṃ phalamayaṃ ca vā || 35
21.36 . jātīphalādi yatkiṃcittena vā dehakalpanā |
21.36 . antarbahirdvayaucityāttadatrotkṛṣṭamucyate || 36
21.37 . tato jālakramānītaḥ sa jīvaḥ suptavatsthitaḥ |
21.37 . manoviśiṣṭadehādisāmagrīprāptyabhāvataḥ || 37
21.38 . na spandate na jānāti na vakti na kilecchati |
21.38 . tādṛśasyaiva saṃskārān sarvān prāgvatprakalpayet || 38
21.39 . nirbījadīkṣāyogena sarvaṃ kṛtvā puroditam |
21.39 . vidhiṃ yojanikāṃ pūrṇāhutyā sākaṃ kṣipecca tam || 39
21.40 . dārbhādidehe mantrāgnāvarpite pūrṇayā saha |
21.40 . muktapāśaḥ śivaṃ yāti punarāvṛttivarjitaḥ || 40
21.41 . sapratyayā tviyaṃ yatra spandate darbhajā tanuḥ |
21.41 . tatra prāṇamanomantrārpaṇayogāttathā bhavet || 41
21.42 . sābhyāsasya tadapyuktaṃ balāśvāsi na tatkṛte |
21.42 . mṛtoddhāroditaireva yathāsaṃbhūti hetubhiḥ || 42
21.43 . jīvatparokṣadīkṣāpi kāryā nirbījikā tu sā |
21.43 . tasyāṃ darbhākṛtiprāyakalpane jālayogataḥ || 43
21.44 . saṃkalpamātreṇākarṣo jīvasya mṛtibhītitaḥ |
21.44 . śiṣṭaṃ prāgvatkuśādyutthākāraviploṣavarjitam || 44
21.45 . pārimityādanaiśvaryātsādhye niyatiyantraṇāt |
21.45 . jālākṛṣṭirvinābhyāsaṃ rāgadveṣānna jāyate || 45
21.46 . parokṣa evātulyābhirdīkṣābhiryadi dīkṣitaḥ |
21.46 . tatrottaraṃ syādbalavatsaṃskārāya tvadhastanam || 46
21.47 . bhuktiyojanikāyāṃ tu bhūyobhirgurubhistathā |
21.47 . kṛtāyāṃ bhogavaicitryaṃ hetuvaicitryayogataḥ || 47
21.48 . parokṣadīkṣaṇe māyottīrṇe bhogāya yojayet |
21.48 . bhogānīpsā durlabhā hi satī vā bhogahānaye || 48
21.49 . uktaṃ hi svānyasaṃvittyoḥ svasaṃvidbalavattarā |
21.49 . bādhakatve bādhikāsau sāmyaudāsīnyayostathā || 49
21.50 . śrīmān dharmaśivo'pyāha pārokṣyāṃ karmapaddhatau |
21.50 . parokṣadīkṣaṇe samyakpūrṇāhutividhau yadi || 50
21.51 . agniściṭiciṭāśabdaṃ sadhūmaṃ pratimuñcati |
21.51 . dhatte nīlāmbudacchāyāṃ muhurjvalati śāmyati || 51
21.52 . vistaro ghorarūpaśca mahīṃ dhāvati cāpyadhaḥ |
21.52 . dhvāṃkṣādyaśravyaśabdo vā tadā taṃ lakṣayedguruḥ || 52
21.53 . brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpaistatsaṅgaiścopapātakaiḥ |
21.53 . tadā tasya na kartavyā dīkṣāsminnakṛte vidhau || 53
21.54 . navātmā phaṭpuṭāntaḥsthaḥ punaḥ pañcaphaḍanvitaḥ |
21.54 . amukasyeti pāpāni dahāmyanu phaḍaṣṭakam || 54
21.55 . iti sāhasriko homaḥ kartavyastilataṇḍulaiḥ |
21.55 . ante pūrṇā ca dātavyā tato'smai dīkṣayā guruḥ || 55
21.56 . parayojanaparyantaṃ kuryāttattvaviśodhanam |
21.56 . pratyakṣe'pi sthitasyāṇoḥ pāpino bhagavanmayīm || 56
21.57 . śaktiṃ prāptavato jyeṣṭhāmevameva vidhiṃ caret |
21.57 . yadi vā daiśikaḥ samyaṅna dīptastasya tatpurā || 57
21.58 . prāyaścittaistathā dānaiḥ prāṇāyāmaiśca śodhanam |
21.58 . kṛtvā vidhimimāṃ cāpi dīkṣāṃ kuryādaśaṅkitaḥ || 58
21.59 . sarvathā vartamāno'pi tattvavinmocayetpaśūn |
21.59 . icchayaiva śivaḥ sākṣāttasmāttaṃ pūjayetsadā || 59
21.60 . śāṭhyaṃ tatra na kāryaṃ ca tatkṛtvādho vrajecchiśuḥ |
21.60 . na punaḥ kīrtayettasya pāpaṃ kīrtayitā vrajet || 60
21.61 . nirayaṃ varjayettasmāditi dīkṣottare vidhiḥ |
21.61 . eṣā parokṣadīkṣā dvidhoditā jīvaditarabhedena || 61

c22 atha śrītantrāloke dvāviṃśatitamamāhnikam

22.1 . liṅgoddhārākhyāmatha vacmaḥ śivaśāsanaikanirdiṣṭām || 1
22.2 . uktaṃ śrīmālinītantre kila pārthivadhāraṇām |
22.2 . uktvā yo yojito yatra sa tasmānna nivartate || 2
22.3 . yogyatāvaśasaṃjātā yasya yatraiva śāsanā |
22.3 . sa tatraiva niyoktavyo dīkṣākāle tatastvasau || 3
22.4 . phalaṃ sarvaṃ samāsādya śive yukto'pavṛjyate |
22.4 . ayukto'pyūrdhvasaṃśuddhiṃ saṃprāpya bhuvaneśataḥ || 4
22.5 . śuddhaḥ śivatvamāyāti dagdhasaṃsārabandhanaḥ |
22.5 . uktvā puṃdhāraṇāṃ coktametadvaidāntikaṃ mayā || 5
22.6 . kapilāya purā proktaṃ prathame paṭale tathā |
22.6 . anena kramayogena saṃprāptaḥ paramaṃ padam || 6
22.7 . na bhūyaḥ paśutāmeti śuddhe svātmani tiṣṭhati |
22.7 . ato hi dhvanyate'rtho'yaṃ śivatattvādhareṣvapi || 7
22.8 . tattveṣu yojitasyāsti punaruddharaṇīyatā |
22.8 . samastaśāstrakathitavastuvaiviktyadāyinaḥ || 8
22.9 . śivāgamasya sarvebhyo'pyāgamebhyo viśiṣṭatā |
22.9 . śivajñānena ca vinā bhūyo'pi paśutodbhavaḥ || 9
22.10 . kramaśca śaktisaṃpāto malahāniryiyāsutā |
22.10 . dīkṣā bodho heyahānirupādeyalayātmatā || 10
22.11 . bhogyatvapāśavatyāgaḥ patikartṛtvasaṃkṣayaḥ |
22.11 . svātmasthitiścetyevaṃ hi darśanāntarasaṃsthiteḥ || 11
22.12 . proktamuddharaṇīyatvaṃ śivaśaktīritasya hi |
22.12 . atha vaiṣṇavabauddhāditantrāntādharavartinām || 12
22.13 . yadā śivārkaraśmyoghairvikāsi hṛdayāmbujam |
22.13 . liṅgoddhṛtistadā pūrvaṃ dīkṣākarma tataḥ param || 13
22.14 . prāgliṅgāntarasaṃstho'pi dīkṣātaḥ śivatāṃ vrajet |
22.14 . tatropavāsya taṃ cānyadine sādhāramantrataḥ || 14
22.15 . sthaṇḍile pūjayitveśaṃ śrāvayettasya vartanīm |
22.15 . eṣa prāgabhavalliṅgī coditastvadhunā tvayā || 15
22.16 . prasannena tadetasmai kuru samyaganugraham |
22.16 . svaliṅgatyāgaśaṅkotthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ ca māsya bhūt || 16
22.17 . acirāttvanmayībhūya bhogaṃ mokṣaṃ prapadyatām |
22.17 . evamastvityathājñāṃ ca gṛhīrvā vratamasya tat || 17
22.18 . apāsyāmbhasi nikṣipya snapayedanurūpataḥ |
22.18 . snātaṃ saṃprokṣayedarghapātrāmbhobhiranantaram || 18
22.19 . pañcagavyaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ tatastasmai samarpayet |
22.19 . tatastaṃ baddhanetraṃ ca praveśya praṇipātayet || 19
22.20 . praṇavo mātṛkā māyā vyomavyāpī ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |
22.20 . bahurūpo'tha netrākhyaḥ sapta sādhāraṇā amī || 20
22.21 . teṣāṃ madhyādekatamaṃ mantramasmai samarpayet |
22.21 . so'pyahorātramevainaṃ japedalpabhugapyabhuk || 21
22.22 . mantramasmai samarpyātha sādhāravidhisaṃskṛte |
22.22 . vahnau tarpitatanmantre vrataśuddhiṃ samācaret || 22
22.23 . pūjitenaiva mantreṇa kṛtvā nāmāsya saṃpuṭam |
22.23 . prāyaścittaṃ śodhayāmi phaṭsvāhetyūhayogataḥ || 23
22.24 . śataṃ sahasraṃ vā hutvā punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ tathā |
22.24 . prayogādvauṣaḍantāṃ ca kṣiptvāhūya vrateśvaram || 24
22.25 . tāro vrateśvarāyeti namaścetyenamarcayet |
22.25 . śrāvayecca tvayā nāsya kāryaṃ kiṃcicchivājñayā || 25
22.26 . tato vrateśvarastarpyaḥ svāhāntena tataśca saḥ |
22.26 . kṣamayitvā visṛjyaḥ syāttato'gneśca visarjanam || 26
22.27 . tacchrāvaṇaṃ ca devāya kṣamasveti visarjanam |
22.27 . tatastṛtīyadivase prāgvatsarvo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 27
22.28 . adhivāsādikaḥ sveṣṭadīkṣākarmāvasānakaḥ |
22.28 . prāgliṅgināṃ mokṣadīkṣā sādhikāravivarjitā || 28
22.29 . sādhakācāryatāmārge na yogyāste punarbhuvaḥ |
22.29 . punarbhuvo'pi jñāneddhā bhavanti gurutāspadam || 29
22.30 . mokṣāyaiva na bhogāya bhogāyāpyabhyupāyataḥ |
22.30 . ityuktavānsvapaddhatyāmīśānaśivadaiśikaḥ || 30
22.31 . śrīdevyā yāmalīyoktitattvasamyakpravedakaḥ |
22.31 . gurvantasyāpyadhodṛṣṭiśāyinaḥ saṃskriyāmimām || 31
22.32 . kṛtvā rahasyaṃ kathayennānyathā kāmike kila |
22.32 . anyatantrābhiṣikte'pi rahasyaṃ na prakāśayet || 32
22.33 . svatantrastho'pi gurvanto gurumajñamupāśritaḥ |
22.33 . tatra paścādanāśvastastatrāpi vidhimācaret || 33
22.34 . ajñācāryamukhāyātaṃ nirvīryaṃ mantrameṣa yat |
22.34 . japtavānsa guruścātra nādhikāryuktadūṣaṇāt || 34
22.35 . tato'sya śuddhiṃ prākkṛtvā tato dīkṣāṃ samācaret |
22.35 . adhodarśanasaṃsthena guruṇā dīkṣitaḥ purā || 35
22.36 . tīvraśaktivaśātpaścādyadā gacchetsa sadgurum |
22.36 . tadāpyasya śiśorevaṃ śuddhiṃ kṛtvā sa sadguruḥ || 36
22.37 . dīkṣādikarma nikhilaṃ kuryāduktavidhānataḥ |
22.37 . prāpto'pi sadgururyogyabhāvamasya na vetti cet || 37
22.38 . vijñānadāne tacchiṣyo yogyatāṃ darśayennijām |
22.38 . sarvathā tvabruvanneṣa bruvāṇo vā viparyayam || 38
22.39 . ajño vastuta eveti tattyaktvetthaṃ vidhiṃ caret |
22.39 . na tirobhāvaśaṅkātra kartavyā buddhiśālinā || 39
22.40 . adhaḥspṛktvaṃ tirobhūtirnordhvopāyavivecanam |
22.40 . siddhānte dīkṣitāstantre daśāṣṭādaśabhedini || 40
22.41 . bhairavīye catuḥṣaṣṭau tānpaśūndīkṣayettrike |
22.41 . siddhavīrāvalīsāre bhairavīye kule'pi ca || 41
22.42 . pañcadīkṣākramopāttā dīkṣānuttarasaṃjñitā |
22.42 . tena sarvo'dharastho'pi liṅgoddhṛtyānugṛhyate || 42
22.43 . yo'pi hṛtsthamaheśānacodanātaḥ suvistṛtam |
22.43 . śāstrajñānaṃ samanvicchetso'pi yāyādbahūngurūn || 43
22.44 . taddīkṣāścāpi gṛhṇīyādabhiṣecanapaścimāḥ |
22.44 . jñānopodbalikāstā hi tattajjñānavatā kṛtāḥ || 44
22.45 . uktaṃ ca śrīmate śāstre tatra tatra ca bhūyasā |
22.45 . āmodārthī yathā bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpātpuṣpāntaraṃ vrajet || 45
22.46 . vijñānārthī tathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ tviti |
22.46 . gurūṇāṃ bhūyasāṃ madhye yato vijñānamuttamam || 46
22.47 . prāptaṃ so'sya gururdīkṣā nātra mukhyā hi saṃvidi |
22.47 . sarvajñānanidhānaṃ tu guruṃ saṃprāpya susthitaḥ || 47
22.48 . tamevārādhayeddhīmāṃstattajjijñāsanonmukhaḥ |
22.48 . iti dīkṣāvidhiḥ prokto liṅgoddharaṇapaścimaḥ || 48

c23 atha śrītantrāloke trayoviṃśatitamamāhnikam

23.1 . athābhiṣekasya vidhiḥ kathyate pārameśvaraḥ || 1
23.2 . yaiṣā putrakadīkṣoktā gurusādhakayorapi |
23.2 . saivādhikāriṇī bhogyatattvayuktimatī kramāt || 2
23.3 . svabhyastajñāninaṃ santaṃ bubhūṣumatha bhāvinam |
23.3 . yogyaṃ jñātvā svādhikāraṃ gurustasmai samarpayet || 3
23.4 . yo naivaṃ veda naivāsāvabhiṣikto'pi daiśikaḥ |
23.4 . samayyādikrameṇeti śrīmatkāmika ucyate || 4
23.5 . yo na vedādhvasandhānaṃ ṣoḍhā bāhyāntarasthitam |
23.5 . sa gururmocayenneti siddhayogīśvarīmate || 5
23.6 . sarvalakṣaṇahīno'pi jñānavān gururiṣyate |
23.6 . jñānaprādhānyamevoktamiti śrīkacabhārgave || 6
23.7 . padavākyapramāṇajñaḥ śivabhaktyekatatparaḥ |
23.7 . samastaśivaśāstrārthaboddhā kāruṇiko guruḥ || 7
23.8 . na svayaṃbhūstasya coktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ parameśinā |
23.8 . abhakto jīvitadhiyā kurvannīśānadhiṣṭhitaḥ || 8
23.9 . paścātmanā svayaṃbhūṣṇurnādhikārī sa kutracit |
23.9 . bhasmāṅkuro vratisuto duḥśīlātanayastathā || 9
23.10 . kuṇḍo golaśca te duṣṭā uktaṃ devyākhyayāmale |
23.10 . punarbhūścānyaliṅgo yaḥ punaḥ śaive pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 10
23.11 . śrīpūrvaśāstre na tveṣa niyamaḥ ko'pi coditaḥ |
23.11 . yathārthatattvasaṃghajñastathā śiṣye prakāśakaḥ || 11
23.12 . yaḥ punaḥ sarvatattvāni vettītyādi ca lakṣaṇam |
23.12 . yogacāre ca yadyatra tantre coditamācaret || 12
23.13 . tathaiva siddhaye seyamājñeti kila varṇitam |
23.13 . yastu karmitayācāryastatra kāṇādivarjanam || 13
23.14 . yataḥ kārakasāmagryātkarmaṇo nādhikaḥ kvacit |
23.14 . devyā yāmalaśāstre ca kāñcyādiparivarjanam || 14
23.15 . taddṛṣṭadoṣātkrodhādeḥ samyakjñātaryasau kutaḥ |
23.15 . guravastu svayaṃbhvādi varjyaṃ yadyāmalādiṣu || 15
23.16 . karmyabhiprāyataḥ sarvaṃ taditi vyācacakṣire |
23.16 . ato deśakulācāradehalakṣaṇakalpanām || 16
23.17 . anādṛtyaiva saṃpūrṇajñānaṃ kuryādgururgurum |
23.17 . prāgvatsaṃpūjya hutvā ca śrāvayitvā cikīrṣitam || 17
23.18 . tato'bhiṣiñcettaṃ śiṣyaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyā tataḥ sakṛt |
23.18 . tanmantrarasatoyena pūrvoktavidhinā guruḥ || 18
23.19 . vibhavena suvistīrṇaṃ tatastasmai vadetsvakam |
23.19 . sarvaṃ kartavyasāraṃ yacchāstrāṇāṃ paramaṃ rahaḥ || 19
23.20 . anugrāhyāstvayā śiṣyāḥ śivaśaktipracoditāḥ |
23.20 . uktaṃ jñānottare caitadbrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā viśaḥ || 20
23.21 . napuṃsakāḥ striyaḥ śūdrā ye cānye'pi tadarthinaḥ |
23.21 . te dīkṣāyāṃ na mīmāṃsyā jñānakāle vicārayet || 21
23.22 . jñānamūlo guruḥ proktaḥ saptasatrīṃ pravartayet |
23.22 . dīkṣā vyākhyā kṛpā maitrī śāstracintā śivaikatā || 22
23.23 . annādidānamityetatpālayetsaptasatrakam |
23.23 . abhiṣekavidhau cāsmai karaṇīkhaṭikādikam || 23
23.24 . sarvopakaraṇavrātamarpaṇīyaṃ vipaścite |
23.24 . so'bhiṣikto guruṃ paścāddakṣiṇābhiḥ prapūjayet || 24
23.25 . jñānahīno guruḥ karmī svādhikāraṃ samarpya no |
23.25 . dīkṣādyadhikṛtiṃ kuryādvinā tasyājñayā punaḥ || 25
23.26 . ityevaṃ śrāvayetso'pi namaskṛtyābhinandayet |
23.26 . tataḥ prabhṛtyasau pūrvo gurustyaktādhikārakaḥ || 26
23.27 . yathecchaṃ vicaredvyākhyādīkṣādau yantraṇojjhitaḥ |
23.27 . kurvanna bādhyate yasmāddīpāddīpavadīdṛśaḥ || 27
23.28 . santāno nādhikārasya cyavo'kurvanna bādhyate |
23.28 . prākca kurvanvihanyeta siddhātantre taducyate || 28
23.29 . yathārthamupadeśaṃ tu kurvannācārya ucyate |
23.29 . na cāvajñā kriyākāle saṃsāroddharaṇaṃ prati || 29
23.30 . na dīkṣeta guruḥ śiṣyaṃ tattvayuktastu garvataḥ |
23.30 . yo'sya syānnarake vāsa iha ca vyādhito bhavet || 30
23.31 . prāptābhiṣekaḥ sa guruḥ ṣaṇmāsānmantrapaddhatim |
23.31 . sarvāṃ tantroditāṃ dhyāyejjapeccātanmayatvataḥ || 31
23.32 . yadaiva tanmayībhūtastadā vīryamupāgataḥ |
23.32 . chindyātpāśāṃstato yatnaṃ kuryāttanmayatāsthitau || 32
23.33 . hṛccakrādutthitā sūkṣmā śaśisphaṭikasaṃnibhā |
23.33 . lekhākārā nādarūpā praśāntā cakrapaṅktigā || 33
23.34 . dvādaśānte nirūḍhā sā sauṣumne tripathāntare |
23.34 . tatra hṛccakramāpūrya japenmantraṃ jvalatprabham || 34
23.35 . cakṣurlomādirandhraughavahajjvālaurvasaṃnibham |
23.35 . yāvacchāntaśikhākīrṇaṃ viśvājyapravilāpakam || 35
23.36 . tadājyadhārāsaṃtṛptamānābhikuharāntaram |
23.36 . evaṃ mantrā mokṣadāḥ syurdīptā buddhāḥ sunirmalāḥ || 36
23.37 . mūlakandanabhonābhihṛtkaṇṭhālikatālugam |
23.37 . ardhendurodhikānādatadantavyāpiśaktigam || 37
23.38 . samanonmanaśuddhātmaparacakrasamāśritam |
23.38 . yatra yatra japeccakre samastavyastabhedanāt || 38
23.39 . tatra tatra mahāmantra iti devyākhyayāmale |
23.39 . vidyāvratamidaṃ proktaṃ mantravīryaprasiddhaye || 39
23.40 . tacca tādātmyameveti yaduktaṃ spandaśāsane |
23.40 . tadākramya balaṃ mantrāḥ sarvajñabalaśālinaḥ || 40
23.41 . pravartante'dhikārāya karaṇānīva dehinām |
23.41 . kṛtavidyāvrataḥ paścāddīkṣāvyākhyādi sarvataḥ || 41
23.42 . kuryādyogyeṣu śiṣyeṣu nāyogyeṣu kadācana |
23.42 . rahasye yojayedvipraṃ parīkṣya viparītataḥ || 42
23.43 . ācārācchaktimapyeva nānyathetyūrmiśāsane |
23.43 . nityādyalpālpakaṃ kuryādyaduktaṃ brahmayāmale || 43
23.44 . cīrṇavidyāvrataḥ sarvaṃ manasā vā smaretpriye |
23.44 . dehasaṃbandhasaṃchannasārvajñyo dambhabhājanam || 44
23.45 . avidandīkṣamāṇo'pi na duṣyeddaiśikaḥ kvacit |
23.45 . jñātvā tvayogyatāṃ nainaṃ dīkṣeta pratyavāyitām || 45
23.46 . buddhvā jñāne śāstrasiddhigurutvādau ca taṃ punaḥ |
23.46 . bhūya eva parīkṣeta tattadaucityaśālinam || 46
23.47 . tatra tatra niyuñjīta natu jātu viparyayāt |
23.47 . nanu tadvastvayogyasya tatrecchā jāyate kutaḥ || 47
23.48 . tadīśādhiṣṭhitecchaiva yogyatāmasya sūcayet |
23.48 . satyaṃ kāpi prabuddhāsāvicchā rūḍhiṃ na gacchati || 48
23.49 . vidyudvatpāpaśīlasya yathā pāpāpavarjane |
23.49 . rūḍhyarūḍhī tadicchāyā api śaṃbhuprasādataḥ || 49
23.50 . aprarūḍhatathecchākastata eva na bhājanam |
23.50 . yaḥ samyagjñānamādāya guruviśvāsavarjitaḥ || 50
23.51 . lokaṃ viplāvayennāsmiñjñāte vijñānamarpayet |
23.51 . ajñāte'pi punarjñāte vijñānaharaṇaṃ caret || 51
23.52 . punaḥpunaryadā jñāto viśvāsaparivarjitaḥ |
23.52 . tadā tamagrato dhyāyetsphurantaṃ candrasūryavat || 52
23.53 . tato nijahṛdambhojabodhāmbarataloditām |
23.53 . svarbhānumalināṃ dhyāyedvāmāṃ śaktiṃ vimohanīm || 53
23.54 . vāmācārakrameṇaināṃ niḥsṛtāṃ sādhyagāminīm |
23.54 . cintayitvā tayā grastaprakāśaṃ taṃ vicintayet || 54
23.55 . anena kramayogena mūḍhabuddherdurātmanaḥ |
23.55 . vijñānamantravidyādyāḥ prakurvantyapakāritām || 55
23.56 . nanu vijñānamātmasthaṃ kathaṃ hartuṃ kṣamaṃ bhavet |
23.56 . ato vijñānaharaṇaṃ kathaṃ śrīpūrva ucyate || 56
23.57 . ucyate nāsya śiṣyasya vijñānaṃ rūḍhimāgatam |
23.57 . tathātve haraṇaṃ kasmātpūrṇayogyatvaśālinaḥ || 57
23.58 . kiṃtveṣa vāmayā śaktyā mūḍho gāḍhaṃ vibhoḥ kṛtaḥ |
23.58 . svabhāvādeva tenāsya vidyādyamapakārakam || 58
23.59 . guruḥ punaḥ śivābhinnaḥ sanyaḥ pañcavidhāṃ kṛtim |
23.59 . kuryādyadi tataḥ pūrṇamadhikāritvamasya tat || 59
23.60 . ato yathā śuddhatattvasṛṣṭisthityormalātyaye |
23.60 . yojanānugrahe kāryacatuṣke'dhikṛto guruḥ || 60
23.61 . śivābhedena tatkuryāttadvatpañcamamapyayam |
23.61 . tirobhāvābhidhaṃ kṛtyaṃ tathāsau śivatātmakaḥ || 61
23.62 . ata eva śive śāstre jñāne cāśvāsabhājanam |
23.62 . gurormūḍhatayā kopadhāmāpi na tirohitaḥ || 62
23.63 . gururhi kupito yasya sa tirohita ucyate |
23.63 . saṃsārī satu devo hi gururna ca mṛṣāvidaḥ || 63
23.64 . tata eva ca śāstrādidūṣako yadyapi krudhā |
23.64 . na dahyate'sau guruṇā tathāpyeṣa tirohitaḥ || 64
23.65 . asmadgurvāgamastveṣa tirobhūte svayaṃ śiśau |
23.65 . na kupyenna śapeddhīmān sa hyanugrāhakaḥ sadā || 65
23.66 . īśecchācoditaḥ pāśaṃ yadi kaṇṭhe nipīḍayet |
23.66 . kimācāryeṇa tatrāsya kāryā syātsahakāritā || 66
23.67 . śivābhinno'pi hi gururanugrahamayīṃ vibhoḥ |
23.67 . mukhyāṃ śaktimupāsīno'nugṛhṇīyātsa sarvathā || 67
23.68 . svātantryamātrajñaptyai tu kathitaṃ śāstra īdṛśam |
23.68 . na kāryaṃ patatāṃ hastālambaḥ sahyo na pātanam || 68
23.69 . ata eva svatantratvādicchāyāḥ punarunmukham |
23.69 . prāyaścittairviśodhyainaṃ dīkṣeta kṛpayā guruḥ || 69
23.70 . ūrdhvadṛṣṭau prapannaḥ sannanāśvastastataḥ param |
23.70 . adhaḥśāstraṃ prapadyāpi na śreyaḥpātratāmiyāt || 70
23.71 . adhodṛṣṭau prapannastu tadanāśvastamānasaḥ |
23.71 . ūrdhvaśāsanabhākpāpaṃ taccojjhecca śivībhavet || 71
23.72 . rājñe druhyannamātyāṅgabhūto'pi hi vihanyate |
23.72 . viparyayastu netyevamūrdhvāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ samāśrayet || 72
23.73 . śrīpūrvaśāstre tenoktaṃ yāvattenaiva noddhṛtaḥ |
23.73 . atra hyartho'yametāvatpūrvoktajñānavṛṃhitaḥ || 73
23.74 . gurustāvatsa evātra tacchabdenāvamṛśyate |
23.74 . tādṛksvabhyastavijñānabhājordhvapadaśālinā || 74
23.75 . anuddhṛtasya na śreya etadanyagurūddhṛteḥ |
23.75 . ata evāmbujanmārkadṛṣṭānto'tra nirūpitaḥ || 75
23.76 . trijagajjyotiṣo hyanyattejo'nyacca niśākṛtaḥ |
23.76 . jñānamanyattrikaguroranyattvadharavartinām || 76
23.77 . ata eva purābhūtagurvabhāvo yadā tadā |
23.77 . tadanyaṃ lakṣaṇopetamāśrayetpunarunmukhaḥ || 77
23.78 . sati tasmiṃstūnmukhaḥ sankasmājjahyādyadi sphuṭam |
23.78 . syādanyatarago doṣo yo'dhikārāpaghātakaḥ || 78
23.79 . doṣaśceha na lokastho doṣatvena nirūpyate |
23.79 . ajñānakhyāpanāyuktakhyāpanātmā tvasau mataḥ || 79
23.80 . śiṣyasyāpi tathābhūtajñānānāśvastarūpatā |
23.80 . mukhyo doṣastadanye hi doṣāstatprabhavā yataḥ || 80
23.81 . na dhvastavyādhikaḥ ko hi bhiṣajaṃ bahu manyate |
23.81 . asūyurnūnamadhvastavyādhiḥ svasthāyate balāt || 81
23.82 . evaṃ jñānasamāśvastaḥ kiṃ kiṃ na gurave caret |
23.82 . no cennūnamaviśvasto viśvasta iva tiṣṭhati || 82
23.83 . ajñānādaya evaite doṣā na laukikā guroḥ |
23.83 . iti khyāpayituṃ proktaṃ mālinīvijayottare || 83
23.84 . na tasyānveṣayedvṛttaṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham |
23.84 . sa eva tadvijānāti yuktaṃ cāyuktameva vā || 84
23.85 . akāryeṣu yadā saktaḥ prāṇadravyāpahāriṣu |
23.85 . tadā nivāraṇīyo'sau praṇatena vipaścitā || 85
23.86 . viśeṣaṇamakāryāṇāmuktābhiprāyameva yat |
23.86 . tenātivāryamāṇo'pi yadyasau na nivartate || 86
23.87 . tadānyatra kvacidgatvā śivamevānucintayet |
23.87 . na hyasya sa gurutve syāddoṣo yenoṣare kṛṣim || 87
23.88 . kuryādvrajenniśāyāṃ vā sa tvarthaprāṇahārakaḥ |
23.88 . tadīyāpriyabhīrustu paraṃ tādṛśamācaret || 88
23.89 . yatastadapriyaṃ naiṣa śṛṇuyāditi bhāṣitam |
23.89 . śrīmātaṅge taduktaṃ ca nādhītaṃ bhūmabhītitaḥ || 89
23.90 . yaccaitaduktametāvatkartavyamiti taddhruvam |
23.90 . tīvraśaktigṛhītānāṃ svayameva hṛdi sphuret || 90
23.91 . upadeśastvayaṃ mandamadhyaśakternijāṃ kramāt |
23.91 . śaktiṃ jvalayituṃ proktaḥ sā hyevaṃ jājvalītyalam || 91
23.92 . dṛḍhānurāgasubhagasaṃrambhābhogabhāginaḥ |
23.92 . svollāsi smarasarvasyaṃ dārḍhyāyānyatra dṛśyate || 92
23.93 . nanveṣa kasmāddṛṣṭāntaḥ kimetenāśubhaṃ kṛtam |
23.93 . citspandaḥ sarvago bhinnādupādheḥ sa tathā tathā || 93
23.94 . bhavetko'pi tirobhūtaḥ punarunmukhito'pi san |
23.94 . vināpi daiśikātprāgvatsvayameva vimucyate || 94
23.95 . prakārastveṣa nātroktaḥ śaktipātabalādgataḥ |
23.95 . asaṃbhāvyatayā cātra dṛḍhakopaprasādavat || 95
23.96 . ityeṣa yo guroḥ prokto vidhistaṃ pālayedguruḥ |
23.96 . anyathā na śivaṃ yāyācchrīmatsāre ca varṇitam || 96
23.97 . anyāyaṃ ye prakurvanti śāstrārthaṃ varjayantyalam |
23.97 . te'rdhanārīśapuragā guravaḥ samayacyutāḥ || 97
23.98 . anyatrāpyadhikāraṃ ca neyādvidyeśatāṃ vrajet |
23.98 . anyatra samayatyāgātkravyādatvaṃ śataṃ samāḥ || 98
23.99 . iyattatratyatātparyaṃ siddhāntagururunnayaḥ |
23.99 . bhavetpiśācavidyeśaḥ śuddha eva tu tāntrikaḥ || 99
23.100 . ṣaḍardhadaiśikaścārdhanārīśabhuvanasthitiḥ |
23.100 . eṣā karmapradhānānāṃ gurūṇāṃ gatirucyate || 100
23.101 . jñānināṃ caiṣa no bandha iti sarvatra varṇitam |
23.101 . sādhakasyābhiṣeke'pi sarvo'yaṃ kathyate vidhiḥ || 101
23.102 . adhikārārpaṇaṃ nātra naca vidyāvrataṃ kila |
23.102 . sādhyamantrārpaṇaṃ tvatra svopayogikriyākrame || 102
23.103 . samaste'pyupadeśaḥ syānnijopakaraṇārpaṇam |
23.103 . abhiṣekavidhirnirūpitaḥ parameśena yathā nirūpitaḥ || 103

c24 atha śrītantrāloke caturviṃśatitamamāhnikam

24.1 . atha śāmbhavaśāsanoditāṃ sarahasyāṃ śṛṇutāntyasaṃskriyām || 1
24.2 . sarveṣāmadharasthānāṃ gurvantānāmapi sphuṭam |
24.2 . śaktipātātpurāproktātkuryādantyeṣṭidīkṣaṇam || 2
24.3 . ūrdhvaśāsanagānāṃ ca samayopahatātmanām |
24.3 . antyeṣṭidīkṣā kartavyā guruṇā tattvavedinā || 3
24.4 . samayācāradoṣeṣu pramādātskhalitasya hi |
24.4 . antyeṣṭidīkṣā kāryeti śrīdīkṣottaraśāsane || 4
24.5 . yatkiṃcitkathitaṃ pūrvaṃ mṛtoddhārābhidhe vidhau |
24.5 . pratimāyāṃ tadevātra sarvaṃ śavatanau caret || 5
24.6 . śrīsiddhātantrakathito vidhireṣa nirūpyate |
24.6 . antimaṃ yadbhavetpūrvaṃ tatkṛtvāntimamādimam || 6
24.7 . saṃhṛtyaikaikamiṣṭiryā sāntyeṣṭirdvitayī matā |
24.7 . pūjādhyānajapāpluṣṭasamaye natu sādhake || 7
24.8 . piṇḍapātādayaṃ muktaḥ khecaro vā bhavetpriye |
24.8 . ācārye tattvasaṃpanne yatra tatra mṛte sati || 8
24.9 . antyeṣṭirnaiva vidyeta śuddhacetasyamūrdhani |
24.9 . mantrayogādibhirye ca māritā narake tu te || 9
24.10 . kāryā teṣāmihāntyeṣṭirguruṇātikṛpālunā |
24.10 . na maṇḍalādikaṃ tvatra bhavecchamāśānike vidhau || 10
24.11 . kecittadapi kartavyamūcire pretasadmani |
24.11 . pūjayitvā vibhuṃ sarvaṃ nyāsaṃ pūrvavadācaret || 11
24.12 . saṃhārakramayogena caraṇānmūrdhapaścimam |
24.12 . tathaiva bodhayedenaṃ kriyājñānasamādhibhiḥ || 12
24.13 . bindunā rodhayettattvaṃ śaktibījena vedhayet |
24.13 . ghaṭṭayennādadeśe tu triśūlena tu tāḍayet || 13
24.14 . suṣumnāntargatenaiva visargeṇa punaḥ punaḥ |
24.14 . tāḍayeta kalāḥ sarvāḥ kampate'sau tataḥ paśuḥ || 14
24.15 . utkṣipedvāmahastaṃ vā tatastaṃ yojayetpare |
24.15 . pratyayena vinā mokṣo hyaśraddheyo vimohitaiḥ || 15
24.16 . tadarthametaduditaṃ natu mokṣopayogyadaḥ |
24.16 . ityūce parameśaḥ śrīkulagahvaraśāsane || 16
24.17 . sādhyo'numeyo mokṣādiḥ pratyayairyadatīndriyaḥ |
24.17 . dīkṣottare ca puryaṣṭavargārpaṇamihoditam || 17
24.18 . tadvidhiḥ śrutipatre'bje madhye devaṃ sadāśivam |
24.18 . īśarudraharibrahmacatuṣkaṃ prāgdigāditaḥ || 18
24.19 . pūjayitvā śrutisparśau rasaṃ gandhaṃ vapurdvayam |
24.19 . dhyahaṃkṛtī manaśceti brahmādiṣvarpayetkramāt || 19
24.20 . eteṣāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śatahomena daiśikaḥ |
24.20 . eṣā sāṃnyāsikī dīkṣā puryaṣṭakaviśodhanī || 20
24.21 . puryaṣṭakasyābhāve ca na svarganarakādayaḥ |
24.21 . tathā kṛtvā na kartavyaṃ laukikaṃ kiṃcanāpi hi || 21
24.22 . uktaṃ śrīmādhavakule śāsanastho mṛteṣvapi |
24.22 . piṇḍapātodakāsrvādi laukikaṃ parivarjayet || 22
24.23 . śivaṃ saṃpūjya cakrārcāṃ yathāśakti samācaret |
24.23 . kramāttridaśamatriṃśatriṃśavatsaravāsare || 23
24.24 . ityukto'ntyeṣṭiyāgo'yaṃ parameśvarabhāṣitaḥ || 24

c25 atha śrītantrāloke pañcaviṃśatitamamāhnikam

25.1 . atha śrāddhavidhiḥ śrīmatṣaḍardhokto nigadyate || 1
25.2 . siddhātantre sūcito'sau mūrtiyāganirūpaṇe |
25.2 . antyeṣṭyā suviśuddhānāmaśuddhānāṃ ca tadvidhiḥ || 2
25.3 . tryahe turye'hni daśame māsi māsyādyavatsare |
25.3 . varṣe varṣe sarvakālaṃ kāryastatsvaiḥ sa pūrvavat || 3
25.4 . tatra prāgvadyajeddevaṃ homayedanale tathā |
25.4 . tato naivedyameva prāggṛhītvā hastagocare || 4
25.5 . gururannamayīṃ śaktiṃ vṛṃhikāṃ vīryarūpiṇīm |
25.5 . dhyātvā tayā samāviṣṭaṃ taṃ sādhyaṃ cintayetsudhīḥ || 5
25.6 . tato'sya yaḥ pāśavoṃ'śo bhogyarūpastamarpayet |
25.6 . bhoktaryekātmabhāvena śiṣya itthaṃ śivībhavet || 6
25.7 . bhogyatānyā tanurdeha iti pāśātmakā matāḥ |
25.7 . śrāddhe mṛtoddhṛtāvantayāge teṣāṃ śivīkṛtiḥ || 7
25.8 . ekenaiva vidhānena yadyapi syātkṛtārthatā |
25.8 . tathāpi tanmayībhāvasiddhyai sarvaṃ vidhiṃ caret || 8
25.9 . bubhukṣostu kriyābhyāsabhūmānau phalabhūmani |
25.9 . hetu tato mṛtoddhāraśrāddhādyasmai samācaret || 9
25.10 . tattvajñānārkavidhvastadhvāntasya tu na ko'pyayam |
25.10 . antyeṣṭiśrāddhavidhyādirupayogī kadācana || 10
25.11 . teṣāṃ tu guru tadvargavargyasabrahmacāriṇām |
25.11 . tatsantānajuṣāmaikyadinaṃ parvadinaṃ bhavet || 11
25.12 . yadāhi bodhasyodrekastadā parvāha pūraṇāt |
25.12 . janmaikyadivasau tena parvaṇī bodhasiddhitaḥ || 12
25.13 . putrako'pi yadā kasmaicana syādupakārakaḥ |
25.13 . tadā mātuḥ pituḥ śaktervāmadakṣāntarālagāḥ || 13
25.14 . nāḍīḥ pravāhayeddevāyārpayeta niveditam |
25.14 . śrīmadbharuṇatantre ca tacchivena nirūpitam || 14
25.15 . tadvāhakālāpekṣā ca kāryā tadrūpasiddhaye |
25.15 . svācchandyenātha tatsiddhiṃ vidhinā bhāvinā caret || 15
25.16 . yasya kasyāpi vā śrāddhe gurudevāgnitarpaṇam |
25.16 . sacakreṣṭi bhavecchrauto natu syātpāśavo vidhiḥ || 16
25.17 . śrīmaukuṭe tathā coktaṃ śivaśāstre sthito'pi yaḥ |
25.17 . pratyeti vaidike bhagnaghaṇṭāvanna sa kiṃcana || 17
25.18 . tathoktadevapūjādicakrayāgāntakarmaṇā |
25.18 . rudratvametyasau janturbhogāndivyānsamaśnute || 18
25.19 . atha vacmaḥ sphuṭaṃ śrīmatsiddhaye nāḍicāraṇam |
25.19 . yā vāhayitumiṣyeta nāḍī tāmeva bhāvayet || 19
25.20 . bhāvanātanmayībhāve sā nāḍī vahati sphuṭam |
25.20 . yadvā vāhayituṃ yeṣṭā tadaṅgaṃ tena pāṇinā || 20
25.21 . āpīḍya kukṣiṃ namayetsā vahennāḍikā kṣaṇāt |
25.21 . evaṃ śrāddhamukhenāpi bhogamokṣobhayasthitim || 21
25.22 . kuryāditi śivenoktaṃ tatra tatra kṛpālunā |
25.22 . śaktipātodaye jantoryenopāyena daiśikaḥ || 22
25.23 . karotyuddharaṇaṃ tattannirvāṇāyāsya kalpate |
25.23 . uddhartā devadevo hi sa cācintyaprabhāvakaḥ || 23
25.24 . upāyaṃ gurudīkṣādidvāramātreṇa saṃśrayet |
25.24 . uktaṃ śrīmanmataṅgākhye munipraśnādanantaram || 24
25.25 . muktirvivekāttattvānāṃ dīkṣāto yogato yadi |
25.25 . caryāmātrātkathaṃ sā syādityataḥ samamuttaram || 25
25.26 . prahasyoce vibhuḥ kasmādbhrāntiste parameśituḥ |
25.26 . sarvānugrāhakatvaṃ hi saṃsiddhaṃ dṛśyatāṃ kila || 26
25.27 . prāptamṛtyorviṣavyādhiśastrādi kila kāraṇam |
25.27 . alpaṃ vā bahu vā tadvadanudhyā muktikāraṇam || 27
25.28 . muktyarthamupacaryante bāhyaliṅgānyamūni tu |
25.28 . iti jñātvā na sandeha itthaṃ kāryo vipaścitā || 28
25.29 . iyataiva kathaṃ muktiriti bhaktiṃ parāṃ śrayet |
25.29 . uktaḥ śrāddhavidhirbhrāntigarātaṅkavimardanaḥ || 29

c26 atha śrītantrāloke ṣaḍviṃśamāhnikam

26.1 . athocyate śeṣavṛttirjīvatāmupayoginī || 1
26.2 . dīkṣā bahuprakāreyaṃ śrāddhāntā yā prakīrtitā |
26.2 . sā saṃskriyāyai mokṣāya bhogāyāpi dvayāya vā || 2
26.3 . tatra saṃskārasiddhyai yā dīkṣā sākṣānna mocanī |
26.3 . anusaṃdhivaśādyā ca sākṣānmoktrī sabījikā || 3
26.4 . tayobhayyā dīkṣitā ye teṣāmājīvavartanam |
26.4 . vaktavyaṃ putrakādīnāṃ tanmayatvaprasiddhaye || 4
26.5 . bubhukṣorvā mumukṣorvā svasaṃvidguruśāstrataḥ |
26.5 . pramāṇādyā saṃskriyāyai dīkṣā hi guruṇā kṛtā || 5
26.6 . tataḥ sa saṃskṛtaṃ yogyaṃ jñātvātmānaṃ svaśāsane |
26.6 . taduktavastvanuṣṭhānaṃ bhuktyai muktyai ca sevate || 6
26.7 . ācāryapratyayādeva yo'pi syādbhuktimuktibhāk |
26.7 . tatpratyūhodayadhvastyai brūyāttasyāpi vartanam || 7
26.8 . svasaṃvidgurusaṃvittyostulyapratyayabhāgapi |
26.8 . śeṣavṛttyā samādeśyastadvighnādipraśāntaye || 8
26.9 . yaḥ sarvathā parāpekṣāmujjhitvā tu sthito nijāt |
26.9 . pratyayādyo'pi cācāryapratyayādeva kevalāt || 9
26.10 . tau sāṃsiddhikanirbījau ko vadeccheṣavṛttaye |
26.10 . kramāttanmayatopāyagurvarcanaratau tu tau || 10
26.11 . tatraiṣāṃ śeṣavṛttyarthaṃ nityanaimittike dhruve |
26.11 . kāmyavarjaṃ yataḥ kāmāścitrāścitrābhyupāyakāḥ || 11
26.12 . tatra nityo vidhiḥ sandhyānuṣṭhānaṃ devatāvraje |
26.12 . gurvagniśāstrasahite pūjā bhūtadayetyayam || 12
26.13 . naimittikastu sarveṣāṃ parvaṇāṃ pūjanaṃ japaḥ |
26.13 . viśeṣavaśataḥ kiṃca pavitrakavidhikramaḥ || 13
26.14 . ācāryasya ca dīkṣeyaṃ bahubhedā vivecitā |
26.14 . vyākhyādikaṃ ca tattasyādhikaṃ naimittikaṃ dhruvam || 14
26.15 . tatrādau śiśave vrūyādgururnityavidhiṃ sphuṭam |
26.15 . tadyogyatāṃ samālokya vitatāvitatātmanām || 15
