26.16 . mukhyetarādimantrāṇāṃ vīryavyāptyādiyogyatām |
26.16 . dṛṣṭvā śiṣye tamevāsmai mūlamantraṃ samarpayet || 16
26.17 . tacchāstradīkṣito hyeṣa niryantrācāraśaṅkitaḥ |
26.17 . na mukhye yogya ityanyasevātaḥ syāttu yogyatā || 17
26.18 . sādhakasya bubhukṣostu sādhakībhāvino'pivā |
26.18 . puṣpapātavaśātsiddho mantro'rpyaḥ sādhyasiddhaye || 18
26.19 . vitate guṇabhūte vā vidhau diṣṭe punarguruḥ |
26.19 . jñātvāsmai yogyatāṃ sāraṃ saṃkṣiptaṃ vidhimācaret || 19
26.20 . tatraiṣa niyamo yadyanmāntraṃ rūpaṃ na tadguruḥ |
26.20 . likhitvā prathayecchiṣye viśeṣādūrdhvaśāsane || 20
26.21 . mantrā varṇātmakāste ca parāmarśātmakāḥ saca |
26.21 . gurusaṃvidabhinnaśvetsaṃkrāmetsā tataḥ śiśau || 21
26.22 . lipisthitastu yo mantro nirvīryaḥ so'tra kalpitaḥ |
26.22 . saṃketabalato nāsya pustakātprathate mahaḥ || 22
26.23 . pustakādhītavidyāścetyuktaṃ siddhāmate tataḥ |
26.23 . ye tu pustakalabdhe'pi mantre vīryaṃ prajānate || 23
26.24 . te bhairavīyasaṃskārāḥ proktāḥ sāṃsiddhikā iti |
26.24 . iti jñātvā guruḥ samyakparamānandaghūrṇitaḥ || 24
26.25 . tādṛśe tādṛśe dhāmni pūjayitvā vidhiṃ caret |
26.25 . yathānyaśiṣyānuṣṭhānaṃ nānyaśiṣyeṇa budhyate || 25
26.26 . tathā kuryādgururguptihānirdoṣavatī yataḥ |
26.26 . devīnāṃ tritayaṃ śuddhaṃ yadvā yāmalayogataḥ || 26
26.27 . devīmekāmatho śuddhāṃ vadedvā yāmalātmikām |
26.27 . tatra mantraṃ sphuṭaṃ vaktrādguruṇopāṃśu coditam || 27
26.28 . avadhāryā pravṛttestamabhyasyenmanasā svayam |
26.28 . tataḥ suśikṣitāṃ sthānadehāntaḥśodhanatrayīm || 28
26.29 . nyāsaṃ dhyānaṃ japaṃ mudrāṃ pūjāṃ kuryātprayatnataḥ |
26.29 . tatra prabhāte saṃbudhya sveṣṭāṃ prāgdevatāṃ smaret || 29
26.30 . kṛtāvaśyakakartavyaḥ śuddho bhūtvā tato gṛham |
26.30 . āśrityottaradigvaktraḥ sthānadehāntaratraye || 30
26.31 . śuddhiṃ vidhāya mantrāṇāṃ yathāsthānaṃ niveśanam |
26.31 . mudrāpradarśanaṃ dhyānaṃ bhedābhedasvarūpataḥ || 31
26.32 . dehāsudhīvyomabhūṣu manasā tatra cārcanam |
26.32 . japaṃ cātra yathāśakti devāyaitannivedanam || 32
26.33 . tanmayībhāvasiddhyarthaṃ pratisandhyaṃ samācaret |
26.33 . anye tu prāgudakpaścāddaśadikṣu catuṣṭayīm || 33
26.34 . sandhyānāmāhuretacca tāntrikīyaṃ na no matam |
26.34 . yāsau kālādhikāre prāksandhyā proktā catuṣṭayī || 34
26.35 . tāmevāntaḥ samādhāya sāndhyaṃ vidhimupācaret |
26.35 . sandhyācatuṣṭayīkṛtyamekasyāmathavā śiśuḥ || 35
26.36 . kuryātsvādhyāyavijñānagurukṛtyāditatparaḥ |
26.36 . sandhyādhyānoditānantatanmayībhāvayuktitaḥ || 36
26.37 . tatsaṃskāravaśātsarvaṃ kālaṃ syāttanmayo hyasau |
26.37 . tato yatheṣṭakāle'sau pūjāṃ puṣpāsavādibhiḥ || 37
26.38 . sthaṇḍilādau śiśuḥ kuryādvibhavādyanurūpataḥ |
26.38 . suśuddhaḥ sanvidhiṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvāntarajapāntakam || 38
26.39 . arghapātraṃ purā yadvadvidhāya sveṣṭamantrataḥ |
26.39 . tena sthaṇḍilapuṣpādi sarvaṃ saṃprokṣayedbudhaḥ || 39
26.40 . tatastatraiva saṃkalpya dvārāsanagurukramam |
26.40 . pūjayecchivatāviṣṭaḥ svadehārcāpuraḥsaram || 40
26.41 . tatastatsthaṇḍilaṃ vīdhravyomasphaṭikanirmalam |
26.41 . bodhātmakaṃ samālokya tatra svaṃ devatāgaṇam || 41
26.42 . pratibimbatayā paśyedbimbatvena ca bodhataḥ |
26.42 . etadāvāhanaṃ mukhyaṃ vyajanānmarutāmiva || 42
26.43 . sarvago'pi marudyadvadvyajanenopajīvitaḥ |
26.43 . arthakṛtsarvagaṃ mantracakraṃ rūḍhestathā bhavet || 43
26.44 . catuṣkapañcāśikayā tadetattattvamucyate |
26.44 . śrīnirmaryādaśāstre ca tadetadvibhunoditam || 44
26.45 . devaḥ sarvagato deva nirmaryādaḥ kathaṃ śivaḥ |
26.45 . āvāhyate kṣamyate vetyevaṃpṛṣṭo'bravīdvibhuḥ || 45
26.46 . vāsanāvāhyate devi vāsanā ca visṛjyate |
26.46 . paramārthena devasya nāvāhanavisarjane || 46
26.47 . āvāhito mayā devaḥ sthaṇḍile ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
26.47 . pūjitaḥ stuta ityevaṃ hṛṣṭvā devaṃ visarjayet || 47
26.48 . prāṇināmaprabuddhānāṃ santoṣajananāya vai |
26.48 . āvāhanādikaṃ teṣāṃ pravṛttiḥ kathamanyathā || 48
26.49 . kālena tu vijānanti pravṛttāḥ patiśāsane |
26.49 . anukrameṇa devasya prāptiṃ bhuvanapūrvikām || 49
26.50 . jñānadīpadyutidhvastasamastājñānasañcayāḥ |
26.50 . kuto vānīyate devaḥ kutra vā nīyate'pi saḥ || 50
26.51 . sthūlasūkṣmādibhedena sa hi sarvatra saṃsthitaḥ |
26.51 . āvāhite mantragaṇe puṣpāsavanivedanaiḥ || 51
26.52 . dhūpaiśca tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ śraddhābhaktibalocitaiḥ |
26.52 . dīptānāṃ śaktinādādimantrāṇāmāsavaiḥ palaiḥ || 52
26.53 . raktaiḥ prāktarpaṇa paścātpuṣpadhūpādivistaraiḥ |
26.53 . āgatasya tu mantrasya na kuryāttarpaṇaṃ yadi || 53
26.54 . haratyardhaśarīraṃ sa ityuktaṃ kila śambhunā |
26.54 . yadyadevāsya manasi vikāsitvaṃ prayacchati || 54
26.55 . tenaiva kuryātpūjāṃ sa iti śambhorviniścayaḥ |
26.55 . sādhakānāṃ bubhukṣūṇāṃ vidhirniyatiyantritaḥ || 55
26.56 . mumukṣūṇāṃ tattvavidāṃ sa eva tu nirargalaḥ |
26.56 . kārye viśeṣamādhitsurviśiṣṭaṃ kāraṇaṃ spṛśet || 56
26.57 . raktakarpāsatūlecchustulyatadbījapuñjavat |
26.57 . santi bhoge viśeṣāśca vicitrāḥ kāraṇeritāḥ || 57
26.58 . deśakālānusandhānaguṇadravyakriyādibhiḥ |
26.58 . svalpā kriyā bhūyasī vā hṛdayāhlādadāyibhiḥ || 58
26.59 . bāhyaiḥ saṃkalpajairvāpi kārakaiḥ parikalpitā |
26.59 . mumukṣorna viśeṣāya naiḥśreyasavidhiṃ prati || 59
26.60 . nahi brahmaṇi śaṃsanti bāhulyālpatvadurdaśāḥ |
26.60 . citaḥ svātantryasāratvāttasyānandaghanatvataḥ || 60
26.61 . kriyā syāttanmayībhūtyai hṛdayāhlādadāyibhiḥ |
26.61 . śivābhedabharādbhāvavargaḥ ścyotati yaṃ rasam || 61
26.62 . tameva parame dhāmni pūjanāyārpayedbudhaḥ |
26.62 . stotreṣu bahudhā caitanmayā proktaṃ nijāhnike || 62
26.63 . adhiśayya pāramārthikabhāvaprasaraprakāśamullasati |
26.63 . yā paramāmṛtadṛktvāṃ tayārcayante rahasyavidaḥ || 63
26.64 . kṛtvādhāradharāṃ camatkṛtirasaprokṣākṣaṇakṣālitāmāttairmānasataḥ svabhāvakusumaiḥ svāmodasandohibhiḥ |
26.64 . ānandāmṛtanirbharasvahṛdayānarghārghapātrakramāttvāṃ devyā saha dehadevasadane devārcaye'harniśam || 64
26.65 . nānāsvādarasāmimāṃ trijagatīṃ hṛccakrayantrārpitāmūrdhvādhyastavivekagauravabharānniṣpīḍya niḥṣyanditam |
26.65 . yatsaṃvitparamāmṛtaṃ mṛtijarājanmāpahaṃ jṛmbhate tena tvāṃ haviṣā pareṇa parame saṃtarpaye'harniśam || 65
26.66 . iti ślokatrayopāttamarthamantarvibhāvayan |
26.66 . yena kenāpi bhāvena tarpayeddevatāgaṇam || 66
26.67 . mudrāṃ pradarśayetpaścānmanasā vāpi yogataḥ |
26.67 . vacasā mantrayogena vapuṣā saṃniveśataḥ || 67
26.68 . kṛtvā japaṃ tataḥ sarvaṃ devatāyai samarpayet |
26.68 . taccoktaṃ kartṛtātattvanirūpaṇavidhau purā || 68
26.69 . tato visarjanaṃ kāryaṃ bodhaikātmyaprayogataḥ |
26.69 . kṛtvā vā vahnigāṃ mantratṛptiṃ proktavidhānataḥ || 69
26.70 . dvārapīṭhaguruvrātasamarpitanivedanāt |
26.70 . ṛte'nyatsvayamaśnīyādagādhe'mbhasyatha kṣipet || 70
26.71 . prāṇino jalajāḥ pūrvadīkṣitāḥ śambhunā svayam |
26.71 . vidhinā bhāvinā śrīmanmīnanāthāvatāriṇā || 71
26.72 . mārjāramūṣikādyairyadadīkṣaiścāpi bhakṣitam |
26.72 . tacchaṅkātaṅkadānena vyādhaye narakāya ca || 72
26.73 . atastattvavidā dhvastaśaṅkātaṅko'pi paṇḍitaḥ |
26.73 . prakaṭaṃ nedṛśaṃ kuryāllokānugrahavāñchayā || 73
26.74 . śrīmanmatamahāśāstre taduktaṃ vibhunā svayam |
26.74 . svayaṃ tu śaṅkāsaṅkocaniṣkāsanaparāyaṇaḥ || 74
26.75 . bhavettathā yathānyeṣāṃ śaṅkā no manasi sphuret |
26.75 . mārjayitvā tataḥ snānaṃ puṣpeṇātha prapūjayet || 75
26.76 . puṣpādi sarvaṃ tatsthaṃ tadagādhāmbhasi nikṣipet |
26.76 . uktaḥ sthaṇḍilayāgo'yaṃ nityakarmaṇi śambhunā || 76

c27 atha śrītantrāloke saptaviṃśatitamamāhnikam

27.1 . athocyate liṅgapūjā sūcitā mālinīmate || 1
27.2 . eteṣāmūrdhvaśāstroktamantrāṇāṃ na pratiṣṭhitam |
27.2 . bahiṣkuryāttato hyete rahasyatvena siddhidāḥ || 2
27.3 . svavīryānandamāhātmyapraveśavaśaśālinīm |
27.3 . ye siddhiṃ dadate teṣāṃ bāhyatvaṃ rūpavicyutiḥ || 3
27.4 . kiṃca coktaṃ samāveśapūrṇo bhoktrātmakaḥ śivaḥ |
27.4 . bhogalāmpaṭyabhāgbhogavicchede nigrahātmakaḥ || 4
27.5 . śāntatvanyakkriyodbhūtajighatsāvṛṃhitaṃ vapuḥ |
27.5 . svayaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yena so'syābhoge vinaśyati || 5
27.6 . uktaṃ jñānottarāyāṃ ca tadetatparameśinā |
27.6 . śivo yāgapriyo yasmādviśeṣānmātṛmadhyagaḥ || 6
27.7 . tasmādrahasyaśāstreṣu ye mantrāstānbudho bahiḥ |
27.7 . na pratiṣṭhāpayejjātu viśeṣādvyaktarūpiṇaḥ || 7
27.8 . ata eva mṛtasyārthe pratiṣṭhānyatra yoditā |
27.8 . sātra śāstreṣu no kāryā kāryā sādhāraṇī punaḥ || 8
27.9 . ā tanmayatvasaṃsiddherā cābhīṣṭaphalodayāt |
27.9 . putrakaḥ sādhako vyaktamavyaktaṃ vā samāśrayet || 9
27.10 . putrakairgururabhyarthyaḥ sādhakastu svayaṃ vidan |
27.10 . yadi tatsthāpayenno cettenāpyarthyo gururbhavet || 10
27.11 . guruścātra nirodhākhye kāla itthaṃ vibhau vadet |
27.11 . jīvatyasminphalāntaṃ tvaṃ tiṣṭherjīvāvadhīti vā || 11
27.12 . liṅgaṃ ca bāṇaliṅgaṃ vā ratnajaṃ vātha mauktikam |
27.12 . pauṣpamānnamatho vāstraṃ gandhadravyakṛtaṃ ca vā || 12
27.13 . natu pāṣāṇajaṃ liṅgaṃ śilpyutthaṃ parikalpayet |
27.13 . dhātūtthaṃ ca suvarṇotthavarjamanyadvivarjayet || 13
27.14 . na cātra liṅgamānādi kvacidapyupayujyate |
27.14 . udāravīryairmantrairyadbhāsitaṃ phaladaṃ hi tat || 14
27.15 . tasyāpi sthaṇḍilādyuktavidhinā śuddhimācaret |
27.15 . mantrārpaṇaṃ tathaiva syānnirodhastūktayuktitaḥ || 15
27.16 . agnau ca tarpaṇaṃ bhūriviśeṣāddakṣiṇā guroḥ |
27.16 . dīnāditṛptirvibhavādyāga ityadhiko vidhiḥ || 16
27.17 . sarveṣvavyaktaliṅgeṣu pradhānaṃ syādakalpitam |
27.17 . tathā ca tatra tatroktaṃ lakṣaṇe pārameśvare || 17
27.18 . sūtre pātre dhvaje vastre svayambhūbāṇapūjite |
27.18 . nadīprasravaṇotthe ca nāhvānaṃ nāpi kalpanā || 18
27.19 . pīṭhaprasādamantrāṃśavelādiniyamo naca |
27.19 . vyaktaṃ vā citrapustādau devadārusuvarṇajam || 19
27.20 . atha dīkṣitasacchilpikṛtaṃ sthāpayate guruḥ |
27.20 . athavā lakṣaṇopetamūrdhatatkarparāśritam || 20
27.21 . paṅkticakrakaśūlābjavidhinā tūramāśrayet |
27.21 . tallakṣaṇaṃ bruve śrīmatpicuśāstre nirūpitam || 21
27.22 . tūre yogaḥ sadā śastaḥ siddhido doṣavarjite |
27.22 . jālakairjarjarai randhrairdantairūnādhikai rujā || 22
27.23 . yukte ca tūre hāniḥ syāttaddhīne yāga uttamaḥ |
27.23 . kāmya eva bhavettūramiti kecitprapedire || 23
27.24 . guravastu vidhau kāmye yatnāddoṣāṃstyajediti |
27.24 . vyācakṣate picuproktaṃ na nitye karmaṇītyadaḥ || 24
27.25 . śrīsiddhātantra uktaṃ ca tūralakṣaṇamuttamam |
27.25 . ekādikacatuṣkhaṇḍe gomukhe pūrṇacandrake || 25
27.26 . padmagorocanāmuktānīrasphaṭikasaṃnibhe |
27.26 . ekādipañcasadrandhravidyārekhānvite śubhe || 26
27.27 . na rūkṣavakraśakaladīrghanimnasabinduke |
27.27 . ślakṣṇayā vajrasūcyātra sphuṭaṃ devīgaṇānvitam || 27
27.28 . sarvaṃ samālikhetpūjyaṃ sarvāvayavasundaram |
27.28 . etadevānusartavyamarghapātre'pi lakṣaṇam || 28
27.29 . śrībrahmayāmale'pyuktaṃ pātraṃ gomukhamuttamam |
27.29 . gajakūrmatalaṃ kumbhavṛttaśaktikajākṛti || 29
27.30 . akṣasūtramatho kuryāttatraivābhyarcayetkramam |
27.30 . vīradhātujalodbhūtamuktāratnasuvarṇajam || 30
27.31 . akṣasūtraṃ kramotkṛṣṭaṃ raudrākṣaṃ vā viśeṣataḥ |
27.31 . śataṃ tithyuttaraṃ yadvā sāṣṭaṃ yadvā tadardhakam || 31
27.32 . tadardhaṃ vātha pañcāśadyuktaṃ tatparikalpayet |
27.32 . vaktrāṇi pañca citspandajñānecchākṛtisaṃgateḥ || 32
27.33 . pañcadhādyantagaṃ caikyamityupāntyākṣago vidhiḥ |
27.33 . śaktitadvatprabhedena tatra dvairūpyamucyate || 33
27.34 . tato dviguṇamāne tu dvirūpaṃ nyāsamācaret |
27.34 . tato'pi dviguṇe sṛṣṭisaṃhṛtidvitayena tam || 34
27.35 . mātṛkāṃ mālinīṃ vātha nyasyetkhaśarasaṃmite |
27.35 . uttame tu dvayīṃ nyasyennyasya pūrvaṃ pracoditān || 35
27.36 . dīkṣāyāṃ mukhyato mantrāṃstānpañcadaśa daiśikaḥ |
27.36 . yadi vā tattvabhuvanakalāmantrapadārṇajaiḥ || 36
27.37 . saṃkhyābhedaiḥ kṛte sūtre taṃ taṃ nyāsaṃ guruścaret |
27.37 . kṛtvākṣasūtraṃ tasyāpi sarvaṃ sthaṇḍilavadbhavet || 37
27.38 . pūjitena ca tenaiva japaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ |
27.38 . vidhiruktastvayaṃ śrīmanmālinīvijayottare || 38
27.39 . cakravadbhramayannetadyadvakti sa japo bhavet |
27.39 . yadīkṣate juhotyetadbodhāgnau saṃpraveśanāt || 39
27.40 . athavārghamahāpātraṃ kuryāttaccottaraṃ param |
27.40 . nārikelamatho bailvaṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ ca vā || 40
27.41 . tasyāpyeṣa vidhiḥ sarvaḥ pratiṣṭhādau prakīrtitaḥ |
27.41 . tanniṣkamparasaiḥ pūrṇaṃ kṛtvāsminpūjayetkramam || 41
27.42 . adhomukhaṃ sadā sthāpyaṃ pūjitaṃ pūjane punaḥ |
27.42 . tatpātramunmukhaṃ tacca riktaṃ kuryānna tādṛśam || 42
27.43 . pūjānte tadrasāpūrṇamātmānaṃ pravidhāya tat |
27.43 . adhomukhaṃ ca saṃpūjya sthāpayetvicakṣaṇaḥ || 43
27.44 . khaṅgaṃ kṛpāṇikāṃ yadvā kartarīṃ makuraṃ ca vā |
27.44 . vimalaṃ tattathā kuryācchrīmatkālīmukhoditam || 44
27.45 . śrībhairavakule'pyuktaṃ kulaparvaprapūjane |
27.45 . sthaṇḍile'gnau paṭe liṅge pātre padme'tha maṇḍale || 45
27.46 . mūrtau ghaṭe'strasaṃghāte dhaṭe sūtre'tha pūjayet |
27.46 . svena svenopacāreṇa saṅkaraṃ varjayediti || 46
27.47 . yathāpsu śāntaye mantrāstadvadastrādiṣu dhruvam |
27.47 . śatrucchedādikartāraḥ kāmyo'taḥ saṅkarojjhitaḥ || 47
27.48 . akāmasya tu te tattatsthānopādhivaśāddhruvam |
27.48 . pāśakartanasaṃśuddhatattvāpyāyādikāriṇaḥ || 48
27.49 . athavā pustakaṃ tādṛgrahaḥśāstrakramombhitam |
27.49 . suśuddhaṃ dīkṣitakṛtaṃ tatrāpyeṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 49
27.50 . itthaṃ svayaṃpratiṣṭheṣu yāvadyāvatsthitirbhavet |
27.50 . vibhavaistarpaṇaṃ śuddhistāvadvicchedavarjanam || 50
27.51 . ata eva yadā bhūridinaṃ maṇḍalakalpanam |
27.51 . tadā dine dine kuryādvibhavaistarpaṇaṃ bahu || 51
27.52 . pratiṣṭhāyāṃ ca sarvatra guruḥ pūrvoditaṃ param |
27.52 . satattvamanusandhāya saṃnidhiṃ sphuṭamācaret || 52
27.53 . siddhe tu tanmayībhāve phale putrakasādhakaiḥ |
27.53 . anyasmai taddvayādanyatarasmai tatsamarpyate || 53
27.54 . tasyāpyeṣa vidhiḥ sarvastadalābhe tu sarvathā |
27.54 . agādhe'mbhasi tatkṣepyaṃ kṣamayitvā visṛjya ca || 54
27.55 . ityeṣa svapratiṣṭhānavidhiḥ śivanirūpitaḥ |
27.55 . parapratiṣṭhite liṅge bāṇīye'tha svayaṃbhuvi || 55
27.56 . sarvamāsanapakṣe prāṅnyasya saṃpūjayetkramam |
27.56 . śuddhāśuddhādhvajāḥ sarve mantrāḥ sarvaḥ śivāntakaḥ || 56
27.57 . adhvā cehāsane proktastatsarvatrārcayedidam |
27.57 . āvāhanavisṛṣṭī tu tatra prāgvatsamācaret || 57
27.58 . uktaṃ tantre'pyaghoreśe svacchande vibhunā tathā |
27.58 . athavā pratyahaṃ proktamānārdhārdhaniyogataḥ || 58
27.59 . kṛtveṣṭaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tatra samastaṃ kramamarcayet |
27.59 . bahuprakārabhinnasya liṅgasyārcā nirūpitā || 59



c28 atha śrītantrāloke aṣṭāviṃśamāhnikam

28.1 . iti nityavidhiḥ prokto naimittikamathocyate || 1
28.2 . niyataṃ bhāvi yannityaṃ tadityasminvidhau sthite |
28.2 . mukhyatvaṃ tanmayībhūtiḥ sarvaṃ naimittikaṃ tataḥ || 2
28.3 . dinādikalpanotthe tu naiyatye sarvanityatā |
28.3 . dinamāsarkṣavarṣādinaiyatyāducyate tadā || 3
28.4 . aśaṅkitavyāvaśyantāsattākaṃ jātucidbhavam |
28.4 . pramātraniyataṃ prāhurnaimittikamidaṃ budhāḥ || 4
28.5 . sandhyādi parvasaṃpūjā pavitrakamidaṃ sadā |
28.5 . nityaṃ niyatarūpatvātsarvasmin śāsanāśrite || 5
28.6 . jñānaśāstragurubhrātṛtadvargaprāptayastathā |
28.6 . tajjanmasaṃskriyābhedāḥ svajanmotsavasaṃgatiḥ || 6
28.7 . śrāddhaṃ vipatpratīkāraḥ pramodo'dbhutadarśanam |
28.7 . yoginīmelakaḥ svāṃśasantānādyaiśca melanam || 7
28.8 . śāstravyākhyāpurāmadhyāvasānāni kramodayaḥ |
28.8 . devatādarśanaṃ svāpnamājñā samayaniṣkṛtiḥ || 8
28.9 . iti naimittikaṃ śrīmattantrasāre nirūpitam |
28.9 . trayoviṃśatibhedena viśeṣārcānibandhanam || 9
28.10 . tatra parvavidhiṃ brūmo dvidhā parva kulākulam |
28.10 . kulāṣṭakakṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ proktaṃ śrīyogasaṃcare || 10
28.11 . abdhīndu munirityetanmāheśyā brahmasantateḥ |
28.11 . pratipatpañcadaśyau dve kaumāryā rasavahniyuk || 11
28.12 . abdhirakṣīndu vaiṣṇavyā aindryāstvastraṃ trayodaśī |
28.12 . vārāhyā randhrarudrau dve caṇḍyā vasvakṣiyugmakam || 12
28.13 . dve dve tithī tu sarvāsāṃ yogeśyā daśamī punaḥ |
28.13 . tasyā apyaṣṭamī yasmāddvitithiḥ sā prakīrtitā || 13
28.14 . anyāścākulaparvāpi vaiparītyena lakṣitam |
28.14 . kulaparveti tadbrūmo yathoktaṃ bhairave kule || 14
28.15 . haiḍare trikasadbhāve trikakālīkulādike |
28.15 . yo'yaṃ prāṇāśritaḥ pūrvaṃ kālaḥ proktaḥ suvistarāt || 15
28.16 . sa cakrabhedasaṃcāre kāṃcitsūte svasaṃvidam |
28.16 . svasaṃvitpūrṇatālābhasamayaḥ parva bhaṇyate || 16
28.17 . parva pūraṇa ityeva yadvā pṝ pūraṇārthakaḥ |
28.17 . parvaśabdo niruktaśca parva tatpūraṇāditi || 17
28.18 . haiḍare'tra ca śabdo'yaṃ dvidhā nāntetaraḥ śrutaḥ |
28.18 . taccakracāraniṣṇātā ye kecitpūrṇasaṃvidaḥ || 18
28.19 . tanmelakasamāyuktāste tatpūjāparāḥ sadā |
28.19 . yo'pyatanmaya eṣo'pi tatkāle svakramārcanāt || 19
28.20 . tadyoginīsiddhasaṅghamelakāttanmayībhavet |
28.20 . yathā prekṣaṇake tattaddraṣṭṛsaṃvidabheditām || 20
28.21 . kramoditāṃ sadya eva labhate tatpraveśanāt |
28.21 . yogābhyāsakramopāttāṃ tathā pūrṇāṃ svasaṃvidam || 21
28.22 . labhante sadya evaitatsaṃvidaikyapraveśanāt |
28.22 . tatkālaṃ cāpi saṃvitteḥ pūrṇatvātkāmadogdhṛtā || 22
28.23 . tena tattatphalaṃ tatra kāle saṃpūjayācirāt |
28.23 . yathā ciropāttadhanaḥ kurvannutsavamādarāt || 23
28.24 . atithiṃ so'nugṛhṇāti tatkālābhijñamāgatam |
28.24 . tathā suphalasaṃsiddhyai yoginīsiddhanāyakāḥ || 24
28.25 . yatnavanto'pi tatkālābhijñaṃ tamanugṛhṇate |
28.25 . uktaṃ ca tatra teneha kule sāmānyatetyalam || 25
28.26 . yasya yaddhṛdaye devi vartate daiśikājñayā |
28.26 . mantro yogaḥ kramaścaiva pūjanātsiddhido bhavet || 26
28.27 . kulācāreṇa deveśi pūjyaṃ siddhivimuktaye |
28.27 . ye parvasveṣu deveśi tarpaṇaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ || 27
28.28 . gurūṇāṃ devatānāṃ ca na kurvanti pramādataḥ |
28.28 . durācārā hi te duṣṭāḥ paśutulyā varānane || 28
28.29 . abhāvānnityapūjāyā avaśyaṃ hyeṣu pūjayet |
28.29 . aṭanaṃ jñānaśaktyādilābhārthaṃ yatprakīrtitam || 29
28.30 . śaktiyāgaśca yaḥ prokto vaśyākarṣaṇamāraṇam |
28.30 . tatsarvaṃ parvadivaseṣvayatnenaiva siddhyati || 30
28.31 . tatsāmānyaviśeṣābhyāṃ ṣoḍhā parva nirūpitam |
28.31 . māsasyādyaṃ pañcamaṃ ca śrīdinaṃ paribhāṣyate || 31
28.32 . utkṛṣṭatvātparvadinaṃ śrīpūrvatvena bhāṣyate |
28.32 . samayo hyeṣa yadguptaṃ tannānupapadaṃ vadet || 32
28.33 . turyāṣṭamānyabhuvanacaramāṇi dvayorapi |
28.33 . pakṣayoriha sāmānyasāmānyaṃ parva kīrtitam || 33
28.34 . yadeteṣu dineṣveva bhaviṣyadgrahabhātmakaḥ |
28.34 . ubhayātmā viśeṣaḥ syāttatsāmānyaviśeṣatā || 34
28.35 . sā caikādaśadhaikasminnekasminvibhunoditā |
28.35 . sajātīyā tu sotkṛṣṭetyevaṃ śambhurnyarūpayat || 35
28.36 . kṛṣṇayugaṃ vahnisitaṃ śrutikṛṣṇaṃ vahnisitamiti pakṣāḥ |
28.36 . arkendujīvacandrā budhayugmendvarkakaviguruvidhu syāt || 36
28.37 . paraphalguścaitramaghe tiṣyaḥ prākphalgukarṇaśatabhiṣajaḥ |
28.37 . mūlaprājāpatye viśākhikā śravaṇasaṃjñayā bhāni || 37
28.38 . randhre tithyarkapare vasurandhre śaśivṛṣāṅkarasarandhrayugam |
28.38 . prathamaniśāmadhyaniśe madhyāhnaśarā dinodayo madhyadinam || 38
28.39 . prathamaniśeti ca samayo mārgaśiraḥprabhṛtimāseṣu |
28.39 . kanyāntyajātha veśyā rāgavatī tattvavedinī dūtī || 39
28.40 . vyāsasamāsātkramaśaḥ pūjyāścakre'nuyāgākhye |
28.40 . sarvatra ca parvadine kuryādanuyāgacakramatiśayataḥ || 40
28.41 . guptāguptavidhānādiyāgacaryākrameṇa sampūrṇam |
28.41 . anuyāgaḥ kila mukhyaḥ sarvasminneva karmaviniyoge || 41
28.42 . anuyāgakālalābhe tasmātprayateta tatparamaḥ |
28.42 . bhagrahasamayaviśeṣo nāśvayuje ko'pi tena tadvarjam || 42
28.43 . velābhagrahakalanā kathitaikādaśasu māseṣu |
28.43 . phālgunamāse śuklaṃ yatproktaṃ dvādaśīdinaṃ parva || 43
28.44 . agratithivedhayogo mukhyatamo'sau viśeṣo'tra |
28.44 . divasaniśe kila kṛtvā tribhāgaśaḥ prathamamadhyamāparavibhāgaḥ || 44
28.45 . pūjākālastatra tribhāgite mukhyatamaḥ kālaḥ |
28.45 . yadi saṃghaṭeta velā mukhyatamā bhagrahau tathā cakram || 45
28.46 . tadyāga ādiyāgastatkāmyaṃ pūjayaiva parvasu siddhyet |
28.46 . dinavelābhagrahakalpanena tatrāpi saumyaraudratvam || 46
28.47 . jñātvā sādhakamukhyastattatkāryaṃ tadā tadā kuryāt |
28.47 . ukto yo'rcākālastaṃ cedullaṅghya bhagrahatithiḥ syāt || 47
28.48 . tamanādṛtya viśeṣaṃ pradhānayetsāmayamiti kecit |
28.48 . neti tvasmadguravo viśeṣarūpā hi tithiriha na velā || 48
28.49 . saṃvedyarūpaśaśadharabhāgaḥ saṃvedakārkakaranikaraiḥ |
28.49 . yāvānyāvati pūrṇaḥ sā hi tithirbhagrahaiḥ sphuṭībhavati || 49
28.50 . tasmānmukhyātra tithiḥ sā ca viśeṣyā graharkṣayogena |
28.50 . velātra na pradhānaṃ yuktaṃ caitattathāhi parameśaḥ || 50
28.51 . śrītrikabhairavakulaśāstreṣūce na parvadivaseṣu |
28.51 . velāyogaṃ kaṃcana tithibhagrahayogato hyanyam || 51
28.52 . tithistu pūjyā pradhānarūpatvāt |
28.52 . śvetābhāve kṛṣṇacchāgālambhaṃ hi kathayanti || 52
28.53 . yatpunarūrmiprabhṛtini śāstre veloditāpi tatkāmyam |
28.53 . mukhyatayoddiśya vidhiṃ tathāca tatra pauṣaparvadine || 53
28.54 . kṛtvārcanamardhaniśi dhyātvā japtvā bahirgatasya yathā |
28.54 . ādeśaḥ phalati tathā māghe cakrādvacaḥ phalati || 54
28.55 . acirādabhīṣṭasiddhiḥ pañcasu maitrī dhanaṃ ca melāpaḥ |
28.55 . cakrasthāne krodhātpāṣāṇasphoṭanena ripunāśaḥ || 55
28.56 . siddhādeśaprāptirmārgāntaṃ kathyate vibhunā |
28.56 . bhagrahayogābhāve velāṃ tu titheravaśyamīkṣeta || 56
28.57 . sā hi tathā sphuṭarūpā titheḥ svabhāvodayaṃ dadyāt |
28.57 . bhagrahatithivelāṃśānuyāyi sarvāṅgasundaraṃ tu dinam || 57
28.58 . yadi labhyeta tadāsminviśeṣatamapūjanaṃ racayet |
28.58 . naca kāmyameva kevalametatparivarjane yataḥ kathitaḥ || 58
28.59 . samayavilopaḥ śrīmadbhairavakula ūrmiśāstre ca |
28.59 . duṣṭā hi durācārāḥ paśutulyāḥ parva ye na viduḥ || 59
28.60 . naca kāmyasyākaraṇe syājjātu pratyavāyitvam |
28.60 . tatrānuyāgasiddhyarthaṃ cakrayāgo nirūpyate || 60
28.61 . mūrtiyāga iti prokto yaḥ śrīyogīśvarīmate |
28.61 . nityaṃ naimittikaṃ karma yadatroktaṃ maheśinā || 61
28.62 . sarvatra cakrayāgo'tra mukhyaḥ kāmye viśeṣataḥ |
28.62 . jñānī yogī ca puruṣaḥ strī vāsminmūrtisaṃjñake || 62
28.63 . yoge prayatnato yojyastaddhi pātramanuttaram |
28.63 . tatsaṃparkātpūrṇatā syāditi traiśirasādiṣu || 63
28.64 . tena sarvaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
28.64 . jñānine yogine vāpi yo dadāti karoti vā || 64
28.65 . dīkṣottare'pi ca proktamannaṃ brahmā raso hariḥ |
28.65 . bhoktā śiva iti jñānī śvapacānapyathoddhareat || 65
28.66 . sarvatattvamayo bhūtvā yadi bhuṅkte sa sādhakaḥ |
28.66 . tena bhojitamātreṇa sakṛtkoṭistu bhojitā || 66
28.67 . atha tattvavidetasminyadi bhuñjīta tatpriye |
28.67 . parisaṃkhyā na vidyeta tadāha bhagavāñchivaḥ || 67
28.68 . bhojyaṃ māyātmakaṃ sarvaṃ śivo bhoktā sa cāpyaham |

28.188 . tatra yadyannijābhīṣṭabhogamokṣopakārakam |
28.188 . pāramparyeṇa sākṣādvā bhaveccidacidātmakam || 188
28.189 . tatpūjyaṃ tadupāyāśca pūjyāstanmayatāptaye |
28.189 . tadupāyo'pi saṃpūjyo mūrtikālakriyādikaḥ || 189
28.190 . upeyasūtisāmarthyamupāyatvaṃ tadarcanāt |
28.190 . tadrūpatanmayībhāvādupeyaṃ śīghramāpnuyāt || 190
28.191 . yathā yathā ca naikaṭyamupāyeṣu tathā tathā |
28.191 . avaśyaṃbhāvi kāryatvaṃ viśeṣāccārcanādike || 191
28.192 . jñānasya kasyacitprāptirbhogamokṣopakāriṇaḥ |
28.192 . yadā tanmukhyamevoktaṃ naimittikadinaṃ budhaiḥ || 192
28.193 . tadupāyaḥ śāstramatra vaktāpyaupayiko guruḥ |
28.193 . tadvidyo'pi gurubhrātā saṃvādājjñānadāyakaḥ || 193
28.194 . guroḥ patnī tathā bhrātā putra ityādiko gaṇaḥ |
28.194 . na yonisaṃbandhavaśādvidyāsaṃbandhajastu saḥ || 194
28.195 . vīryāruṇaparīṇāmadehāhantāpratiṣṭhitāḥ |
28.195 . dehopakārasantānā jñāteye pariniṣṭhitāḥ || 195
28.196 . tathāca smṛtiśāstreṣu santaterdāyahāritā |
28.196 . yuktaiva tāvānsa hyukto bhedāddūrāntikatvataḥ || 196
28.197 . ye tu tyaktaśarīrāsthā bodhāhambhāvabhāginaḥ |
28.197 . bodhopakārasantānadvayātte bandhutājuṣaḥ || 197
28.198 . tatretthaṃ prāgyadā paśyecchaktyunmīlitadṛkkriyaḥ |
28.198 . dehastāvadayaṃ pūrvapūrvopādānanirmitaḥ || 198
28.199 . ātmā vikārarahitaḥ śāśvatatvādahetukaḥ |
28.199 . svātantryātpunarātmīyādayaṃ channa iva sthitaḥ || 199
28.200 . punaśca prakaṭībhūya bhairavībhāvabhājanam |
28.200 . tatrāsya prakaṭībhāve bhuktimuktyātmake bhṛśam || 200
28.201 . ya upāyaḥ samucito jñānasantāna eṣa saḥ |
28.201 . kramasphuṭībhavattādṛksadṛśajñānadhārayā || 201
28.202 . galadvijātīyatayā prāpyaṃ śīghraṃ hi labhyate |
28.202 . evaṃ cānādisaṃsārocitavijñānasantateḥ || 202
28.203 . dhvaṃse lokottaraṃ jñānaṃ santānāntaratāṃ śrayet |
28.203 . asaṃsārocitodāratathāvijñānasantateḥ || 203
28.204 . kāraṇaṃ mukhyamādyaṃ tadguruvijñānamātmagam |
28.204 . atyantaṃ svaviśeṣāṇāṃ tatrārpaṇavaśātsphuṭam || 204
28.205 . upādānaṃ hi tadyuktaṃ dehabhede hi satyapi |
28.205 . dehasantatigau bhedābhedau vijñānasantateḥ || 205
28.206 . na tathātvāya yogīcchāviṣṭaśāvaśarīravat |
28.206 . yoginaḥ paradehādijīvattāpādane nijam || 206
28.207 . dehamatyajato nānājñānopādānatā na kim |
28.207 . tena vijñānasantānaprādhānyādyaunasantateḥ || 207
28.208 . anyonyaṃ gurusantāno yaḥ śivajñānaniṣṭhitaḥ |
28.208 . itthaṃ sthite trayaṃ mukhyaṃ kāraṇaṃ sahakāri ca || 208
28.209 . ekakāraṇakāryaṃ ca vastvityeṣa gurorgaṇaḥ |
28.209 . guruḥ kāraṇamatroktaṃ tatpatnī sahakāriṇī || 209
28.210 . yato niḥśaktikasyāsya na yāge'dhikṛtirbhavet |
28.210 . antaḥsthodārasaṃvittiśakterbāhyāṃ vināpi tām || 210
28.211 . sāmarthyaṃ yogino yadvadvināpi sahakāriṇam |
28.211 . ekajanyā bhrātaraḥ syustatsadṛgyastu ko'pi saḥ || 211
28.212 . punaḥ paramparāyogādguruvargo'pi bhaṇyate |
28.212 . mukhya eṣa tu santānaḥ pūjyo mānyaśca sarvadā || 212
28.213 . gurvādīnāṃ ca sambhūtau dīkṣāyāṃ prāyaṇe'pi ca |
28.213 . yadahastaddhi vijñānopāyadehādikāraṇam || 213
28.214 . evaṃ svajanmadivaso vijñānopāya ucyate |
28.214 . tādṛgbhogāpavargādihetordehasya kāraṇam || 214
28.215 . dīkṣādikaśca saṃskāraḥ svātmano yatra cāhni tat |
28.215 . bhavejjanmadinaṃ mukhyaṃ jñānasantānajanmataḥ || 215
28.216 . svakaṃ mṛtidinaṃ yattu tadanyeṣāṃ bhaviṣyati |
28.216 . naimittikaṃ mṛto yasmācchivābhinnastadā bhavet || 216
28.217 . tatra prasaṅgānmaraṇasvarūpaṃ brūmahe sphuṭam |
28.217 . vyāpako'pi śivaḥ svecchākḷptasaṅkocamudraṇāt || 217
28.218 . vicitraphalakarmaughavaśāttattaccharīrabhāk |
28.218 . śarīrabhāktvaṃ caitāvadyattadgarbhasthadehagaḥ || 218
28.219 . saṃvitteḥ śūnyarūḍhāyāḥ prathamaḥ prāṇanodayaḥ |
28.219 . garbhasthadehanirmāṇe tasyaiveśvaratā punaḥ || 219
28.220 . asaṅkocasya tanvādikartā teneśa ucyate |
28.220 . sa vāyvātmā dṛḍhe tasmindehayantre cidātmanā || 220
28.221 . preryamāṇo vicarati bhastrāyantragavāyuvat |
28.221 . ataḥ prāggāḍhasaṃsuptotthitavatsa prabuddhyate || 221
28.222 . kramāddehena sākaṃ ca prāṇanā syādbalīyasī |
28.222 . tatrāpi karmaniyatibalātsā prāṇanākṣatām || 222
28.223 . gṛhṇāti śūnyasuṣirasaṃvitsparśādhikatvataḥ |
28.223 . evaṃ krameṇa saṃpuṣṭadehaprāṇabalo bhṛśam || 223
28.224 . bhogānkarmakṛtānbhuṅkte yonyayonijadehagaḥ |
28.224 . uktaṃ ca gahvarābhikhye śāstre śītāṃśumaulinā || 224
28.225 . yathā gṛhaṃ viniṣpādya gṛhī samadhitiṣṭhati |
28.225 . tathā dehī tanuṃ kṛtvā kriyādiguṇavarjitaḥ || 225
28.226 . kiñcitsphuraṇamātraḥ prāgniṣkalaḥ so'pi śabdyate |
28.226 . sphuṭendriyāditattvastu sakalātmeti bhaṇyate || 226
28.227 . ityādi śrīgahvaroktaṃ tata eva paṭhedbahu |
28.227 . kṣaye tu karmaṇāṃ teṣāṃ dehayantre'nyathāgate || 227
28.228 . prāṇayantraṃ vighaṭate dehaḥ syātkuḍyavattataḥ |
28.228 . nāḍīcakreṣu saṅkocavikāsau viparītataḥ || 228
28.229 . bhaṅgaḥ śoṣaḥ klidirvātaśleṣmāgnyapacayoccayaiḥ |
28.229 . ityevamādi yatkiñcitprāksaṃsthānopamardakam || 229
28.230 . dehayantre vighaṭanaṃ tadevoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
28.230 . tasminvighaṭite yantre sā saṃvitprāṇanātmatām || 230
28.231 . gṛhṇāti yonije'nyatra vā dehe karmacitrite |
28.231 . sa dehaḥ pratibudhyeta prasuptotthitavattadā || 231
28.232 . tasyāpi bhogataddhānimṛtayaḥ prāgvadeva hi |
28.232 . visṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārā ete karmabalādyataḥ || 232
28.233 . ato niyatikālādivaicitryānuvidhāyinaḥ |
28.233 . anugrahastu yaḥ so'yaṃ svasvarūpe vikasvare || 233
28.234 . jñaptyātmeti kathaṃ karmaniyatyādi pratīkṣate |
28.234 . karmakālaniyatyādi yataḥ saṅkocajīvitam || 234
28.235 . saṅkocahānirūpe'sminkathaṃ heturanugrahe |
28.235 . anugrahaśca kramikastīvraśceti vibhidyate || 235
28.236 . prākcaiṣa vistarātprokta iti kiṃ punaruktibhiḥ |
28.236 . tena dīkṣāśivajñānadagdhasaṅkocabandhanaḥ || 236
28.237 . dehānte śiva eveti nāsya dehāntarasthitiḥ |
28.237 . ye'pi tattvāvatīrṇānāṃ śaṃkarājñānuvartinām || 237
28.238 . svayambhūmunidevarṣimanujādibhuvāṃ gṛhe |
28.238 . mṛtāste tatpuraṃ prāpya pureśairdīkṣitāḥ kramāt || 238
28.239 . martye'vatīrya vā no vā śivaṃ yāntyapunarbhavāḥ |
28.239 . tatra svayambhuvo dvedhā ke'pyanugrahatatparāḥ || 239
28.240 . ke'pi svakṛtyāyātāṃśasthānamātropasevinaḥ |
28.240 . ye'nugrahārthamājñaptāsteṣu yo mriyate naraḥ || 240
28.241 . so'nugrahaṃ sphuṭaṃ yāti vinā martyāvatārataḥ |
28.241 . yastu svakāryaṃ kurvāṇastatsthānaṃ nāṃśatastyajet || 241
28.242 . yathā gaurī tapasyantī kaśmīreṣu guhāgatā |
28.242 . tatraiva vā yathā dhyānoḍḍāre naraharirvibhuḥ || 242
28.243 . vitastāṃ nayato daityāṃstrāsayandṛpta utthitaḥ |
28.243 . sāligrāme yathā viṣṇuḥ śivo vā svopabhoginaḥ || 243
28.244 . tapasyantau badaryāṃ ca naranārāyaṇau tathā |
28.244 . ityevamādayo devāḥ svakṛtyāṃśasthitāstathā || 244
28.245 . ārādhitāḥ svocitaṃ tacchīghraṃ vidadhate phalam |
28.245 . svakṛtyāṃśasthitānāṃ ca dhāmni ye'ntaṃ vrajanti te || 245
28.246 . tatra bhogāṃstathā bhuktvā martyeṣvavatarantyapi |
28.246 . martyāvatīrṇāste tattadaṃśakāstanmayāḥ punaḥ || 246
28.247 . taddīkṣājñānacaryādikramādyānti śivātmatām |
28.247 . sthāvarādyāstiryagantāḥ paśavo'smindvaye mṛtāḥ || 247
28.248 . svakarmasaṃskriyāvedhāttalloke citratājuṣaḥ |
28.248 . puṃsāṃ ca paśumātrāṇāṃ sālokyamavivekataḥ || 248
28.249 . avivekastadviśeṣānunmeṣānmauḍhyatastathā |
28.249 . sthāvarādyāstathābhāvamuttarottaratāṃ ca vā || 249
28.250 . prapadyante na te sākṣādrudratāṃ tāṃ kramātpunaḥ |
28.250 . haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇe nānātarukulākule || 250
28.251 . ityetadāgameṣūktaṃ tata eva pure pure |
28.251 . kṣetramānaṃ bruve śrīmatsarvajñānādiṣūditam || 251
28.252 . liṅgāddhastaśataṃ kṣetramācāryasthāpite sati |
28.252 . svayambhūte sahasraṃ tu tadardhamṛṣiyojite || 252
28.253 . tattvavitsthāpite liṅge svayambhūsadṛśaṃ phalam |
28.253 . atattvavidyadācāryo liṅgaṃ sthāpayate tadā || 253
28.254 . punarvidhirbhaveddoṣo hyanyathobhayadūṣakaḥ |
28.254 . ahamanyaḥ parātmānyaḥ śivo'nya iti cenmatiḥ || 254
28.255 . na mocayenna muktaśca sarvamātmamayaṃ yataḥ |
28.255 . tasmāttattvavidā yadyatsthāpitaṃ liṅgamuttamam || 255
28.256 . tadevāyatanatvena saṃśrayedbhuktimuktaye |
28.256 . uktaṃ śrīratnamālāyāṃ jñātvā kālamupasthitam || 256
28.257 . mokṣārthī na bhayaṃ gacchettyajeddehamaśaṅkitaḥ |
28.257 . tīrthāyatanapuṇyeṣu kālaṃ vā vañcayetpriye || 257
28.258 . ayogināmayaṃ panthā yogī yogena vañcayet |
28.258 . vañcane tvasamarthaḥ san kṣetramāyatanaṃ vrajet || 258
28.259 . tīrthe samāśrayāttasya vañcanaṃ tu vijāyate |
28.259 . anena ca dharādyeṣu tattveṣvabhyāsayogataḥ || 259
28.260 . tāvatsiddhijuṣo'pyuktā muktyai kṣetropayogitā |
28.260 . samyagjñānini vṛttāntaḥ purastāttūpadekṣyate || 260
28.261 . paśūnāmeṣa vṛttānto ye tu tattattvadīkṣitāḥ |
28.261 . te tadīśasamīpatvaṃ yānti svaucityayogataḥ || 261
28.262 . yogyatāvaśasaṃjātā yasya yatraiva vāsanā |
28.262 . sa tatraiva niyoktavyaḥ pureśāccordhvaśuddhibhāk || 262
28.263 . iti śrīpūrvakathitaṃ śrīmatsvāyambhuve'pica |
28.263 . yo yatrābhilaṣedbhogānsa tatraiva niyojitaḥ || 263
28.264 . siddhibhāṅmantrasāmarthyādityādyanyatra varṇitam |
28.264 . ye tu tattattvavijñānamantracaryādivartinaḥ || 264
28.265 . mṛtāste tatra tadrudrasayuktvaṃ yānti kovidāḥ |
28.265 . teṣāṃ sayuktvaṃ yātānāmapi saṃskārato nijāt || 265
28.266 . tathā tathā vicitraḥ syādavatārastadaṃśataḥ |
28.266 . siddhāntādau purāṇeṣu tathāca śrūyate bahu || 266
28.267 . tulye rudrāvatāratve citratvaṃ karmabhogayoḥ |
28.267 . anekaśaktikhacitaṃ yato bhāvasya yadvapuḥ || 267
28.268 . śaktibhyo'rthāntaraṃ naiṣa tatsamūhādṛte bhavet |
28.268 . tena śaktisamūhākhyāttasmādrudrādyadaṃśataḥ || 268
28.269 . kṛtyaṃ taducitaṃ siddhyetsoṃ'śo'vatarati sphuṭam |
28.269 . ye cādharaprāptadīkṣāstadāsthānujjhitāḥ pare || 269
28.270 . tattve mṛtāḥ kāṣṭhavatte'dhare'pyutkarṣabhāginaḥ |
28.270 . ye tūjjhitatadutkarṣāste taduttarabhāginaḥ || 270
28.271 . ye'pyūrdhvatattvadīkṣāste vinā tāvadvivekataḥ |
28.271 . prāptādharāntā api taddīkṣāphalasubhāginaḥ || 271
28.272 . atyaktāsthā hi te tatra dīkṣāyāmapi śāstritāt |
28.272 . vinā vivekādāsthāṃ te śritā lokaprasiddhitaḥ || 272
28.273 . paśumātrasya sālokyaṃ sāmīpyaṃ dīkṣitasya tu |
28.273 . tatparasya tu sāyujyamityuktaṃ parameśinā || 273
28.274 . yastūrdhvaśāstragastatra tyaktāsthaḥ saṃśayena saḥ |
28.274 . vrajannāyatanaṃ naiva phalaṃ kiñcitsamaśnute || 274
28.275 . uktaṃ tadviṣayaṃ caitaddevadevena yadvṛthā |
28.275 . dīkṣā jñānaṃ tathā tīrthaṃ tasyetyādi savistaram || 275
28.276 . yastu tāvadayogyo'pi tathāste sa śivālaye |
28.276 . paścādāsthānibandhena tāvadeva phalaṃ bhajet || 276
28.277 . nadīnagahradaprāyaṃ yacca puṇyaṃ na tanmṛtau |
28.277 . utkṛṣṭaṃ tanmṛtānāṃ tu svargabhogopabhogitā || 277
28.278 . ye punaḥ prāptavijñānavivekā maraṇāntike |
28.278 . adharāyataneṣvāsthāṃ śritāste'tra tirohitāḥ || 278
28.279 . tajjñānadūṣaṇoktaṃ yatteṣāṃ syātkila pātakam |
28.279 . tattatpureśadīkṣādikramānnaśyediti sthitiḥ || 279
28.280 . dīkṣāyatanavijñānadūṣiṇo ye tu cetasā |
28.280 . ācaranti ca tatte'tra sarve nirayagāminaḥ || 280
28.281 . jñānāyatanadīkṣādāvāsthābandhaparicyutiḥ |
28.281 . vyāpāravyāhṛtairjñeyā tānyapi dvividhāni ca || 281
28.282 . yāni jātucidapyeva svāsthye nodamiṣanpunaḥ |
28.282 . asvāsthye dhātudoṣotthānyeva tadbhogamātrakam || 282
28.283 . dhātudoṣācca saṃsārasaṃskārāste prabodhitāḥ |
28.283 . chidragā api bhūyiṣṭhajñānadagdhā na rohiṇaḥ || 283
28.284 . ye tu kaivalyabhāgīyāḥ svāsthye'nunmiṣitāḥ sadā |
28.284 . asvāsthye conmiṣantyete saṃskārāḥ śaktipātataḥ || 284
28.285 . yataḥ sāṃsārikāḥ pūrvagāḍhābhyāsopasaṃskṛtāḥ |
28.285 . ityūce bhujagādhīśastacchidreṣviti sūtrataḥ || 285
28.286 . ye tu kaivalyabhāgīyāḥ pratyayāste na jātucit |
28.286 . abhyastāḥ saṃsṛterbhāvāttenaite śaktipātataḥ || 286
28.287 . vyāpāravyāhṛtaistena dhātudoṣaprakopitaiḥ |
28.287 . aprāptaniścayāmarśaiḥ suptamattopamānakaiḥ || 287
28.288 . viparītairapi jñānadīkṣāgurvādidūṣakaiḥ |
28.288 . tirobhāvo na vijñeyo hṛdaye rūḍhyabhāvataḥ || 288
28.289 . ata eva prabuddho'pi karmotthānbhogarūpiṇaḥ |
28.289 . yamakiṅkarasarpādipratyayāndehago bhajet || 289
28.290 . naitāvatā na mukto'sau mṛtirbhogo hi janmavat |
28.290 . sthitivacca tato duḥkhasukhābhyāṃ maraṇaṃ dvidhā || 290
28.291 . ato yathā prabuddhasya sukhaduḥkhavicitratāḥ |
28.291 . sthitau na ghnanti muktatvaṃ maraṇe'pi tathaiva tāḥ || 291
28.292 . ye punaryoginaste'pi yasmiṃstattve subhāvitāḥ |
28.292 . cittaṃ niveśayantyeva tattattvaṃ yāntyaśaṅkitāḥ || 292
28.293 . śrīsvacchande tataḥ proktaṃ gandhadhāraṇayā mṛtāḥ |
28.293 . ityādi mālinīśāstre dhāraṇānāṃ tathā phalam || 293
28.294 . eteṣāṃ maraṇābhikhyo bhogo nāsti tu ye tanum |
28.294 . dhāraṇābhistyajantyāśu paradehapraveśavat || 294
28.295 . etāvānmṛtibhogo hi marmacchinmūḍhatākṣagā |
28.295 . dhvāntābilatvaṃ manasi taccaiteṣu na vidyate || 295
28.296 . tathāhi mānasaṃ yatnaṃ tāvatsamadhitiṣṭhati |
28.296 . ahaṃrūḍhyā pare dehe yāvatsyādbuddhisaṃcaraḥ || 296
28.297 . prāṇacakraṃ tadāyattamapi saṃcarate pathā |
28.297 . tenaivātaḥ prabuddhyeta paradehe'kṣacakrakam || 297
28.298 . makṣikā makṣikārājaṃ yathotthitamanūtthitāḥ |
28.298 . sthitaṃ cānuviśantyevaṃ cittaṃ sarvākṣavṛttayaḥ || 298
28.299 . ato'sya paradehādisaṃcāre nāsti melanam |
28.299 . akṣāṇāṃ madhyagaṃ sūkṣmaṃ syādetaddehavatpunaḥ || 299
28.300 . evaṃ paraśarīrādicāriṇāmiva yoginām |
28.300 . tattattattvaśarīrāntaścāriṇāṃ nāsti mūḍhatā || 300
28.301 . te cāpi dvividhā jñeyā laukikā dīkṣitāstathā |
28.301 . pūrve śivāḥ syuḥ kramaśaḥ pare tadbhogamātrataḥ || 301
28.302 . dīkṣāpyūrdhvādharānekabhedayojanikāvaśāt |
28.302 . bhidyamānā yogināṃ syādvicitraphaladāyinī || 302
28.303 . ye tu vijñāninaste'tra dvedhā kampretaratvataḥ |
28.303 . tatra ye kampravijñānāste dehāntedśivāḥ sphuṭam || 303
28.304 . yato vijñānameteṣāmutpannaṃ naca susphuṭam |
28.304 . vikalpāntarayogena nacāpyunmūlitātmakam || 304
28.305 . ato dehe pramādottho vikalpo dehapātataḥ |
28.305 . naśyedavaśyaṃ taccāpi budhyate jñānamuttamam || 305
28.306 . saṃskārakalpanātiṣṭhadadhvastīkṛtamantarā |
28.306 . prāptapākaṃ saṃvarīturapāye bhāsate hi tat || 306
28.307 . ye tu svabhyastavijñānamayāḥ śivamayāḥ sadā |
28.307 . jīvanmuktā hi te naiṣāṃ mṛtau kāpi vicāraṇā || 307
28.308 . yathāhi jīvanmuktānāṃ sthitau nāsti vicāraṇā |
28.308 . sukhiduḥkhivimūḍhatve, mṛtāvapi tathā na sā || 308
28.309 . śrīratnamālāśāstre taduvāca parameśvaraḥ |
28.309 . svaśāstre cāpyahīśāno viśvādhāradhurandharaḥ || 309
28.310 . rathyāntare mūtrapurīṣamadhye caṇḍālagehe niraye śmaśāne |

28.311 . sacintācintakatvoktiretāvatsaṃbhavasthitim || 311

28.313 . anantakārikā caiṣā prāhedaṃ bandhakaṃ kila |

28.314 . apiśabdādaluptasmṛtyā vā saṃbhāvyate kila |

28.316 . bhedapradatvenaiṣāpi dhvastā tena viśokatā || 316

28.320 . tānyenaṃ na vidurbhinnaṃ taiḥ sa mukto'bhidhīyate |

28.322 . tasminbodhāntare līnaḥ karmakartāpyanañjanaḥ |

28.324 . tasya bhāvo nacābhāvaḥ saṃsthānaṃ naca kalpanā |
28.324 . etadevāntarāgūrya gururgītāsvabhāṣata || 324
28.325 . yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaranbhāvaṃ tyajatyante kalevaram |

28.326 . tasmātsarveṣu kāleṣu māmanusmara yudhya ca |

28.327 . kramādrajastamolīnaḥ karmayonivimūḍhagaḥ |
28.327 . tatrendriyāṇāṃ saṃmohaśvāsāyāsaparītatā || 327
28.328 . ityādimṛtibhogo'yaṃ dehe na tyajanaṃ tanoḥ |
28.328 . yastvasau kṣaṇa evaikaścaramaḥ prāṇanātmakaḥ || 328
28.329 . yadanantaramevaiṣa dehaḥ syātkāṣṭhakuḍyavat |

28.330 . tata eva hi taddehasukhaduḥkhādikojjhitā |

28.331 . adṛṣṭābhyāsabhūyastvaśaktipātādihetukāt |

28.333 . prākprasphuredyadadhikaṃ deho'sau cidadhiṣṭhiteḥ |

28.334 . saivātra līnatā proktā sattve rajasi tāmase |
28.334 . nīlapītādike jñeye yataḥ prākkalpitāṃ tanum || 334
28.335 . adhiṣṭhāyaiva saṃvittiradhiṣṭhānaṃ karotyalam |
28.335 . ato'dhiṣṭheyamātrasya śarīratve'pi kuḍyataḥ || 335
28.336 . dehasyāsti viśeṣo yatsarvādhiṣṭheyapūrvatā |

28.337 . vedyānāṃ kintu dehasya nityāvyabhicaritvataḥ |

28.338 . smṛtyā prācyānubhavanakṛtasaṃskāracitrayā |
28.338 . yuktyānayāsmatsantānaguruṇā kallaṭena yat || 338
28.339 . dehāviśeṣe prāṇākhyadārḍhyaṃ heturudīritam |
28.339 . tadyuktamanyathā prāṇadārḍhye ko heturekataḥ || 339
28.340 . dehatvasyāviśeṣe'pītyeṣa praśno na śāmyati |
28.340 . smaranniti śatā hetau tadrūpaṃ pratipadyate || 340
28.341 . prāksmaryate yato dehaḥ prākcitādhiṣṭhitaḥ sphuran |
28.341 . ataḥ smaraṇamantyaṃ yattadasarvajñamātṛṣu || 341
28.342 . na jātu gocaro yasmāddehāntaraviniścayaḥ |
28.342 . yattu bandhupriyāputrapānādismaraṇaṃ sphuṭam || 342
28.343 . na taddehāntarāsaṅgi na tadantyaṃ yato bhavet |
28.343 . kasyāpi tu śarīrānte vāsanā yā prabhotsyate || 343
28.344 . dehasattve tadaucityājjāyetānubhavaḥ sphuṭaḥ |
28.344 . yathā purāṇe kathitaṃ mṛgapotakatṛṣṇayā || 344
28.345 . muniḥ ko'pi mṛgībhāvamabhyuvāhādhivāsitaḥ |
28.345 . tatra so'nubhavo heturna janmāntarasūtaye || 345
28.346 . tasyaitadvāsanā hetuḥ kākatālīyavatsa tu |
28.346 . nanu kasmāttadevaiṣa smarati ityāha yatsadā || 346
28.347 . tadbhāvabhāvitastena tadevaiṣa smaratyalam |
28.347 . evamasmi bhaviṣyāmītyeṣa tadbhāva ucyate || 347
28.348 . bhaviṣyato hi bhavanaṃ bhāvyate na sataḥ kvacit |
28.348 . kramātsphuṭatvakaraṇaṃ bhāvanaṃ parikīrtyate || 348
28.349 . sphuṭasya cānubhavanaṃ na bhāvanamidaṃ sphuṭam |
28.349 . tadaharjātabālasya paśoḥ kīṭasya vā taroḥ || 349
28.350 . mūḍhatve'pi tadānīṃ prāgbhāvanā hyabhavatsphuṭā |
28.350 . sā tanmūḍhaśarīrānte saṃskārapratibodhanāt || 350
28.351 . smṛtidvāreṇa taddehavaicitryaphaladāyinī |
28.351 . deśādivyavadhāne'pi vāsanānāmudīritāt || 351
28.352 . ānantaryaikarūpatvātsmṛtisaṃskārayorataḥ |
28.352 . tathānubhavanārūḍhyā sphuṭasyāpi tu bhāvitā || 352
28.353 . bhāvyamānā na kiṃ sūte tatsantānasadṛgvapuḥ |
28.353 . tattādṛktādṛśairbandhuputramitrādibhiḥ saha || 353
28.354 . bhāsate'pi pare loke svapnavadvāsanākramāt |

28.356 . svapne nāsti sa ityeṣā vākpramāṇavivarjitā |
28.356 . ya evaite tu dṛśyante jāgratyete mayekṣitāḥ || 356
28.357 . svapna ityastu mithyaitattatpramātṛvacobalāt |

28.358 . na śocanti na cekṣante māmityatrāsti kā pramā |

28.359 . pramātrantarasadbhāvaḥ saṃvinniṣṭho na tadgataḥ |

28.361 . dehānte budhyate no cetsyādanyādṛkprabodhanam |

28.362 . mucyate janturityuktaṃ prāksaṃskārabalatvataḥ |
28.362 . nipātābhyāmantaśabdātsmaraṇācchaturantyataḥ || 362
28.363 . pādācca nikhilādardhaślokācca samanantarāt |

28.364 . ajñātvaitattu sarve'pi kuśakāśāvalambinaḥ |
28.364 . yattadorvyatyayaṃ kecitkecidanyādṛśaṃ kramam || 364
28.365 . bhinnakramau nipātau ca tyajatīti ca saptamīm |

28.366 . naca taddarśitaṃ mithyā svāntasammohadāyakam |

28.367 . adhigamyoditaṃ tena mṛtyorbhītirvinaśyati |
28.367 . viditamṛtisatattvāḥ saṃvidambhonidhānādacalahṛdayavīryākarṣaniṣpīḍanottham |
28.367 . amṛtamiti nigīrṇe kālakūṭe'tra devā yadi pivatha tadānīṃ niścitaṃ vaḥ śivatvam || 367
28.368 . utsavo'pi hi yaḥ kaścillaukikaḥ so'pi saṃmadam |
28.368 . saṃvidabdhitaraṅgābhaṃ sūte tadapi parvavat || 368
28.369 . etena ca vipaddhvaṃsapramodādiṣu parvatā |

28.370 . purakṣobhādyadbhutaṃ yattatsvātantrye svasaṃvidaḥ |

28.371 . yoginīmelako dvedhā haṭhataḥ priyatastathā |

28.373 . tena tatparva tadvacca svasantānādimelanam |

28.374 . melake'nyonyasaṅghaṭṭapratibimbādvikasvarā |

28.375 . bahudarpaṇavaddīptaḥ sarvāyetāpyayatnataḥ |

28.377 . nṛttādau viṣaye prāptā pūrṇānandatvamaśnute |

28.381 . ekāṃ saṃvidamāviśya cakre tāvanti pūjayet |

28.382 . prastutaṃ svasamācāraṃ tena sākaṃ samācaret |

28.384 . praveśaṃ saṃpaviṣṭasya na vicāraṃ tu kārayet |

28.385 . akṛtvā taṃ samācāraṃ punaścakraṃ prapūjayet |

28.386 . devyāyāmalaśāstrādau tuhinābhīśumaulinā |

28.387 . sarvaśāstrārthavicceti gururbhinno'padiśyate |

28.388 . nānyathā tadabhāvaścetsarvathā so'pyathācaret |
28.388 . śrībhairavakule coktaṃ kalpādijñatvamīdṛśam || 388
28.389 . gurorlakṣaṇametāvatsaṃpūrṇajñānataiva yā |

28.390 . devyāyāmala uktaṃ taddvāpañcāśāhva āhnike |

28.391 . ucchuṣmaśavaracaṇḍagumataṅgaghorāntakograhalahalakāḥ |

28.392 . te svāṃśacittavṛttikrameṇa pauruṣaśarīramāsthāya |

28.393 . sarve'limāṃsanidhuvanadīkṣārcanaśāstrasevane niratāḥ |

28.394 . itthaṃ vijñāya sadā śiṣyaḥ sampūrṇaśāstraboddhāram |

28.397 . liptāyāṃ bhuvi pīṭhe caturasre paṅkajatrayaṃ kajage |

28.398 . madhye vāgīśānīṃ dakṣottarayorgurūngaṇeśaṃ ca |

28.399 . sāmānyavidhiniyuktārghapātrayogena cakramatha samyak |
28.399 . santarpya vyākhyānaṃ kuryātsambandhapūrvakaṃ matimān || 399
28.400 . sūtrapadavākyapaṭalagranthakramayojanena sambandhāt |
28.400 . avyāhatapūrvāparamupavṛhya nayeta vākyāni || 400
28.401 . maṇḍūkaplavasiṃhāvalokanādyairyathāyathaṃ nyāyaiḥ |

28.402 . tantrāvartanabādhaprasaṅgatarkādibhiśca sannyāyaiḥ |
28.402 . vastu vadedvākyajño vastvantarato viviktatāṃ vidadhat || 402
28.403 . yadyadvyāhṛtipadavīmāyāti tadeva dṛḍhatarairnyāyaiḥ |

28.404 . dṛḍharacitapūrvapakṣaproddharaṇapathena vastu yadvācyam |

28.405 . bhāṣā nyāyo vādo layaḥ kramo yadyadeti śiṣyasya |

28.406 . vācyaṃ vastu samāpya pratarpaṇaṃ pūjanaṃ bhaveccakre |

28.407 . vyākhyānte kṣamayitvā visṛjya sarvaṃ kṣipedagādhajale |

28.408 . viśeṣapūjanaṃ kuryātsamayebhyaśca niṣkṛtau |
28.408 . avikalpamaterna syuḥ prāyaścittāni yadyapi || 408
28.409 . tathāpyatattvavidvargānugrahāya tathā caret |

28.410 . prāyaścittavidhiḥ prokta iti devyā pracodite |
28.410 . satyaṃ smaraṇameveha sakṛjjaptaṃ vimocayet || 410
28.411 . sarvasmātkarmaṇo jālātsmṛtitattvakalāvidaḥ |
28.411 . tathāpi sthitirakṣārthaṃ kartavyaścodito vidhiḥ || 411
28.412 . atattvavedino ye hi caryāmātraikaniṣṭhitāḥ |

28.413 . tasmādvikalparahitaḥ saṃvṛtyuparato yadi |
28.413 . śāstracaryāsadāyattaiḥ saṅkaraṃ tadvivarjayet || 413
28.414 . saṅkaraṃ vā samanvicchetprāyaścittaṃ samācaret |
28.414 . yathā teṣāṃ na śāstrārthe dolārūḍhā matirbhavet || 414
28.415 . yatsvayaṃ śivahastākhye vidhau saṃcoditaṃ purā |

28.416 . śaktināśānmahādoṣo narakaṃ śāśvataṃ priye |

28.417 . kulajānāṃ samākhyātā niṣkṛtirduṣṭakartarī |
28.417 . śrīpūrve samayānāṃ tu śodhanāyoditaṃ yathā || 417
28.418 . mālinī mātṛkā vāpi japyā lakṣatrayāntakam |
28.418 . pratiṣṭhitasya pūrāderdarśane'nadhikāriṇā || 418
28.419 . prāyaścittaṃ prakartavyamiti śrībrahmayāmale |

28.420 . devadravyahṛdākāraprahartā liṅgabhedakaḥ |

28.421 . śaktivyaṅgatvakṛdyogijñānihantā vilopakaḥ |
28.421 . naimittikānāṃ lakṣādikramāddvidviguṇaṃ japet || 421
28.422 . vratena kenacidyukto mitabhugbrahmacaryavān |

28.423 . lakṣajāpaṃ tataḥ kuryādityuktaṃ brahmayāmale |

28.424 . aparedyuḥ sadā kāryaṃ siddhayogīśvarīmate |

28.425 . sa guruḥ sarvadā grāhyastyaktvānyaṃ tatsthitaṃ tvapi |

28.426 . kṛtvārcayeta tatrasthamadhvānaṃ sakalāntakam |
28.426 . tato vijñapayedbhaktyā tadadhiṣṭhitaye gurum || 426
28.427 . sa tatra pūjyaḥ svairmantraiḥ puṣpadhūpārghavistaraiḥ |
28.427 . samālambhanasadvastrairnaivedyaistarpaṇaiḥ kramāt || 427
28.428 . āśāntaṃ pūjayitvainaṃ dakṣiṇābhiryajecchiśuḥ |

28.429 . atoṣayitvā tu guruṃ dakṣiṇābhiḥ samantataḥ |

c29 atha śrītantrāloke ekonatriṃśamāhnikam

29.1 . atha samucitādhikāriṇa uddiśya rahasya ucyate'tra vidhiḥ |
