29.230 . viditau bhavatastatra gururmokṣaprado bhavet || 230
29.231 . tātparyamasya pādasya sa siddhīḥ saṃprayacchati |
29.231 . gururyaḥ sādhakaḥ prāksyādanyo mokṣaṃ dadātyalam || 231
29.232 . anayoḥ kathayejjñānaṃ trividhaṃ sarvamapyalam |
29.232 . svakīyājñāṃ ca vitaretsvakriyākaraṇaṃ prati || 232
29.233 . ṣaṭkaṃ kāraṇasaṃjñaṃ yattathā yaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ |
29.233 . sākaṃ bhairavanāthena tadaṣṭakamudāhṛtam || 233
29.234 . pratyekaṃ tasya sārvātmyaṃ paśyaṃstāṃ vṛttimātmagām |
29.234 . cakṣurādau saṃkramayedyatra yatrendriye guruḥ || 234
29.235 . sa eva pūrṇaiḥ kalaśairabhiṣekaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ |
29.235 . vinā bāhyairapītyuktaṃ śrīvīrāvalibhairave || 235
29.236 . sadya eva tu bhogepsoryogātsiddhatamo guruḥ |
29.236 . kuryātsadyastathābhīṣṭaphaladaṃ vedhadīkṣaṇam || 236
29.237 . vedhadīkṣā ca bahudhā tatra tatra nirūpitā |
29.237 . sā cābhyāsavatā kāryā yenordhvordhvapraveśataḥ || 237
29.238 . śiṣyasya cakrasaṃbhedapratyayo jāyate dhruvaḥ |
29.238 . yenāṇimādikā siddhiḥ śrīmālāyāṃ ca coditā || 238
29.239 . ūrdhvacakradaśālābhe piśācāveśa eva sā |
29.239 . mantranādabinduśaktibhujaṅgamaparātmikā || 239
29.240 . ṣoḍhā śrīgahvare vedhadīkṣoktā parameśinā |
29.240 . jvālākulaṃ svaśāstroktaṃ cakramaṣṭārakādikam || 240
29.241 . dhyātvā tenāsya hṛccakravedhanānmantravedhanam |
29.241 . ākāraṃ navadhā dehe nyasya saṃkramayettataḥ || 241
29.242 . nyāsayogena śiṣyāya dīpyamānaṃ mahārciṣam |
29.242 . pāśastobhāttatastasya paratattve tu yojanam || 242
29.243 . iti dīkṣottare dṛṣṭo vidhirme śaṃbhunoditaḥ |
29.243 . nādoccāreṇa nādākhyaḥ sṛṣṭikramaniyogataḥ || 243
29.244 . nādena vedhayeccittaṃ nādavedha udīritaḥ |
29.244 . bindusthānagataṃ cittaṃ bhrūmadhyapathasaṃsthitam || 244
29.245 . hṛllakṣye vā maheśāni binduṃ jvālākulaprabham |
29.245 . tena saṃbodhayetsādhyaṃ bindvākhyo'yaṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 245
29.246 . śāktaṃ śaktimaduccārādgandhoccāreṇa sundari |
29.246 . śṛṅgāṭakāsanasthaṃ tu kuṭilaṃ kuṇḍalākṛtim || 246
29.247 . anuccāreṇa coccārya vedhayennikhilaṃ jagat |
29.247 . evaṃ bhramaravedhena śāktavedha udāhṛtaḥ || 247
29.248 . sā caiva paramā śaktirānandapravikāsinī |
29.248 . janmasthānātparaṃ yāti phaṇapañcakabhūṣitā || 248
29.249 . kalāstattvāni nandādyā vyomāni ca kulāni ca |
29.249 . brahmādikāraṇānyakṣāṇyeva sā pañcakātmikā || 249
29.250 . evaṃ pañcaprakārā sā brahmasthānavinirgatā |
29.250 . brahmasthāne viśantī tu taḍillīnā virājate || 250
29.251 . praviṣṭā vedhayetkāyamātmānaṃ pratibhedayet |
29.251 . evaṃ bhujaṅgavedhastu kathito bhairavāgame || 251
29.252 . tāvadbhāvayate cittaṃ yāvaccittaṃ kṣayaṃ gatam |
29.252 . kṣīṇe citte sureśāni parānanda udāhṛtaḥ || 252
29.253 . nendriyāṇi na vai prāṇā nāntaḥkaraṇagocaraḥ |
29.253 . na mano nāpi mantavyaṃ na mantā na manikriyā || 253
29.254 . sarvabhāvaparikṣīṇaḥ paravedha udāhṛtaḥ |
29.254 . manuśaktibhuvanarūpajñāpiṇḍasthānanāḍiparabhedāt || 254
29.255 . navadhā kalayantyanye vedaṃ guravo rahasyavidaḥ |
29.255 . māyāgarbhāgnivarṇaughayukte tryaśriṇi maṇḍale || 255
29.256 . dhyātvā jvālākarālena tena granthīn vibhedayet |
29.256 . puṣpairhanyādyojayecca pare mantrābhidho vidhiḥ || 256
29.257 . nāḍyāviśyānyatarayā caitanyaṃ kandadhāmani |
29.257 . piṇḍīkṛtya paribhramya pañcāṣṭaśikhayā haṭhāt || 257
29.258 . śaktiśūlāgragamitaṃ kvāpi cakre niyojayet |
29.258 . śaktyeti śākto vedho'yaṃ sadyaḥpratyayakārakaḥ || 258
29.259 . ādhārānnirgatayā śikhayā jyotsnāvadātayā rabhasāt |
29.259 . aṅguṣṭhamūlapīṭhakrameṇa śiṣyasya līnayā vyomni || 259
29.260 . dehaṃ svacchīkṛtya kṣādīnāntān smaranpuroktapuryoghān |
29.260 . nijamaṇḍalanirdhyānātpratibimbayate bhuvanavedhaḥ || 260
29.261 . bhrūmadhyoditabaindavadhāmāntaḥ kāṃcidākṛtiṃ rucirām |
29.261 . tādātmyena dhyāyecchiṣyaṃ paścācca tanmayīkuryāt || 261
29.262 . iti rūpavedha uktaḥ sā cehākṛtirupaiti dṛśyatvam |
29.262 . ante tatsāyujyaṃ śiṣyaścāyāti tanmayībhūtaḥ || 262
29.263 . vijñānamaṣṭadhā yaddhrāṇādikabuddhisaṃjñakaraṇāntaḥ |
29.263 . tatsvasvanāḍisūtrakrameṇa saṃcārayecchiṣye || 263
29.264 . abhimānadārḍhyabandhakrameṇa vijñānasaṃjñako vedhaḥ |
29.264 . hṛdayavyomani sadyo divyajñānārkasamudayaṃ dhatte || 264
29.265 . piṇḍaḥ paraḥ kalātmā sūkṣmaḥ puryaṣṭako bahiḥ sthūlaḥ |
29.265 . chāyātmā sa parāṅmukha ādarśādau ca saṃmukho jñeyaḥ || 265
29.266 . iti yaḥ piṇḍavibhedastaṃ rabhasāduttarottare śamayet |
29.266 . tattadnalane kramaśaḥ paramapadaṃ piṇḍavedhena || 266
29.267 . yadyaddehe cakraṃ tatra śiśoretya viśramaṃ kramaśaḥ |
29.267 . ujjvalayettaccakraṃ sthānākhyastatphalaprado vedhaḥ || 267
29.268 . nāḍyaḥ pradhānabhūtāstisro'nyāstadgatāstvasaṃkhyeyāḥ |
29.268 . ekīkārastābhirnāḍīvedho'tra tatphalakṛt || 268
29.269 . abhilaṣitanāḍivāho mukhyābhiścakṣurādiniṣṭhābhiḥ |
29.269 . adbodhaprāptiḥ syānnāḍīvedhe vicitrabahurūpā || 269
29.270 . lāṅgūlākṛtibalavatsvanāḍisaṃvoṣṭitāmaparanāḍīm |
29.270 . āsphoṭya siddhamapi bhuvi pātayati haṭhānmahāyogī || 270
29.271 . paravedhaṃ samasteṣu cakreṣvadvaitamāmṛśan |
29.271 . paraṃ śivaṃ prakurvīta śivatāpattido guruḥ || 271
29.272 . śrīmadvīrāvalikule tathā cetthaṃ nirūpitam |
29.272 . abhedyaṃ sarvathā jñeyaṃ madhyaṃ jñātvā na lipyate || 272
29.273 . tadvibhāgakrame siddhaḥ sa gururmocayetpaśūn |
29.273 . guroragre viśecchiṣyo vaktraṃ vaktre tu vedhayet || 273
29.274 . rūpaṃ rūpe tu viṣayairyāvatsamarasībhavet |
29.274 . citte samarasībhūte dvayoraunmanasī sthitiḥ || 274
29.275 . ubhayośconmanogatyā tatkāle dīkṣito bhavet |
29.275 . śaśibhāskarasaṃyoge jīvastanmayatāṃ vrajet || 275
29.276 . atra brahmādayo devā muktaye mokṣakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |
29.276 . nirudhya raśmicakraṃ svaṃ bhogamokṣāvubhāvapi || 276
29.277 . grasate yadi taddīkṣā śārvīyaṃ parikīrtitā |
29.277 . sa eṣa mokṣaḥ kathito niḥspandaḥ sarvajantuṣu || 277
29.278 . agnīṣomakalāghātasaṅghātātspandanaṃ haret |
29.278 . bāhyaṃ prāṇaṃ bāhyagataṃ timirākārayogataḥ || 278
29.279 . niryātaṃ romakūpaistu bhramantaṃ sarvakāraṇaiḥ |
29.279 . madhyaṃ nirlakṣyamāsthāya bhramayedvisṛjettataḥ || 279
29.280 . saṃghaṭṭotpāṭayogena vedhayedgranthipañcakam |
29.280 . saṃghaṭṭavṛttiyugalaṃ madhyadhāma vicintayet || 280
29.281 . nātmavyomabahirmantradehasaṃdhānamācaret |
29.281 . dīkṣeyaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ śivatāpattidāyikā || 281
29.282 . dīkṣānte dīpakān paktvā samastaiḥ sādhakaiḥ saha |
29.282 . caruḥ prāśyaḥ kulācāryairmahāpātakanāśanaḥ || 282
29.283 . iti śrīratnamālāyāmūnādhikavidhistu yaḥ |
29.283 . sa eva pātakaṃ tasya praśamo'yaṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 283
29.284 . pare'hani guroḥ kāryo yāgastena vinā yataḥ |
29.284 . na vidhiḥ pūrṇatāṃ yāti kuryādyatnena taṃ tataḥ || 284
29.285 . yena yena gurustuṣyettattadasmai nivedayet |
29.285 . cakracaryāntarāle'syā vidhiḥ saṃcāra ucyāte || 285
29.286 . alipātraṃ susaṃpūrṇaṃ vīrendrakarasaṃsthitam |
29.286 . avalokya paraṃ brahma tatpivedājñayā guroḥ || 286
29.287 . tarpayitvā tu bhūtāni gurave vinivedayet |
29.287 . kṛtvā bhuvi guruṃ natvādāya saṃtarpya khecarīḥ || 287
29.288 . svaṃ mantraṃ tacca vanditvā dūtīṃ gaṇaptiṃ gurūn |
29.288 . kṣetrapaṃ vīrasaṅghātaṃ gurvādikramaśastataḥ || 288
29.289 . vīraspṛṣṭaṃ svayaṃ dravyaṃ pivennaivānyathā kvacit |
29.289 . parabrahmaṇyavettāro'gamāgamavivarjitāḥ || 289
29.290 . lobhamohamadakrodharāgamāyājuṣaśca ye |
29.290 . taiḥ sākaṃ na ca kartavyametacchreyorthinātmani || 290
29.291 . yāgādau yāgamadhye ca yāgānte gurupūjane |
29.291 . naimittikeṣu prokteṣu śiṣyaḥ kuryādimaṃ vidhim || 291
29.292 . iti rahasyavidhiḥ paricarcito gurumukhānubhavaiḥ suparisphuṭaḥ |

c30 atha śrītantrāloke triṃśamāhnikam

30.1 . atha yathocitamantrakadambakaṃ trikakulakramayogi nirūpyate |
30.1 . tāvadvimarśānārūḍhadhiyāṃ tātsiddhaye kramāt || 1
30.2 . pratibuddhā hi te mantrā vimarśaikasvabhāvakāḥ |
30.2 . svatantrasyaiva ciddhāmnaḥ svātantryātkartṛtāmayāḥ || 2
30.3 . yamāviśanti cācāryaṃ taṃ tādātmyanirūḍhitaḥ |
30.3 . svatantrīkurvate yānti karaṇānyapi kartṛtām || 3
30.4 . ādhāraśaktau hrīṃ pṛthvīprabhṛtau tu catuṣṭaye |
30.4 . kṣlāṃ kṣvīṃ vaṃ kṣamiti prāhuḥ kramādvarṇacatuṣṭayam || 4
30.5 . haṃ nāle yaṃ tathā raṃ laṃ vaṃ dharmādicatuṣṭaye |
30.5 . ṛṃ ṝṃ ḻaṃ ḻḷṃ catuṣke ca viparītakramādbhavet || 5
30.6 . omauṃ hastrayamityetadvidyāmāyākalātraye |
30.6 . anusvāravisargau ca vidyeśeśvaratattvayoḥ || 6
30.7 . kādibhāntāḥ kesareṣu prāṇo'ṣṭasvarasaṃyutaḥ |
30.7 . sabinduko daleṣvaṣṭasvatha svaṃ nāma dīpitam || 7
30.8 . śaktīnāṃ navakasya syācchaṣasā maṇḍalatraye |
30.8 . sabindukāḥ kṣmaṃ prete jraṃ śūlaśṛṅgeṣu kalpayet || 8
30.9 . pṛthagāsanapūjāyāṃ kramānmantrā ime smṛtāḥ |
30.9 . saṃkṣepapūjane tu prāgādyamantyaṃ ca bījakam || 9
30.10 . ādāyādhāraśaktyādiśūlaśṛṅgāntamarcayet |
30.10 . agnimārutapṛthvyambusaṣaṣṭhasvarabindukam || 10
30.11 . ratiśekharamantro'sya vaktrāṅgaṃ hrasvadīrghakaiḥ |
30.11 . agniprāṇāgnisaṃhārakālendrāmbusamīraṇāḥ || 11
30.12 . saṣaṣṭhasvarabirndvadhacandrādyāḥ syurnavātmanaḥ |
30.12 . bindunādādikā vyāptiḥ śrīmattraiśirase mate || 12
30.13 . kṣepākrānticidudbodhadīpanasthāpanānyatha |
30.13 . tatsaṃvittistadāpattiriti saṃjñābhiśabditā || 13
30.14 . etāvatī mahāvyāptirmūrtitvenātra kīrtitā |
30.14 . pariṇāmastallayaśca namaskāraḥ sa ucyate || 14
30.15 . eṣa tryarṇojjhito'dhastāddīrghaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ svarairyutaḥ |
30.15 . ṣaḍaṅgāni hṛdādīni vaktrāṇyasya ca kalpayet || 15
30.16 . kṣayaravalabījaistu dīptairbinduvibhūṣitaiḥ |
30.16 . jhakārasaṃhṛtiprāṇāḥ saṣaṣṭhasvarabindukāḥ || 16
30.17 . eṣa bhairavasadmāvaścandrārdhādivibhūṣitaḥ |
30.17 . mātṛkāmālinīmantrau prāgeva samudāhṛtau || 17
30.18 . oṃkāro'tha caturthyantā saṃjñā natiriti kramāt |
30.18 . gaṇeśādiṣu mantraḥ syādbījaṃ yeṣu na coditam || 18
30.19 . nāmādyakṣaramākārabinducandrādidīpitam |
30.19 . sarveṣāmeva bījānāṃ taccaturdaśaṣaṣṭhayuk || 19
30.20 . āmantritānyaghoryāditritayasya kramoditaiḥ |
30.20 . bījairvisargiṇī māyā huṃ hakāro visargavān || 20
30.21 . punardevītrayasyāpi kramādāmantraṇatrayam |
30.21 . dvitīyasminpade'kāra ekārasyeha ca smṛtaḥ || 21
30.22 . tataḥ śaktidvayāmantro luptaṃ tatrāntyamakṣaram |
30.22 . he'gnivarṇāvubhau pañcasvarayuktau parau pṛthak || 22
30.23 . akārayuktāvastraṃ huṃ ha visargī punaḥ śaraḥ |
30.23 . tāreṇa saha vasvagnivarṇārdhārṇadvayādhikā || 23
30.24 . eṣā parāparādevyā vidyā śrītrikaśāsane |
30.24 . pañcaṣaṭpañcavedākṣivahninetrākṣaraṃ padam || 24
30.25 . aghoryādau saptake syātpivanyāḥ pariśiṣṭakam |
30.25 . pratyekavarṇago'pyuktaḥ siddhayogīśvarīmate || 25
30.26 . devatācakravinyāsaḥ sa bahutvānna lipyate |
30.26 . māyā visargiṇī huṃ phaṭceti mantro'parātmakaḥ || 26
30.27 . parāyāstūktasadvyāptirjīvaḥ sahacaturdaśaḥ |
30.27 . sānekabhedā triśiraḥśāstre proktā maheśinā || 27
30.28 . svarūpato vibhinnāpi racanānekasaṅkulā |
30.28 . jīvaḥ prāṇastha evātra prāṇo vā jīvasaṃsthitaḥ || 28
30.29 . ādhārādheyabhāvena avinābhāvayogataḥ |
30.29 . haṃsaṃ cāmṛtamadhyasthaṃ kālarudravibheditam || 29
30.30 . bhuvaneśaśiroyuktamanaṅgadvayayojitam |
30.30 . dīptāddīptataraṃ jñeyaṃ ṣaṭcakrakramayojitam || 30
30.31 . prāṇaṃ daṇḍāsanasthaṃ tu guhyaśaktīcchayā yutam |
30.31 . pareyaṃ vācikoddiṣṭā mahājñānasvarūpataḥ || 31
30.32 . sphuṭaṃ bhairavahṛjjñānamidaṃ tvekākṣaraṃ param |
30.32 . amṛtaṃ kevalaṃ khasthaṃ yadvā sāvitrikāyutam || 32
30.33 . śūnyadvayasamopetaṃ parāyā hṛdayaṃ param |
30.33 . yugmayāge prasiddhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tattvavedibhiḥ || 33
30.34 . anye'pyekākṣarā ye tu ekavīravidhānataḥ |
30.34 . guptā guptatarāste tu aṃgābhijanavarjitāḥ || 34
30.35 . yaṣṭavyāḥ sādhakendraistu kulasthāḥ siddhidāyakāḥ |
30.35 . kulakramavidhānena sūkṣmavijñānayogataḥ || 35
30.36 . anuṣṭheyāḥ sadā devi striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā |
30.36 . sakāro dīrghaṣaṭkena yukto'ṅgānyānanāni tu || 36
30.37 . syātsa eva paraṃ hrasvapañcasvarakhasaṃyutaḥ |
30.37 . oṃkāraiḥ pañcabhirmantro vidyāṅgahṛdayaṃ bhavet || 37
30.38 . praṇavaścāmṛte tejomālini svāhayā saha |
30.38 . ekādaśākṣaraṃ brahmaśirastanmālinīmate || 38
30.39 . vedavedani hūṃ phaṭca praṇavādiyutā śikhā |
30.39 . vajriṇe vajradharāya svāhetyoṃkārapūrvakam || 39
30.40 . ekādaśākṣaraṃ varma puruṣṭutamiti smṛtam |
30.40 . tāro dvijihvaḥ khaśarasvarayugjīva eva ca || 40
30.41 . netrametatprakāśātma sarvasādhāraṇaṃ smṛtam |
30.41 . tāraḥ ślīṃ paśu huṃ phatca tadastraṃ rasavarṇakam || 41
30.42 . laraṭakṣavayairdīrghaiḥ samayuktaiḥ sabindukaiḥ |
30.42 . indrādayastadastrāṇi hrasvairviṣṇuprajāpatī || 42
30.43 . smṛtau turyadvitīyābhyāṃ hrasvābhyāṃ padmacakrake |
30.43 . namaḥ svāhā tathā vauṣaṭhuṃ vaṣaṭphaṭca jātayaḥ || 43
30.44 . aṅgeṣu kramaśaḥ ṣaṭsu karmasvatha tadātmikāḥ |
30.44 . jape home tathāpyāye samuccāṭe'tha śāntike || 44
30.45 . abhicāre ca mantrāṇāṃ namaskārādijātayaḥ |
30.45 . akṣiṣaṇmunivargebhyo dvitīyāḥ saha bindunā || 45
30.46 . yonyarṇena ca mātṝṇāṃ sadmāvaḥ kālakarṣiṇī |
30.46 . ādyojjhito vāpyantena varjito vātha saṃmataḥ || 46
30.47 . jīvaḥ prāṇapuṭāntaḥsthaḥ kālānalasamadyutiḥ |
30.47 . atidīptastu vāmāṃghrirbhūṣito mūrdhni bindunā || 47
30.48 . dakṣajānugataścāyaṃ sarvamātṛgaṇārcitaḥ |
30.48 . anena prāṇitāḥ sarve dadate vāñchitaṃ phalam || 48
30.49 . sadbhāvaḥ paramo hyeṣa mātṝṇāṃ bhairavasya ca |
30.49 . tasmādenaṃ japenmantrī ya icchetsiddhimuttamām || 49
30.50 . rudraśaktisamāveśo nityamatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
30.50 . yasmādeṣā parā śaktirbhedenānyena kīrtitā || 50
30.51 . yāvatyaḥ siddhayastantre tāḥ sarvāḥ kurute tviyam |
30.51 . aṅgavaktrāṇi cāpyasyāḥ prāgvatsvaraniyogataḥ || 51
30.52 . daṇḍo jīvastriśūlaṃ ca dakṣāṅgulyaparastanau |
30.52 . nābhikaṇṭhau marudrudrau visargaḥ satriśūlakaḥ || 52
30.53 . sarvayoginicakrāṇāmadhipo'yamudāhṛtaḥ |
30.53 . asyāpyuccāraṇādeva saṃvittiḥ syātpuroditā || 53
30.54 . mahācaṇḍeti tu yogeśvaṛ ityaṣṭavarṇakam |
30.54 . navārṇeyaṃ guptatarā sadbhāvaḥ kālakarṣiṇī || 54
30.55 . śrīḍāmare mahāyāge parātparataroditā |
30.55 . sudhācchedakaṣaṇṭhādyairbījaṃ chedakamasvaram || 55
30.56 . adhyardhārṇā kālarātriḥ kṣurikā mālinīmate |
30.56 . śatāvartanayā hyasyā jāyate mūrdhni vedanā || 56
30.57 . evaṃ pratyayamālocya mṛtyujiddhyānamāśrayet |
30.57 . naināṃ samuccareddevi ya iccheddīrghajīvitam || 57
30.58 . dvirdaṇḍāgnī śūlanabhaḥprāṇāśchettranalau tathā |
30.58 . kūṭāgnī savisargāśca pañcāpyete'tha pañcasu || 58
30.59 . vyomasviti śivenoktaṃ tantrasadbhāvaśāsane |
30.59 . chedinī kṣurikeyaṃ syādyayā yojayate pare || 59
30.60 . bindvindvanalakūṭāgnimarutṣaṣṭhasvarairyutam |
30.60 . āpādatalamūrdhāntaṃ smaredastramidaṃ jvalat || 60
30.61 . kuñcanaṃ cāṅgulīnāṃ tu kartavyaṃ codanaṃ tataḥ |
30.61 . jānvādiparacakrāntaṃ cakrāccakraṃ tu kuñcayet || 61
30.62 . kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu sadyonirvāṇakaṃ param |
30.62 . athocyate brahmavidyā sadyaḥpratyayadāyinī || 62
30.63 . śivaḥ śrībhūtirājo yāmasmabhyaṃ pratyapādayat |
30.63 . sarveṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ maraṇe samupasthite || 63
30.64 . yayā paṭhitayotkramya jīvo yāti nirañjanam |
30.64 . yā jñānino'pi saṃpūrṇakṛtyasyāpi śrutā satī || 64
30.65 . prāṇādicchedajāṃ mṛtyuvyathāṃ sadyo vyapohati |
30.65 . yāmākarṇya mahāmohavivaśo'pi kramādgataḥ || 65
30.66 . prabodhaṃ vaktṛsāṃmukhyamabhyeti rabhasātsvayam |
30.66 . paramapadāttvamihāgāḥ sanātanastvaṃ jahīhi dehāntam || 66
30.67 . pādāṅguṣṭhādi vibho nibandhanaṃ bandhanaṃ hyugram |
30.67 . āryāvākyamidaṃ pūrvaṃ bhuvanākhyaiḥ padairbhavet || 67
30.68 . gulphānte jānugataṃ jatrusthaṃ bandhanaṃ tathā meḍhre |
30.68 . jahihi puramagryamadhyaṃ hṛtpadmāttvaṃ samuttiṣṭha || 68
30.69 . etāvadbhiḥ padairetadāryāvākyaṃ dvitīyakam |
30.69 . haṃsa hayagrīva vibho sadāśivastvaṃ paro'si jīvākhyaḥ || 69
30.70 . ravisomavahnisaṅghadṛbindudeho hahaha samutkrāma |
30.70 . tṛtīyamāryāvākyaṃ prāksaṃkhyairekādhikaiḥ padaiḥ || 70
30.71 . haṃsamahāmantramayaḥ sanātanastvaṃ śubhāśubhāpekṣī |
30.71 . maṇḍalamadhyaniviṣṭaḥ śaktimahāsetukāraṇamahārthaḥ || 71
30.72 . kamalobhayaviniviṣṭaḥ prabodhamāyāhi devatādeha |
30.72 . āryāvākyamidaṃ sārdhaṃ rudrasaṃkhyapaderitam || 72
30.73 . niḥśvāse tvapaśabdasya sthāne'styupa iti dhvaniḥ |
30.73 . ajñānāttvaṃ baddhaḥ prabodhitottiṣṭha devāde || 73
30.74 . etatpañcamamāryārdhavākyaṃ syātsaptabhiḥ padaiḥ |
30.74 . vraja tālusāhvayāntaṃ hyauḍambaraghaṭṭitaṃ mahādvāram || 74
30.75 . prāpya prayāhi haṃho haṃho vā vāmadevapadam |
30.75 . āryyāvākyamidaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ syāccaturdaśabhiḥ padaiḥ || 75
30.76 . granthīśvara paramātman śānta mahātālurandhramāsādya |
30.76 . utkrama he deheśvara nirañjanaṃ śivapadaṃ prayāhyāśu || 76
30.77 . āryāvākyaṃ saptamaṃ syāttaccaturdaśabhiḥ padaiḥ |
30.77 . prabhañjanastvamityevaṃ pāṭho niḥśvāsaśāsane || 77
30.78 . ākramya madhyamārgaṃ prāṇāpānau samāhṛtya |
30.78 . dharmādharmau tyaktvā nārāyaṇa yāhi śāntāntam || 78
30.79 . āryāvākyamidaṃ proktamaṣṭamaṃ navabhiḥ padaiḥ |
30.79 . he brahman he viṣṇo he rudra śivo'si vāsudevastvam || 79
30.80 . agnīṣomasanātanamṛtpiṇḍaṃ jahihi he mahākāśa |
30.80 . etadbhuvanasaṃkhyātairāryyāvākyaṃ prakīrtitam || 80
30.81 . sanātma tripiṇḍamiti mahākośamiti sthitam |
30.81 . padatrayaṃ tu niḥśvāsamukuṭottarakādiṣu || 81
30.82 . aṅguṣṭhamātramamalamāvaraṇaṃ jahihi he mahāsūkṣma |
30.82 . āryyāvākyamidaṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ padairdaśamamucyate || 82
30.83 . alaṃ dviriti sūkṣmaṃ cetyevaṃ śrīmukuṭottare |
30.83 . puruṣastvaṃ prakṛtimayairbaddho'haṅkāratantunā bandhaiḥ || 83
30.84 . abhavābhava nityodita paramātmaṃstyaja sarāgamadhvānam |
30.84 . etattrayodaśapadaṃ syādāryāvākyamuttamam || 84
30.85 . hrīṃhūṃmantraśarīramavilambamāśu tvamehi dehāntam |
30.85 . āryārdhavākyametatsyāddvādaśaṃ ṣaṭpadaṃ param || 85
30.86 . tadidaṃ guṇabhūtamayaṃ tyaja sva ṣoṭkośikaṃ piṇḍam |
30.86 . syāttrayodaśamāryārdhaṃ padaiḥ saptabhirīdṛśam || 86
30.87 . mā dehaṃ bhūtamayaṃ pragṛhyatāṃ śāśvataṃ mahādeham |
30.87 . āryārdhavākyaṃ tāvadbhiḥ padairetaccaturdaśam || 87
30.88 . maṇḍalamamalamanantaṃ tridhā sthitaṃ gaccha bhittvaitat |
30.88 . āryārdhavākyamaṣṭābhiḥ padaiḥ pañcadaśaṃ tvidam || 88
30.89 . sakaleyaṃ brahmavidyā syātpañcadaśabhiḥ sphuṭaiḥ |
30.89 . vākyaiḥ pañcākṣaraistvasyā niṣkalā parikīrtyate || 89
30.90 . prativākyaṃ yayādyantayojitā paripaṭhyate |
30.90 . tāro māyā vedakalo mātṛtāro navātmakaḥ || 90
30.91 . iti pañcākṣarāṇi syuḥ proktavyāptyanusārataḥ |
30.91 . binduprāṇāmṛtajalaṃ marutṣaṣṭhasvarānvitam || 91
30.92 . etena śaktyuccārasthabījenālabhyate paśuḥ |
30.92 . kṛtadīkṣāvidhiḥ pūrvaṃ brahmaghno'pi viśuddhyati || 92
30.93 . laghutvena tulāśuddhiḥ sadyaḥpratyayakāriṇī |
30.93 . tāraḥ śamarayaiḥ piṇḍo natiśca caturarṇakam || 93
30.94 . śākinīstobhanaṃ marma hṛdayaṃ jīvitaṃ tvidam |
30.94 . ṣaṣṭhaprāṇatrikūṭordhvabāhuśūlākhyabindubhiḥ || 94
30.95 . anackanāsādhovaktracandrakhaṇḍaiśca maṇḍitam |
30.95 . hṛdayaṃ bhairavākhyaṃ tu sarvasaṃhārakārakam || 95
30.96 . agnimaṇḍalamadhyasthabhairavānalatāpitāḥ |
30.96 . vaśamāyānti śākinyaḥ sthānametena ceddahet || 96
30.97 . visarjayettāḥ prathamamanyathā cchidrayanti tāḥ |
30.97 . hrīṃ klīṃ vleṃ klemebhirvarṇairdvādaśasvarabhūṣitaiḥ || 97
30.98 . priyamelāpanaṃ nāma hṛdayaṃ sampuṭaṃ japet |
30.98 . pratyekamathavā dvābhyāṃ sarvairvā vidhiruttamaḥ || 98
30.99 . tulāmelakayogaḥ śrītantrasadbhāvaśāsane |
30.99 . ya uktaḥ śambhunāthena sa mayā darśitaḥ kramāt || 99
30.100 . atha vittavihīnānāṃ prapannānāṃ ca tattvataḥ |
30.100 . deśakālādidoṣeṇa na tathādhyavasāyinām || 100
30.101 . prakartavyā yathā dīkṣā śrīsantatyāgamoditā |
30.101 . kathyate hāṭakeśānapātālādhipacoditā || 101
30.102 . śrīnātha ārya bhagavannetattritayaṃ hi kanda ādhāre |
30.102 . varuṇo macchando bhagavatta iti trayamidaṃ hṛdaye || 102
30.103 . dharmādivargasaṃjñāścatvāraḥ kaṇṭhadeśagāḥ pūjyāḥ |
30.103 . hrīṃśrīṃpūrvāḥ sarve sambodhajuṣaśca pādaśabdāntāḥ || 103
30.104 . mūrdhatale vidyātrayamuktaṃ bhāvyatha mano'bhiyogena |
30.104 . kusumairānandairvā bhāvanayā vāpi kevalayā || 104
30.105 . guruṇā tattvavidā kila śiṣyo yadi mokṣamātrakṛtahṛdayaḥ |
30.105 . mokṣaikadānacaturā dīkṣā seyaṃ paropaniṣaduktā || 105
30.106 . etaddīkṣādīkṣita etadvidyātrayaṃ smaran hṛdaye |
30.106 . bāhyārcādi vinaiva hi vrajati paraṃ dhāma dehānte || 106
30.107 . praṇavo māyā bindurvarṇatrayamāditaḥ kuryāt |
30.107 . padapañcakasya saṃbodhanayuktasyāgnidayitānte || 107
30.108 . siddhasādhani tatpūrvaṃ śabdabrahmasvarūpiṇi |
30.108 . samastabandhaśabdena sahitaṃ ca nikṛntani || 108
30.109 . bodhani śivasadbhāvajananyāmantritaṃ ca tat |
30.109 . pañcāṣṭarandhratryaṣṭārṇakrameṇa padapañcakam || 109
30.110 . khapañcārṇā parabrahmavidyeyaṃ mokṣadā śivā |
30.110 . anuttarecche ghāntaśca satrayodaśasusvaraḥ || 110
30.111 . asya varṇatrayasyānte tvantaḥsthānāṃ catuṣṭayam |
30.111 . vargādyaśvau tryasrabinduyukpānto'rṇatrayādataḥ || 111
30.112 . mahāhāṭakaśabdādyamīśvarītyarṇasaptakam |
30.112 . āmantritaṃ kṣamasveti tryarṇaṃ pāpāntakāriṇi || 112
30.113 . ṣaḍarṇaṃ pāpaśabdādivimohanipadaṃ tataḥ |
30.113 . pāpaṃ hana dhuna dvirdvirdaśārṇaṃ padamīdṛśam || 113
30.114 . pañcamyantaṃ ṣaḍarṇaṃ syādrudraśaktivaśāditi |
30.114 . tata ekākṣaraṃ yattadvisargabrahma kīrtitam || 114
30.115 . tadanackatakāreṇa sahaikībhāvataḥ paṭhet |
30.115 . randhrābdhivarṇā vidyeyaṃ dīkṣāvidyeti kīrtitā || 115
30.116 . māyārṇañca pare brahme caturvidye padatrayam |
30.116 . aṣṭārṇamatha pañcārṇaṃ yogadhāriṇisaṃjñitam || 116
30.117 . ātmāntarātmaparamātmarūpaṃ ca padatrayam |
30.117 . ekārāntaṃ bodhanasthaṃ daśārṇaṃ parikīrtitam || 117
30.118 . rudraśaktīti vedārṇaṃ syādrudradayite'tha me |
30.118 . pāpaṃ dahadahetyeṣā dvādaśārṇā catuṣpadī || 118
30.119 . saumye sadāśive yugmaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ bindviṣusāvahā |
30.119 . sārdhavarṇacatuṣkaṃ tadityeṣā samayāpahā || 119
30.120 . vidyā sārdhārṇakhaśarasaṃkhyā sā pārameśvarī |
30.120 . etadvidyātrayaṃ śrīmadbhūtirājo nyarūpayat || 120
30.121 . yaḥ sākṣādabhajacchrīmāñśrīkaṇṭho mānuṣīṃ tanum |
30.121 . atra vīryaṃ puraivoktaṃ sarvatrānusaredguruḥ || 121
30.122 . arthabījapraveśāntaruccārādyanusārataḥ |
30.122 . nahi tatkiṃcanāpyasti yatpurā na nirūpitam || 122
30.123 . niṣphalā punaruktistu nāsmabhyaṃ jātu rocate |
30.123 . ityevaṃ mantravidyādisvarūpamupavarṇitam || 123


c31 atha śrītantrāloke ekatriṃśamāhnikam

31.1 . atha maṇḍalasadbhāvaḥ saṃkṣepeṇābhidhīyate |
31.1 . sādhayitvā diśaṃ pūrvāṃ sūtramāsphālayetsamam || 1
31.2 . tadardhayitvā madhyaprākpratīcīṣvaṅkayetpunaḥ |
31.2 . tato'pyardhatadardhārdhamānataḥ pūrvapaścimau || 2
31.3 . aṅkayettāvatā dadyātsūtreṇa bhramayugmakam |
31.3 . matsyasandhidvayaṃ tvevaṃ dakṣiṇottarayorbhavet || 3
31.4 . tanmadhye pātayetsūtraṃ dakṣiṇottarasiddhaye |
31.4 . yadi vā prākparāktulyasūtreṇottaradakṣiṇe || 4
31.5 . aṅkayedaparādaṅkātpūrvādapi tathaiva te |
31.5 . matsyamadhye kṣipetsūtramāyataṃ dakṣiṇottare || 5
31.6 . matakṣetrārdhamānena madhyāddikṣvaṅkayettataḥ |
31.6 . sūtrābhyāṃ digdvayotthābhyāṃ matsyaḥ syātpratikoṇagaḥ || 6
31.7 . matsyeṣu vedāḥ sūtrāṇītyevaṃ syāccaturasrakam |
31.7 . ekasmātprabhṛti proktaṃ śatāntaṃ maṇḍalaṃ yataḥ || 7
31.8 . siddhātantre maṇḍalānāṃ śataṃ tatpīṭha ucyate |
31.8 . yattanmadhyagataṃ mukhyaṃ maṇḍalānāṃ trayaṃ smṛtam || 8
31.9 . madhyaśūlaṃ tritriśūlaṃ navaśūlamiti sphuṭam |
31.9 . tatra śūlavidhānaṃ yaduktaṃ bhedairanantakaiḥ || 9
31.10 . tadyoni maṇḍalaṃ brūmaḥ sadbhāvakramadarśitam |
31.10 . vedāśrite caturhaste tribhāgaṃ sarvatastyajet || 10
31.11 . bhāgaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sarvaṃ tattatkṣetraṃ vibhājayet |
31.11 . brahmasūtradvayasyātha madhyaṃ brahmapadaṃ sphuṭam || 11
31.12 . kṛtvāvadhiṃ tato lakṣyaṃ caturthaṃ sūtramāditaḥ |
31.12 . tatastiryagvrajetsūtraṃ caturthaṃ tadanantare || 12
31.13 . koṣṭhe cendudvayaṃ kuryādbahirbhāgārdhabhāgataḥ |
31.13 . tayorlagnaṃ brahmasūtrāttṛtīye marmaṇi sthitam || 13
31.14 . koṣṭhakārdhe'paraṃ ceti yugmamantarmukhaṃ bhavet |
31.14 . brahmasūtrāddvitīyasmin haste marmaṇi niścalam || 14
31.15 . kṛtvā pūrṇenduyugalaṃ vartayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ |
31.15 . brahmasūtragatātṣaṣṭhāttiryagbhāgāttṛtīyake || 15
31.16 . kṛtvārdhakoṣṭhake sūtraṃ pūrṇacandrāgralambitam |
31.16 . bhramayedunmukhaṃ khaṇḍacandrayugvahnibhāgagam || 16
31.17 . tiryagbhāgadvayaṃ tyaktvā khaṇḍendoḥ paścimāttataḥ |
31.17 . koṇaṃ yāvattathā syācca kuryātkhaṇḍaṃ bhramadvayam || 17
31.18 . sutīkṣṇakuṭilāgraṃ tadekaṃ śṛṅgaṃ prajāyate |
31.18 . dvitīyasminnapi proktaḥ śṛṅga eṣa vidhiḥ sphuṭaḥ || 18
31.19 . madhyaśṛṅge'tha kartavye tṛtīye ūrdhvakoṣṭhake |
31.19 . caturthārdhe ca candrārdhadvayamantarmukhaṃ bhavet || 19
31.20 . tacca pūrṇendumekaṃ prāgvartitaṃ prāpnuyādyathā |
31.20 . anyonyagranthiyogena baddhāratvaṃ prajāyate || 20
31.21 . evaṃ dvitīyapārśve'sya khaṇḍendudvayavartanāt |
31.21 . madhyābhyāṃ gaṇḍikā śliṣṭā parābhyāmagrato nayet || 21
31.22 . sūtraṃ pārśvadvaye yena tīkṣṇaṃ syānmadhyaśṛṅgagam |
31.22 . pārśvadvayādhare paścādbrahmasūtraṃ dvitīyakam || 22
31.23 . avadhānena saṃgrāhyamācāryeṇohavedinā |
31.23 . bhavetpaścānmukho mantrī tasmiṃśca brahmasūtrake || 23
31.24 . madhyaśṛṅgaṃ varjayitvā sarvaḥ pūrvodito vidhiḥ |
31.24 . tato yadunmukhaṃ khaṇḍacandrayugmaṃ puroditam || 24
31.25 . tato dvayena kartavyā gaṇḍikāntaḥsusaṃgatā |
31.25 . dvayenāgragasūtrābhyāṃ madhyaśṛṅgadvayaṃ bhavet || 25
31.26 . adho bhāgavivṛddhyāsya padmaṃ vṛttacatuṣṭayam |
31.26 . tataścakraṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ dvādaśāraṃ dvidhātha tat || 26
31.27 . madhye kuleśvarīsthānaṃ vyoma vā tilakaṃ ca vā |
31.27 . padmaṃ vātha ṣaḍaraṃ vā viyaddvādaśakaṃ ca vā || 27
31.28 . tritriśūle'tra saptāre śliṣṭamātreṇa madhyataḥ |
31.28 . padmānāmatha cakrāṇāṃ vyomnāṃ vā saptakaṃ bhavet || 28
31.29 . miśritaṃ vātha saṃkīrṇaṃ samāsavyāsabhedataḥ |
31.29 . tataḥ kṣetrārdhamānena kṣetraṃ tatrādhikaṃ kṣipet || 29
31.30 . tatra daṇḍaḥ smṛto bhāgaḥ ṣaḍarāmalasārakaḥ |
31.30 . sutīkṣṇāgraḥ suraktābhaḥ kṣaṇādāveśakārakaḥ || 30
31.31 . yā sā kuṇḍalinī devī taraṅgākhyā mahormiṇī |
31.31 . sā ṣaḍaśreṇa kandākhye sthitā ṣaḍdevatātmikā || 31
31.32 . aṣṭabhāgaiśca vistīrṇo dīrghaścāpi tadardhataḥ |
31.32 . tato dvārāṇi kāryāṇi citravartanayā kramāt || 32
31.33 . vedāśrāyatarūpāṇi yadivā vṛttamātrataḥ |
31.33 . spaṣṭaśṛṅgamatho kuryādyadivā vaiparītyataḥ || 33
31.34 . unmukhaṃ candrayugmaṃ vā bhaṅktvā kuryāccatuṣṭayam |
31.34 . kuṭilo madhyataḥ spaṣṭo'dhomukhaḥ pārśvagaḥ sthitaḥ || 34
31.35 . uttāno'rdho'samaḥ pūrṇaḥ śliṣṭo granthigatastathā |
31.35 . candrasyetthaṃ dvādaśadhā vartanā bhramabhedinī || 35
31.36 . antarbahirmukhatvena sā punardvividhā matā |
31.36 . tadbhedānmaṇḍalānāṃ syādasaṅkhyo bhedavistaraḥ || 36
31.37 . pīṭhavīthībahiarbhūmikaṇṭhakarṇakapolataḥ |
31.37 . śobhopaśobhāsaṃbhedādguṇarekhāvikalpataḥ || 37
31.38 . svastikadvitayādyaṣṭatayāparyantabhedataḥ |
31.38 . bhāvābhāvavikalpena maṇḍalānāmanantatā || 38
31.39 . tato rajāṃsi deyāni yathāśobhānusārataḥ |
31.39 . sindūraṃ rājavartaṃ ca khaṭikā ca sitottamā || 39
31.40 . uttamāni rajāṃsīha devatātrayayogataḥ |
31.40 . parā candrasamaprakhyā raktā devī parāparā || 40
31.41 . aparā sā parā kālī bhīṣaṇā caṇḍayoginī |
31.41 . dṛṣṭvaitanmaṇḍalaṃ devyaḥ sarvā nṛtyanti sarvadā || 41
31.42 . anarcite'pyadīkṣeṇa dṛṣṭe dīkṣyeta mātṛbhiḥ |
31.42 . kiṃvātibahunoktena tritriśūlārasaptakāḥ || 42
31.43 . śūlayāgāḥ ṣaṭsahasrāṇyevaṃ sārdhaśatadvayam |
31.43 . yā sā devī parā śaktiḥ prāṇavāhā vyavasthitā || 43
31.44 . viśvāntaḥ kuṇḍalākārā sā sākṣādatra vartitā |
31.44 . tattvāni tattvadevyaśca viśvamasminpratiṣṭhitam || 44
31.45 . atrordhve tantumātreṇa tisraḥ śūlāragāḥ sthitāḥ |
31.45 . āsanatvena cecchādyā bhogamokṣaprasādhikāḥ || 45
31.46 . tāstu mokṣaikakāmasya śūlārāviddhamadhyakāḥ |
31.46 . tasmādenaṃ mahāyāgaṃ mahāvibhavavistaraiḥ || 46
31.47 . pūjayedbhūtikāmo vā mokṣakāmo'pivā budhaḥ |
31.47 . asya darśanamātreṇa bhūtavetālaguhyakāḥ || 47
31.48 . palāyante daśa diśaḥ śivaḥ sākṣātprasīdati |
31.48 . mandaśaktibalāviddho'pyetanmaṇḍalapūjanāt || 48
31.49 . satataṃ māsaṣaṭkena trikajñānaṃ samaśnute |
31.49 . yatprāpya heyopādeyaṃ svayameva vicārya saḥ || 49
31.50 . dehānte syādbhairavātmā siddhikāmo'tha siddhyati |
31.50 . maṇḍalasyāsya yo vyāptiṃ devatānyāsameva ca || 50
31.51 . vartanāṃ ca vijānāti sa gurustrikaśāsane |
31.51 . tasya pādarajo mūrdhni dhāryaṃ śivasamīhinā || 51
31.52 . atra sṛṣṭisthitidhvaṃsān kramāttrīnapi pūjayet |
31.52 . turyaṃ tu madhyato yadvā sarveṣu paripūrakam || 52
31.53 . catustriśūlaṃ vā guptadaṇḍaṃ yāgaṃ samācaret |
31.53 . tatra tatpūjayetsamyaksphuṭaṃ kramacatuṣṭayam || 53
31.54 . ityetatkathitaṃ gupte ṣaḍardhahṛdaye pare |
31.54 . ṣaṭke proktaṃ sūcitaṃ śrīsiddhayogīśvarīmate || 54
31.55 . agrataḥ sūtrayitvā tu maṇḍalaṃ sarvakāmadam |
31.55 . mahāśūlasamopetaṃ padmacakrādibhūṣitam || 55
31.56 . dvāre dvāre likhecchūlaṃ varjayitvā tu paścimam |
31.56 . koṇeṣvapica vā kāryaṃ mahāśūlaṃ drumānvitam || 56
31.57 . amṛtāmbhobhavārīṇāṃ śūlāgre tu trikaṃ trikam |
31.57 . śūla itthaṃ prakartavyamaṣṭadhā tattridhāpivā || 57
31.58 . evaṃ saṃsūcitaṃ divyaṃ khecarīṇāṃ puraṃ tviti |
31.58 . sthānāntare'pi kathitaṃ śrīsiddhātantraśāsane || 58
31.59 . kajaṃ madhye tadardhena śūlaśṛṅgāṇi tāni tu |
31.59 . śūlāṅkaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kalpyaṃ kamalāṅkaṃ ca pūraṇe || 59
31.60 . atha śūlābjavinyāsaḥ śrīpūrve triśiromate |
31.60 . siddhātantre trikakule devyāyāmalamālayoḥ || 60
31.61 . yathoktaḥ sāraśāstre ca tantrasadbhāvaguhyayoḥ |
31.61 . tathā pradarśyate spaṣṭaṃ yadyapyuktakramādgataḥ || 61
31.62 . vedāśrite trihaste prākpūrvamardha vibhājayet |
31.62 . hastārdhaṃ sarvatastyaktvā pūrvodagyāmyadiggatam || 62
31.63 . tryaṅgulaiḥ koṣṭhakairūrdhvaistiryakcāṣṭadvidhātmakaiḥ |
31.63 . dvau dvau bhāgau parityajya punardakṣiṇasaumyagau || 63
31.64 . brahmaṇaḥ pārśvayorjīvāccaturthātpūrvatastathā |
31.64 . bhāgārdhabhāgamānaṃ tu khaṇḍacandradvayaṃ dvayam || 64
31.65 . tayorantastṛtīye tu dakṣiṇottarapārśvayoḥ |
31.65 . jīve khaṇḍenduyugalaṃ kuryādantarbhramādbudhaḥ || 65
31.66 . tayoraparamarmasthaṃ khaṇḍendudvayakoṭigam |
31.66 . bahirmukhaṃ bhramaṃ kuryātkhaṇḍacandradvayaṃ dvayam || 66
31.67 . tadvadbrahmaṇi kurvīta bhāgabhāgārdhasaṃmitam |
31.67 . tato dvitīyabhāgānte brahmaṇaḥ pārśvayordvayoḥ || 67
31.68 . dve rekhe pūrvage neye bhāgatryaṃśaśame budhaiḥ |
31.68 . ekārdhendūrdhvakoṭisthaṃ brahmasūtrāgrasaṅgatam || 68
31.69 . sūtradvayaṃ prakurvīta madhyaśṛṅgaprasiddhaye |
31.69 . tadagrapārśvayorjīvātsūtramekāntare dhṛtam || 69
31.70 . ādidvitīyakhaṇḍendukoṇātkoṇāntamānayet |
31.70 . tayorevāparājjīvātprathamārdhendukoṇataḥ || 70
31.71 . tadvadeva nayetsūtraṃ śṛṅgadvitayasiddhaye |
31.71 . kṣetrārdhe cāpare daṇḍo dvikaraśchannapañcakaḥ || 71
31.72 . ṣaḍvistṛtaṃ caturdīrghaṃ tadadho'malasārakam |
31.72 . vedāṅgulaṃ ca tadadho mūlaṃ tīkṣṇāgramiṣyate || 72
31.73 . ādikṣetrasya kurvīta dikṣu dvāracatuṣṭayam |
31.73 . hastāyāmaṃ tadardhaṃ vā vistārādapi tatsamam || 73
31.74 . dviguṇaṃ bāhyataḥ kuryāttataḥ padmaṃ yathā śṛṇu |
31.74 . ekaikabhāgamānāni kuryādvṛttāni vedavat || 74
31.75 . dikṣvaṣṭau punarapyaṣṭau jīvasūtrāṇi ṣoḍaśa |
31.75 . dvayordvayoḥ punarmadhye tatsaṃkhyātāni pātayet || 75
31.76 . eṣāṃ tṛtīyavṛttasthaṃ pārśvajīvasamaṃ bhramam |
31.76 . etadantaṃ prakurvīta tato jīvāgramānayet || 76
31.77 . yatraiva kutracitsaṅgastatsaṃbandhe sthirīkṛte |
31.77 . tatra kṛtvā nayenmantrī patrāgrāṇāṃ prasiddhaye || 77
31.78 . ekaikasmindale kuryātkesarāṇāṃ trayaṃ trayam |
31.78 . dviguṇāṣṭāṅgulaṃ kāryaṃ tadvacchṛṅgakajatrayam || 78
31.79 . karṇikā pītavarṇena mūlamadhyāgrabhedataḥ |
31.79 . sitaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kāryaṃ kesarajālakam || 79
31.80 . dalāni śuklavarṇāni prativāraṇayā saha |
31.80 . pīṭhaṃ tadvaccatuṣkoṇaṃ karṇikārdhasamaṃ bahiḥ || 80
31.81 . sitaraktapītakṛṣṇaistatpādān vahnitaḥ kramāt |
31.81 . caturbhirapi śṛṅgāṇi tribhirmaṇḍalamiṣyate || 81
31.82 . daṇḍaḥ syānnīlaraktena pītamāmalasārakam |
31.82 . raktaṃ śūlaṃ prakurvīta yattatpūrvaṃ prakalpitam || 82
31.83 . paścāddvārasya pūrveṇa tyaktvāṅgulacatuṣṭayam |
31.83 . dvāraṃ vedāśri vṛttaṃ vā saṃkīrṇaṃ vā vicitritam || 83
31.84 . ekadvitripuraṃ tulyaṃ sāmudgamathavobhayam |
31.84 . kapolakaṇṭhaśobhopaśobhādibahucitritam || 84
31.85 . vicitrākārasaṃsthānaṃ vallīsūkṣmagṛhānvitam |
31.85 . śrīdevyāyāmale tūktaṃ kṣetre vedāśrite sati || 85
31.86 . ardhaṃ dvādaśadhā kṛtvā tiryagūrdhvaṃ ca tiryajam |
31.86 . bhāgamekaṃ svapārśvordhvaṃ guruḥ samavatārayet || 86
31.87 . madhyasthaṃ taṃ tribhāgaṃ ca tadante bhramayedubhau |
31.87 . bhāgamekaṃ parityajya tanmadhye bhramayetpunaḥ || 87
31.88 . tṛtīyāṃśordhvato bhrāmyamūrdhvāṃśaṃ yāvadantataḥ |
31.88 . caturthāṃśāttadūrdhvaṃ tu ūrdhvādho yojayetpunaḥ || 88
31.89 . tanmānādūrdhvamābhrāmya caturthena niyojayet |
31.89 . ūrdhvādyojayate sūtraṃ brahmasūtrāvadhi kramāt || 89
31.90 . kramādvaipulyataḥ kṛtvā aṃśaṃ vai hrāsayetpunaḥ |
31.90 . ardhabhāgapramāṇastu daṇḍo dviguṇa iṣyate || 90
31.91 . bhāgaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhītvā tu ubhayoratha gocarāt |
31.91 . bhrāmyaṃ pippalavatpatraṃ vartanaiṣā tvadho bhavet || 91
31.92 . ṣoḍaśāṃśe likhetpadmaṃ dvādaśāṅgulalopanāt |
31.92 . tadūrdhvaṃ madhyabhāge tu vārijanma samālikhet || 92
31.93 . madhyaśṛṅgāvasāne tu tṛtīyaṃ vilikhettataḥ |
31.93 . savyāsavye tathaiveha kaṭisthābje samālikhet || 93
31.94 . karṇikā pītalā raktapītaśuklaṃ ca kesaram |
31.94 . dalāni padmabāhyasthā śuklā ca prativāraṇī || 94
31.95 . śūlaṃ kṛṣṇena rajasā brahmarekhā sitā punaḥ |
31.95 . śūlāgraṃ jvālayā yuktaṃ śūladaṇḍastu pītalaḥ || 95
31.96 . śūlamadhye ca yatpadmaṃ tatreśaṃ pūjayetsadā |
31.96 . asyordhve tu parāṃ dakṣe'nyāṃ vāme cāparāṃ budhaḥ || 96
31.97 . yā sā kālāntakā devī parātītā vyavasthitā |
31.97 . grasate śūlacakraṃ sā tvicchāmātreṇa sarvadā || 97
31.98 . śāntirūpā kalā hyeṣā vidyārūpā parā bhavet |
31.98 . aparā tu pratiṣṭhā syānnivṛttistu parāparā || 98
31.99 . bhairavaṃ daṇḍa ūrdhvasthaṃ rūpaṃ sādāśivātmakam |
31.99 . catasraḥ śaktayastvasya sthūlāḥ sūkṣmāstvanekadhā || 99
31.100 . eṣa yāgaḥ samākhyāto ḍāmarākhyastriśaktikaḥ |
31.100 . atha traiśirase śūlābjavidhirdṛṣṭo'bhilikhyate || 100
31.101 . vāmāmṛtādibhirmukhyaiḥ pavitraiḥ sumanoramaiḥ |
31.101 . bhūmiṃ rajāṃsi karaṇīṃ khaṭikāṃ mūlato'rcayet || 101
31.102 . caturaśre caturhaste madhye śūlaṃ karatrayam |
31.102 . caṇḍo dvihasta ūrdhvādhaḥpīṭhayugvipulastvasau || 102
31.103 . vasvaṅgulaḥ prakartavyaḥ sūtratrayasamanvitaḥ |
31.103 . dvādaśāṅgulamānena daṇḍamūle tu pīṭhikā || 103
31.104 . dairghyāttūcchrāyāccordhve ca caturaṅgulamānataḥ |
31.104 . ūrdhve'pyucchrāyato vedāṅgulā dairghyāddaśāṅgulā || 104
31.105 . śūlamūlagataṃ pīṭhīmadhyaṃ khābdhisamāṅgulam |
31.105 . kṛtvā daṇḍaṃ triśūlaṃ tu tribhirbhāgaiḥ samantataḥ || 105
31.106 . aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇaiḥ syāddhastamātraṃ samantataḥ |
31.106 . śūlāgraṃ śūlamadhyaṃ tacchūlamūlaṃ tu tadbhavet || 106
31.107 . vedī madhye prakartavyā ubhayośca ṣaḍaṅgulam |
31.107 . dvādaśāṅguladīrghā tu ubhayoḥ pārśvayostathā || 107
31.108 . caturaṅgulamucchrāyānmūle vedīṃ prakalpayet |
31.108 . ubhayoḥ pārśvayoścaivamardhacandrākṛtiṃ tathā || 108
31.109 . bhrāmayetkhaṭikāsūtraṃ kaṭiṃ kuryāddviraṅgulām |
31.109 . vaipulyāddairghyato devi caturaṅgulamānataḥ || 109
31.110 . yādṛśaṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge vāme tadvatprakalpayet |
31.110 . madhye śūlāgravaipulyādaṅgulaśca adhordhvataḥ || 110
31.111 . caturaṅgulamānena vaipulyāttu ṣaḍaṅgulā |
31.111 . ucchrāyāttu tataḥ kāryā gaṇḍikā tu svarūpataḥ || 111
31.112 . pīṭhordhve tu prakartavyaṃ śūlamūlaṃ tu suvrate |
31.112 . śūlāgramaṅgulaṃ kāryaṃ sutīkṣṇaṃ tu ṣaḍaṅgulam || 112
31.113 . arāmadhyaṃ prakartavyamarādhastu ṣaḍaṅgulam |
31.113 . caturaṅgulanimnaṃ tu madhyaṃ tu parikalpayet || 113
31.114 . pūrvāparaṃ tadeveha madhye śūlaṃ tu tadbahiḥ |
31.114 . kārayeta tribhiḥ sūtrairekaikaṃ vartayeta ca || 114
31.115 . kajatrayaṃ tu śūlāgraṃ vedāṃśairdvādaśāṅgulam |
31.115 . kramāddakṣānyamadhyeṣu tryaṣṭadvādaśapatrakam || 115
31.116 . cakratrayaṃ vātapuraṃ padmamaṣṭāṅgulārakam |
31.116 . vidyābhikhyaṃ śūlamūle rajaḥ paścātprapātayet || 116
31.117 . triśūlaṃ daṇḍaparyantaṃ rājavartena pūrayet |
31.117 . sūtratrayasya pṛṣṭhe tu śuklaṃ cārātrayaṃ bhavet || 117
31.118 . śuklena rajasā śūlamūle vidyāmbujaṃ bhavet |
31.118 . raktaṃ raktāsitaṃ śuklaṃ kramādūrdhvāmbujatrayam || 118
31.119 . śuklena vyomarekhā syātsā sthaulyādaṅgulaṃ bahiḥ |
31.119 . tāṃ tyaktvā vedikā kāryā hastamātraṃ pramāṇātaḥ || 119
31.120 . vaipulyatriguṇaṃ dairghyātprākāraṃ caturaśrakam |
31.120 . samantato'tha dikṣu syurdvārāṇi karamātrataḥ || 120
31.121 . tridhā vibhajya kramaśo dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ |
31.121 . kaṇṭhaṃ kapolaṃ śobhāṃ tu upaśobhāṃ tadantataḥ || 121
31.122 . prākāraṃ caturaśraṃ tu sabhūrekhāsamanvitam |
31.122 . sitaraktapītakṛṣṇai rajobhiḥ kārayettataḥ || 122
31.123 . raktai rajobhirmadhyaṃ tu yathāśobhaṃ tu pūrayet |
31.123 . asyā vyāptau purā coktaṃ tatraivānusarecca tat || 123
31.124 . arātrayavibhāgastu praveśo nirgamo bhramaḥ |
31.124 . anāhatapadavyāptiḥ kuṇḍalyā udayaḥ paraḥ || 124
31.125 . hṛdi sthāne gatā devyastriśūlasya sumadhyame |
31.125 . nābhisthaḥ śūladaṇḍastu śūlamūlaṃ hṛdi sthitam || 125
31.126 . śaktisthānagataṃ prāntaṃ prānte cakratrayaṃ smaret |
31.126 . utkṣipyotkṣipya kalayā dehamadhyasvarūpataḥ || 126
31.127 . śūladaṇḍāntamadhyasthaśūlamadhyāntagocaram |
31.127 . praviśenmūlamadhyāntaṃ prāntānte śaktiveśmani || 127
31.128 . aspandakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ekadā spandavartanam |
31.128 . mūlamānandamāpīḍya śaktitrayapadaṃ viśet || 128
31.129 . tatra pūjyaṃ prayatnena jāyante sarvasiddhayaḥ |
31.129 . samastādhvasamāyogātṣoḍhādhvavyāptibhāvataḥ || 129
31.130 . samastamantracakrādyairevamādiprayatnataḥ |
31.130 . ṣaṭtriṃśattattvaracitaṃ triśūlaṃ paribhāvayet || 130
31.131 . viṣuvatsthena vinyāso mantrāṇāṃ maṇḍalottame |
31.131 . kāryo'smin pūjite yatra sarveśvarapadaṃ bhajet || 131
31.132 . svastikenātha kartavyaṃ yuktaṃ tasyocyate vidhiḥ |
31.132 . nāḍikāḥ sthāpayetpūrvaṃ muhūrtaṃ parimāṇataḥ || 132
31.133 . śakravāruṇadiksthāśca yāmyasaumyagatāstathā |
31.133 . ekonatriṃśadvaṃśāḥ syurṛjutiryaggatāstathā || 133
31.134 . aṣṭau marmaśatānyekacatvāriṃśacca jāyate |
31.134 . vaṃśairviṣayasaṃkhyaiśca padmaṃ yugmendumaṇḍalam || 134
31.135 . rasasaṃkhyairbhavetpīṭhaṃ svastikaṃ sarvakāmadam |
31.135 . vasusaṃkhyairdvāravīthāvevaṃ bhāgaparikramaḥ || 135
31.136 . randhravipraśarāgnīṃśca lupyedbāhyāntaraṃ kramāt |
31.136 . marmāṇi ca caturdikṣu madhyāddvāreṣu sundari || 136
31.137 . vahnibhūtamunivyomabāhyagarbhe purīṣu ca |
31.137 . lopayeccaiva marmāṇi antarnāḍivivarjitān || 137
31.138 . dvāraprākārakoṇeṣu netrānalaśarānṛtūn |
31.138 . nāḍayo brahmavaṃśasya lopyā netrādrasasthitāḥ || 138
31.139 . vahnernetrānalau lopyau vedānnetrayugaṃ rasāt |
31.139 . netraṃ saumyagataṃ lopyaṃ pūrvādvedānalau rasāt || 139
31.140 . lokasthā nāḍikā hitvā netrādvedāgnayaḥ kramāt |
31.140 . śarairvahnigataṃ caiva yugaṃ netrāgnayo rasāt || 140
31.141 . netrātpūrvagatāccaiva sumerurdvārasaṃjñitaḥ |
31.141 . svastikā ca purī ramyā caturdikṣu sthitāvubhau || 141
31.142 . marmaṇāṃ ca śate dve ca ṛṣibhirguṇitā diśaḥ |
31.142 . netrādikāṃśca saṃmārjya mārgamadhyātsuśobhane || 142
31.143 . ṛṣitrayakṛte madhye viṣayaiḥ karṇikā bhavet |
31.143 . netrīkṛtānvasūn patraṃ netraṃ sakṛdvibhājitam || 143
31.144 . vahniṃ vasugataṃ kṛtvā śaśāṅkasthāṃśca lopayet |
31.144 . vahnīṣuṛṣimadhyācca lopyaṃ pīṭhendukāvadhi || 144
31.145 . brahmaṇo netraviṣayānnetrādvedānalau haret |
31.145 . sāgare netrakaṃ lopyaṃ nāḍayaḥ pūrvadiggatāḥ || 145
31.146 . bhūtanetragatānmūrdhnā netrāddvivahnidṛktrikāt |
31.146 . saumyagātpīṭhakoṇeṣu lopayeta caturṣvapi || 146
31.147 . dalāni kāryāṇi sitaiḥ kesaraṃ raktapītalaiḥ |
31.147 . karṇikā kanakaprakhyā pallavāntāśca lohitāḥ || 147
31.148 . vyomarekhā tu susitā vartulābjāntanīlabhāḥ |
31.148 . pīṭhaṃ rekhātrayopetaṃ sitalohitapītalam || 148
31.149 . svastikāśca caturvarṇā agnerīśānagocarāḥ |
31.149 . vīthī vidrumasaṃkāśā svadikṣvastrāṇi bāhyataḥ || 149
31.150 . indranīlanibhaṃ vajraṃ śaktiṃ padmamaṇiprabhām |
31.150 . daṇḍaṃ hāṭakasaṃkāśaṃ vaktraṃ tasyātilohitam || 150
31.151 . nīladyutisamaṃ khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ vatsakasaprabham |
31.151 . dhvajaṃ puṣpaphalopetaṃ pañcaraṅgaiśca śobhitam || 151
31.152 . gadā hemanibhātyugrā nānāratnavibhūṣitā |
31.152 . śūlaṃ nīlāmbujasamaṃ jvaladvahnyugraśekharam || 152
31.153 . tasyopari sitaṃ padmamīṣatpītāruṇaprabham |
31.153 . cakraṃ hemanibhaṃ dīptamarā vaiḍūryasaṃnibhāḥ || 153
31.154 . arāmadhyaṃ supītaṃ ca bāhyaṃ jvālāruṇaṃ bhavet |
31.154 . mandiraṃ devadevasya sarvakāmaphalapradam || 154
31.155 . śrīsiddhāyāṃ śūlavidhiḥ prākkṣetre caturaśrite |
31.155 . hastamātraṃ tridhā sūryānnavakhaṇḍaṃ yathā bhavet || 155
31.156 . madhye śūlaṃ ca tatretthaṃ madhyabhāgaṃ tridhā bhajet |
31.156 . navabhiḥ koṣṭhakairyuktaṃ tato'yaṃ vidhirucyate || 156
31.157 . madhyabhāgatrayaṃ tyaktvā madhye bhāgadvayasya tu |
31.157 . adhastādbhrāmayetsūtraṃ śaśāṅkaśakalākṛti || 157
31.158 . ubhayato bhrāmayettatra yathāgre hākṛtirbhavet |
31.158 . koṭyāṃ tatra kṛtaṃ sūtraṃ nayedrekhāṃ tu pūrvikām || 158
31.159 . aparadvārapūrveṇa tyaktvāṅgulacatuṣṭayam |
31.159 . rekhāṃ vināśayetprājño yathā śūlākṛtirbhavet || 159
31.160 . śūlāgre tvardhahastena tyaktvā padmāni kārayet |
31.160 . adhaḥ śṛṅgatrayaṃ hastamadhye padmaṃ sakarṇikam || 160
31.161 . mukhāgre dhārayetsūtraṃ tribhirhastaistu pātayet |
31.161 . madhye cordhvaṃ tataḥ kuryādadhastādaṅguladvayam || 161
31.162 . rekhādvayaṃ pātayeta yathā śūlaṃ bhavatyapi |
31.162 . adhobhāgādibhiścordhvaṃ tatra rekhā prapadyate || 162
31.163 . samīkṛtya tataḥ sūtre ūrdhve dve evameva tu |
31.163 . madhyaṃ padmaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ śūlādhastādyaśasvini || 163
31.164 . ityeṣa maṇḍalavidhiḥ kathitaḥ saṃkṣepayogato mahāgurubhiḥ |

c32 atha śrītantrāloke dvātriṃśamāhnikam

32.1 . atha kathaye mudrāṇāṃ gurvāgamagītamatra vidhim | 0
32.1 . mudrā ca pratibimbātmā śrīmaddevyākhyayāmale |
32.1 . uktā bimbodayaśrutyā vācyadvayavivecanāt || 1
32.2 . bimbātsamudayo yasyā ityuktā pratibimbatā |
32.2 . vimbasya yasyā udaya ityuktā tadupāyatā || 2
32.3 . mudaṃ svarūpalābhākhyaṃ dehadvāreṇa cātmanām |
32.3 . rātyarpayati yattena mudrā śāstreṣu varṇitā || 3
32.4 . tatra pradhānabhūtā śrīkhecarī devatātmikā |
32.4 . niṣkalatvena vikhyātā sākalyena triśūlinī || 4
32.5 . karaṅkiṇī krodhanā ca bhairavī lelihānikā |
32.5 . mahāpretā yogamudrā jvālinī kṣobhiṇī dhruvā || 5
32.6 . ityevaṃbahubhedeyaṃ śrīkhecaryeva gīyate |
32.6 . anyāstadaṅgabhūtāstu padmādyā mālinīmate || 6
32.7 . tāsāṃ bahutvāmukhyatvayogābhyāṃ neha varṇanam |
32.7 . śrīkhecarīsamāviṣṭo yadyatsthānaṃ samāśrayet || 7
32.8 . devīsaṃnidhaye tatsyādalaṃ kiṃ ḍambarairvṛthā |
32.8 . kāmye karmaṇi tāśca syurmukhyāḥ kasyāpi jātucit || 8
32.9 . mudrā caturvidhā kāyakaravākcittabhedataḥ || 9
32.10 . tatra pūrṇena rūpeṇa khecarīmeva varṇaye |
32.10 . baddhvā padmāsanaṃ yogī nābhāvakṣeśvaraṃ kṣipet || 10
32.11 . daṇḍākāraṃ tu taṃ tāvannayedyāvatkakhatrayam |
32.11 . nigṛhya tatra tattūrṇaṃ prerayetkhatrayeṇa tu || 11
32.12 . etāṃ baddhvā khe gatiḥ syāditi śrīpūrvaśāsane |
32.12 . dhvanijyotirmarudyuktaṃ cittaṃ viśramya copari || 12
32.13 . anenābhyāsayogena śivaṃ bhittvā paraṃ vrajet |
32.13 . jatrvadhastātkarau kṛtvā vāmapādaṃ ca dakṣiṇe || 13
32.14 . vidāryāsyaṃ kaniṣṭhābhyāṃ madhyamābhyāṃ tu nāsikām |
32.14 . anāme kuñcayetprājño bhrūbhaṅgaṃ tarjanīdvayam || 14
32.15 . jihvāṃ ca cālayenmantrī hāhākāraṃ ca kārayet |
32.15 . triśūlena prayogeṇa brahmarandhramupasthitaḥ || 15
32.16 . padaṃ santyajya tanmātraṃ sadyastyajati medinīm |
32.16 . śūnyāśūnyalaye kṛtvā ekadaṇḍe'nilānalau || 16
32.17 . śaktitritayasambaddhe adhiṣṭhātṛtridaivate |
32.17 . triśūlaṃ tadvijānīyādyena vyomotpatedbudhaḥ || 17
32.18 . ākāśabhāvaṃ santyajya sattāmātramupasthitaḥ |
32.18 . śūlaṃ samarasaṃ kṛtvā rase rasa iva sthitaḥ || 18
32.19 . ekadaṇḍaṃ sa vijñāya triśūlaṃ khacaraṃ priye |
32.19 . baddhvā tu khecarīṃ mudrāṃ dhyātvātmānaṃ ca bhairavam || 19
32.20 . khecarīcakrasaṃjuṣṭaṃ sadyastyajati medinīm |
32.20 . tyaktāṃśako nirācāro niḥśaṅko lokavarjitaḥ || 20
32.21 . avadhūto nirācāro nāhamasmīti bhāvayam |
32.21 . mantraikaniṣṭhaḥ saṃpaśyan dehasthāḥ sarvadevatāḥ || 21
32.22 . hlādodvegāsmitākruṣṭanidrāmaithunamatsare |
32.22 . rūpādau vā kartṛkarmakaraṇeṣu ca sarvaśaḥ || 22
32.23 . nāhamasmīti manvāna ekībhūtaṃ vicintayan |
32.23 . karṇākṣimukhanāsādicakrasthaṃ devatāgaṇam || 23
32.24 . grahītāraṃ sadā paśyan khecaryā siddhyati sphuṭam |
32.24 . vidyāśaṅkī malāśaṅkī śāstraśaṅkī na siddhyati || 24
32.25 . śivo raviḥ śivo vahniḥ paktṛtvātsa purohitaḥ |
32.25 . tatrasthā devatāḥ sarvā dyotayantyo'khilaṃ jagat || 25
32.26 . kaniṣṭhayā vidāryāsyaṃ tarjanībhyāṃ bhruvau tathā |
32.26 . anāme madhyame vaktre jihvayā tālukaṃ spṛśet || 26
32.27 . eṣā karaṅkiṇī devī jvālinīṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam |
32.27 . hanurlalāṭagau hastau prasāryāṅgulitaḥ sphuṭau || 27
32.28 . cālayedvāyuvegena kṛtvāntarbhrukuṭīṃ budhaḥ |
32.28 . vidāryāsyaṃ sajihvaṃ ca hāhākāraṃ tu kārayet || 28
32.29 . eṣā jvālinyagnicakre tayā cāṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
32.29 . japedyadi tataḥ siddhyettrailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 29
32.30 . paradeheṣu cātmānaṃ paraṃ cātmaśarīrataḥ |
32.30 . paśyeccarantaṃ hānādādgamāgamapadasthitam || 30
32.31 . navacchidragataṃ caikaṃ nadantaṃ vyāpakaṃ dhruvam |
32.31 . anayā hi khacārī śrīyogasañcāra ucyate || 31
32.32 . kulakuṇḍalikāṃ baddhvā aṇorantaravedinīm |
32.32 . vāmo yo'yaṃ jagatyasmiṃstasya saṃharaṇodyatām || 32
32.33 . svasthāne nirvṛtiṃ labdhvā jñānāmṛtarasātmakam |
32.33 . vrajetkandapadaṃ madhye rāvaṃ kṛtvā hyarāvakam || 33
32.34 . yāvajjīvaṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ piṇḍādhāraṃ ca kāmikam |
32.34 . tatra tāṃ bodhayitvā tu gatiṃ buddhvā kramāgatām || 34
32.35 . cakrobhayanibaddhāṃ tu śākhāprāntāvalambinīm |
32.35 . mūlasthānādyathā devi tamogranthiṃ vidārayet || 35
32.36 . vajrākhyāṃ jñānajenaiva tathā śākhobhayāntataḥ |
32.36 . koṇamadhyaviniṣkrāntaṃ liṅgamūlaṃ vibhedayet || 36
32.37 . tatra saṅghaṭṭitaṃ cakrayugmamaikyena bhāsate |
32.37 . vaiparītyāttu nikṣipya dvidhābhāvaṃ vrajatyataḥ || 37
32.38 . ūrvādyaṅguṣṭhakālāgniparyante sā vinikṣipet |
32.38 . gamāgamanasañcāre caretsā liṅgaliṅginī || 38
32.39 . tatra tatpadasaṃyogādunmīlanavidhāyinī |
32.39 . yo jānāti sa siddhyettu rasādānavisargayoḥ || 39
32.40 . sasaṅgamamidaṃ sthānamūrmiṇyunmīlanaṃ param |
32.40 . eṣa kramastato'nyo'pi vyutkramaḥ khecarī parā || 40
32.41 . yonyādhāreti vikhyātā śūlamūleti śabdyate |
32.41 . varṇāstatra layaṃ yānti hyavarṇe varṇarūpiṇi || 41
32.42 . nādiphāntaṃ samuccārya kauleśaṃ dehasaṃnibham |
32.42 . ākramya prathamaṃ cakraṃ khe yantre pādapīḍitam || 42
32.43 . nādaṃ vai śaktisadgarbhaṃ sadgarbhātkaulinīpadam |
32.43 . bījapañcakacāreṇa śūlabhedakrameṇa tu || 43
32.44 . hṛcchūlagranthibhedaiścidrudraśaktiṃ prabodhayet |
32.44 . vāyucakrāntanilayaṃ bindvākhyaṃ nābhimaṇḍalam || 44
32.45 . āgacchellambikāsthānaṃ sūtradvādaśanirgatam |
32.45 . candracakravilomena praviśedbhūtapañjare || 45
32.46 . bhūyastu kurute līlāṃ māyāpañjaravartinīm |
32.46 . punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ saṃhṛtiśca khecaryā kriyate budhaiḥ || 46
32.47 . śrīmadvīrāvalīyoga eṣa syātkhecarīvidhiḥ |
32.47 . cumbākāreṇa vaktreṇa yattattvaṃ śrūyate param || 47
32.48 . grasamānamidaṃ viśvaṃ candrārkapuṭasaṃpuṭe |
32.48 . tenaiva syātkhagāmīti śrīmatkāmika ucyate || 48
32.49 . bhavānmuktvā drāvayanti pāśānmudrā hi śaktayaḥ |
32.49 . mukhyāsāṃ khecarī sā ca tridhoccāreṇa vācikī || 49
32.50 . triśiromudgaro devi kāyikī paripaṭhyate |
32.50 . nāsāṃ netradvayaṃ cāpi hṛtstanadvayameva ca || 50
32.51 . vṛṣaṇadvayaliṅgaṃ ca prāpya kāyaṃ gatā tviyam |
32.51 . bhavasthānābhavasthānamuccāreṇāvadhārayet || 51
32.52 . mānasīyamitastvanyāḥ padmādyā aṣṭa mudrikāḥ |
32.52 . mātṛvyūhakule tāḥ syurasyāstu parivāragāḥ || 52
32.53 . śarīraṃ tu samastaṃ yatkūṭākṣarasamākṛti |
32.53 . eṣā mudrā mahāmudrā bhairavasyeti gahvare || 53
32.54 . sūpaviṣṭaḥ padmake tu hastāgrāṅguliraśmibhiḥ |
32.54 . parāṅmukhairjhaṭityudyadraśmibhiḥ pṛṣṭhasaṃsthitaiḥ || 54
32.55 . antaḥsthitiḥ khecarīyaṃ saṃkocākhyā śaśāṅkinī |
32.55 . tasmādeva samuttambya bāhū caivāvakuñcitau || 55
32.56 . samyagvyomasu saṃsthānādvyomākhyā khecarī matā |
32.56 . muṣṭidvitayasaṅghaṭṭāddhṛdi sā hṛdayāhvāyā || 56
32.57 . śāntākhyā sā hastayugmamūrdhvādhaḥ sthitamudgatam |
32.57 . samadṛṣṭyāvalokyaṃ ca bahiryojitapāṇikam || 57
32.58 . eṣaiva śaktimudrā cedadhodhāvitapāṇikā |
32.58 . daśānāmaṅgulīnāṃ tu muṣṭibandhādanantaram || 58
32.59 . drākkṣepātkhecarī devī pañcakuṇḍalinī matā |
32.59 . saṃhāramudrā caiṣaiva yadyūrdhvaṃ kṣipyate kila || 59
32.60 . utkrāmaṇī jhagityeva paśūnāṃ pāśakartarī |
32.60 . śvabhre sudūre jhaṭiti svātmānaṃ pātayanniva || 60
32.61 . sāhasānupraveśena kuñcitaṃ hastayugmakam |
32.61 . adhovīkṣaṇaśīlaṃ ca samyagdṛṣṭisamanvitam || 61
32.62 . vīrabhairavasaṃjñeyaṃ khecarī bodhavardhinī |
32.62 . aṣṭadhetthaṃ varṇitā śrībhargāṣṭakaśikhākule || 62
32.63 . evaṃ nānāvidhānbhedānāśrityaikaiva yā sthitā |
32.63 . śrīkhecarī tayāviṣṭaḥ paraṃ bījaṃ prapadyate || 63
32.64 . ekaṃ sṛṣṭimayaṃ bījaṃ yadvīryaṃ sarvamantragam |
32.64 . ekā mudrā khecarī ca mudraughaḥ prāṇito yayā || 64
32.65 . tadevaṃ khecarīcakrarūḍhau yadrūpamullaset |
32.65 . tadeva mudrā mantavyā śeṣaḥ syāddehavikriyā || 65
32.66 . yāgādau tanmadhye tadavasitau jñānayogaparimarśe |
32.66 . vighnapraśame pāśacchede mudrāvidheḥ samayaḥ || 66
32.67 . bodhāveśaḥ sannidhiraikyena visarjanaṃ svarūpagatiḥ |
32.67 . śaṅkādalanaṃ cakrodayadīptiriti kramātkṛtyam || 67
32.68 . iti mudrāvidhiḥ proktaḥ sugūḍho yaḥ phalapradaḥ |

c33 atha śrītantrāloke trayastriṃśamāhnikam

33.1 . athāvasarasaṃprāpta ekīkāro nigadyate | 0
33.1 . yaduktaṃ cakrabhedena sārdhaṃ pūjyamiti trikam |
33.1 . tatraiṣa cakrabhedānāmekīkāro diśānayā || 1
33.2 . viśvā tadīśā hāraudrī vīranetryambikā tathā |
33.2 . gurvīti ṣaḍare devyaḥ śrīsiddhāvīradarśitāḥ || 2
33.3 . māheśī brāhmaṇī skāndī vaiṣṇavyaindrī yamātmikā |
33.3 . cāmuṇḍā caiva yogīśītyaṣṭāghoryādayo'thavā || 3
33.4 . agninirṛtivāyvīśamātṛbhirdvādaśānvitāḥ |
33.4 . nandā bhadrā jayā kālī karālī vikṛtānanā || 4
33.5 . kroṣṭukī bhīmamudrā ca vāyuvegā hayānanā |
33.5 . gambhīrā ghoṣaṇī ceti caturviṃśatyare vidhiḥ || 5
33.6 . siddhirvṛddhirdyutirlakṣmīrmedhā kāntiḥ sudhā dhṛtiḥ |
33.6 . dīptiḥ puṣṭirmatiḥ kīrtiḥ susthitiḥ sugatiḥ smṛtiḥ || 6
33.7 . suprabhā ṣoḍaśī ceti śrīkaṇṭhādikaśaktayaḥ |
33.7 . baliśca balinandaśca daśagrīvo haro hayaḥ || 7
33.8 . mādhavaḥ ṣaḍare cakre dvādaśāre tvamī smṛtāḥ |
33.8 . dakṣaścaṇḍo haraḥ śauṇḍī pramatho bhīmamanmathau || 8
33.9 . śakuniḥ sumatirnando gopālaśca pitāmahaḥ |
33.9 . śrīkaṇṭho'nantasūkṣmau ca trimūrtiḥ śaṃbareśvaraḥ || 9
33.10 . arghīśo bhārabhūtiśca sthitiḥ sthāṇurharastathā |
33.10 . jhaṇṭhibhautikasadyojānugrahakrūrasainikāḥ || 10
33.11 . dvyaṣṭau yadvāmṛtastena yuktāḥ pūrṇābhataddravāḥ |
33.11 . oghormisyandanāṅgāśca vapurudgāravaktrakāḥ || 11
33.12 . tanusecanamūrtīśāḥ sarvāmṛtadharo'paraḥ |
33.12 . śrīpāṭhācchaktayaścaitāḥ ṣoḍaśaiva prakīrtitāḥ || 12
33.13 . saṃvartalakulibhṛgusitabakakhaṅgipinākibhujagabalikālāḥ |
33.13 . dviśchagalāṇḍau śikhiśoṇameṣamīnatridaṇḍi sāṣāḍhi || 13
33.14 . devīkāntatadardhau dārukahalisomanāthaśarmāṇaḥ |
33.14 . jayavijayajayantājitasujayajayarudrakīrtanāvahakāḥ || 14
33.15 . tanmūrtyutsāhadavardhanāśca balasubalabhadradāvahakāḥ |
33.15 . tadvāndātā ceśo nandanasamabhadratanmūrtiḥ || 15
33.16 . śivadasumanaḥspṛhaṇakā durgo bhadrākhyakālaśca |
33.16 . ceto'nugakauśikakālaviśvasuśivāstathāparaḥ kopaḥ || 16
33.17 . śrutyagnyare syurete strīpāṭhācchaktayastvetāḥ |
33.17 . juṃkāro'thāgnipatnīti ṣaḍare ṣaṇṭhavarjitāḥ || 17
33.18 . dvādaśāre tatsahitāḥ ṣoḍaśāre svarāḥ kramāt |
33.18 . halastaddviguṇe'ṣṭāre yādyaṃ hāntaṃ tu tattrike || 18
33.19 . dvātriṃśadarake sāntaṃ binduḥ sarveṣu mūrdhani |
33.19 . evamanyānbahūṃścakrabhedānasmātprakalpayet || 19
33.20 . eka eva cidātmaiṣa viśvāmarśanasārakaḥ |
33.20 . śaktistadvānato mātā śabdarāśiḥ prakīrtitau || 20
33.21 . tayoreva vibhāge tu śaktitadvatprakalpane |
33.21 . śabdarāśirmālinī ca kṣobhātma vapurīdṛśam || 21
33.22 . tathāntaḥsthaparāmarśabhedane vastutastrikam |
33.22 . anuttarecchonmeṣākhyaṃ yato viśvaṃ vimarśanam || 22
33.23 . ānandeśormiyoge tu tatṣaṭkaṃ samudāhṛtam |
33.23 . antaḥsthoṣmasamāyogāttadaṣṭakamudāhṛtam || 23
33.24 . tadāmṛtacatuṣkonabhāve dvādaśakaṃ bhavet |
33.24 . tadyoge ṣoḍaśākhyaṃ syādevaṃ yāvadasaṃkhyatā || 24
33.25 . viśvamekaparāmarśasahatvātprabhṛti sphuṭam |
33.25 . aṃśāṃśikāparāmarśān paryante sahate yataḥ || 25
33.26 . ataḥ pañcāśadaikātmyaṃ svaravyaktivirūpatā |
33.26 . vargāṣṭakaṃ varṇabheda ekāśītikalodayaḥ || 26
33.27 . iti pradarśitaṃ pūrvamardhamātrāsahatvataḥ |
33.27 . svarārdhamapyasti yataḥ svaritasyārdhamātrakam || 27
33.28 . tasyādita udāttaṃ tatkathitaṃ padavedinā |
33.28 . itthaṃ saṃvidiyaṃ yājyasvarūpāmarśarūpiṇī || 28
33.29 . abhinnaṃ saṃvidaścaitaccakrāṇāṃ cakravālakam |
33.29 . svāmyāvaraṇabhedena bahudhā tatprayojayet || 29
33.30 . parāparā parā cānyā sṛṣṭisthititirodhayaḥ |
33.30 . mātṛsadbhāvarūpā tu turyā viśrāntirucyate || 30
33.31 . tacca prakāśaṃ vaktrasthaṃ sūcitaṃ tu pade pade |
33.31 . turye viśrāntirādheyā mātṛsadbhāvasāriṇi || 31
33.32 . tathāsya viśvamābhāti svātmatanmayatāṃ gatam |
33.32 . ityeṣa śāstrārthasyokta ekīkāro gurūditaḥ || 32

c34 atha śrītantrāloke catustriṃśamāhnikam

34.1 . ucyate'tha svasvarūpapraveśaḥ kramasaṅgataḥ | 0
34.1 . yadetadbahudhā proktamāṇavaṃ śivatāptaye |
34.1 . tatrāntarantarāviśya viśrāmyetsavidhe pade || 1
34.2 . tato'pyāṇavasaṃtyāgācchāktīṃ bhūmimupāśrayet |
34.2 . tato'pi śāmbhavīmevaṃ tāratamyakramātsphuṭam || 2
34.3 . itthaṃ kramoditavibodhamahāmarīcisaṃpūritaprasarabhairavabhāvabhāgī |
34.3 . ante'bhyupāyanirapekṣatayaiva nityaṃ svātmānamāviśati garbhitaviśvarūpam || 3
34.4 . kathito'yaṃ svasvarūpapraveśaḥ parameṣṭhinā |

c35 atha śrītantrāloke pañcatriṃśamāhnikam

35.1 . athocyate samastānāṃ śāstrāṇāmiha melanam |
35.1 . iha tāvatsama sto'yaṃ vyavahāraḥ purātanaḥ || 1
35.2 . prasiddhimanusandhāya saiva cāgama ucyate |
35.2 . anvayavyatirekau hi prasiddherupajīvakau || 2
35.3 . svāyattatve tayorvyaktipūge kiṃ syāttayorgatiḥ |
35.3 . pratyakṣamapi netrātmadīpārthādiviśeṣajam || 3
35.4 . apekṣate tatra mūle prasiddhiṃ tāṃ tathātmikām |
35.4 . abhitaḥsaṃvṛte jāta ekākī kṣudhitaḥ śiśuḥ || 4
35.5 . kiṃ karotu kimādattāṃ kena paśyatu kiṃ vrajet |
35.5 . nanu vastuśatākīrṇe sthāne'pyasya yadeva hi || 5
35.6 . paśyato jighrato vāpi spṛśataḥ saṃprasīdati |
35.6 . cetastadevādāya drākso'nvayavyatirekabhāk || 6
35.7 . hanta cetaḥprasādo'pi yo'sāvarthaviśeṣagaḥ |
35.7 . so'pi prāgvāsanārūpavimarśaparikalpitaḥ || 7
35.8 . na pratyakṣānumānādibāhyamānaprasādajaḥ |
35.8 . prāgvāsanopajīvyetatpratibhāmātrameva na || 8
35.9 . na mṛdabhyavahārecchā puṃso bālasya jāyate |
35.9 . prāgvāsanopajīvī cedvimarśaḥ sā ca vāsanā || 9
35.10 . prācyā cedāgatā seyaṃ prasiddhiḥ paurvakālikī |
35.10 . naca cetaḥprasattyaiva sarvo vyavahṛtikramaḥ || 10
35.11 . mūlaṃ prasiddhistanmānaṃ sarvatraiveti gṛhyatām |
35.11 . pūrvapūrvopajīvitvamārgaṇe sā kvacitsvayam || 11
35.12 . sarvajñarūpe hyekasminniḥśaṅkaṃ bhāsata purā |
35.12 . vyavahāro hi naikatra samastaḥ ko'pi mātari || 12
35.13 . tenāsarvajñapūrvatvamātreṇaiṣā na siddhyati |
35.13 . bahusarvajñapūrvatve na mānaṃ cāsti kiṃcana || 13
35.14 . bhogāpavargataddhetuprasiddhiśataśobhitaḥ |
35.14 . tadvimarśasvabhāvo'sau bhairavaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 14
35.15 . tataścāṃśāṃśikāyogātsā prasiddhiḥ paramparām |
35.15 . śāstraṃ vāśritya vitatā lokānsaṃvyavahārayet || 15
35.16 . tayaivāśaiśavātsarve vyavahāradharājuṣaḥ |
35.16 . santaḥ samupajīvanti śaivamevādyamāgamam || 16
35.17 . apūrṇāstu pare tena na mokṣaphalabhāginaḥ |
35.17 . upajīvanti yāvattu tāvattatphalabhāginaḥ || 17
35.18 . bālyāpāye'pi yadbhoktumannameṣa pravartate |
35.18 . tatprasiddhyaiva nādhyakṣānnānumānādasambhavāt || 18
35.19 . naca kāpyatra doṣāśāśaṅkāyāśca nivṛttitaḥ |
35.19 . prasiddhiścāvigānotthā pratītiḥ śabdanātmikā || 19
35.20 . mātuḥ svabhāvo yattasyāṃ śaṅkate naiṣa jātucit |
35.20 . svakṛtatvavaśādeva sarvavitsa hi śaṅkaraḥ || 20
35.21 . yāvattu śivatā nāsya tāvatsvātmānusāriṇīm |
35.21 . tāvatīmeva tāmeṣa prasiddhiṃ nābhiśaṅkate || 21
35.22 . anyasyāmabhiśaṅkī syādbhūyastāṃ bahu manyate |
35.22 . evaṃ bhāviśivatvo'mūṃ prasiddhiṃ manyate dhruvam || 22
35.23 . eka evāgamaścāyaṃ vibhunā sarvadarśinā |
35.23 . darśito yaḥ pravṛtte ca nivṛtte ca pathi sthitaḥ || 23
35.24 . dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu pūrṇāpūrṇādibhedataḥ |
35.24 . vicitreṣu phaleṣveka upāyaḥ śāmbhavāgamaḥ || 24
35.25 . tasminviṣayavaiviktyādvicitraphaladāyini |
35.25 . citropāyopadeśo'pi na virodhāvaho bhavet || 25
35.26 . laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ sāṅkhyaṃ yogādi pāñcarātrakam |
35.26 . bauddhārhatanyāyaśāstraṃ padārthakramatantraṇam || 26
35.27 . siddhāntatantraśāktādi sarvaṃ brahmodbhavaṃ yataḥ |
35.27 . śrīsvacchandādiṣu proktaṃ sadyojātādibhedataḥ || 27
35.28 . yathaikatrāpi vedādau tattadāśramagāminaḥ |
35.28 . saṃskārāntaramatrāpi tathā liṅgoddhṛtādikam || 28
35.29 . yathāca tatra pūrvasminnāśrame nottarāśramāt |
35.29 . phalameti tathā pāñcarātrādau na śivātmatām || 29
35.30 . eka evāgamastasmāttatra laukikaśāstrataḥ |
35.30 . prabhṛtyāvaiṣṇavādbauddhācchaivātsarvaṃ hi niṣṭhitam || 30
35.31 . tasya yattatparaṃ prāpyaṃ dhāma tattrikaśabditam |
35.31 . sarvāvibhedānucchedāttadeva kulamucyate || 31
35.32 . yathordhvādharatābhāksu dehāṅgeṣu vibhediṣu |
35.32 . ekaṃ prāṇitamevaṃ syāttrikaṃ sarveṣu śāstrataḥ || 32
35.33 . śrīmatkālīkule coktaṃ pañcasrotovivarjitam |
35.33 . daśāṣṭādaśabhedasya sārametatprakīrtitam || 33
35.34 . puṣpe gandhastile tailaṃ dehe jīvo jale'mṛtam |
35.34 . yathā tathaiva śāstrāṇāṃ kulamantaḥ pratiṣṭhitam || 34
35.35 . tadeka evāgamo'yaṃ citraścitre'dhikāriṇi |
35.35 . tathaiva sā prasiddhirhi svayūthyaparayūthyagā || 35
35.36 . sāṃkhyaṃ yogaṃ pāñcarātraṃ vedāṃścaiva na nindayet |
35.36 . yataḥ śivodbhavāḥ sarva iti svacchandaśāsane || 36
35.37 . ekasmādāgamāccaite khaṇḍakhaṇḍā vyapoddhṛtāḥ |
35.37 . loke syurāgamāstaiśca jano bhrāmyati mohitaḥ || 37
35.38 . anekāgamapakṣe'pi vācyā viṣayabheditā |
35.38 . avaśyamūrdhvādharatāsthityā prāmāṇyasiddhaye || 38
35.39 . anyathā naiva kasyāpi prāmāṇyaṃ siddhyati dhruvam |
35.39 . nityatvamavisaṃvāda iti no mānakāraṇam || 39
35.40 . asminnaṃśe'pyamuṣyaiva prāmāṇyaṃ syāttathoditeḥ |
35.40 . anyathāvyākṛtau kḷptāvasatyatve prarocane || 40
35.41 . atiprasaṅga sarvasyāpyāgamasyāpabādhakaḥ |
35.41 . avaśyopetya ityasminmāna āgamanāmani || 41
35.42 . avaśyopetyamevaitacchāstraniṣṭhānirūpaṇam |
35.42 . pradhāne'ṅge kṛto yatnaḥ phalavānvastuto yataḥ || 42
35.43 . ato'smin yatnavān ko'pi bhavecchaṃbhupracoditaḥ |
35.43 . tatra tatra ca śāstreṣu nyarūpyata maheśinā || 43
35.44 . etāvatyadhikārī yaḥ sa durlabha iti sphuṭam |
35.44 . itthaṃ śrīśambhunāthena mamoktaṃ śāstramelanam || 44

c36 atha śrītantrāloke ṣaṭtriṃśamāhnikam

36.1 . āyātiratha śāstrasya kathyate'vasarāgatā |0
36.1 . śrīsiddhādivinirdiṣṭā gurubhiśca nirūpitā |
36.1 . bhairavo bhairavī devī svacchando lākulo'ṇurāṭ || 1
36.2 . gahaneśo'bjajaḥ śakro guruḥ koṭyapakarṣataḥ |
36.2 . navabhiḥ kramaśo'dhītaṃ navakoṭipravistaram || 2
36.3 . etaistato guruḥ koṭimātrātpādaṃ vitīrṇavān |
36.3 . dakṣādibhya ubhau pādau saṃvartādibhya eva ca || 3
36.4 . pādaṃ ca vāmanādibhyaḥ pādārdhaṃ bhārgavāya ca |
36.4 . pādapādaṃ tu balaye pādapādastu yo'paraḥ || 4
36.5 . siṃhāyārdhaṃ tataḥ śiṣṭāddvau bhāgau vinatābhuve |
36.5 . pādaṃ vāsukināgāya khaṇḍāḥ saptadaśa tvamī || 5
36.6 . svargādardhaṃ rāvaṇo'tha jahre rāmo'rdhamapyataḥ |
36.6 . vibhīṣaṇamukhādāpa guruśiṣyavidhikramāt || 6
36.7 . khaṇḍairekānnaviṃśatyā vibhaktaṃ tadabhūttataḥ |
36.7 . khaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍaṃ cāṣṭakhaṇḍaṃ proktapādādibhedataḥ || 7
36.8 . pādo mūloddhārāvuttaravṛhaduttare tathā kalpaḥ |
36.8 . sāṃhitakalpaskandāvanuttaraṃ vyāpakaṃ tridhā tisraḥ || 8
36.9 . devyo'tra nirūpyante kramaśo vistāriṇaiva rūpeṇa |
36.9 . navame pade tu gaṇanā na kāciduktā vyavacchidāhīne || 9
36.10 . rāmācca lakṣmaṇastasmātsiddhāstebhyo'pi dānavāḥ |
36.10 . guhyakāśca tatastebhyo yogino nṛvarāstataḥ || 10
36.11 . teṣāṃ krameṇa tanmadhye bhraṣṭaṃ kālāntarādyadā |
36.11 . tadā śrīkaṇṭhanāthājñāvaśātsiddhā avātaram || 11
36.12 . tryambakāmardakābhikhyaśrīnāthā advaye dvaye |
36.12 . dvayādvaye ca nipuṇāḥ krameṇa śivaśāsane || 12
36.13 . ādyasya cānvayo jajñe dvitīyo duhitṛkramāt |
36.13 . sa cārdhatryambakābhikhyaḥ saṃtānaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ || 13
36.14 . ataścārdhacatasro'tra maṭhikāḥ saṃtatikramāt |
36.14 . śiṣyapraśiṣyairvistīrṇāḥ śataśākhaṃ vyavasthitaiḥ || 14
36.15 . adhyuṣṭasaṃtatisrotaḥsārabhūtarasāhṛtim |
36.15 . vidhāya tantrāloko'yaṃ syandate sakalānrasān || 15
36.16 . uktāyātirupādeyabhāvo nirṇīyate'dhunā |

c37 atha śrītantrāloke saptatriṃśamāhnikam

37.1 . uktanītyaiva sarvatra vyavahāre pravartite |
37.1 . prasiddhāvupajīvyāyāmavaśyagrāhya āgamaḥ || 1
37.2 . yathā laukikadṛṣṭyānyaphalabhāktatprasiddhitaḥ |
37.2 . samyagvyavaharaṃstadvacchivabhāktatprasiddhitaḥ || 2
37.3 . tadavaśyagrahītavye śāstre svāṃśopadeśini |
37.3 . manākphale'bhyupādeyatamaṃ tadviparītakam || 3
37.4 . yathā khageśvarībhāvaniḥśaṅkatvādviṣaṃ vrajet |
37.4 . kṣayaṃ karmasthitistadvadaśaṅkādbhairavatvataḥ || 4
37.5 . yadārṣe pātahetūktaṃ tadasminvāmaśāsane |
37.5 . āśusiddhyai yataḥ sarvamārṣaṃ māyodarasthitam || 5
37.6 . tacca yatsarvasarvajñadṛṣṭaṃ taccāpi kiṃ bhavet |
37.6 . yadaśeṣopadeśena sūyate'nuttaraṃ phalam || 6
37.7 . yathādharādharaproktavastutattvānuvādataḥ |
37.7 . uttaraṃ kathitaṃ saṃvitsiddhaṃ taddhi tathā bhavet || 7
37.8 . yaduktādhikasaṃvittisiddhavastunirūpaṇāt |
37.8 . apūrṇasarvavitproktirjñāyate'dharaśāsane || 8
37.9 . ūrdhvaśāsanavastvaṃśe dṛṣṭvāpica samujjhite |
37.9 . adhaḥ śāstreṣu māyātvaṃ lakṣyate sargarakṣaṇāt || 9
37.10 . śrīmadānandaśāstrādau proktaṃ ca parameśinā |
37.10 . ṛṣivākyaṃ bahukleśamadhruvālpaphalaṃ mitam || 10
37.11 . naiva pramāṇayedvidvān śaivamevāgamaṃ śrayet |
37.11 . tadārṣe pātahetūktaṃ tadasmin vāmaśāsane || 11
37.12 . āśusiddhyai yataḥ sarvamārṣaṃ māyodarasthitam |
37.12 . yathā khageśvarībhāvaniḥśaṅkatvādviṣaṃ vrajet || 12
37.13 . kṣayaṃ karmasthitistadvadaśaṅkādbhairavatvataḥ |
37.13 . ajñatvānupadeṣṭṛtvasaṃdaṣṭe'dharaśāsane || 13
37.14 . etadviparyayādgrāhyamavaśyaṃ śivaśāsanam |
37.14 . dvāvāptau tatra ca śrīmacchrīkaṇṭhalakuleśvarau || 14
37.15 . dvipravāhamidaṃ śāstraṃ mamyaṅniḥśreyasapradam |
37.15 . prācyasya tu yathābhīṣṭabhogadatvamapi sthitam || 15
37.16 . tacca pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ śaktivaicitryacitritam |
37.16 . pañcasrota iti proktaṃ śrīmacchrīkaṇṭhaśāsanam || 16
37.17 . daśāṣṭādaśadhā srotaḥpañcakaṃ yattato'pyalam |
37.17 . utkṛṣṭaṃ bhairavābhikhyaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭivibheditam || 17
37.18 . śrīmadānandaśāstrādau proktaṃ bhagavatā kila |
37.18 . samūhaḥ pīṭhametacca dvidhā dakṣiṇavāmataḥ || 18
37.19 . mantro vidyeti tasmācca mudrāmaṇḍalagaṃ dvayam |
37.19 . mananatrāṇadaṃ yattu mantrākhyaṃ tatra vidyayā || 19
37.20 . upodbalanamāpyāyaḥ sā hi vedyārthabhāsinī |
37.20 . mantrapratikṛtirmudrā tadāpyāyanakārakam || 20
37.21 . maṇḍalaṃ sāramuktaṃ hi maṇḍaśrutyā śivāhvayam |
37.21 . evamanyonyasaṃbhedavṛtti pīṭhacatuṣṭayam || 21
37.22 . yatastasmādbhavetsarvaṃ pīṭhe pīṭhe'pi vastutaḥ |
37.22 . pradhānatvāttasya tasya vastuno bhinnatā punaḥ || 22
37.23 . kathitā sādhakendrāṇāṃ tattadvastuprasiddhaye |
37.23 . pratyekaṃ taccaturdhaivaṃ maṇḍalaṃ mudrikā tathā || 23
37.24 . mantro vidyeti ca pīṭhamutkṛṣṭaṃ cottarottam |
37.24 . vidyāpīṭhapradhānaṃ ca siddhayogīśvarīmatam || 24
37.25 . tasyāpi paramaṃ sāraṃ mālinīvijayottaram |
37.25 . uktaṃ śrīratnamālāyāmetacca parameśinā || 25
37.26 . aśeṣatantrasāraṃ tu vāmadakṣiṇamāśritam |
37.26 . ekatra militaṃ kaulaṃ śrīṣaḍardhakaśāsane || 26
37.27 . siddhānte karma bahulaṃ malamāyādirūṣitam |
37.27 . dakṣiṇaṃ raudrakarmāḍhyaṃ vāmaṃ siddhisamākulam || 27
37.28 . svalpapuṇyaṃ bahukleśaṃ svapratītivivarjitam |
37.28 . mokṣavidyāvihīnaṃ ca vinayaṃ tyaja dūrataḥ || 28
37.29 . yasminkāle ca guruṇā nirvikalpaṃ prakāśitam |
37.29 . muktastenaiva kālena yantraṃ tiṣṭhati kevalam || 29
37.30 . mayaitatsrotasāṃ rūpamanuttarapadāddhruvāt |
37.30 . ārabhya vistareṇoktaṃ mālinīślokavārtike || 30
37.31 . jijñāsustata evedamavadhārayituṃ kṣamaḥ |
37.31 . vayaṃ tūktānuvacanamaphalaṃ nādriyāmahe || 31
37.32 . itthaṃ dadadanāyāsājjīvanmuktimahāphalam |
37.32 . yathepsitamahābhogadātṛtvena vyavasthitam || 32
37.33 . ṣaḍardhasāraṃ sacchāstramupādeyamidaṃ sphuṭam |
37.33 . ṣaṭtriṃśatā tattvabalena sūtā yadyapyanantā bhuvanāvalīyam |
37.33 . brahmāṇḍamatyantamanoharaṃ tu vaicitryavarjaṃ nahi ramyabhāvaḥ || 33
37.34 . bhūrādisaptapurapūrṇatame'pi tasminmanye dvitīyabhuvanaṃ bhavanaṃ sukhasya |
37.34 . kvānyatra citragatisūryaśaśāṅkaśobhirātrindivaprasarabhogavibhāgabhūṣā || 34
37.35 . tatrāpica tridivabhogamahārghavarṣadvīpāntarādadhikameva kumārikāhvam |
37.35 . yatrādharādharapadātparamaṃ śivāntamāroḍhumapyadhikṛtiḥ kṛtināmanarghā || 35
37.36 . prākkarmabhogipaśutocitabhogabhājā kiṃ janmanā nanu sukhaikapade'pi dhāmni |
37.36 . sarvo hi bhāvini paraṃ paritoṣameti saṃbhāvite natu nimeṣiṇi vartamāne || 36
37.37 . kanyāhvaye'pi bhuvane'tra paraṃ mahīyān deśaḥ sa yatra kila śāstravarāṇi cakṣuḥ |
37.37 . jātyandhasadmani na janma na ko'bhinindedbhinnāñjanāyitaravipramukhaprakāśe || 37
37.38 . niḥśeṣaśāstrasadanaṃ kila madhyadeśastasminnajāyata guṇābhyadhiko dvijanmā |
37.38 . ko'pyatrigupta iti nāmaniruktagotraḥ śāstrābdhicarvaṇakalodyadagastyagotraḥ || 38
37.39 . tamatha lalitādityo rājā nijaṃ puramānayatpraṇayarabhasātkaśmīrākhyaṃ himālayamūrdhagam |
37.39 . adhivasati yadgaurīkāntaḥ karairvijayādibhiryugapadakhilaṃ bhogāsāraṃ rasātparicarcitum || 39
37.40 . sthāne sthāne munibhirakhilaiścakrire yannivāsā yaccādhyāste pratipadamidaṃ sa svayaṃ candracūḍaḥ |
37.40 . tanmanye'haṃ samabhilaṣitāśeṣasiddhernasiddhyai kaśmīrebhyaḥ paramatha puraṃ pūrṇavṛtterna tuṣṭyai || 40
37.41 . yatra svayaṃ śāradacandraśubhrā śrīśāradeti prathitā janeṣu |
37.41 . śāṇḍilyasevārasasuprasannā sarvaṃ janaṃ svairvibhavairyunakti || 41
37.42 . nāraṅgāruṇakānti pāṇḍuvikacadballāvadātacchavi prodbhinnāmalamātuluṅgakanakacchāyābhirāmaprabham |
37.42 . kerīkuntalakandalīpratikṛtiśyāmaprabhābhāsvaraṃ yasmiñśakticatuṣṭayojjvalamalaṃ madyaṃ mahābhairavam || 42
37.43 . trinayanamahākopajvālāvilīna iha sthito madanaviśikhavrāto madyacchalena vijṛmbhate |
37.43 . kathamitarathā rāgaṃ mohaṃ madaṃ madanajvaraṃ vidadhadaniśaṃ kāmātaṅkairvaśīkurute jagat || 43
37.44 . yatkāntānāṃ praṇayavacasi prauḍhimānaṃ vidatte yannirvighnaṃ nidhuvanavidhau sādhvasaṃ saṃdhunoti |
37.44 . yasmin viśvāḥ kalitarucayo devatāścakracaryastanmārdvīkaṃ sapadi tanute yatra bhogāpabargau || 44
37.45 . udyadgaurāṅkuravikasitaiḥ śyāmaraktaiḥ palāśairantargāḍhāruṇarucilasatkesarālīvicitraiḥ |
37.45 . ākīrṇā bhūḥ pratipadamasau yatra kāśmīrapuṣpaiḥ samyagdevītritayayajanodyānamāviṣkaroti || 45
37.46 . sarvo lokaḥ kaviratha budho yatra śūro'pi vāgmī candroddyotā masṛṇagatayaḥ pauranāryaśca yatra |
37.46 . yatrāṅgārojjvalavikasitānantasauṣumṇamārgagrastārkendurgaganavimalo yoginīnāṃ ca vargaḥ || 46
37.47 . śrīmatparaṃ pravaranāma puraṃ ca tatra yannirmame pravarasena iti kṣitīśaḥ |
37.47 . yaḥ svapratiṣṭhitamaheśvarapūjanānte vyomotpatannudasṛjatkila dhūpaghaṇṭām || 47
37.48 . āndolanoditamanoharavīranādaiḥ sā cāsya tatsucaritaṃ prathayāṃbabhūva |
37.48 . sadvṛttasāragurutaijasamūrtayo hi tyaktā api prabhuguṇānadhikaṃ dhvananti || 48
37.49 . saṃpūrṇacandravimaladyutivīrakāntāgāḍhāṅgarāgaghanakuṅkumapiñjaraśrīḥ |
37.49 . proddhūtavetasalatāsitacāmaraughairājyābhiṣekamaniśaṃ dadatī smarasya || 49
37.50 . rodhaḥpratiṣṭhitamaheśvarasiddhaliṅgasvāyaṃbhuvārcanavilepanagandhapuṣpaiḥ |
37.50 . āvarjyamānatanuvīcinimajjanaughavidhvastapāpmamunisiddhamanuṣyavandyā || 50
37.51 . bhogāpavargaparipūraṇakalpavallī bhogaikadānarasikāṃ surasiddhasindhum |
37.51 . nyakkurvatī harapinākakalāvatīrṇā yadbhūṣayatyavirataṃ taṭinī vitastā || 51
37.52 . tasmin kuverapuracārisiṃtāṃśumaulisāṃmukhyadarśanavirūḍhapavitrabhāve |
37.52 . vaitastarodhasi nivāsamamuṣya cakre rājā dvijasya parikalpitabhūrisaṃpat || 52
37.53 . tasyānvaye mahati ko'pi varāhaguptanāmā babhūva bhagavān svayamantakāle |
37.53 . gīrvāṇasindhulaharīkalitāgramūrdhā yasyākarotparamanugrahamāgraheṇa || 53
37.54 . tasyātmajaścukhalaketi jane prasiddhaścandrāvadātadhiṣaṇo narasiṃhaguptaḥ |
37.54 . yaṃ sarvaśāstrarasamajjanaśubhracittaṃ māheśvarī paramalaṃkurute sma bhaktiḥ || 54
37.55 . tāruṇyasāgarataraṅgabharānapohya vairāgyapotamadhiruhya dṛḍhaṃ haṭhena |
37.55 . yo bhaktirohaṇamavāpya maheśacintāratnairalaṃ dalayati sma bhavāpadastāḥ || 55
37.56 . tasyātmajo'bhinavagupta iti prasiddhaḥ śrīcandracūḍacaraṇābjaparāgapūtaḥ |
37.56 . mātā vyayūyujadamuṃ kila bālya eva daivaṃ hi bhāviparikarmaṇi saṃskaroti || 56
37.57 . mātā paraṃ bandhuriti pravādaḥ snoho'tigāḍhīkurute hi pāśān |
37.57 . tanmūlabandhe galite kilāsya manye sthitā jīvata eva muktiḥ || 57
37.58 . pitrā sa śabdagahane kṛtasaṃpraveśastarkārṇavormipṛṣatāmalapūtacittaḥ |
37.58 . sāhityasāndrarasabhogaparo maheśabhaktyā svayaṃgrahaṇadurmadayā gṛhītaḥ || 58
37.59 . sa tanmayībhūya na lokavartanīmajīgaṇatkāmapi kevalaṃ punaḥ |
37.59 . tadīyasaṃbhogavivṛddhaye purā karoti dāsyaṃ guruveśmasu svayam || 59
37.60 . ānandasaṃtatimahārṇavakarṇadhāraḥ saddaiśikairakavarātmajavāmanāthaḥ |
37.60 . śrīnāthasaṃtatimahāmbaragharmakāntiḥ śrībhūtirājatanayaḥ svapitṛprasādaḥ || 60
37.61 . traiyambakaprasarasāgaraśāyisomānandātmajotpalajalakṣmaṇaguptanāthaḥ |
37.61 . turyākhyasaṃtatimahodadhipūrṇacandraḥ śrīsomataḥ sakalavitkila śaṃbhunāthaḥ || 61
37.62 . śrīcandraśarmabhavabhaktivilāsayogānandābhinandaśivaśaktivicitranāthāḥ |
37.62 . anye'pi dharmaśivavāmanakodbhaṭaśrībhūteśabhāskaramukhapramukhā mahāntaḥ || 62
37.63 . ete sevārasaviracitānugrahāḥ śāstrasārapauḍhādeśaprakaṭasubhagaṃ svādhikāraṃ kilāsmai |
37.63 . yatsaṃprāduryadapi ca janānnaikṣatākṣetrabhūtān svātmārāmastadayamaniśaṃ tattvasevāraso'bhūt || 63
37.64 . so'nugrahītumatha śāṃbhavabhaktibhājaṃ svaṃ bhrātaramakhilaśāstravimarśapūrṇam |
37.64 . yāvanmanaḥ praṇidadhāti manorathākhyaṃ tāvajjanaḥ katipayastamupāsasāda || 64
37.65 . śrīśaurisaṃjñatanayaḥ kila karṇanāmā yo yauvane viditaśāṃbhavatattvasāraḥ |
37.65 . dehaṃ tyajan prathayati sma janasya satyaṃ yogacyutaṃ prati mahāmunikṛṣṇavākyam || 65
37.66 . tadbālamitramatha mantrisutaḥ prasiddhaḥ śrīmandra ityakhilasāraguṇābhirāmaḥ |
37.66 . lakṣmīsarasvati samaṃ yamalaṃcakāra sāpatnakaṃ tirayate subhagaprabhāvaḥ || 66
37.67 . anye pitṛvyatanayāḥ śivaśaktiśubhrāḥ kṣemotpalābhinavacakrakapadmaguptāḥ |
37.67 . ye saṃpadaṃ tṛṇamamaṃsata śaṃbhusevāsaṃpūritaṃ svahṛdayaṃ hṛdi bhāvayantaḥ || 67
37.68 . ṣaḍardhaśāstreṣu samastameva yenādhijagme vidhimaṇḍalādi |
37.68 . sa rāmagupto guruśaṃbhuśāstrasevāvidhivyagrasamagramārgaḥ || 68
37.69 . anyo'pi kaścana janaḥ śivaśaktipātasaṃpreraṇāparavaśasvakaśaktisārthaḥ |
37.69 . abhyarthanāvimukhabhāvamaśikṣitena tenāpyanugrahapadaṃ kṛta eṣa vargaḥ || 69
37.70 . ācāryamabhyarthayate sma gāḍhaṃ saṃpūrṇatantrādhigamāya samyak |
37.70 . jāyeta daivānugṛhītabuddheḥ saṃpatprabandhaikarasaiva saṃpat || 70
37.71 . so'pyabhyupāgamadabhīpsitamasya yadvā svātodyameva hi ninartiṣato'vatīrṇam |
37.71 . so'nugrahapravaṇa eva hi sadgurūṇāmājñāvaśena śubhasūtimahāṅkureṇa || 71
37.72 . vikṣiptabhāvaparihāramatho cikīrṣanmandraḥ svake puravare sthitimasya vavre |
37.72 . ābālagopamapi yatra maheśvarasya dāsyaṃ janaścarati pīṭhanivāsakalpe || 72
37.73 . tasyābhavatkila pitṛvyavadhūrvidhātrā yā nirmame galitasaṃsṛticitracintā |
37.73 . śītāṃśumaulicaraṇābjaparāgamātrabhūṣāvidhirvihitavatsalikocitākhyā || 73
37.74 . mūrtā kṣameva karuṇeva gṛhītadehā dhāreva vigrahavatī śubhaśīlatāyāḥ |
37.74 . vairāgyasāraparipākadaśeva pūrṇā tattvārtharatnarucirasthitirohaṇorvī || 74
37.75 . bhrātāpi tasyāḥ śaśiśubhramaulerbhaktyā paraṃ pāvitacittavṛttiḥ |
37.75 . sa śaurirātteśvaramantribhāvastatyāja yo bhūpatimantribhāvam || 75
37.76 . tasya snuṣā karṇavadhūrvidhūtasaṃsāravṛttiḥ sutamekameva |
37.76 . yāsūta yogeśvaridattasaṃjñaṃ nāmānurūpasphuradarthatattvam || 76
37.77 . yāmagrage vayasi bhartṛviyogadīnāmanvagrahīttrinayanaḥ svayameva bhaktyā |
37.77 . bhāviprabhāvarabhaseṣu janeṣvanarthaḥ satyaṃ samākṛṣati so'rthaparamparāṇām || 77
37.78 . bhaktyullasatpulakatāṃ sphuṭamaṅgabhūṣāṃ śrīśaṃbhunāthanatimeva lalāṭikāṃ ca |
37.78 . śaivaśrutiṃ śravaṇabhūṣaṇamapyavāpya saubhāgyamabhyadhikamudvahati sma yāntaḥ || 78
37.79 . ambābhidhānā kila sā guruṃ taṃ svaṃ bhrātaraṃ śaṃbhudṛśābhyapaśyat |
37.79 . bhāviprabhāvojjvalabhavyabuddhiḥ sato'vajānāti na bandhubuddhyā || 79
37.80 . bhrātā tadīyo'bhinavaśca nāmnā na kevalaṃ saccaritairapi svaiḥ |
37.80 . pītena vijñānarasena yasya tatraiva tṛṣṇā vavṛdhe nikāmam || 80
37.81 . so'nyaśca śāṃbhavamarīcicayapraṇaśyatsaṃkocahārdanalinīghaṭitojjvalaśrīḥ |
37.81 . taṃ lumpakaḥ paricacāra samudyameṣu sādhuḥ samāvahati hanta karāvalambam || 81
37.82 . itthaṃ gṛhe vatsalikāvitīrṇe sthitaḥ samādhāya matiṃ bahūni |
37.82 . pūrvaśrutānyākalayan svabuddhyā śāstrāṇi tebhyaḥ samavāpa sāram || 82
37.83 . sa tannibandhaṃ vidadhe mahārthaṃ yuktyāgamodīritatantratattvam |
37.83 . ālokamāsādya yadīyameṣa lokaḥ sukhaṃ saṃcaritā kriyāsu || 83
37.84 . santo'nugṛhṇīta kṛtiṃ tadīyāṃ hṛhṇīta pūrvaṃ vidhireṣa tāvat |
37.84 . tato'pi gṛhṇātu bhavanmatiṃ sā sadyo'nugṛhṇātu ca tattvadṛṣṭyā || 84
37.85 . idamabhinavaguptaprombhitaṃ śāstrasāraṃ śiva niśamaya tāvatsarvataḥśrotratantraḥ |
37.85 . tava kila nutireṣā sā hi tvadrūpacarcetyabhinavaparituṣṭo lokamātmīkuruṣva || 85
